### [1] [AdaGround: Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming with Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36874)
*Bingchao Wang,Yongcheng Jing,Chengyu Wang,Jun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 GUI grounding 框架 AdaGround，通过按目标尺寸自适应缩放、验证器循环精修与结构化组合定位，在高分辨率小目标场景显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率屏幕上的 GUI grounding 主要失败于小目标，放大（zoom-in）是常见的免训练补救手段；但现有缩放流程存在三个缺陷：(1) 缩放倍率不与目标尺寸匹配；(2) 无条件接受放大后的答案；(3) 从不回溯错误答案。

Method: 免训练三组件框架：(1) Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming——依据模型自身对目标尺寸的估计，为每个样本设定放大倍率、视场与缩放深度；(2) Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement——用单个带标记点击的 Yes/No 验证器，决定每次缩放的保留或拒绝、在缩放链之间选择、触发重新 grounding，并通过存在性查询决定何时弃答；(3) Compositional Structure Grounding——由表头组合出电子表格单元格。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL 模型上，AdaGround 超越当前最优的免训练缩放方法与众多已训练 GUI 模型；在 OSWorld-G 与 ScreenSpot-Pro 上较骨干模型提升 7.4 至 20.6 个点，且增益可迁移至留出的 UI-Vision。

Conclusion: 通过尺寸自适应的缩放策略、闭环验证与结构化解耦定位，可在完全不训练的情况下显著改善高分辨率小目标的 GUI grounding，并具备跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: GUI grounding on high-resolution screens fails mostly on small targets, and zooming in is a common training-free remedy. However, most existing zooming pipelines do not match the zoom to the target's size, accept the zoomed answer unconditionally and never revisit a wrong answer. To address these issues, we propose AdaGround, a training-free framework with three components: (1) Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming, which sets the magnification, field of view and zoom depth of each sample from the model's own estimate of the target size; (2) Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement, which uses one marked-click Yes/No verifier to keep or reject each zoom, choose between zoom chains, trigger re-grounding and, with an existence query, decide when to abstain; and (3) Compositional Structure Grounding, which composes spreadsheet cells from their headers. On Qwen3-VL models, AdaGround surpasses state-of-the-art training-free zooming methods and many trained GUI models, improving accuracy over the backbone by 7.4 to 20.6 points on OSWorld-G and ScreenSpot-Pro, and its gains carry over to held-out UI-Vision. Code is available at https://github.com/bcwang-sjtu/AdaGround

</details>


### [2] [Cross-modal Steering Vectors Exist Across Both Image and Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36269)
*Donghao Zeng,Nishant Subramani,Tamar Rott Shaham,Benno Krojer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在统一多模态模型中验证引导向量的零样本跨模态迁移，表明语言与图像生成共享概念与机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型中的概念与机制究竟是跨模态统一表征，还是各模态独立实现？已有研究多基于表征相似性等相关性证据，缺乏因果层面的检验。

Method: 提出"跨模态引导"框架：先在某一模态的token上寻找能成功控制生成的引导向量，再将其零样本加到另一模态token上检验是否同样生效；在具备共享参数的多模态统一模型（UMMs）上，针对情感、物体尺寸、正式/口语语言风格等多样概念，双向测试文本→图像与图像→文本的迁移，并分析其几何特征。

Result: 引导向量可双向零样本迁移；即使高度模态特有的概念，也能在另一模态诱发一致且可解释的效果（如正式服装与设计风格）；尽管存在明显模态间隙，跨模态迁移性强的引导向量对同一概念指向相近方向，呈现几何签名。

Conclusion: 统一多模态模型中视觉与语言共享部分概念表征与控制机制，为更通用、模态无关的行为控制方法提供了依据。

Abstract: Are concepts and mechanisms unified across modalities in current AI models, or instead implemented separately for each one? We operationalize this broad question of modality unification by studying cross-modal steering: 1) finding vectors that successfully steer generation on tokens of one modality, which then 2) transfer zero-shot to steer generation when added to tokens of another modality. Our study goes beyond simple correlational alignment (e.g. representational similarity) because steering is fundamentally causal. We study cross-modal steering in several unified multimodal models (UMMs), since they possess shared parameters for language and image generation, focusing on these two modalities. Across diverse steered concepts such as emotion and object size, we show that steering vectors transfer zero-shot in both directions (text-to-image & image-to-text). Even for concepts that are highly unique to one modality (e.g. formal vs. colloquial language), steering vectors often induce a consistent, interpretable effect in the other modality, such as formal clothing and design. This transfer has a geometric signature too: despite a clear modality gap separating text and image representations, steering vectors with strong cross-modal transferability tend to point in similar directions for the same concept. Our findings shed light on how vision and language interact inside unified multimodal models and point towards more general, modality-agnostic approaches for controlling their behavior.

</details>


### [3] [Representing the Unseen: Rethinking Visual Imagination for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37437)
*Zengqi Zhao,Zun Wang,Shoubin Yu,Mohit Bansal,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImagiRep基准系统比较RGB与六种替代表征在空间想象中的作用，发现准确想象RGB并不保证准确推理，GEARS自适应表征选择的测试时策略优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型普遍以生成RGB视图作为支持空间推理的默认接口，但RGB的流行主要源于其便于视觉生成，而非因其对下游推理最优。同一想象状态的不同空间问题可能需要不同表征。

Method: 构建ImagiRep——基于模拟器的922题基准，涵盖7类问题、2种设定（视角想象Viewpoint Imagination与跨视角整合Cross-view Integration），对同一未观测状态提供oracle RGB及六种替代表征（深度、全景视图、俯视几何等），并考察由世界模型估计状态得到的表征。基于此提出GEARS轻量测试时自适应表征选择策略。

Result: 受控比较显示：即使想象RGB准确，也不能保证空间推理准确；不同问题受益于不同表征；该趋势在世界模型估计状态上同样成立；仅用估计RGB可能误导推理，而替代表征保留互补优势；GEARS优于现有空间想象方法。

Conclusion: 对未观测状态的推理不能仅依赖生成RGB视图，表征选择是空间想象的关键环节，需针对问题自适应选择表征。

Abstract: Visual world models increasingly support spatial reasoning by generating RGB views of unobserved states. However, RGB has become the default interface largely because it is well suited for visual generation, rather than necessarily being the most effective representation for downstream reasoning. Different spatial questions may benefit from different representations of the same imagined state: top-down geometry can facilitate global distance comparisons, while panoramic views provide broader context for reasoning about open space. To isolate the role of representation in spatial imagination, we introduce ImagiRep, a simulator-grounded benchmark of 922 questions spanning seven question types across two settings, Viewpoint Imagination and Cross-view Integration, providing oracle RGB and six alternative representations (e.g., depth, panoramic views, and top-down geometry) of the same unobserved state. This controlled comparison reveals that even accurate imagined RGB does not guarantee accurate spatial reasoning, and different questions benefit from different representations. Similar trends hold for representations derived from world-model-estimated states. Notably, estimated RGB alone can mislead spatial reasoning, while alternative representations retain complementary benefits. Building on these findings, we introduce GEARS, a lightweight test-time strategy for adaptive representation selection, outperforming existing spatial imagination methods and showing that reasoning about unseen states requires more than generating RGB views.

</details>


### [4] [Zooming without Zooming v2: From Seeing Details to Connecting Evidence for Generalizable Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37037)
*Lai Wei,Yatai Li,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Renjie Gu,Linhan Cao,Junkai Lin,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Linghe Kong,Yue Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出数据合成框架 EvidenceConnect，通过单图内相关区域的放大视图生成"连接证据"类问题，配合答案一致性过滤与区域消融校验保证质量，并用 RLVR 训练得到 ZwZ2，在细粒度/空间感知及多个下游任务上取得泛化提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间感知与多模态长上下文理解是具身智能与多模态智能体的关键能力，但针对这两类任务合成高质量训练数据困难且昂贵。作者观察到二者共享"连接证据（connecting evidence）"这一底层能力——定位相关视觉事实、绑定到正确实体、恢复关系并组合成答案，而该能力对应的数据远比对任务本身的数据更易合成。

Method: EvidenceConnect：利用单张图像中相关区域的放大视图生成需比较、聚合或链式推理视觉证据的问题。质量控制采用两道机制：(1) 答案一致性——仅当多个独立 VLM 给出相同答案时保留参考答案；(2) 区域消融测试——移除所选区域后若答案仍成立则丢弃该问题。随后在合成数据上用 RLVR 训练基础 VLM，得到 Zooming without Zooming v2 (ZwZ2)。

Result: 尽管合成数据未针对任何下游基准定制，ZwZ2 仍提升了细粒度与空间感知能力，并在具身空间推理、多模态长上下文理解、多模态智能体任务及通用视觉推理上获得进一步增益。

Conclusion: EvidenceConnect 是监督"证据连接"能力的有效框架；强化这一共享底层能力可带来更具泛化性的多模态感知提升。

Abstract: Multimodal perception forms the foundation of multimodal intelligence. In particular, spatial perception and multimodal long-context understanding are two pivotal tasks that underpin embodied AI and multimodal agents. However, synthesizing targeted, high-quality training data for these tasks remains difficult and costly. Our key insight is that these tasks share an underlying capability, which we call connecting evidence: identifying relevant visual facts, binding them to the correct entities, recovering their relations, and composing them into an answer. Crucially, targeted training data are far easier to synthesize for this capability than for these tasks themselves. To strengthen this capability, we propose EvidenceConnect, an easy-to-implement data synthesis framework that uses zoomed views of related regions within a single image to generate questions requiring comparison, aggregation, or chaining of visual evidence. To ensure quality, a reference answer is retained only when independent VLMs agree on it, and region-ablation tests discard questions whose answer survives the removal of selected regions. We then train base VLMs on the resulting data with RLVR, obtaining Zooming without Zooming v2 (ZwZ2). Although the synthesized data are not tailored to any downstream benchmark, ZwZ2 improves fine-grained and spatial perception, with further gains on embodied spatial reasoning, multimodal long-context understanding, multimodal agent tasks, and general visual reasoning. These results establish EvidenceConnect as an effective framework for supervising evidence connection and suggest that strengthening this shared capability leads to more generalizable multimodal perception.

</details>


### [5] [Mining the Nineteenth Century for the Thirteenth: Register-Scale Forced Alignment and HTR Bootstrapping via Legacy Regesta and Antiquarian Editions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36956)
*Tamas Kovacs,Daniel Luger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文评估19世纪旧学术成果（Pressutti regesta、部分版本、日期等）能否在缺乏现代转写的教宗登记册（Reg. Vat. 9 第3年后）中辅助手写文本识别（HTR），并提出三层分工框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Honorius III登记册的HTR依赖现代专家转写，但仅第1-2年有可用转写；其余仅存Pressutti regesta与部分旧版本。作者希望量化这些"legacy层"对HTR的实际贡献，以指导未转写登记册的识别。

Method: 在第1-203对页（现代转写与旧层并存）上做对照实验：先审计转写质量；正交层比较原始版本与投影到教廷拼写并重放现代源行的零标签自适应；词汇层评估legacy词典的OOV覆盖与纠错能力，并蒸馏预训练中世纪NER模型到识别器NER头；拓扑层用regest、日期子句及罗马历日期定位条目起始，并以版本一致性估计CER。

Result: 正交层：拼写投影+重放使134个第2年页面WER下降1.50点（95% CI -1.84~-1.15），弥合与oracle差距的33%；拼写在年份分界处变化，笔迹仅在fol. 191r明显变化。词汇层：legacy词典覆盖未编辑条目OOV词82.2%，以66.7%精度标记识别错误；蒸馏NER在专家验证基准上F1达82.1%，与识别后最佳标注器持平。拓扑层：regest、结尾日期子句及罗马历日期定位73.9%条目起始；版本一致性估计fol. 203后CER为7.80%。

Conclusion: 旧学术层无法替代现代转写，但可在未转写登记册中起"引导、校验、切分"作用，在正交、词汇、拓扑三层支撑HTR。

Abstract: Handwritten text recognition (HTR) for the registers of Pope Honorius III depends on modern expert transcriptions, which exist only for regnal years 1-2 of Reg. Vat. 9. For the remaining registers, nineteenth-century scholarship offers Pressutti's regesta and partial editions. On fol. 1-203, where both layers exist, we measure what this legacy layer can contribute, on transcriptions that we first audit against it, and find a division of labor across three tiers. At the orthographic tier, raw editions are at best neutral for adapting a recognizer to the second regnal year and harmful without replay, whereas projection onto curial spelling with replay of modern source lines turns them into zero-label adaptation targets: pooled over all 134 year-2 sides, the WER falls by 1.50 points (95% CI -1.84 to -1.15), closing 33% of the gap to an oracle; the spelling changes at the year split, through the scribe or the transcriber, whereas the hand changes visibly only at fol. 191r. At the lexical tier, the corpus-wide legacy lexicon contains 82.2% of the out-of-vocabulary words of unedited entries and flags recognition errors with 66.7% precision, and labels from pre-trained medieval NER models, distilled into the recognizer's NER head, reach F1 82.1% on an expert validated benchmark, level with the best tagger applied after recognition. At the topological tier, Pressutti's regests, the closing datum clauses, and the regests' dates converted to the chancery's Roman form find 73.9% of entry starts in the recognized line stream, and edition agreement estimates a CER of 7.80% beyond fol. 203. Antiquarian scholarship thus cannot replace modern transcription, but it can steer, check, and segment HTR across the untranscribed registers.

</details>


### [6] [Decoupling Consistency and Conflict across MLLM Layers for Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36569)
*Kai Qian,Dongjie Xu,Weijie Shi,Jiaqi Wang,Haolun Ma,Jia Zhu,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多层级一致性与冲突解耦方法MLCCD，利用多模态大模型不同层的差异化知识，捕捉稀疏且层级可变的图文冲突以检测多模态假新闻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态假新闻威胁网络内容可信度。真实新闻图文语义通常一致，假新闻则含少量、层级不一的冲突（物体、场景、事件、主张不匹配）。现有方法以粗粒度、静态方式处理一致性与冲突，难以捕捉稀疏冲突、适应不同冲突强度。

Method: 1) 用MLLM最终决策表示筛选任务相关的视觉/文本token，防止无关内容稀释稀疏冲突；2) 保留在相邻层间表示剧烈变化的token，以获取各层新增知识；3) 将该知识解耦为一致性证据与冲突证据，各用独立router跨层聚合，以适应不同冲突层级。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，MLCCD优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 跨层表示知识可用于细粒度解耦图文一致性与冲突，是对抗多模态假新闻的有效范式。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of Web content faces a growing threat from multimodal fake news. Real news usually shows aligned image-text semantics, while fake news often contains a few conflicts at varying levels, such as mismatches in objects, scenes, events, and claims. However, existing methods usually treat consistency and conflict in a coarse and static way, limiting their ability to capture sparse conflicts and adapt to varying conflict levels. Inspired by the distinct knowledge encoded across layers in large language models, we propose Multi-Level Consistency and Conflict Decoupling (MLCCD), which captures conflicts at different levels from different layers of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Specifically, we first use the MLLM's final decision representation to select visual and textual tokens relevant to the task, preventing irrelevant content from diluting sparse conflicts. Then, we preserve tokens whose representations change sharply between adjacent layers, capturing the knowledge each layer adds. Finally, we decouple this knowledge into consistency and conflict evidence and aggregate each across layers with its own router, adapting to varying conflict levels. Experiments on three benchmarks show that MLCCD outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [7] [Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning with Multi-Branch Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36809)
*Kegeng Tang,Jingbo Wang,Yangming Zhang,Huanshu Zhang,Shaogang Ren,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning，一种分支感知的潜在视觉推理框架，生成多条问题条件化的软假设分支，并用软对比分支-证据对齐目标训练，在3B/7B上优于骨干与单路径基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将中间推理移入视觉潜在空间的方法通常采用单一线性推理轨迹，当存在多个相关视觉假设或竞争性证据区域时受限；复杂视觉推理需定位并比较多处问题相关的视觉证据。

Method: 生成多条问题条件化的潜在视觉分支，作为图像token上的软假设；利用边界框证据标注构建目标证据表示，并引入软对比分支-证据对齐目标，使证据相关分支对齐标注区域、与负证据形成对比；在Qwen2.5-VL骨干上实例化，采用标准SFT训练。

Result: 在感知密集型视觉推理基准上，3B与7B规模均相对对应骨干和单路径潜在推理基线取得整体提升。

Conclusion: 多分支潜在视觉表示配合对比式证据对齐可有效缓解单路径潜在推理的局限，为多证据视觉推理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have made substantial progress in visual question answering, but complex visual reasoning often requires locating and comparing multiple pieces of question-relevant visual evidence. Existing methods that move intermediate reasoning into the visual latent space usually follow a single linear reasoning trajectory, which can be limiting when multiple visual hypotheses or competing evidence regions are relevant. We propose Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning, a branch-aware latent reasoning framework that generates multiple question-conditioned latent visual branches as soft hypotheses over image tokens. To train these branches, our method uses bounding-box evidence annotations to build target evidence representations and introduces a soft contrastive branch-evidence alignment objective. This objective encourages evidence-relevant branches to align with annotated visual regions while contrasting them against negative evidence. We instantiate our method on Qwen2.5-VL backbones and train it with standard supervised fine-tuning. Experiments on perception-intensive visual reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves overall improvements over the corresponding backbone and single-path latent reasoning baselines at both 3B and 7B scales.

</details>


### [8] [SOMA: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Egocentric Behavior and Body-Motion Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36384)
*Matea Tashkovska,Naël Ouerghemi,Haozhe Qi,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出SOMA基准，用于评测多模态大模型在第一人称视角下对人体精细运动的理解能力，覆盖身体构型、运动动力学与时间推理三个维度，共1,632题；37个模型评测显示最强模型零样本准确率仅50.0%，显著低于人类的68.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评测集中于物体、场景与粗粒度动作，缺乏对身体本身运动推理的评估；第一人称视角下身体仅部分可见、线索被遮挡且时间分散，使该任务尤为困难，而相关能力评估基本空白。

Method: 构建SOMA基准：1,632道人工校验的第一人称视频问答，分属身体构型、运动动力学、时间推理三类能力轴；评测37个闭源、开源及专用第一人称MLLM，并辅以大规模人类评测；进行误差分析；同时提出对齐的视频—身体运动—语言训练数据集及联合学习多模态的基线模型。

Result: 人类仅凭视频准确率为68.5%，任务本身困难；最强模型Qwen3.6-27B零样本仅50.0%。误差分析显示模型常误读佩戴者自身身体、误判事件时间顺序，并存在偏好右手、漏数重复次数等偏差。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在身体运动理解上存在明显能力缺口与系统性失败模式；作者通过公开基准、训练数据与多模态基线缩小与人类的差距，并期望推动身体运动理解与多模态推理的后续研究。

Abstract: Perceiving human body motion is critical for multimodal systems that understand and assist people. Yet current evaluations of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) largely focus on objects, scenes, and coarse actions, leaving their ability to reason about the body itself poorly assessed. This is especially challenging from the egocentric perspective, in which the body is only partially visible and motion must be inferred from occluded and temporally distributed cues. We introduce SOMA, a benchmark for fine-grained body-motion understanding from egocentric video, comprising 1,632 human-verified questions across three capability axes: body configuration, motion dynamics, and temporal reasoning. We evaluate 37 leading closed-source, open-source, and specialized egocentric MLLMs, complemented by an extensive human evaluation. We find that humans also find this task difficult from video alone (68.5% accuracy), yet clearly outperform the strongest MLLM (Qwen3.6-27B), which reaches 50.0% zero-shot accuracy. Our error analysis shows that MLLMs often misread the wearer's own body, misjudge events in time, and are prone to biases such as favoring the right hand or under-counting repetitions. To narrow the gap to human performance and support further progress, we introduce a training dataset of aligned video, body motion, and language supervision, and a multimodal baseline that jointly learns from these modalities. By identifying common failure modes and missing capabilities in current MLLMs, we hope SOMA will inspire future research on body-motion understanding and multimodal reasoning. Full benchmark, training data, and code will be released at: https://cnai.epfl.ch/soma

</details>


### [9] [Learning to Read Braille: Benchmarking and Improving Braille Recognition Capabilities of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36195)
*Kartik Jangra,Atharva Gawane,Siddharth Pulipaka,Hemachandran Karah,Raj Dabre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出盲文图像识别基准 BrailleBench（259 张真实盲文图像），揭示通用 VLM 直接转写盲文能力不足，并证明针对性的合成数据监督可显著提升真实场景盲文转写性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉理解与文档转写进展迅速，但现代视觉语言模型（VLM）在从图像中读取盲文这一基础无障碍任务上表现薄弱，且缺乏真实场景评测基准。

Method: 1) 人工构建 BrailleBench 基准，含 259 张真实一级、二级盲文图像；2) 系统评测闭源、开源权重、OCR 专用及工具辅助的多模态系统；3) 设计含多样版面与图像扰动的合成盲文生成流水线，并用其训练 2B 规模 VLM。

Result: 独立通用大模型直接转写盲文表现差，工具辅助系统明显更好；2B VLM 在合成数据训练后在 BrailleBench 上取得一级 87%、二级 61% 的完全匹配率，超过独立 VLM 并在二级盲文上取得所评测系统中的最高完全匹配分数；同时发现先前合成数据训练的模型在其合成验证集上可达 96% 而在真实一级 BrailleBench 上至多 3.26%，即合成高精度不保证真实照片精度。

Conclusion: 研究指出多模态系统在无障碍相关能力上存在明显缺口，并表明针对性合成监督能有效提升真实世界盲文转写性能，但合成验证精度与真实照片泛化之间存在显著鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in visual understanding and document transcription, modern vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with a fundamental accessibility task: reading Braille from images. We introduce BrailleBench, a manually curated benchmark of 259 real-world Grade-1 and Grade-2 Braille images. We evaluate proprietary, open-weight, OCR-specialized, and tool-assisted multimodal systems and find that standalone general-purpose models perform poorly at directly transcribing Braille images, whereas tool-assisted systems perform much better. We design a synthetic Braille generation pipeline with diverse layouts and image perturbations. A 2B VLM model trained on our synthetic data reaches 87% exact match on Grade-1 and 61% on Grade-2 BrailleBench, outperforming standalone VLMs and achieving the highest Grade-2 exact match score among the evaluated systems. We further show that high accuracy on synthetic validation data does not guarantee high accuracy on real photographs in our experiments: models trained on prior synthetic data reach up to 96% on their synthetic validation set but at most 3.26% on Grade-1 BrailleBench. Our results identify an accessibility-relevant capability gap in multimodal systems and show that targeted synthetic supervision can improve real-world Braille transcription.

</details>


### [10] [Physiognomy Revisited: How Vision-Language Models Encode Sensitive Attributes Through Image Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35910)
*Soumyajit Datta,Arka Dutta,Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示两个主流视觉语言模型（VLM）在图像生成中会按"受保护身份"（种族、性取向、犯罪性）转换人脸，遵从/拒绝行为不确定且群体敏感，生成图像统计上可区分并强化刻板印象，且这些偏见会回流到文本推理任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在带来收益的同时存在难以预见的失败模式，且这些失败模式可能对弱势群体造成不成比例的伤害；作者希望系统检验VLM在生成图像时如何编码受保护身份属性。

Method: 选取两个知名视觉语言模型，要求其将人脸图像转换为特定种族身份（如西班牙裔、黑人、白人、亚裔）与性取向（如同性恋/异性恋）；系统地测量模型的遵从率与拒绝率、结果的非确定性与群体敏感性，并通过统计分析检验生成图像是否可区分；进一步检验图像生成阶段引入的表征偏见是否会迁移到文本推理与人物刻画任务，并扩展到"犯罪性"维度。

Result: 模型对上述转换请求往往予以遵从；遵从与拒绝行为具有非确定性且随群体不同而变化；生成图像在统计上可被区分，并常常放大人脸相关的深层刻板印象；图像生成中引入的偏见会在文本推理与人物刻画中重新出现；该问题不限于种族与性取向，在犯罪性维度上同样存在，带来AI辅助画像（profiling）的风险。

Conclusion: VLM的图像生成存在系统性的身份表征偏见，且偏见具有跨模态扩散性，构成对弱势群体的潜在伤害与滥用于画像的风险，需引起关注与治理。

Abstract: Emergent generative AI technologies, while remarkably beneficial across several tasks and domains, can suffer from unforeseen vulnerabilities and difficult-to-anticipate failure modes. Such failure modes can disparately affect vulnerable communities. This paper investigates two well-known vision language models (VLMs) and inspects how these models encode protected identities while generating images. We show that when VLMs are requested to transform human face images to reflect race identities (e.g., Hispanic, Black, White, or Asian) and sexual orientation (e.g., gay or straight), VLMs often comply with such requests. We demonstrate that compliance (and refusal) is non-deterministic, group-sensitive, and generated images are statistically distinguishable, often accentuating deep-seated stereotypes. We further show that representation biases introduced during image generation can re-emerge in textual reasoning and characterization tasks. Finally, we show that this worrisome finding is not limited to racial identity or sexual orientation, image generation can perpetuate bias around criminality, raising concerning possibilities of AI-assisted profiling.

</details>


### [11] [PRISM-BENCH: Diagnosing Grounded and Faithful Multimodal Reasoning Beyond Final-Answer Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36554)
*Vansh Bhatia,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Parsa Ashrafi Fashi,Igor Gilitschenski,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PRISM-BENCH 基准（1000 道专家标注题，附标准化推理轨迹），用"感知探针"和"轨迹对齐"两项诊断揭示前沿 VLM 约 85% 的失败源于视觉感知/grounding 错误，并引入效率感知指标 CORE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只看最终答案准确率，无法判断答案是否有图像证据支撑、所陈述的推理是否真的反映了这些证据，以及得出答案的代价。而这些恰恰是系统能否落地的关键属性。

Method: 1) 构建 PRISM-BENCH：1,000 道专家筛选问题，覆盖五类代表性不足的推理类别，每题附一名标注员撰写、另一名标注员校验的理想化推理轨迹；2) 感知探针：让模型基于自己写出的图像描述作答，以归因错误来源；3) 轨迹对齐度：衡量模型推理与理想轨迹的一致性，并做长度控制检验；4) 提出 CORE 效率感知指标。

Result: 感知探针将前沿模型约 85% 的失败归因于感知错误，瓶颈在视觉接地；轨迹对齐度可预测全部 25 个系统的答案正确性（macro AUROC 0.66），且在控制长度后依然成立；CORE 相对准确率重排 24.2% 的模型对且与回答长度不相关；25 个开放/闭源系统中最高准确率仅 66.0%；在有人类基线的四个类别上，最佳系统落后人类 18.4 分；近期前沿进展主要来自识别能力而非空间接地。

Conclusion: 单纯准确率不足以刻画 VLM 的可部署性，需同时评估感知接地、推理忠实性与效率；PRISM-BENCH 表明当前系统的核心短板是视觉感知而非推理，且空间接地能力未见实质提升。

Abstract: Final-answer accuracy reports whether a vision-language model was right, but not whether its answer was grounded in the image, whether its stated reasoning reflects that evidence, or what the answer cost to produce. Those are the properties that decide whether a system can be deployed. We present PRISM-BENCH, a benchmark of 1,000 expert-curated questions spanning five under-represented reasoning categories, in which every item carries an idealized reasoning trace written by one human annotator and validated by another. The traces support two diagnostics that accuracy cannot provide. A perception probe, in which the model answers from a description it wrote itself, attributes roughly 85% of frontier-model failures to misperception and places the bottleneck at grounding. Trace alignment predicts answer correctness for all 25 systems tested (macro AUROC 0.66) and survives length control, so it measures reasoning content and not verbosity. We further introduce CORE, an efficiency-aware metric that reorders 24.2% of model pairs relative to accuracy while remaining uncorrelated with response length. Across 25 open and closed systems, including GPT-5 and Claude Opus 4.6, the best accuracy is 66.0%. On the four categories with a human baseline the best system trails humans by 18.4 points, and recent frontier gains fall on recognition and not on spatial grounding.

</details>


### [12] [Uncovering Shortcut Amplification in Video RLVR through the Lens of Perceptual Uncertainty Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37211)
*Jiafeng Liang,Xianhao Ou,Tao Ren,Rongchuan Mu,Runxuan Liu,Dandan Tu,Ming Liu,See-Kiong Ng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示视频RLVR存在"捷径放大"与"感知不确定性坍缩"现象，即训练主要压缩文本先验而非利用视觉证据，并提出双焦自蒸馏策略优化（BSPO）以重塑优势函数，提升视频推理与视觉忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升大型多模态模型的视频推理能力，但结果级（outcome-level）优化会放大捷径：模型愈发偏好文本先验答案，即使视觉证据并不支持。作者希望刻画该行为的训练动力学机制。

Method: 从感知不确定性视角分析训练动力学：对比视频条件与纯文本条件下的预测不确定性演化，并进行梯度方向分析。基于发现提出BSPO：以组内正确/错误rollout提炼经验推理原则、定位推理敏感token；引入捷径感知的视觉扰动，惩罚在关键视觉证据被削弱时仍不变的预测；通过自适应重加权原始RL优势实现自蒸馏正则。

Result: 分析发现"感知不确定性坍缩"：RL持续锐化答案分布，而视觉证据带来的不确定性下降逐步减弱；梯度方向分析表明置信度提升与视觉基础的 uncertainty reduction 对齐度下降，转而由文本先验压缩主导。实验验证BSPO提升视频推理性能并增强视觉忠实性。

Conclusion: 结果级RL优化存在系统性视觉捷径风险，需在优化信号层面引入视觉基础约束；BSPO在保留RLVR任务收益的同时，促使更新兼具推理敏感性与视觉依据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for enhancing the video reasoning capability of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs). Despite its empirical effectiveness, we show that outcome-level optimization can introduce an undesirable shortcut amplification effect, whereby models increasingly favor text-prior answers even when they are not supported by the visual evidence. To characterize this behavior, we investigate the training dynamics of video RLVR through the lens of perceptual uncertainty, analyzing how predictive uncertainty evolves under video-conditioned and text-only contexts. Our analysis identifies a perceptual uncertainty collapse phenomenon, in which RL training continuously sharpens the model's answer distribution while the uncertainty reduction attributable to visual evidence progressively diminishes. A further gradient-direction analysis reveals that such confidence enhancement becomes increasingly less aligned with visually grounded uncertainty reduction and is instead dominated by text-prior compression. Motivated by these findings, we propose Bifocal Self-distillation Policy Optimization (BSPO), a self-distillation framework that regulates policy updates with two complementary distributional signals. Specifically, BSPO derives empirical reasoning principles from intra-group correct and incorrect rollouts to identify reasoning-sensitive tokens, and introduces shortcut-aware visual perturbation to penalize predictions that remain invariant when task-relevant visual evidence is weakened. By adaptively reweighting the original RL advantage, BSPO preserves the task-solving benefits of RLVR while promoting updates that are both reasoning-sensitive and visually grounded. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of BSPO in improving video reasoning performance and strengthening visual faithfulness.

</details>


### [13] [Evaluating AlphaEarth Embeddings for Crop Classification: Separability, Temporal Dynamics, and Cross-Regional Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36857)
*Priyanka Goyal,Maitreyee Sar,Adway Mitra,Manjira Sinha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一个与表征无关的评估框架，从几何可分性、时间动态、空间鲁棒性与跨区域泛化四个维度系统比较AlphaEarth 64维嵌入与Sentinel-2反射率、NDVI物候、谐波特征在作物分类中的表现，揭示表征丰富性与跨区域迁移性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感农业监测正从像素级光谱分析转向表征驱动建模，但针对学习型表征在空间、时间与域泛化维度上的系统评估仍显不足；地理空间基础模型生成的高维嵌入对农业应用的效用尚未被充分理解。

Method: 构建表征不可知的评估框架，整合几何可分性（Fisher比、Bhattacharyya距离、Mahalanobis距离）、时间动态、空间鲁棒性（残差空间自相关）与跨区域泛化（美国→印度）四项指标；使用2017–2023年美国本土（CONUS）与2021年北印度田间尺度数据，对比AlphaEarth 64维嵌入与传统特征（Sentinel-2反射率、NDVI物候、谐波特征）。

Result: 传统特征线性可分性强（Fisher比）；嵌入在分布感知分离上更优，光谱相似类别（玉米/大豆）的Bhattacharyya与Mahalanobis距离高出一个数量级（3.9 vs. 0.10）。嵌入独特捕捉多年轮作动态，可分性比达1.784，而所有工程特征最高仅1.003；嵌入的误差残差空间自相关更低（平均Moran's I≈0.17）。但谐波与NDVI特征跨大陆迁移性更强（美国→印度Macro F1：0.493 vs. 嵌入0.326），体现更好的分布对齐。组合特征空间性能均衡，支持混合建模。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了表征丰富性与跨区域迁移性之间的根本权衡，为大规模作物分类中的特征选择提供了结构化框架，并支持混合（嵌入+工程特征）建模策略。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO)-based agricultural monitoring is shifting from pixel-level spectral analysis toward representation-driven modeling, yet systematic evaluation of learned representations across spatial, temporal, and domain-generalization dimensions remains limited. Geospatial foundation models generate high-dimensional embeddings encoding spatio-temporal land-surface properties, but their utility for agricultural applications remains insufficiently understood. This study presents a representation-agnostic evaluation framework integrating geometric separability, temporal dynamics, spatial robustness, and cross-regional generalisation. A 64-dimensional AlphaEarth embedding space is benchmarked against Sentinel-2 reflectance, NDVI-derived phenology, and harmonic features using field-scale data from the Conterminous United States (CONUS; 2017-2023) and Northern India (2021).
  Traditional features achieve strong linear separability (Fisher ratio), while embeddings provide superior distribution-aware separation -- Bhattacharyya and Mahalanobis distances up to an order of magnitude higher for spectrally similar pairs (e.g. Corn/Soybeans: 3.9 vs. 0.10). Embeddings uniquely capture multi-year crop rotation dynamics, reaching a separability ratio of 1.784 versus a maximum of 1.003 across all engineered features, and exhibit lower residual spatial autocorrelation of errors (mean Moran's $I \approx 0.17$). However, harmonic and NDVI features show stronger cross-continental transferability (US $\to$ India Macro F1: 0.493 vs. 0.326 for embeddings), reflecting better distributional alignment. The combined feature space yields balanced performance, supporting hybrid modeling. These findings reveal a fundamental trade-off between representational richness and cross-region transferability, providing a structured framework for feature selection in large-scale crop classification.

</details>


### [14] [PerceptRL: Making Visual Perception Rewardable in MLLMs from Answers to Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37471)
*Tongkun Guan,Zhibo Yang,Mingkun Yang,Haocheng Wang,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptRL 通过在 MLLM 后训练中引入感知强化学习（单轮感知 RL + 多轮感知智能体 RL）与坐标注入、定位感知过程奖励，将稠密图像描述中的可验证错误转化为带规则奖励的 QA 训练信号，在七个基准上平均准确率达 71.6%，超过同工具基线 15.7 个百分点、最强前沿 VLM 5.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型后训练阶段细粒度视觉感知仍是瓶颈，感知错误会限制以推理为核心的优化收益。图像-描述预训练虽能提升感知，但其影响会随后续阶段数据配比与目标变化而衰减。后训练中继续使用描述监督的难点在于：描述主观且信息稠密，难以设计可验证的奖励。

Method: 提出两级感知强化：1）Perception RL 用多智能体流水线从稠密图像描述中提取经核验的错误，并将对应视觉事实转为固定答案标签、规则化奖励的 QA 对；Chain QA 关联相关错误，多选题 QA 组合不相关错误；设专门路由针对高错误领域并定制验证，另设领域无关的通用路由覆盖其余错误。2）Perception Agentic RL 通过多轮视觉工具检查处理单轮难以解决的细粒度与稠密情形；针对工具使用模型仍存在的坐标漂移（错误裁剪/标注扭曲证据并传播误差），采用 Coordinate Injection 修正冷启动轨迹中的空间操作，并用定位感知的过程奖励在终答正确性之外监督中间空间保真度。训练流程为共享冷启动 SFT → 单轮 Perception RL → 多轮 Perception Agentic RL。

Result: 在统一工具使用协议下的七个基准上，PerceptRL 平均准确率 71.6%，分别超过使用相同工具的基座模型 15.7 个百分点、超过所评估的最强前沿 VLM 5.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将稠密描述中的可验证感知错误转化为规则奖励 QA，并结合智能体式多轮工具检查与空间操作监督，可有效缓解 MLLM 后训练的细粒度感知瓶颈；该方案在统一工具协议下优于基座模型与强前沿 VLM，表明感知层面的强化对后训练收益具有独立且显著的贡献。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception remains a bottleneck during post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as perceptual errors can constrain gains from reasoning-focused optimization. Although image-caption pre-training improves perception, its influence can weaken as subsequent stages change data mixtures and objectives. Continuing caption supervision during post-training is challenging because subjective, information-dense descriptions complicate verifiable reward design. We present PerceptRL, which strengthens perception at two complementary levels. Perception RL uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract verified errors from dense image captions and convert the corresponding visual facts into QA pairs with fixed answer labels and rule-based rewards. Chain QA links correlated errors, while multiple-choice QA combines unrelated errors. A specialized route targets high-error domains with tailored verification, and a domain-agnostic general route covers errors beyond these domains. Perception Agentic RL addresses fine-grained and dense cases unresolved by single-turn Perception RL through multi-turn inspection with visual tools. We observe coordinate drift even in models trained to use tools, where incorrect crops or annotations distort evidence and propagate errors. Coordinate Injection corrects spatial operations in cold-start trajectories, while a grounding-aware process reward supervises intermediate spatial fidelity alongside final-answer correctness. Training proceeds through shared cold-start SFT, single-turn Perception RL, and multi-turn Perception Agentic RL. Across seven benchmarks under a shared tool-use protocol, PerceptRL achieves 71.6% average accuracy, surpassing the base model with the same tools and the strongest evaluated frontier VLM by 15.7 and 5.2 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond the Estimate: Wiener-Informed Decoding for Easier Training of Pixel-Space Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36192)
*Ziqiao Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Wiener-informed Decoding (WiD)，利用冻结的二阶统计（Wiener 估计）既做直接预测又组织解码器计算，在像素空间加速速度场学习，同等质量下减少约 37.5% 训练轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在像素空间学习速度场困难且缓慢，作者试图探究粗粒度二阶图像统计能否降低该任务的难度。

Method: 先做消融观察：冻结的 Wiener 估计可改善速度学习，且收益超出其线性预测本身；冻结基与像素解码也带来类似互补增益。据此设计 WiD：一条可训练的 Wiener 预测路径，加上系数接口把特征注入原生解码器并回传预测系数修正，各组件用数据统计初始化；频谱与坐标变换等所有新增参数在原始像素空间速度目标下联合训练，且不修改原生模块。

Result: 跨模型规模与解码器架构，WiD 加速训练并提升生成质量，参数开销小；梯度轨迹更平滑，与表征对齐方法兼容；在类条件与文生图、多分辨率上均有效。ImageNet 256×256 上 PixelDiT-XL + WiD 在 500 epoch 达到 FID 1.53，相当于 PixelDiT 少用 37.5% 训练轮次的质量。

Conclusion: 统计去噪器的作用不止于其估计值：其计算结构本身也能缓解像素空间训练，为像素空间生成模型提供一种轻量、模块兼容的训练加速方案。

Abstract: Learning velocity fields directly in pixel space is difficult and slow. We show that coarse second-order image statistics can ease this task: frozen Wiener estimates improve velocity learning, with gains extending beyond the linear predictions they explicitly provide. Moreover, we observe similar but complementary gains from frozen bases and pixel decoding. These observations motivate Wiener-informed Decoding (WiD), which uses statistical knowledge both for direct prediction and to organize decoder computation without modifying native blocks. WiD combines a trainable Wiener prediction path with coefficient interfaces that inject features into the native decoder and return its predicted coefficient corrections, with these components initialized with data statistics. All introduced parameters, including the spectrum and coordinate transforms, are trained jointly under the original pixel-space velocity objective. Across model scales and decoder architectures, WiD accelerates training and improves generation quality with modest parameter overhead. It also yields smoother gradient trajectories and remains effective with representation alignment. WiD improves class-conditional and text-to-image generation across different resolutions. On ImageNet 256 x 256, PixelDiT-XL with WiD reaches FID 1.53 after 500 epochs, achieving comparable quality to PixelDiT with 37.5% fewer training epochs. Statistical denoisers contribute beyond the estimate: their computational structure can also ease pixel-space training.

</details>


### [16] [CrowdCC: Jointly Benchmarking Corruption Robustness and Deployment Quantization in Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36340)
*S Mahmudul Hasan,Lu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrowdCC基准，在部署链路中联合评估23种腐蚀（6类、3档严重度）与FP32/FP16/INT8量化对人群计数模型鲁棒性与成本的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署面临双重偏移：采集—传输链路导致输入退化，而模型需量化以满足资源约束；但腐蚀鲁棒性与部署量化此前被分开评估，缺乏对二者联合作用及真实部署成本的统一考察。

Method: 构建受控腐蚀基准CrowdCC：23种部署动机腐蚀、3档严重度、6个腐蚀族、4个人群计数数据集、26个模型；在A100上以TensorRT引擎按FP32/FP16/PTQ INT8精度评测，并在对应Jetson Orin NX配置上采集吞吐与能耗；几何或可见性变化时同步调整头部标注。

Result: 采集链腐蚀造成的退化最大；人群密度对绝对误差与相对误差影响不同；干净精度不能完全预测腐蚀性能；INT8相比FP16更显著地改变腐蚀鲁棒性且无系统方向，也未必提升延迟或能耗；INT8推理效率与编译引擎中的reformat开销强相关，INT8覆盖不足时亦出现减速；精度会改变腐蚀误差排序与鲁棒性—成本前沿。

Conclusion: 腐蚀鲁棒性与量化效果不可分离评估；模型选型须同时考虑腐蚀鲁棒性与实测部署成本，CrowdCC提供了可复现的联合评测框架与代码。

Abstract: Crowd-counting models deployed on edge devices face two shifts: inputs are degraded along the capture and transmission pipeline, while models are quantized to satisfy resource constraints. Yet corruption robustness and deployment quantization are typically evaluated separately. We introduce CrowdCC, a controlled corruption benchmark that jointly evaluates these effects using 23 deployment-motivated corruptions at three severity levels, four crowd-counting datasets, and 26 models using A100 TensorRT engines at FP32, FP16, and post-training INT8 precision, paired with throughput and energy measurements from corresponding Jetson Orin NX configurations. The corruptions span six families, with head annotations adjusted when geometry or visibility changes. Our evaluation shows that capture-chain corruptions consistently produce the largest degradation across the evaluated models, while crowd density affects absolute and relative error differently. Clean accuracy does not fully predict corrupted performance. INT8 alters corruption robustness substantially more than FP16, with no systematic direction, and does not consistently improve latency or energy. INT8 inference efficiency is strongly associated with reformat overhead in the compiled engine, while slowdowns are also observed under limited INT8 coverage. Precision changes corrupted-error rankings and robustness-cost frontiers, showing why model selection must consider corruption robustness and measured deployment cost together. The code is available at https://github.com/Tulane-Computer-Architecture-Systems/CrowdCC-Release.

</details>


### [17] [GCSR-OCC: Geometry Calibration and Semantic Routing for Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37149)
*Bohua Wang,Congyang Zhao,Tianxiong Lv,Chenxu Zhao,He Zheng,Hangqi Fan,Leigang Luo,Zhihao Liao,Yihong Guo,Yang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GCSR-OCC：通过证据守恒高斯到占据转换与多模式查询路由，实现单目开放词汇三维占据预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯重建再转体素占据的方法存在高斯尺度膨胀、局部重叠过度导致占据失真，以及高斯跨多类别与3D–2D语义对齐中的语义混杂问题。

Method: 提出EC-G2O算子（核质量归一化、p范数聚合、可学习增益）校准占据响应；提出MM-QGSR，为每个高斯匹配多个语义模式到文本查询，并经可学习文本条件路由器聚合查询特定证据到体素；辅以细粒度子类查询与平滑语义聚合。

Result: 在无3D语义标注下，Occ-ScanNet上开放词汇占据预测达SOTA：几何IoU 63.31%，语义mIoU 27.44%；几何聚合延迟由19.76 ms降至12.88 ms。

Conclusion: 几何校准与查询条件语义路由可有效抑制高斯占据失真与语义混合，在不依赖3D语义标注的情况下提升开放词汇占据预测精度与效率。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D occupancy prediction represents scene geometry and semantics in a unified voxel grid, enabling recognition beyond predefined categories. One approach reconstructs scenes with 3D Gaussians and converts them into voxel occupancy. However, Gaussian scale inflation and excessive overlap can distort voxel occupancy predictions, while semantic mixing can arise within Gaussians spanning multiple categories and during 3D--2D semantic feature alignment. We propose GCSR-OCC, which combines geometry calibration and multi-mode query-conditioned semantic routing for indoor occupancy prediction from a single RGB image. The Evidence-Conserving Gaussian-to-Occupancy operator (EC-G2O) uses kernel mass normalization, $p$-norm aggregation, and a learnable gain to suppress scale inflation and excessive local evidence accumulation while calibrating occupancy responses. Multi-mode Query-First Gaussian Semantic Routing (MM-QGSR) matches multiple semantic modes per Gaussian to text queries and aggregates query-specific evidence into voxels through a learnable text-conditioned router. Fine-grained subclass queries and smoothed semantic aggregation further refine voxel-level predictions. Without 3D semantic annotations, GCSR-OCC achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary occupancy prediction on Occ-ScanNet, with 63.31% geometric IoU and 27.44% semantic mIoU. It also reduces geometric aggregation latency from 19.76 ms to 12.88 ms. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [18] [A Lightweight Attention-Based CNN with Saliency-Map Interpretability for Skin Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37314)
*Sanjana Islam Kasfia,Md. Ismiel Hossen Abir,AKM Bahalul Haque,Abir Mahmud Shahariar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种轻量级注意力卷积网络（CNN-Attention），通过通道注意力实现皮肤病变二分类，在87%准确率下仅用195万参数（7.45 MB），参数量与存储约为MobileNet的一半，并以显著性图提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤癌高发且危及生命，早期准确诊断可显著提高生存率；但良恶性病变在皮肤镜图像中视觉相似，人工判读困难。同时现有高精度模型（如VGG、ResNet）参数量与存储开销大，且缺乏决策可解释性。

Method: 构建基于通道注意力的轻量级CNN-Attention模型，使网络聚焦皮肤镜图像中的信息性区域并保持低计算复杂度；使用公开数据集（训练2637张、测试660张），采用平衡类别权重处理类别不平衡；引入显著性图（saliency map）作为可解释性手段；与VGG16、VGG19、ResNet101、MobileNet进行对比分析。

Result: 测试准确率87%，参数量195万、存储7.45 MB，与最佳基线MobileNet精度持平但参数量与存储约为其一半；ROC AUC为0.9475，PR AUC为0.9430；显著性图可直观呈现影响模型判定的图像区域。

Conclusion: 该框架在分类性能、计算效率与可解释性三方面取得较好平衡，验证了轻量注意力机制在皮肤病变自动筛查中的可行性；但结论基于单一公开二分类数据集，泛化性与多类别、临床场景适用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Skin cancer is among the most prevalent and life threatening forms of cancer worldwide. Early and accurate diagnosis significantly improves patient survival rates. However, manual dermoscopic assessment remains challenging due to visual similarity. This study proposes an Explainable AI-driven framework for automated binary classification of skin lesions using a lightweight attention-based convolutional neural network (CNN-Attention). The model integrates channel attention to focus on informative regions of dermoscopic images while maintaining low computational complexity. A publicly available dataset comprising 2,637 training and 660 test images was used, with balanced class weights. The proposed model achieved 87 percent accuracy with 1.95 million parameters and 7.45 MB of storage, matching the best baseline (MobileNet) while using approximately half as many parameters and half as much storage. ROC AUC and PR AUC scores reached 0.9475 and 0.9430, respectively. Additionally, saliency maps were incorporated as an explainability technique to visualize regions that influence model decisions. Comparative analysis with VGG16,VGG19,ResNet101,and MobileNet demonstrates the proposed models effectiveness in balancing classification results, computational efficiency, and interpretability.

</details>


### [19] [Adapting Video Models for Robot Control via Motion-guided Asymmetric Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36624)
*Taeyoung Kim,Junyoung Sung,Heeseung Kwon,Minseop Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoGAP通过不对称分配噪声，将视觉预测监督聚焦于动态区域，以提升世界动作模型（WAM）的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM继承视频预训练的视觉预测目标，对图像所有区域统一处理，但不同区域对机器人控制的关联程度差异显著，标准监督存在空间分配不合理的问题。

Method: 提出运动引导非对称预测（MoGAP）：在共享视频扩散主干上增加仅训练用的辅助预测路径，对动态区域施加更强噪声、静态区域施加较弱噪声，使模型以低损坏静态区域为上下文重建高损坏动态区域；运动线索直接来自训练视频，无需外部教师模型或额外模态。

Result: 在多个机器人操作基准与不同WAM架构上性能一致提升：GR-1桌面任务上Fast-WAM（Wan2.2-5B）成功率38.8%→47.5%，Cosmos3-Nano 52.3%→62.3%。

Conclusion: 视觉监督的空间分配是WAM设计的关键维度，聚焦动态区域能有效将预训练视频表征适配到机器人控制，MoGAP作为一种轻量、无需外部监督的辅助训练策略具有良好通用性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video generative models for robotic control by jointly predicting future visual observations and actions. The visual prediction objective plays a key role in adapting pretrained video representations to the target robot domain. Yet standard visual prediction inherited from video pretraining treats all visual regions uniformly, despite their differing relevance to robotic control. We therefore investigate the spatial allocation of visual supervision in WAMs and show that focusing supervision on dynamic regions can substantially improve policy performance. Building on this finding, we introduce Motion-Guided Asymmetric Prediction (MoGAP), which augments WAM training with motion-focused visual prediction. MoGAP allocates noise asymmetrically across regions, training the model to reconstruct more heavily corrupted dynamic regions using less corrupted static regions as context. MoGAP is trained alongside standard video-action prediction through a training-only auxiliary prediction path within the shared video diffusion backbone. It uses motion cues derived directly from training videos, requiring neither an external teacher nor additional modalities. Across robot manipulation benchmarks and WAM architectures, MoGAP consistently improves policy performance, increasing success rates on the GR-1 tabletop benchmark from 38.8% to 47.5% for Fast-WAM (Wan2.2-5B) and from 52.3% to 62.3% for Cosmos3-Nano.

</details>


### [20] [LociArt: A Unified Paradigm for Free-Style Local Video Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36456)
*Yuxuan Chen,Feng Xie,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Zepeng Wang,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LociArt，一个统一的自由风格局部视频风格化范式，核心方法 GRNStyle 借极坐标风格感知掩码与表示—几何条件实现精确区域—风格关联，并构建 LociArt-100K 数据集与含 SPS 指标的 LociArt Benchmark。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频风格化多为整场景变换，而局部艺术化编辑需要精确的“区域—风格”关联、空间可控性与时序一致性；非目标内容还需保持不变，缺少统一的学习与评测框架。

Method: GRNStyle 基于生成精化网络（GRN），引入极坐标风格感知掩码，将风格身份以等范数方向嵌入选定区域；并用表示—几何条件化把编码后的掩码特征与原始掩码空间支撑结合，融合时空上下文与几何控制。监督方面设计自动化流程（全局风格化→视频目标移除→抠像→合成）构建 100K 局部风格化成对数据集 LociArt-100K。

Result: 构建 LociArt Benchmark：80 段源视频、84 个目标掩码，覆盖 168 个前景/背景风格化案例，并提出风格原型相似度（SPS）评估区域风格质量，辅以非目标保持与时序一致性指标。实验表明 GRNStyle 在区域风格质量与空间控制上优于现有方法，同时保持良好时序一致性；定性结果显示仅用单区域单风格成对数据训练，也能实现多区域同风格或不同风格的联合风格化。

Conclusion: LociArt 通过统一的范式、方法、数据集与基准，为可控局部视频风格化的学习与评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Video stylization typically transforms entire scenes, while local artistic editing requires precise region--style association, spatial control, and temporal consistency. We introduce LociArt, a unified paradigm for free-style local video stylization that explicitly specifies where to stylize and what style to apply while preserving non-target content. At its core, GRNStyle builds on Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) and introduces a polar style-aware mask that embeds style identities as equal-norm directions within selected regions. Complementary representation--geometry conditioning combines encoded mask features with raw-mask spatial support, integrating spatiotemporal context with geometric control. To provide paired supervision, we develop an automated pipeline combining global stylization, video object removal, matting, and compositing, and construct the LociArt-100K Dataset with 100K local stylization pairs. We further establish the LociArt Benchmark, comprising 80 source videos and 84 target masks covering 168 foreground/background stylization cases, with a Style Prototype Similarity (SPS) evaluation scheme for regional style quality and complementary measures of non-target preservation and temporal consistency. Experiments show that GRNStyle outperforms existing methods in regional style quality and spatial control while maintaining strong temporal consistency. Qualitative examples further illustrate joint stylization of multiple regions with the same or different learned styles, despite training only on single-region, single-style pairs. Together, these components provide a unified foundation for learning and evaluating controllable local video stylization. Project page: https://higherhu.github.io/lociart/.

</details>


### [21] [Learning to Look Beyond Screens: Transferable Visual Observation from GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37093)
*Ruilin Yao,Xinyu Qiu,Tianyu Zou,Yi Rong,Shengwu Xiong,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LBS，用GUI grounding作为空间监督来源，经SFT初始化+RL多轮裁剪-检视决策，训练可迁移的主动视觉观察行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉感知正从被动识别转向主动、任务导向的观察；作者希望找到一个可扩展的空间监督来源，用于学习能跨出GUI领域迁移的观察行为。

Method: 以GUI grounding为监督范式：先在教师模型生成的GUI grounding轨迹上做监督初始化（SFT），建立视觉检视原语；再用强化学习训练多轮"裁剪-检视"（crop-and-inspect）决策。基于Qwen3.5-35B-A3B与Qwen3.8-27B两个骨干构建模型。

Result: 固定推理设置下，两骨干相对各自基线一致提升；超出SFT的增益可迁移至PerceptionBench（两个骨干）以及HR-Bench、ChartQAPro（Qwen3.5-35B-A3B），表明观察策略可泛化到GUI特定模仿之外。

Conclusion: SFT与RL角色互补：SFT建立视觉检视原语，RL提升其下游效用；GUI grounding是一种实用的监督范式，可支撑可迁移的主动视觉感知与更广泛的多模态推理。代码与模型已公开。

Abstract: Visual perception is evolving from passive recognition toward active, task-oriented observation. We investigate GUI grounding as a scalable source of spatial supervision for learning observation behaviors that transfer beyond interfaces. We introduce LBS (Look Beyond Screens), a vision-language model trained through supervised initialization on teacher-generated GUI grounding trajectories, followed by reinforcement learning for multi-turn crop-and-inspect decisions. Under fixed inference settings, LBS models based on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and Qwen3.8-27B consistently improve over their corresponding baselines. Gains beyond supervised fine-tuning transfer to PerceptionBench for both backbones and to HR-Bench and ChartQAPro for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, supporting observation strategies that generalize beyond GUI-specific imitation. Further analysis suggests complementary roles for the two stages: SFT establishes visual inspection primitives, while RL improves their downstream effectiveness. These findings support GUI grounding as a practical supervision paradigm for transferable active visual perception and broader multimodal reasoning. Our code and model are publicly available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/UI-Venus/tree/LBS and https://huggingface.co/inclusionAI/LBS-27B, respectively.

</details>


### [22] [Interpretable Deep Learning Models for Breast Cancer Diagnosis Using Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37026)
*Sami Alnawali,Muhammad Atif Saeed,Abdul Hadi,Azka Atiq,Akhtar Jamil,Ahmad Din*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在BreakHis组织病理数据集上按患者级划分，基准测试十种ImageNet预训练模型做良恶性分类，并用四种可解释AI方法解释预测，DenseNet121取得99.4%准确率和0.999 AUC的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在乳腺癌诊断中潜力显著，但可解释性不足制约临床落地；作者希望通过系统性基准评测与解释方法对比，推动可解释、可信的诊断工具应用。

Method: 在BreakHis数据集上采用患者级划分避免数据泄漏；对ResNet50、VGG19、InceptionV3、DenseNet121、Xception、MobileNetV2、EfficientNet、ConvNeXt、ViT、Swin Transformer十种ImageNet预训练模型进行迁移学习微调，比较良恶性二分类性能；并使用Grad-CAM、LIME、Integrated Gradients与LRP四种归因方法对预测进行解释，评估解释的临床相关性。

Result: DenseNet121表现最佳，测试准确率99.4%、AUC-ROC 0.999，且以p<0.001显著优于其他所有模型；Transformer类模型整体落后于CNN；四种解释方法一致地将高亮区域集中于细胞核密集区与腺体结构等具有诊断意义的区域。

Conclusion: 迁移学习结合可解释AI可构建兼具准确性与可解释性的乳腺癌辅助诊断工具，具备临床转化潜力。

Abstract: Deep learning shows promise for breast cancer diagnosis, but limited interpretability hinders clinical adoption. We benchmark ten ImageNet-pretrained models (ResNet50, VGG19, InceptionV3, DenseNet121, Xception, MobileNetV2, EfficientNet, ConvNeXt, ViT, and Swin Transformer) for benign-versus-malignant classification on the BreakHis histopathology dataset using a patient-level split, and explain their predictions with Grad-CAM, LIME, Integrated Gradients, and LRP. DenseNet121 performed best, with 99.4% test accuracy and an AUC-ROC of 0.999, and significantly outperformed all other models (p < 0.001). Transformer-based models trailed the CNNs. The explanations consistently highlighted nuclei-rich and glandular regions of diagnostic relevance. Combining transfer learning with explainable AI can thus yield accurate, interpretable tools for breast cancer diagnosis.

</details>


### [23] [LearnPrunerV2: Learnable Progressive Token Pruning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36986)
*Rinyoichi Takezoe,Yaqian Li,Weiren Wang,Zihao Bo,Huihong Shi,Anzhou Hou,Mo Guang,Jiale Yan,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全可学习的视觉token剪枝框架LearnPrunerV2，融合视觉显著性与查询相关性估计token重要性，并引入预算确定剪枝（BDP）实现固定逐层预算的硬件友好调度，在LLaVA-v1.5剪掉77.8%视觉token仍保留99.8%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM视觉token数量巨大导致计算开销过高；现有token剪枝方法存在两大缺陷：1）剪枝准则不可靠，因注意力内在偏置或查询无关设计而无法准确定位任务相关区域；2）剪枝策略刚性、非自适应，忽略token冗余随模型深度动态演化。

Method: 将此前两阶段启发式框架LearnPruner扩展为全可学习架构：在VLM主干每一层嵌入轻量可学习模块，通过shadow token捕获外部查询相关性，并与内在视觉显著性融合来估计token重要性；发现跨层一致的"阶梯式"冗余模式后，提出Budget-Deterministic Pruning（BDP），以固定逐层预算建立硬件友好的剪枝调度。

Result: 在多种基准和VLM上，LearnPrunerV2持续优于先前SOTA方法；在LLaVA-v1.5上剪除77.8%视觉token时仍保留原始性能的99.8%，实现更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 全可学习的重要性估计结合确定性的逐层预算调度，可有效解决准则不可靠与调度不自适应的问题，在保持精度近乎无损的同时大幅削减视觉token，为VLM高效推理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal capabilities but suffer from prohibitive computational overhead due to the massive number of visual tokens. While token pruning is a promising solution, existing methods still suffer from two major drawbacks: 1) unreliable pruning criteria that fail to accurately localize task-specific regions due to inherent attention biases or query-agnostic designs, and 2) suboptimal pruning strategies that employ rigid, non-adaptive schedules, overlooking the dynamic evolution of token redundancy across different model depths. To address these challenges, we propose LearnPrunerV2, which extends the previous two-stage heuristic framework (LearnPruner) into a fully learnable architecture. Specifically, we integrate lightweight learnable modules into every layer of the VLM backbone, which estimate token importance by fusing intrinsic visual saliency with extrinsic query relevance captured via a shadow token. By uncovering a consistent staircase redundancy pattern across VLM layers, we further introduce Budget-Deterministic Pruning (BDP) to establish a hardware-friendly schedule with fixed per-layer budgets. Extensive experimental results across various benchmarks and VLMs demonstrate that LearnPrunerV2 consistently outperform previous state-of-the-art methods. Notably, LearnPrunerV2 preserves 99.8% of the original performance when pruning 77.8% of visual tokens on LLaVA-v1.5, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [24] [Dense Representation Learning and Differentiable Shape Modeling for Spine Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36951)
*Augustin Daridon,Sofiane Sifaoui,Ayman Assi,Mohamad Karam,Wafa Skalli,Elsa Angelini,Loic Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将脊柱X光关键点检测重构为像素级对比表示学习问题，用密集像素嵌入与关键点原型匹配，并结合可学习的形状先验解决定位歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱X光关键点检测是估计脊柱形变严重程度、构建生物力学模型和术前规划的关键步骤；传统热图回归范式在不同椎骨角点之间判别性不足。

Method: 将解剖关键点检测建模为像素级对比表示学习：令密集像素嵌入与关键点原型相匹配以生成热图；再将所得热图与全局脊柱关键点构型的可学习形状先验结合，消解定位歧义。方法仅依赖通用U-Net骨干。

Result: 在公开AASCE数据集及两个私有脊柱X光数据集上，该方法的定位精度与临床参数估计性能与最先进方法具有竞争力；即使使用通用U-Net骨干，所得热图也比传统热图回归更具判别性。

Conclusion: 像素级对比表示学习重构关键点检测可显著提升热图判别性，配合形状先验形成简单实用的脊柱关键点检测方案；代码与预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Spine landmark detection from spinal X-ray is a key step for estimating spine deformation severity, for building biomechanical models, and for pre-operative planning. We reframe anatomical landmark detection as a pixel-level contrastive representation learning problem, matching dense pixel embeddings with landmark prototypes. Surprisingly, even with a generic U-Net backbone, this formulation results in more discriminative heatmaps compared to the traditional heatmap regression paradigm, leading to improved discrimination between vertebral corners on distinct vertebra. Furthermore, we combine the resulting heatmaps with learnable shape priors over the global configuration of spinal landmarks to resolve ambiguities in landmark locations, yielding a simple and practical approach to spinal landmark detection. Our approach is competitive, in terms of landmark accuracy and clinical parameter estimation, with the state-of-the-art on the public AASCE dataset and on two private spinal X-ray datasets. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/BioMedTP/Pix2Point.

</details>


### [25] [AxisQ: Depth-Adaptive Channel--Frequency Quantization for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36844)
*Chi Ruan,Tianqi Li,Xin He,Xue Geng,Ming-Ming Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AxisQ，一种深度自适应比特分配框架，在固定每层平均激活比特预算下，浅层按通道域、深层按局部频率域分配精度，并结合边界感知Hessian近似重建，显著提升低比特量化下的伪装目标检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标检测依赖微弱纹理与边界线索，激进的训练后量化（PTQ）会破坏这些线索。现有PTQ方法主要优化量化器或重建网络块，且在分层骨干网络中始终使用单一精度分配域，未考虑特征深度对分配域适配性的影响。

Method: 提出AxisQ：观察到有用的分配轴随特征深度变化，故在固定每层平均激活比特预算下，浅层特征在通道域分配精度、深层特征在局部频率域分配精度，并利用COD感知敏感性更好保留细微前景区域与模糊边界；同时构造边界感知Hessian近似用于重建，融入边界加权梯度与误差统计。

Result: 在多种COD模型与基准上，低比特宽度下精度优于最强基线。W2A2量化下，CAMO数据集上CFRN的加权F-measure相对提升12.7%，CamoFormer-P提升33.3%。

Conclusion: 依据特征深度自适应选择分配域，可作为分层COD模型的可迁移量化策略。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) depends on weak texture and boundary cues that can be destroyed by aggressive post-training quantization (PTQ). Existing PTQ methods mainly optimize quantizers or reconstruct network blocks, while using a single precision-allocation domain throughout hierarchical backbones. Instead, we introduce AxisQ, a depth-adaptive bit-allocation framework motivated by the observation that the useful allocation axis changes with feature depth. Under a fixed per-layer average activation-bit budget, AxisQ allocates precision in the channel domain for shallow features and in the local-frequency domain for deep features, using COD-aware sensitivity to better preserve subtle foreground regions and ambiguous boundaries. We also formulate a boundary-aware Hessian approximation for reconstruction by incorporating boundary-weighted gradient and error statistics. Experiments across multiple COD models and diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy over the strongest baselines at low bit widths, with relative weighted F-measure gains of 12.7% for CFRN and 33.3% for CamoFormer-P on CAMO under W2A2 quantization. These results support adapting the allocation domain to feature depth as a transferable quantization strategy for hierarchical COD models.

</details>


### [26] [CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset for Real-World Face Enhancement and Recognition in Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36781)
*Manish Das,Ajay Kumar Yadav,Shiv Ram Dubey,Satish Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向监控场景的人脸超分辨率与身份识别框架，并构建真实非配对数据集CVBL-CCTV-Face，在三种设置下训练测试增强模型并实现实时身份验证部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实监控CCTV人脸图像受低分辨率、运动模糊、噪声和遮挡等退化影响，导致人脸识别不可靠，需要兼顾超分辨率增强与身份识别的实用框架。

Method: 构建CVBL-CCTV-Face数据集（非配对：8MP IP半球CCTV采集的低质量人脸图像与受控条件拍摄的高质量参考图像配对）；设计模块化系统，包含人脸检测、基于深度学习的低质量人脸超分辨率、以及通过嵌入模型提取L2归一化特征进行身份识别；增强模型按三种不同设置训练与测试。

Result: 增强模型在三种设置下完成训练与测试；系统在真实实时非约束环境中完成身份验证部署。

Conclusion: 该工作提供真实监控人脸数据集与模块化超分辨—识别一体化框架，具备实时部署可行性，可用于监控场景下的身份验证。

Abstract: In real-world surveillance settings, facial captures from closed-circuit television systems suffer from degradations such as low resolution, motion blur, noise, and occlusions, which undermine dependable face recognition. In this study, we propose a framework for super-resolution and identity recognition, aimed at surveillance applications. For this purpose, we present the CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset which is a dataset of real-world images taken in an unpaired manner where each pair consists of a low-quality (LQ) image of a face captured by a 8MP IP Dome CCTV camera together with a high-quality (HQ) reference image that is taken in controlled settings. In order to cope with the various problems posed by such datasets, we construct a modular system that involves face detection, deep learning-based super-resolution of low-quality face images, and identity recognition through L2-normalized embeddings extracted by an embedding model. Enhancement models are trained and tested according to three different setups, and the system is deployed for identity verification in a real-time unconstrained environment.

</details>


### [27] [Efficient Remote Sensing Image-Text Retrieval via Interaction-Free Recall and Text-Guided Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36756)
*Xi Chen,Xu Chen,Xiamgyang Jia,Wei Wang,Xu Zhang,Zhenyuan Shun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先快后精"(FTF)检索框架,以无交互多粒度召回实现高效初筛,再用无参数文本引导跨模态重排精化局部语义对齐,兼顾细粒度对齐与检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像具有密集多目标分布与复杂背景,现有方法要么依赖复杂跨模态交互导致检索效率低,要么依赖大规模视觉-语言适配、需海量数据与算力,难以同时实现细粒度跨模态对齐与高效检索。

Method: 构建FTF两阶段框架:1)无交互多粒度召回,预计算图库嵌入并与查询嵌入匹配,避免全库token级文本-图像交互;2)基于平衡文本引导交互块(BTIB)的无参数文本引导跨模态重排,仅在Top-K候选集内精化局部语义对应;3)设计模态间与模态内联合损失,在多粒度表示上共同优化跨模态对齐。

Result: 在公开基准上的大量实验表明,FTF在取得有竞争力检索精度的同时,相较现有方法显著提升检索效率。

Conclusion: 通过"快速召回+精细重排"的解耦设计,FTF在不引入大规模适配训练的前提下实现了精度与效率的平衡,为遥感图文检索提供了可扩展的有效范式。

Abstract: Remote sensing (RS) image-text retrieval plays a critical role in understanding massive RS imagery. However, the dense multi-object distribution and complex backgrounds in RS imagery make it difficult to simultaneously achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment and efficient retrieval. Existing methods either rely on complex cross-modal interactions that lead to low retrieval efficiency, or depend on large-scale vision-language adaptation, which requires massive data and computational resources. To address these issues, we develop a fast-then-fine (FTF) retrieval framework centered on interaction-free multi-granular recall and parameter-free text-guided cross-modal reranking with the Balanced Text-guided Interaction Block (BTIB). Specifically, interaction-free multi-granular recall pre-computes gallery embeddings and matches them with the corresponding query embedding without full-gallery token-level text-image interaction, while text-guided cross-modal reranking with BTIB refines local semantic correspondence within the top-K candidate set. Furthermore, an inter- and intra-modal loss is designed to jointly optimize cross-modal alignment across multi-granular representations. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that the FTF achieves competitive retrieval accuracy while significantly improving retrieval efficiency compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [28] [SO-101 Bench: Accessible Spatial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36420)
*Truman Hickok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SO-101 Bench：基于 Isaac Lab 数字孪生的语言条件空间操作仿真基准，含 4 任务/5 评分族、138 物体与三档泛化划分、916 评测回合与 3,629 条仿真演示；孪生与真机策略排序完全一致（Spearman ρ=1.00, MMRV=0.000），最佳学习策略约 61%，LLM 代码代理达 83.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SO-101 等低成本机械臂已成为机器人学习的主要入口，但尚无开箱即用的策略能在其上完成空间约束的语言条件任务；现有 SO-101 基准仅限真机、任务简单、留出物体少，缺乏可复现、可泛化的标准化评测。

Method: 构建真实 SO-101 装置的 Isaac Lab 数字孪生；设置四个任务、按五个族评分；138 个物体分 seen / unseen-instance / unseen-class 三档，含留出外观、rotate-to-fit 规划、遮挡记忆子集；916 个评测回合；提供 3,629 条仿真演示，所有学习策略在等算力预算下微调；用 5 个无真机数据训练的策略（4 个 VLA + 1 个 LLM agent）做 sim-to-real 排序校验。

Result: 孪生复现真机排序：同权重在未见环境与未见物体上真机运行，5 策略排序完全一致（Spearman ρ=1.00，MMRV=0.000），但仿真高估成功率；9 个 VLA 与世界-动作模型中最佳学习策略约 61%，空间放置、未见类别、rotate-to-fit 规划仍为开放问题；写代码并将抓取交由学习策略的 LLM agent 达 83.2%，消融显示优势来自 harness 与语言模型的选择。

Conclusion: SO-101 Bench 为低成本机械臂的语言条件空间操作提供了可仿真、可泛化的标准化评测，其数字孪生具备可靠的策略相对排序能力，可作为真机之前的高效筛选工具；但绝对成功率仍有偏乐观，空间推理类任务尚未解决，agentic harness 是当前性能提升的关键杠杆。

Abstract: Low-cost arms such as the SO-101 have become the most common entry point to robot learning, yet no released policy performs spatially constrained, language-conditioned tasks on them out of the box, and existing SO-101 benchmarks run only on physical hardware with simple tasks and few held-out objects. We introduce SO-101 Bench, a simulated benchmark for language-conditioned spatial manipulation built on an Isaac Lab digital twin of a real SO-101 setup. It has four tasks scored as five families, 138 objects in seen, unseen-instance and unseen-class splits, 916 evaluation episodes with subsets for held-out appearances, rotate-to-fit planning and occlusion memory, and 3,629 simulated demonstrations on which every learned policy is fine-tuned under an equal compute budget. The twin ranks policies as the real robot does: five policies trained without real data, four VLAs and an LLM agent, keep their exact ranking when the same weights run on the physical arm in an unseen environment with unseen objects (Spearman rho = 1.00, MMRV = 0.000), although simulation overestimates success. Across nine VLAs and world-action models, the best learned policies score near 61%, with spatial placement, unseen object classes and rotate-to-fit planning remaining open. An LLM agent that writes code and hands grasping to a learned policy scores 83.2%, and an ablation shows that this lead comes from the choice of harness and the choice of language model.

</details>


### [29] [Evaluating Federated Vision-Language and Vision-Only Models for Privacy-Aware Breast Cancer Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36379)
*Malithi Mithsara Wanniarachchi Kankanamge,Ning Yang,Sree Bash Debnath,Md Zobaer Islam,Abdur R. Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究隐私感知的联邦学习框架，将预训练视觉-语言模型与客户端级差分隐私结合用于乳腺影像，并在两个公开数据集上系统评估模型性能、混杂因素、隐私代价与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在乳腺影像领域有潜力，但标注数据有限、机构数据碎片化以及隐私顾虑阻碍集中式训练，因此需要隐私保护下的协作建模方案。

Method: 构建隐私感知联邦学习框架，结合预训练VLM与客户端级差分隐私；比较通用CLIP、生物医学BiomedCLIP与ImageNet预训练ViT-B/16基线，并做去除文本通路的CLIP ViT-B/16消融，在含/不含差分隐私条件下于两个公开乳腺影像数据集上评估。

Result: 联邦学习下五个模型对"正常vs患病"判别均较优（ROC-AUC 0.940–0.990），但仅凭采集年份在同一测试划分上即达0.974，提示存在混杂；"良性vs恶性"判别不确定（ROC-AUC 0.504–0.637，置信区间均含0.50）；去除文本通路保持精度且通信成本减半；差分隐私使ROC-AUC下降0.375，归因于对完整模型状态而非仅可训练参数的扰动。

Conclusion: 开发乳腺影像联邦AI系统时须重视混杂因素评估、预测可靠性、隐私实现方式与部署效率；表面高性能可能源于数据捷径而非真实病理识别。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) shows promise for breast imaging, but limited labeled data, institutional data fragmentation, and privacy concerns hinder centralized training. We investigate a privacy-aware federated learning framework combining pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) with client-level differential privacy. We compare general-purpose CLIP models, biomedical BiomedCLIP, and an ImageNet-pretrained ViT-B/16 baseline on two public breast imaging datasets, with and without differential privacy. We also evaluate a CLIP ViT-B/16 ablation that removes the text pathway. Evaluation includes patient-level ROC-AUC with bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, baseline comparisons, threshold sensitivity, inter-model agreement, and communication cost. Under federated learning, all five models achieve high normal-versus-sick discrimination (ROC-AUC: 0.940-0.990). However, acquisition year alone achieves 0.974 on the same test partition, raising concerns about confounding rather than genuine pathology recognition. Benign-versus-malignant discrimination remains uncertain (ROC-AUC: 0.504-0.637), with all confidence intervals including 0.50. Removing the text pathway preserves classification accuracy while halving communication cost. Differential privacy reduces ROC-AUC by 0.375, attributable to perturbing the full model state rather than only trainable parameters. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating confounding, predictive reliability, privacy implementation, and deployment efficiency when developing federated AI systems for breast imaging.

</details>


### [30] [Diagnosing and Correcting Bias in MLLM for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36210)
*Xusheng Liang,Jianqiao Sun,Hao Zhang,Yulei Niu,Hengshuang Zhao,Jiawei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S-MME基准诊断MLLM长视频问答中的捷径偏差，并提出免训练框架CLEAR，通过反事实视角估计偏差效应并在推理时干预隐状态，从而在五个基准上一致提升预测准确率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答的答案常依赖散布在视频中的少数关键片段，而内存限制迫使MLLM以极低帧率采样，导致稀疏性并可能遗漏关键证据。作者认为单纯扩大上下文规模无法根本解决问题，性能受限于偏差，即虚假的语言相关性和显著但无关的视觉观察所带来的误导性线索。

Method: 1) 构建S-MME基准，系统诊断MLLM的捷径偏差行为；2) 提出免训练框架CLEAR（Counterfactual Long-video Evidence-Aware Reasoning）：将完整视频-问题对作为完整视角，仅保留问题或局部显著视觉片段构造增广视角以估计偏差效应，随后在推理阶段干预隐状态以缓解该偏差，使正确答案能被高置信度预测。

Result: 在五个基准上的实验表明该方法具有泛化性，可集成于不同MLLM，并在预测性能与鲁棒性上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: CLEAR为长视频理解的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）提供了一个互补方向。

Abstract: Long-video question answering is challenging since the answer often hinges on a few decisive moments scattered throughout the video, while memory constraints force Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to sample frames at extremely low rates, resulting in sparsity that risks omitting key evidence. Though aggressively enlarging the context size is intuitive, we argue that performance remains fundamentally constrained by the bias, \emph{i.e.}, the misleading cues caused by spurious \textit{linguistic} correlations and salient yet irrelevant \textit{visual} observations. In this paper, we first introduce {S-MME}, a benchmark designed to systematically diagnose shortcut-biased behavior in MLLMs. To mitigate this failure, we further propose a training-free framework, Counterfactual Long-video Evidence-Aware Reasoning (CLEAR). By treating the full video-question pair as a complete view, CLEAR estimates bias effects via augmented views by only keeping the question or locally salient visual clips. Then, we intervene hidden states to mitigate the discrepancy at inference such that the correct answer can be predicted confidently. With experimental studies across five benchmarks, we show that our method is generalizable and can be integrated in different MLLMs, yielding consistent gains in prediction and robustness. We hope CLEAR can offer a complementary direction in test-time scaling for long-video understanding.

</details>


### [31] [Frugal Handwritten Text Recognition for Sub-Saharan Civil-Status Registers: A Smartphone-to-Records Pipeline with CPU-Only Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36540)
*Ousmane Hamadou,Jean Louis Kedieng Ebongue Fendji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向撒哈拉以南非洲手写民事登记册的低成本（消费级拍摄+仅CPU推理）数字化流水线，并发布乍得某市政登记的1,568张手机图像语料，验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲的手写民事登记册被拍照却极少转录，内容不可检索；当地机构普遍缺乏GPU、稳定网络与外部数字化服务预算，因此需评估"节俭型"流水线能否使这些记录机器可读。

Method: 构建含1,568张手机拍摄的出生、婚姻、死亡记录图像语料（185条含行级转写）；流水线含全经典预处理阶段（笔记本CPU约2.6图/秒）与基于预训练模型微调约4小时的Kraken识别器（免费级GPU），识别端CPU推理。

Result: 在人工校正行切分、且测试集书写者见诸训练集的单登记册设置下，微调将CER从14.53%降至0.50%、WER从56.93%降至3.37%；六核CPU上模型61 MiB、峰值内存低于2.6 GiB、单图平均少于6秒（不含行切分）；下游信息抽取中残余识别错误在角色解析上约损失9个F1点，而微调与预训练识别器间差距为36.7点。

Conclusion: 结果仅证明单一登记册内的可行性，而非对未见书写者或站点的性能；作者详细讨论了该局限。

Abstract: Handwritten civil-status registers across Sub-Saharan Africa are being photographed, but rarely transcribed, which leaves their content unsearchable. Local administrations that hold these records typically lack GPUs, reliable connectivity, and the budget for external digitization services. We study whether a frugal pipeline, with consumer-grade capture and CPU-only inference, can make such registers machine-readable. We introduce a corpus of 1,568 smartphone images of handwritten birth, marriage, and death records from a municipal registry in Chad, with line-level transcriptions for 185 records. Our pipeline combines a fully classical preprocessing stage, running at about 2.6 images per second on a laptop CPU, with a Kraken recognizer fine-tuned from a pretrained model in about four hours on a free-tier GPU. On manually corrected line segmentation and a single-registry test set whose writers also appear in training, fine-tuning reduces the character error rate from 14.53% to 0.50% and the word error rate from 56.93% to 3.37%. On a six-core laptop CPU, the recognizer weighs 61 MiB, peaks below 2.6 GiB of RAM, and processes an image in under 6 s on average, excluding line segmentation. Finally, a downstream information-extraction task shows that residual recognition errors cost about 9 F1 points on role resolution, compared with a 36.7-point gap between the fine-tuned and pretrained recognizers. These results are evidence of feasibility within one registry, not of performance on unseen writers or sites; we discuss these limitations in detail.

</details>


### [32] [Taming Noise Initialization for Zero-shot Video Restoration with Image Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37290)
*Chong Wang,Zixuan Fu,Hao Cheng,Siyuan Yang,Alex C. Kot,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"驯服噪声初始化"的零样本视频复原框架：用DDIM反演退化帧生成保留时序相关性的初始噪声，配合自适应噪声混合与测量引导轨迹注意力，在DAVIS、REDS4、Set8上优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频扩散模型算力成本高，难以直接用于视频复原；而借用图像扩散模型的现有做法采用逐帧独立或完全相同的高斯噪声初始化，无法捕获和利用退化视频帧内在的时序相关性。

Method: 1) 在退化视频帧上直接执行DDIM inversion生成初始噪声，以保留帧间时序相关性；2) 自适应噪声混合策略，在抑制DDIM反演所编码的退化模式的同时，逐步将噪声引导向高斯分布；3) 测量引导的轨迹注意力（measurement-guided trajectory attention），增强跨帧特征对齐与时序一致性。

Result: 在多项视频复原任务上，方法优于现有零样本方法，在DAVIS、REDS4、Set8数据集上均取得更优结果。

Conclusion: 噪声初始化是零样本视频复原中被忽视却关键的一环；以退化帧的DDIM反演噪声为起点并结合混合与轨迹注意力，可在不预训练视频扩散模型的前提下有效利用时序先验，实现更强的一致性与复原质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in restoring high-quality images in a zero-shot manner. Unfortunately, extending this success to video restoration is challenging due to the substantial computational resources required to pre-train video diffusion models. A rising trend seeks to address video restoration by utilizing a pre-trained image diffusion model, initiating restoration from either frame-wise independent or identical Gaussian noise. Yet, these approaches often fall short, as such simple noise initiations fail to capture and utilize the inherent temporal correlations within degraded video frames. In this work, we propose taming noise initialization for zero-shot video restoration, which preserves the intrinsic temporal correlations by generating initial noises directly using DDIM inversion on the corrupted video frames themselves. Building on this, we introduce an adaptive noise blending strategy that suppresses degradation patterns encoded during DDIM inversion while gradually steering the noise toward a Gaussian distribution. To further emphasize temporal consistency, we propose measurement-guided trajectory attention to better align features across frames. Extensive experiments on multiple video restoration tasks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing zero-shot approaches, consistently achieving superior results on the DAVIS, REDS4, and Set8 datasets.

</details>


### [33] [Zhengrong-Spatial-Aware 1.0: Advancing 3D Multimodal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37216)
*Yu Zhang,Xiaogang Wang,Yuhang Cheng,Jingrui Li,Wenbo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文实现并验证了两条端到端多模态适配流水线，用于自定义空间数据集上的3D感知视觉问答：一是为HiSpatial-3B引入米制尺度3D点图，二是用QLoRA对Qwen3-VL（8B/32B）做参数高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向几何感知与通用多模态的空间推理缺乏可复现、端到端的适配套件；作者希望在自建空间数据集上打通数据转换、标注对齐、几何预处理、指令构建、混合域训练到评测的全栈流程，并考察域内适配与跨域迁移效果，同时探究专业化的同时如何保持通用多模态能力。

Method: 构建两条流水线：流水线一微调HiSpatial-3B，以RGB图像结合单目估计的米制尺度3D点图作为几何增强输入；流水线二基于QLoRA对Qwen3-VL（8B与32B）进行内存高效的监督微调。工程栈涵盖数据集格式转换、图像-标注对齐、单目几何预处理、指令数据构造、混合域训练、检查点管理、批量推理与自动化评测。

Result: 目标空间基准上，HiSpatial适配将总体准确率由微调前1.73%提升至3,600步时的79.89%；在另行采集的风格化切分上由14.9%提升至55.1%，显示超出原始训练集的迁移能力。Qwen3-VL流水线中，Qwen3-VL-8B由20.74%提升至45.27%，Qwen3-VL-32B由30.79%提升至47.60%。

Conclusion: 两条互补且可复现的适配流水线分别面向几何感知与通用多模态的空间推理，为后续米制3D推理、空间定位与域适配多模态系统研究奠定工程基础；研究同时指出训练数据组成是专业化过程中保持通用多模态能力的核心因素。

Abstract: This report presents the implementation and validation of two end-to-end multimodal adaptation pipelines for 3D-aware visual question answering on a custom spatial dataset. The first pipeline fine-tunes HiSpatial-3B, which augments RGB observations with metric-scale 3D point maps; the second adapts Qwen3-VL through memory-efficient QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning. The completed engineering stack covers dataset conversion, image-annotation alignment, monocular geometry preprocessing, instruction construction, mixed-domain training, checkpoint management, batch inference, and automated evaluation. On the target spatial benchmark, HiSpatial adaptation increases overall accuracy from 1.73% before fine-tuning to 79.89% at 3,600 steps. On a separately collected styled split, accuracy improves from 14.9% to 55.1%, indicating transfer beyond the original training set. The Qwen3-VL pipeline is likewise implemented end-to-end for both 8B- and 32B-scale models using mixed-domain QLoRA adaptation. The resulting accuracy improves from 20.74% to 45.27% for Qwen3-VL-8B and from 30.79% to 47.60% for Qwen3-VL-32B. Together, these results establish two complementary and reproducible adaptation pipelines for geometry-aware and general-purpose multimodal spatial reasoning. These results establish a reproducible engineering foundation for subsequent work on metric 3D reasoning, spatial grounding, and domain-adapted multimodal systems, while also identifying training-data composition as a central factor in preserving general multimodal capability during specialization.

</details>


### [34] [Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633)
*Siyoon Jin,Dahyun Chung,Honggyu An,Sangbeom Lim,Wonjin Nam,Jaewoo Jung,WonJun Moon,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出跨视角时序目标对应任务，并给出统一框架XTrack，基于预训练视频DiT实现RGB到掩码的隐空间传输，联合建模跨视角匹配与时序跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角对应方法多采用逐帧匹配或序列跟踪，在外观变化或遮挡下易传播对应错误，缺乏跨视角与时间双重维度上保持目标身份的能力。

Method: 将预训练视频DiT改造用于RGB-to-mask隐空间传输，利用其语义与时空先验；引入focal cross-view attention alignment、hard negative conditioning与local context crop策略以增强跨视角定位；并构建伪标签策管流程，将稀疏的Ego-Exo4D标注转为稠密监督以支持视频级训练。

Result: 实验表明XTrack作为统一框架有效，具备稳健的时序一致性与身份保持能力。

Conclusion: 将跨视角匹配与时序跟踪统一于单一视频DiT框架，并辅以注意力对齐与伪标签稠密化，是解决跨视角时序目标对应的有效途径。

Abstract: Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. However, existing methods often rely on frame-wise matching or sequential tracking, which can propagate correspondence errors under appearance changes or occlusions. Therefore, we introduce cross-view temporal object correspondence, which requires preserving object identity across both views and time from a query mask. To address this, we propose XTrack, a unified framework which repurposes a pretrained video DiT with semantic and spatiotemporal priors for RGB-to-mask latent transport, jointly modeling cross-view matching and temporal tracking. However, naive video DiTs do not guarantee accurate cross-view grounding and often attend to semantically similar yet incorrect objects across views. Thus, we introduce focal cross-view attention alignment, hard negative conditioning, and local context crop strategies to ensure reliable grounding. Finally, beyond these architectural enhancements, we also tackle the lack of dense annotation by introducing a pseudo-label curation pipeline that converts sparse Ego-Exo4D annotations into dense supervision to enable effective video-level training. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

</details>


### [35] [Learning to Code the World: Harness Evolution for Compositional 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37046)
*Haoyu Yao,Chenguo Lin,Ruize Du,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneEvolve 将 3D 场景重建表述为可执行代码生成任务，通过强教师智能体迭代进化外层 harness，使小型开源模型无需参数更新即可重建可编辑、可组合的 3D 场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张真实图像重建可编辑、可组合的 3D 场景需要同时推理几何、物体结构与空间关系，对模型能力要求高；小型开源模型难以直接胜任，而依赖大模型部署成本高、推理慢。

Method: 提出 SceneEvolve 框架：(1) SceneAct——基于参数化基元、层级组合与语义空间关系的组合式语义动作接口；(2) SceneHarness-RSI——利用更强的教师智能体，根据开发集重建反馈迭代进化 harness。进化过程中模型参数、动作语义与执行后端保持固定，最终获得可复用的重建流程。
