Result: 在两个域内数据集和一个域外数据集上，Qwen3.8-27B 版本四项重建指标全部提升，平均推理时间降低 47.0%；相较 GPT-5.6-Sol 重建质量高 32.3%，等效部署成本降低 59.8%；进化所得 harness 可迁移至 Kimi-K3，性能与 GPT-6-Astra 相当。

Conclusion: 以代码为场景表示、以 harness 进化为能力放大手段，可在冻结参数条件下显著提升小模型的可编辑 3D 场景重建质量、效率与成本效益，并具备跨模型与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing editable and compositional 3D scenes from a real image requires jointly reasoning about geometry, object structure, and spatial relationships. We introduce SceneEvolve, a framework that represents 3D scenes as executable code and improves reconstruction by evolving the harness around a small open-source agent. SceneEvolve combines SceneAct, a compositional semantic action interface built on parameterized primitives, hierarchical composition, and semantic spatial relations, with SceneHarness-RSI, which leverages a stronger teacher agent to iteratively evolve the harness using feedback from development-set reconstructions. Throughout harness evolution, the model parameters, action semantics, and execution backend remain fixed. The resulting harness provides a reusable reconstruction procedure that enables the small open-source model to reconstruct unseen scenes without parameter updates or teacher assistance. Across two in-domain datasets and one out-of-domain dataset, SceneEvolve with the comparatively compact Qwen3.8-27B model improves all four evaluated reconstruction metrics while reducing mean inference time by 47.0% relative to the base model. More notably, despite relying on a substantially smaller model, SceneEvolve achieves 32.3% higher reconstruction quality than the large proprietary GPT-5.6-Sol model, while reducing mean equivalent deployment cost by 59.8%. Furthermore, the harness evolved using Qwen3.8-27B also generalizes to Kimi-K3, achieving comparable reconstruction performance to GPT-6-Astra. The project page is https://haoyu-agent.github.io/SceneEvolve.

</details>


### [36] [PrinGS: Privacy Preservation in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36168)
*Gaurav Behera,Abhinav Raundhal,Archisha Panda,Saumya Balina,Gargi Shroff,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）表示的隐私保护问题，提出表示层匿名化框架：给定训练好的splat与含敏感内容的参考视图，直接在底层3D表示中定位并匿名化敏感区域，使所有后续渲染视角均不含敏感信息，后训练匿名化耗时不到1秒；并扩展出隐私优先训练策略，在优化过程中阻止敏感内容嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS已成为高保真新视角合成与三维重建的主流表示，但其学习到的表示编码了人脸、车牌、文档、屏幕等敏感视觉信息；当训练好的splat被共享或部署时会产生显著隐私风险，而该问题此前研究不足。

Method: 提出表示层匿名化框架：用户提供标识敏感内容的参考视图，方法在其底层3D表示中定位对应区域并直接匿名化，保证敏感信息在任何新视角渲染中都不出现，同时保持场景几何与视觉保真度；并扩展出隐私优先（privacy-by-design）训练策略，从一开始训练图像即生成隐私保护splat。

Result: 后训练匿名化可在1秒内完成；人脸相似度度量（FSM）低至9.79%（对应人脸检测率FDR为39.47%），敏感文本经匿名化后OCR字符错误率达1.00，即完全不可读。

Conclusion: 该工作为隐私感知的三维表示研究提供了基础性起点，并推动该方向的后续研究。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has rapidly become the preferred representation for high-fidelity novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, the learned representation encodes sensitive visual information including human faces, license plates, documents, and computer screens, raising significant privacy concerns when trained splats are shared or deployed. In this work, we address the underexplored problem of privacy preservation in Gaussian Splatting by proposing a representation-level anonymization framework that directly anonymizes a trained splat. Given a trained splat and a user-provided reference view identifying sensitive content, our method localizes the corresponding regions in the underlying 3D representation and directly anonymizes them, ensuring that the sensitive information is absent from all subsequently rendered novel views while preserving the overall scene geometry and visual fidelity. We achieve this post-training anonymization in less than 1 second. Furthermore, we extend our framework with a privacy-by-design training strategy that prevents sensitive content from being embedded into the representation during optimization, enabling the generation of privacy-preserving splats directly from training images. Our method achieves a remarkably low Face Similarity Measure (FSM) of 9.79% at a retained Face Detection Rate (FDR) of 39.47% and makes sensitive text completely unreadable with an OCR Character Error Rate of 1.00. Our work aims to serve as the primary stepping stone towards privacy-aware 3D representations and motivates further research along these directions.

</details>


### [37] [Learning and Reasoning with Geometry: How Generation Helps Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36022)
*Rui Yang,Yanwei Li,Sili Chen,Jingjia Huang,Yi Lin,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的几何理解与生成模型GeoUG，从训练期几何监督与推理期显式计算证据两方面提升空间理解；结合多尺度深度编解码、3DCueBench基准与自适应交错推理，在CV-Bench-3D、3DCueBench、DA-2K上分别取得97.9%、90.8%、94.2%的循环准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像/视频生成器已能产出空间一致的视觉内容，但如何利用这一能力提升空间理解仍缺乏探索。作者希望回答两个问题：训练阶段几何监督是否有益，推理阶段是否需要并何时显式生成几何证据。

Method: 1) GeoUG：将深度生成与多视角生成同空间理解统一在同一模型中；2) 设计多尺度深度编解码器，使预训练视觉自编码器支持度量深度生成；3) 构建3DCueBench，在平衡所选2D线索的同时可控调节几何难度，用以诊断理解增益来源；4) 自适应交错推理：模型先给出草稿答案，再决策是否生成几何证据以确认或修正，决策通过强化学习训练，以准确率增益与生成开销的权衡为目标。

Result: 联合训练在空间理解上带来任务依赖的增益；生成深度对需要细粒度几何判别的问题带来更大推理增益。GeoUG在CV-Bench-3D、3DCueBench、DA-2K上的循环准确率分别为97.9%、90.8%、94.2%，且生成调用次数少于“总是生成”策略。

Conclusion: 生成能力（尤其是深度生成）可作为有效的几何监督与推理期证据来源；但收益依任务几何需求而异，因此按需触发的自适应交错推理优于无条件生成，在精度与计算成本之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Image and video generators can produce spatially consistent visual content, but how to use this capability to improve spatial understanding remains underexplored. We study this problem from two perspectives: geometric supervision during training and explicit geometric evidence during inference. To this end, we introduce a unified geometric understanding and generation model (GeoUG) that integrates depth and multi-view generation with spatial understanding. First, we design a multi-scale depth codec that enables metric-depth generation using a pretrained visual autoencoder, and show that joint training yields task-dependent gains in spatial understanding. Second, we introduce 3DCueBench to control geometric difficulty while balancing selected 2D cues, and find that generated depth provides larger reasoning gains on questions requiring fine-grained geometric discrimination. Building on this finding, we develop adaptive interleaved reasoning, in which GeoUG first drafts an answer and then decides whether to generate geometric evidence to confirm or revise it. We train this decision through reinforcement learning, balancing accuracy gains over retaining the draft against generation cost. GeoUG achieves circular accuracies of 97.9%, 90.8%, and 94.2% on CV-Bench-3D, 3DCueBench, and DA-2K with fewer generation calls than always generating.

</details>


### [38] [RangeRectQuant: Compressing Scientific Fields for Every Future Rectangular Sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36873)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xinling Wen,Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RangeRectQuant 是一种栅格量化方法，通过协调各像素取值使任意矩形（由前缀和确定）的统计误差均落在给定预算内，同时保持像素值域与指定精确样本，算法为线性时间、单行工作空间，在地理留出的建成区与水体分布图上实现显著存储节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学栅格常在"未来要测量的区域尚不可知"时就已存储。若对每个像素独立设定误差界，而单一绝对误差预算要覆盖所有未来矩形，则必须做到精确整数重建，代价过高。作者希望允许像素协同变化（每个像素都可改变），只要保证任意矩形求和准确，从而在满足统一误差预算的前提下获得更大的存储压缩。

Method: 利用前缀和表示：任一矩形总和由四个前缀和确定，故只需控制前缀和误差。RangeRectQuant 在保持像素值域和指定精确样本的同时约束前缀和误差；逐行位置精确刻画"剩余像素仍能满足约束"的可行误差集合，证明其构成单个整数区间（即便存在多种完成方式）；通过一次反向遍历求区间端点，再用一次正向遍历依端点选择量化像素值。

Result: 所得栅格对所有轴对齐矩形均满足规定误差预算；计算量为线性，工作空间仅与一行成正比；在地理留出（geographically held-out）的建成区地表与水体出现频率地图实验中，相较满足相同要求的其他方法取得显著存储节省。数据生产方一次编码后，后续用户可直接测量任意矩形，无需原始源数据、重新编码或按查询定制校正。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，"逐像素独立误差界"并非保证矩形求和精度的必要条件；通过前缀和层面的协同量化，可在统一误差预算下大幅提升压缩率，且算法高效（线性时间、单行空间），适合一次性编码、多次任意矩形测量的实际数据发布与使用场景。

Abstract: Scientific rasters are often stored before the regions to be measured are known. Independent pixel-error bounds can force exact integer reconstruction when one absolute error budget covers every future rectangle. Coordinated changes allow every pixel to change while every rectangular sum remains accurate. A prefix sum accumulates values from the top-left corner. Four such sums determine any rectangular total. RangeRectQuant preserves the pixel range and designated exact samples while controlling errors in prefix sums. At each row position, we characterize exactly which errors let the remaining pixels meet these constraints. These errors form a single integer interval, even when many completions exist. A backward pass finds its endpoints; a forward pass uses them to choose quantized pixels. The resulting field meets the prescribed error budget for every axis-aligned rectangle, with linear work and workspace proportional to one row. Experiments on geographically held-out built-surface and water-occurrence maps show substantial storage savings over alternatives meeting the same requirements. Data producers encode once; later users measure arbitrary rectangles without the source, re-encoding, or query-specific correction.

</details>


### [39] [Forget the Data, Keep the Capability: Switchable Unlearning for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36858)
*Jingong Chen,Kyle Gao,Guangyuan Li,Chulwoo Pack,Sijia Liu,Kwanghee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoME 是一个可切换的"机器遗忘"框架，将预训练骨干拆分为保留检索能力的核心参数与承载遗忘知识的零初始化寄生参数，通过梯度隔离训练，实现"翻_flag"即可在 Full 与 Core 模式间切换，以低成本满足删除特定个体训练数据的隐私合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私合规要求重识别模型能选择性地"遗忘"某些个体，同时保持对其他人的识别能力。传统做法需要重训练，成本高；MoME 旨在让同一模型通过模式切换实现遗忘，避免重新训练。

Method: 将单一骨干分为两部分：保留检索能力的核心参数，和携带全量数据知识、零初始化的寄生参数；二者在梯度隔离下训练，使核心参数永不接收遗忘集的梯度。关闭寄生分支得到 Core 模式（满足删除请求），开启则得到 Full 模式（保留被删数据所学知识）。理论上证明 Core 在审计出的耦合通道范围内独立于遗忘集，并提出基于秩的 Forgetting-Rank 统计量在已训练模型中审计这种独立性。

Result: 在 Market-1501、VeRi-776 和 RegDB 上，Core 在三种成员推断攻击下表现与重训练参考模型相当，且检索性能优于后者；该检索优势可迁移到 ResNet 与 CLIP 骨干。

Conclusion: MoME 用"翻_flag"取代重训练即可履行删除请求，在保证遗忘合规的同时不牺牲检索精度，并提供了可审计的独立性理论保障，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Privacy compliance requires re-identification models to selectively forget individuals while preserving their ability to recognize others. For this, we present MoME, a switchable framework that can adapt pre-trained backbones for privacy compliance, allowing the same model to switch between Full mode for standard inference and Core mode for unlearned inference. MoME splits one backbone into core parameters that preserve retrieval and zero-initialized parasitic parameters that carry the full-data knowledge, and training them under gradient isolation so the core never receives a forget-set gradient. Disabling the branch yields Core, which satisfies a request to delete a person's training data, and enabling it yields Full, which retains what the deleted data taught, so honoring the request is a flag flip rather than a retraining run. We prove Core independent of the forget set up to an audited set of coupling channels and introduce the rank-based Forgetting-Rank statistic to audit that independence in a trained model. On Market-1501, VeRi-776 and RegDB, Core reads at a retrained reference under three membership attacks while retrieving above it, and the retrieval advantage carries to ResNet and CLIP backbones. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Jingong-Chen/MoME.

</details>


### [40] [uTHCD-C: A Corruption-Robustness Benchmark for Tamil Handwritten Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36850)
*Preethi S,Meeradevi T,Arun Prakash A*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出uTHCD-C——首个面向孤立泰米尔手写字符识别(HCR)的标准化多损坏鲁棒性基准,包含10种损坏类型×5个严重度的配置,并系统评测9种CNN与Transformer架构的鲁棒性、精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泰米尔手写字符识别评测多集中于干净测试图像,缺乏标准化的多损坏鲁棒性基准,难以比较不同架构在真实退化条件下的表现。

Method: 基于无约束泰米尔手写字符数据库(uTHCD)构建基准;定义10种损坏类型(含手写特异的笔画细化损坏)与5级严重度;对9种卷积与Transformer架构采用五折分层交叉验证与书写者独立测试;以VGG16-BN为参考模型计算归一化平均损坏误差(mCE),并对比参数量、计算复杂度与推理吞吐。

Result: ConvNeXt与Swin取得最低mCE(0.75与0.83),ShuffleNet与MnasNet较高(1.55与1.85);效率测量刻画了鲁棒性与计算开销之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: uTHCD-C通过标准化损坏配置、评测协议与对比结果,为鲁棒且计算高效的泰米尔HCR研究建立了可复现的基准与比较基线。

Abstract: Evaluation of Tamil handwritten character recognition (HCR) has largely focused on clean test images, and a standardized multi-corruption robustness benchmark for isolated Tamil HCR has not been established. We introduce uTHCD-C, a corruption-robustness benchmark based on the unconstrained Tamil Handwritten Character Database (uTHCD). The benchmark defines ten corruption types, including a handwriting-specific stroke-thinning corruption, at five severity levels. We evaluate nine convolutional and transformer-based architectures using five-fold stratified cross-validation and writer-independent testing. Robustness is quantified using mean Corruption Error (mCE), normalized against a VGG16-BN reference. We additionally compare parameter count, computational complexity, and inference throughput to characterize the computational requirements of the evaluated architectures. The results establish comparative robustness baselines for Tamil HCR, revealing substantial differences across architectures: ConvNeXt and Swin achieve the lowest mCE values (0.75 and 0.83, respectively), whereas ShuffleNet and MnasNet exhibit higher values (1.55 and 1.85, respectively). The accompanying efficiency measurements characterize the trade-offs between corruption robustness and computational requirements. By providing standardized corruption configurations, evaluation protocols, and comparative results, uTHCD-C establishes a reproducible basis for future research on robust and computationally efficient Tamil HCR.

</details>


### [41] [MVRCap: Towards Comprehensive Video Captioning via Hierarchical Multi-Dimensional Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36838)
*Weihuang Lin,Xianing Chen,Zhibin Wang,Congzhi Zhang,Yiwei Ma,Jiayi Ji,Miao Chen,Jun Song,YuCheng,Bo Zheng,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVRCap框架，将视频描述生成重构为由结构化验证驱动的强化学习任务，通过细粒度可验证奖励实现密集监督，在VDC、CAPability、VC4VG上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型难以生成全面视频描述，主因是监督信号过于粗粒度，难以捕获时空动态与潜在逻辑意图。

Method: 将视频描述建模为强化学习任务；构建细粒度可验证奖励机制，由三部分构成：基于新建MVRCap-QA数据集的分层概念覆盖奖励、事实性验证奖励、简洁性约束；并构建MVRCap-SFT高质量数据集（六维度结构化标注）用于RL策略的初始化。

Result: 在VDC、CAPability、VC4VG等权威基准上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 以结构化可验证奖励提供密集监督的RL式训练范式，可有效提升视频描述的全面性、事实一致性与语义密度。

Abstract: Despite significant progress, current models struggle to generate comprehensive video captions, constrained primarily by coarse-grained supervision, which often fails to capture spatial-temporal dynamics and the underlying logical intent. To address this issue, we introduce MVRCap, a novel framework that reformulates video captioning as a reinforcement learning task steered by structured verification. Central to our approach is a Fine-Grained Verifiable Reward mechanism, serving as dense supervision to enforce rigorous visual-semantic alignment. Specifically, we construct this reward system anchored by three complementary pillars: a Hierarchical Concept Coverage Reward utilizing our newly constructed MVRCap-QA dataset to ensure multi-dimensional completeness and semantic depth, a Factuality Verification Reward to enhance factual consistency, and a Conciseness Constraint to maximize semantic density. Furthermore, we curate the high-quality MVRCap-SFT dataset, providing structured annotations across six key dimensions, to serve as a robust foundational initialization for the RL policy in this training pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MVRCap achieves state-of-the-art performance on authoritative benchmarks including VDC, CAPability, and VC4VG.

</details>


### [42] [Drop the Details, Get the Gist: Dynamic Conceptual Representation for Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36832)
*Ryoya Yoshiyama,Bowen Wang,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出将概念瓶颈模型（CBM）从静态图像扩展到程序性视频理解：用片段级概念分数向量序列表征多步视频，并输入 Transformer 编码器预测类别，同时探索概念贡献度估计；在烹饪与家务活动视频上的实验表明粗粒度概念已足够分类，细粒度文本描述可能并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CBM 研究多集中于静态图像，视频中的时序动态在概念化表征中尚未被充分探索；同时需要为视频分类提供可解释的、基于语言的理由。

Method: 将多步视频表示为片段级概念分数向量构成的时间序列，输入 Transformer 编码器进行类别预测；并提出估计各概念贡献度的方式以增强可解释性；在三个不同粒度的概念集上评估。

Result: 在烹饪与家务活动视频上，粗粒度概念集即可满足分类需求，性能足够，说明细粒度文本描述对这类视频可能不必要。

Conclusion: 基于概念的方法可有效扩展到程序性视频理解，且概念粒度的选择存在性价比权衡：较粗的概念即可支撑分类，为后续视频概念模型设计提供简化依据。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), which route predictions through a layer of human-interpretable concepts, have substantially improved the interpretability of visual classification by exposing an explicit, language-based rationale for each prediction. Most existing work focuses on static images, and temporal dynamics in videos have been underexplored in conceptual representation. We propose a framework that extends concept-based image recognition to procedural video understanding. It represents a multi-step video as a temporal sequence of segment-level concept score vectors and feeds this sequence into a Transformer encoder to predict the class label. We also explore a way to estimate the contribution of each concept for further interpretability. We evaluated three concept sets of different granularities on cooking and household-activity videos and found that coarser-grained concepts are sufficient for classifying these videos, which implies that fine-grained textual descriptions may be unnecessary.

</details>


### [43] [Evaluation-Guided Demonstration Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Policies: The Winning Entry in the MetaFine Open Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36765)
*Lifan Zheng,Guotao Chen,Zhenyang Li,Shujie Cui,Qiang Huang,Fan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对 MetaFine Open Competition 五个任务各微调一个 π_0.5 策略，通过修正示范动作并引入专家续接数据修复抓取/推动/插入失败，最终以 96.66 分排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛中单一策略难以兼顾五个差异较大的任务，且本地评估暴露了反复出现的抓取、推动与插入失败，需要在保持原有示范有效性的前提下提升鲁棒性。

Method: 为每个任务单独微调 π_0.5 策略，采用任务特定的训练数据混合比例与每次预测执行的动作数；从官方示范与专家流程出发，针对失败模式修正示范动作，并从策略实际到达的状态加入专家续接轨迹，同时保留标称示范；在每条指令上逐项比较 checkpoint，防止单项提升掩盖其他项的下降。

Result: 提交方案 MAI 总体排名第一，得分 96.66。

Conclusion: 任务级专门化微调 + 失败驱动的数据增强（示范修正与专家续接）配合逐指令的细粒度 checkpoint 选择，是提升多任务操作策略性能的有效路径。

Abstract: We fine-tuned a separate $π_{0.5}$ policy for each of the five tasks in the MetaFine Open Competition, with a task-specific training mixture and number of actions executed per prediction. Training started from the official demonstrations and expert procedures. Local evaluation exposed recurring grasping, pushing, and insertion failures. We addressed them by revising the demonstrated motions and by adding expert continuations from states the policy reached, while keeping nominal demonstrations in training. Checkpoints were compared on every instruction, so that a gain on one instruction could not hide a loss on another. Our submission, MAI, ranked first overall with a score of 96.66.

</details>


### [44] [Readout and Operating-Point Sensitivity in Event-Stream Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36743)
*Yifan Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在DailyDVS-200的520段片段与47名参与者的真实事件累积数据上，按参与者不交叉划分训练/验证/测试，系统比较冻结编码器的表征在事件触发记录候选选择中的表现。结论：未发现JEPA特有优势，表征比较受读出方式与阈值校准影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件触发记录需要同时兼顾目标捕获率与误触发率，但表征比较会随读出方式（CLS vs patch均值）和阈值校准而变化。作者希望以标准化、参与者不交叉的评测回答"是否存在JEPA专属优势"这一问题，并检验轻量基线在实际工作点下的表现是否被忽视。

Method: 使用520段DailyDVS-200片段、47名参与者，采用participant-disjoint的训练/验证/测试划分。原始研究变化冻结编码器规模（S/B）、累积时长与时间视图数；审稿后扩展加入两个有监督预训练卷积编码器、一个61,586参数的可训练CNN（三个随机种子）、十次重复的参与者重划分，以及阈值校准敏感性分析。以手语握手或跌倒策略为主策略，在50 ms/8视图设定下比较CLS与patch mean读出，并用归一化部分AUC（低假阳性率区间）与固定阈值捕获/误触发计数评估。

Result: 50 ms/8视图、主策略下：DINO-S CLS捕获101/132目标、40/388误触发；patch mean为78/132与35/388。DINO-B CLS的选定阈值捕获率较低，但折级低FPR部分AUC更高（0.746 vs 0.732）。MobileNetV3-Small捕获97/132、53/388误触发。十次相关重划分下，平均归一化部分AUC：DINO-B CLS 0.738，MobileNet 0.674。原始LeJEPA的S→B捕获差异为8.3个百分点，但多重校正后的区间包含零。时长与视图数效应依策略而变。

Conclusion: 未发现既定的JEPA专属优势；轻量基线与实际工作点对结论影响显著，时长/视图数效应依赖策略。证据限于已使用开发数据上的离线、动作定义候选选择，不能说明个人记录价值、每小时连续误报率或眼镜端能耗。

Abstract: Event-triggered recording needs a useful combination of target capture and false triggers, yet representation comparisons can change with readout and threshold calibration. We evaluate real event accumulations from 520 DailyDVS-200 clips and 47 participants using participant-disjoint training, validation, and testing. The original study varies frozen encoder scale, accumulation duration, and temporal view count; a post-review extension adds two supervised-pretrained convolutional encoders, a 61,586-parameter trained CNN with three seeds, ten repeated participant partitions, and calibration sensitivity. At 50 ms/eight views under the primary handshake-or-fall policy, DINO-S CLS captures 101/132 targets with 40/388 false triggers, versus 78/132 and 35/388 for patch mean. DINO-B CLS has lower selected-threshold capture but higher fold-wise low-FPR partial AUC (0.746 versus 0.732). MobileNetV3-Small captures 97/132 with 53/388 false triggers, demonstrating why lightweight baselines and actual operating points matter. Across ten dependent repartitions, mean normalized partial AUC is 0.738 for DINO-B CLS and 0.674 for MobileNet. The original LeJEPA S-to-B capture difference is 8.3 points, but its multiplicity-adjusted interval includes zero. Duration and view-count effects remain policy dependent. We find no established JEPA-specific advantage. The evidence concerns offline action-defined candidate selection on previously used development data; it does not establish personal recording value, continuous false alarms per hour, or eyewear energy.

</details>


### [45] [Hy-Nomas: Towards Free-form Interactive World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36729)
*Qiaoling Chen,Jiaxiang Cheng,Xueqing Deng,Tiankai Hang,Ji Hou,Jiaqi Li,Yifan Liu,Tao Lu,Junshu Tang,Yu Tang,Yao Teng,Yu Tian,Heng Wang,Weizhi Wang,Xiyao Wang,Xilun Wu,Mingyu Yang,Yangbin Yu,Rui Zhao,Longjun Cao,Linghao Chen,Haoyi Duan,Jiaming Han,Mingji Han,Enhua Jiang,Shunkai Li,Yanzhe Liang,Qinwei Ma,Cong Wang,Lei Wang,Bing Wu,Ke Xing,Ray Ying,Zhentao Yu,Key Zhang,Yuan Zhou,Zhengguang Zhou,Kai Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hy-Nomas 是一个 8B 扩散主干的可交互视频世界模型，支持多轮自由文本指令与连续相机控制，通过将结构化视频描述与动作感知的因果注意力对齐来区分持久上下文与局部动作窗口，并结合提示增强器、多阶段课程训练与扩散蒸馏，在人评中优于现有世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界生成要求模型在连续多轮指令下既保持相关历史信息，又体现动作后果。现有方法难以在长时序中同时维护描述性上下文的一致性并将动作指令约束在其应有的时间窗口内。

Method: 1) 8B 扩散主干，生成以当前输入与缓存历史为条件的变长视频片段；2) 核心设计：将结构化视频描述与动作感知因果注意力对齐——描述性上下文跨片段可见，动作指令限定于其意图时间窗口；3) 视觉-语言 Prompt Enhancer 将用户请求与多模态交互历史转译为下一片段的结构化条件；4) 多阶段课程学习，依次引入因果生成、文本交互、相机控制与基于模型自生成历史的训练；5) 最后进行扩散蒸馏。

Result: 人类评估中，Hy-Nomas 相对于现有世界模型表现出更优性能；同时开源模型权重、代码与基准，并提供轻量适配流程。

Conclusion: 通过结构化描述与动作感知因果注意力的对齐、提示增强与课程式训练，可在多轮交互中实现可控且历史一致的视频世界生成，并通过开源与轻量适配工作流支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Interactive world generation requires models to follow successive commands while preserving relevant history and action consequences. We present Hy-Nomas, a video world model with an 8B diffusion backbone that supports free-form text instructions and continuous camera control across multiple interaction turns. It generates variable-length segments conditioned on current inputs and cached history. Our central design aligns structured video captions with action-aware causal attention: descriptive context remains accessible across segments, while action instructions are restricted to their intended temporal windows. A vision-language Prompt Enhancer (PE) translates user requests and multimodal interaction histories into structured conditions for the next segment. A multi-stage curriculum progressively introduces causal generation, text interaction, camera control, and training on model-generated histories, followed by diffusion distillation. In human evaluation, Hy-Nomas shows superior performance over existing world models. We release model weights, code and benchmarks, together with a lightweight adaptation workflow, to support reproducible research on interactive world generation.

</details>


### [46] [SAGE: Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder for Universal Vision Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36719)
*Le Yang,Yucheng Huang,Junyu Chen,Qi Dang,Xin Kong,Haifeng Huo,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE 是一种增强的 ViT-Adapter 骨干网络，通过显式利用自监督 ViT 表示中的显著性、全局上下文与高频细节先验，统一在多个密集预测任务上取得 SOTA 或竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将自监督 ViT 迁移到密集预测的方法把 ViT 当作被动特征提取器，未能充分利用其表示中隐含的丰富先验（显著性、全局上下文、高频细节），导致先验利用率不足、性能受限。

Method: 三项互补创新：(1) 显著性引导架构：提取 DINO 风格 CLS-token 显著性用于特征门控与高频增强，并结合动态多层聚合与局部细节分支；(2) 退火式训练范式：早期提升收敛与特征质量，后期抑制特征漂移；(3) 两阶段质量感知训练机制：在单次训练中由 focal loss 平滑过渡到 Varifocal Loss。共享同一骨干，各任务配专用解码器。

Result: COCO 检测 62.8 box AP / 55.3 mask AP；COCO 关键点 78.5 AP；ADE20K 语义分割 60.3 mIoU、实例分割 44.9 mask AP；TinyImageNet 分类 94.76% Top-1；DIOR-R 旋转目标检测 78.8 AP50。据称是首个在华为昇腾 NPU 上实现多任务 SOTA 的框架。

Conclusion: 显式挖掘并注入自监督 ViT 的内在校验先验（显著性、全局与高频信息）可显著提升密集预测性能；退火式与质量感知的两阶段训练策略进一步稳定并优化收敛。该框架以单一骨干实现多任务领先表现，并验证了在昇腾 NPU 平台上的可行性（代码与预训练模型待内审后发布）。

Abstract: When adapting self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) to dense prediction, existing methods often treat the ViT as a passive feature extractor and may underuse the rich priors encoded in its representations, including saliency, global context, and high-frequency details. We propose SAGE (Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder), an enhanced ViT-Adapter backbone that explicitly exploits these priors through three complementary innovations: (1) a saliency-guided architecture that extracts DINO-style class-token (CLS) saliency to guide feature gating and high-frequency enhancement, together with dynamic multi-layer aggregation and a local-detail branch; (2) an annealing-based training paradigm designed to improve convergence and feature quality during early training while reducing feature drift later in training; and (3) a two-stage, quality-aware training mechanism that smoothly transitions from focal loss to Varifocal Loss (VFL) within a single training run. Across the evaluated tasks (Fig.~\ref{fig:fig_com}), SAGE achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) or competitive results on COCO (62.8 box AP and 55.3 mask AP), COCO keypoint estimation (78.5 AP), ADE20K (60.3 mIoU for semantic segmentation and 44.9 mask AP for instance segmentation), TinyImageNet classification (94.76\% Top-1 accuracy), and DIOR-R oriented object detection (78.8 AP$_{50}$). The same backbone is used across tasks, with task-specific decoders. To the best of our knowledge, SAGE is the first framework to achieve multi-task state-of-the-art results on Huawei Ascend NPUs. Code and pretrained models will be released after internal review.

</details>


### [47] [ARISE: Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement for Continual Multimodal Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36509)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yaxiong Wang,Yujiao Wu,Lianwei Wu,Qi Zhang,Li Zhu,Chaofeng Dong,Le Wu,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将多模态伪造检测重构为"攻击增量持续检测"问题，提出 Fable-Bench 基准与 ARISE 方法，通过真实样本高斯混合模型参考、GRASP 各向异性稳定性约束与 VAR 脆弱性感知回放，在不改变推理接口的前提下提升历史保持与最终平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态伪造检测普遍假设训练攻击与部署攻击分布近似（静态假设），但生成器、编辑系统与多模态大模型不断引入新伪造机制与取证线索，该假设失效。因此需要一个固定二分类接口、可持续学习新攻击且不遗忘旧攻击的检测范式。

Method: 1) 形式化为 attack-incremental continual multimodal forgery detection，并构建 Fable-Bench，含联合模态、文本侧、图像侧三类攻击演化协议。2) ARISE 方法：对投影后的真实回放表征拟合 real-only 高斯混合模型，作为紧凑历史参考。3) GRASP：基于高斯责任度构造样本自适应各向异性度量，强约束历史稳定表征方向、其余方向保留可塑性。4) VAR：由真实密度退化、真假边界混淆、真实模式占位漂移估计分阶段脆弱性，并施加有界回放权重。所有自适应信号仅取自训练期回放统计，推理仅用原始多模态主干与二分类头。

Result: 在三种攻击演化协议与多种检测器架构上，相较代表性持续学习基线，所提方法在历史任务保持与最终平均性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 以"真实类可复用几何"为核心参考、结合责任度感知稳定性约束与脆弱性加权回放，可有效缓解多模态伪造检测中的灾难性遗忘，且不增加推理开销，为开放攻击演化下的持续检测提供可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal forgery detection is commonly studied under a static assumption: training attacks are expected to approximate those encountered after deployment. This assumption becomes increasingly fragile as generators, editing systems, and multimodal large language models continually introduce new forgery mechanisms and forensic evidence. We formulate this problem as attack-incremental continual multimodal forgery detection, where the binary real/fake interface remains fixed while emerging attacks must be learned without forgetting previous ones. We introduce Fable-Bench, which organizes attack evolution through complementary joint-modality, text-side, and image-side protocols. We further propose ARISE (Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement), motivated by the open-ended forged class and the comparatively reusable geometry of authentic content. ARISE fits a real-only Gaussian mixture model to projected authentic replay representations as a compact historical reference. Meanwhile, the proposed Gaussian-Responsibility-Aware Stability Preservation (GRASP) derives a sample-adaptive anisotropic metric that strongly constrains historically stable representation directions while retaining plasticity elsewhere. Our Vulnerability-Aware Replay (VAR) further estimates stage-wise vulnerability from authentic-density degradation, forged-to-authentic boundary confusion, and authentic-mode occupancy drift, then assigns bounded replay emphasis to fragile stages. All adaptive signals are obtained from training-time replay statistics, while inference uses only the original multimodal backbone and binary classification head. Experiments across three attack-evolution protocols and multiple detector architectures demonstrate improved historical retention and final average performance over representative continual-learning baselines.

</details>


### [48] [TimeLineXR: An Interactive Exploration of Time in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37349)
*Nidham Tekaya,Markus Passecker,Matthias Zeppelzauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLineXR 是一个交互式演示系统，将 VLM 嵌入空间中的非线性时间流形转为可探索的 3D 时间轴：网页端与 WebXR 端共享同一预测管线（CLIP 编码 → 余弦核 KPCA 投影 → 贴合 Bézier 时间轴），用户可上传图像并读取其估计的首次出现年份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Tekaya 等，2025）证明 VLM 能估计物体的时间起源，并在嵌入空间中以低维非线性流形编码时间；但该结构对终端用户不可见、不可查询、无法与自有图像交互，也无法查看查询点在时间轴上的相对位置。

Method: 构建双客户端演示：网页端支持上传/选择图像并在彩色编码 3D 时间轴上定位；Three.js WebXR 场景以房间尺度渲染同一时间轴，支持抓取、缩放、检视，并通过"投掷图像卡片"进行查询。两端运行相同流水线并复用同一缓存时间索引：CLIP 编码查询 → 余弦核 KPCA 投影 → 吸附至 Bézier 时间轴。

Result: 每张图像被映射为时间轴上的一个点，用户可比较不同物体的落点并读取其估计的首次出现年份；提供开源代码与复现步骤。

Conclusion: 该工作将定量但难以触及的时间流形结构转化为可交互、可查询的可视化体验，降低了用户对 VLM 时间理解能力的检视门槛。

Abstract: Recently, it has been shown that vision-language models (VLMs) have a certain intrinsic understanding of time. They can estimate the temporal origin of objects and encode time along a low-dimensional, non-linear manifold inside their embedding space~\cite{tekaya2025matter}. That result is quantitative and reproducible, but the structure itself stays out of reach for end users: they cannot see it, query it with their own images, or inspect where a query lands relative to other years on the time axis. We present TimeLineXR, an interactive demonstration that turns this time axis into an explorable 3D scene. A web demo lets users upload or select images and see each placed on a color-coded 3D time axis; a Three.js WebXR scene renders the same time axis as a room-scale representation that can be grabbed, scaled, inspected, and queried by throwing image cards into it. Both clients run the same prediction pipeline against the same cached temporal index: encode the query with CLIP, project it through cosine-kernel KPCA, and snap it to the Bézier time axis. The system turns each image into a point on the time axis, so users can compare where different objects land and read off their estimated first year of appearance.Code source and reproduction steps can be found in: https://github.com/sterngefeuert/timeline-xr.

</details>


### [49] [GradCO: Behavior-Aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37267)
*Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Jiale Huang,Fangxu Liu,Jinhe Bi,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GradCO方法用于文本行人异常检索（TPAS），通过行为感知MoE分流外观/正常行为/异常行为，并用冲突卸载锚点缓解梯度冲突、层级意图对齐聚合专家结果，在五个基准上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPAS需在保留身份线索的同时区分细粒度行为，存在两大挑战：同身份不同行为的难负样本在细粒度对齐时引发梯度冲突；强外观线索在聚合时掩盖微弱的行为证据。

Method: GradCO包含三个组件：(1) Behavior-Aware MoE（B-MoE）构建外观流、正常行为流与异常行为流，各专家角色由检索目标端到端学习，无需专家专属语义标签；(2) Conflict Offload Learning（COL）在相似度计算中引入配对条件的卸载锚点（pair-conditioned offload anchors），减轻主文本表示上的冲突梯度负担；(3) Hierarchical Intent Alignment（HIA）以查询为条件加权聚合各专家的相似度与特征。

Result: 在五个TPAS/TIPR基准上的实验表明GradCO显著优于现有SOTA方法，并验证了各设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: GradCO通过梯度冲突卸载与行为感知专家分流，有效解决TPAS中的梯度冲突与行为证据被淹没问题，为兼顾身份保持与行为区分提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) retrieves a target pedestrian using natural-language descriptions of appearance and normal or anomalous behavior. Unlike conventional text-based person retrieval, TPAS must preserve identity cues while distinguishing fine-grained behaviors. It presents two challenges: same-identity, different-behavior hard negatives cause gradient conflict during granular alignment, while strong appearance cues can obscure weak behavioral evidence during aggregation. We propose GradCO (behavior-aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading), comprising three complementary components. Behavior-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (B-MoE) forms appearance, normal-behavior, and anomalous-behavior streams whose roles are learned from retrieval objectives without expert-specific semantic labels. Conflict Offload Learning (COL) uses pair-conditioned offload anchors in similarity computation to reduce the conflicting-gradient burden on the main text representations. Hierarchical Intent Alignment (HIA) aggregates expert similarities and features using query-conditioned weights. Experiments on five TPAS/TIPR benchmarks show GradCO significantly surpasses SOTA methods and validate our design.

</details>


### [50] [GameVerse: A Minute-Scale Gameplay Dataset for Long-Horizon Interactive World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37257)
*Kang He,Wenshuo Peng,Chuanhao Li,Zihui Gao,Xiaojie Xu,Zhengyuan Lin,Yuzhe Ding,Donghong Ji,Kaipeng Zhang,Yongtao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GameVerse——一个面向长时程交互式世界建模的分钟级游戏视频数据集，规模69.7K片段、1,655小时、3.496亿帧，含1,358小时连续两分钟序列，并配有时间稳定相机轨迹与事件对齐的分层字幕，实验表明其可显著延长交互式世界模型的时间一致跨度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型难以维持约一分钟以上的rollout一致性，其瓶颈不在模型容量，而在长时程训练数据稀缺：现有游戏视频数据集多为不足10秒的片段，且整段粗粒度字幕缺乏区分度，无法支撑可控的长时程生成。

Method: 构建统一数据处理流水线：场景一致的分段、质量过滤、相机位姿估计与基于事件的字幕标注；每条片段配以跨分钟保持一致的时序稳定相机轨迹，并将字幕区分为静态场景上下文与动态前景事件，按事件边界而非固定时间窗对齐。数据覆盖多种第一/第三人称玩法。

Result: 数据集规模与多样性远超现有公开数据集（量级上长约一个数量级）；综合分析显示其在规模、多样性及标注质量上均优于已有数据；训练实验表明在GameVerse上训练可显著扩展交互式世界模型的时间跨度。

Conclusion: 长时程训练数据的稀缺是世界模型长时程一致性的关键制约，GameVerse通过分钟级连续序列与事件对齐的细粒度标注填补该空白，为可控长时程交互式世界建模提供数据基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in interactive world models have made it possible to generate controllable, dynamic environments from action inputs. Yet current models struggle to maintain coherence beyond roughly one minute of rollout, a limitation rooted not in model capacity but in the scarcity of long-horizon training data. Existing gameplay video datasets typically consist of clips under ten seconds, with captions that describe whole clips in coarse and undifferentiated text, which is insufficient for controllable long-horizon generation. We address this with GameVerse, a minute-scale gameplay video dataset purpose-built for long-horizon interactive world modeling. GameVerse contains 69.7K clips spanning 1,655 hours and 349.6 million frames across diverse first- and third-person gameplay, including 1,358 hours of continuous two-minute sequences, an order of magnitude longer than prior datasets. Clips pass through a unified pipeline of scene-consistent segmentation, quality filtering, camera pose estimation, and event-based caption annotation. Each clip is paired with a temporally stable camera trajectory that remains coherent across minutes, together with captions that separate static scene context from dynamic foreground events and align to event boundaries rather than fixed time windows. Comprehensive analysis shows that GameVerse surpasses existing public datasets in scale, diversity, and annotation quality. Experiments further demonstrate that training on GameVerse substantially extends the temporal horizon of interactive world models.

</details>


### [51] [MCP-Net: Real-Time Coronary Perception Network and Topological Analysis for Robotic Guidewire Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37127)
*Sahar Nasirihaghighi,Tianyuan Wang,Kenan Niu,Stefano Stramigioli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以多任务冠状动脉感知网络（MCP-Net）为核心的仿真驱动框架，从单帧透视图像联合完成血管分割、导丝尖端定位与狭窄定位，并转化为结构化血管图及导航路径图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助PCI中导丝导航需要结构化的冠脉场景感知，而现有方法多将血管分割与器械定位割裂处理，难以支撑下游导航。

Method: 基于三维冠脉CTA生成含血管结构、模拟导丝、不同严重度狭窄及多样几何形态的仿真透视图像，并提供仿真真值；MCP-Net 单帧联合预测血管分割、导丝尖端定位和狭窄定位；将预测结果转为含中心线、分叉点、端点、分支方向与分支角的结构化血管图，并把狭窄关联到图节点，自动选取图根节点生成至目标病变的路径图（roadmap）。

Result: 框架能够联合恢复多项冠脉感知目标，并转换为可解释的血管表示，适用于下游机器人导航。

Conclusion: 仿真驱动的多任务感知加图结构化表示，可为机器人导丝导航提供结构化、可解释的场景理解；当前为仿真验证阶段。

Abstract: Coronary guidewire navigation during percutaneous coronary intervention requires continuous interpretation of vessel anatomy, guidewire position, and target stenosis under two-dimensional X-ray fluoroscopy. While existing approaches commonly address vessel segmentation or device localization as separate tasks, a robotic system requires a more structured perception of the coronary scene to support downstream navigation. In this work, we present a simulation-driven framework centered on a Multi-Task Coronary Perception Network (MCP-Net) for robotic guidewire navigation, integrating coronary vessel segmentation, vascular topology extraction, and roadmap generation. Starting from three-dimensional coronary CT angiography, we generate simulated fluoroscopy images containing vascular structures, simulated guidewires, stenotic lesions with varying severity, and diverse vascular geometries, together with simulation-derived ground truth. MCP-Net jointly performs coronary vessel segmentation, guidewire-tip localization, and stenosis localization from a single fluoroscopic frame. These predictions are subsequently converted into a structured vascular graph containing centerlines, bifurcations, endpoints, branch directions, and branch angles. By associating the predicted stenosis with this graph, the framework generates a roadmap from the automatically selected graph root toward the target lesion. The results demonstrate that the proposed framework can jointly recover coronary perception targets and transform them into an interpretable vascular representation suitable for downstream robotic navigation.

</details>


### [52] [Ground-to-Satellite Knowledge Distillation for Crop Classification under Temporal and Geographic Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37021)
*Miro Miranda,Rocco Sedona,Valerio Marsocci,Gabriele Cavallaro,Claudia Paris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用地面照片作为“特权监督”，通过知识蒸馏将冻结CLIP特征的照片教师模型的知识迁移到基于AlphaEarth与TESSERA预训练卫星嵌入的分类器上，在仅使用卫星数据推理的前提下提升作物类型制图精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续、及时更新的作物类型制图对农业监测至关重要，但常受限于参考数据不足；地面照片与卫星时序对同一地块提供互补视角，然而推理阶段并非每个卫星像元都有对应地面照片，限制了其在可扩展制图中的应用。

Method: 构建配对的地面照片与卫星影像数据集（17类作物）。以基于冻结CLIP特征的照片教师模型监督学生分类器（分别基于AlphaEarth与TESSERA表示），照片仅在训练阶段参与，采用知识蒸馏范式。

Result: 知识蒸馏相较仅用卫星的分类一致提升；对冻结嵌入，在时间偏移下优势扩大，最高达+5.5个macro-F1点；在地理留出场景中提升更明显（+3.7 vs 分布内+3.0）。

Conclusion: 地面视角监督可补充卫星嵌入表征并提升其鲁棒性，且不改变推理阶段的输入要求，为缺乏参考数据的可扩展作物制图提供可行路径。

Abstract: Continuous and up-to-date crop type mapping is essential for agricultural monitoring but is often hampered by limited reference data. The growing availability of ground-level field photographs offers a promising way to address this challenge. When combined with satellite time series, these images provide complementary perspectives on the same parcel, capturing crop characteristics from distinctly different perspectives. However, field photographs are not always available for each satellite pixel at inference time, limiting their potential in scalable crop type mapping. We investigate whether ground photographs can provide privileged supervision for crop classification built on pretrained satellite embeddings. A photo teacher based on frozen CLIP features supervises classifiers trained on AlphaEarth and TESSERA representations; photographs are used only during training. On a paired field photo and satellite imagery dataset with 17 crop classes, knowledge distillation consistently improves over satellite-only classification; for frozen embeddings the margin grows under temporal shift, reaching up to +5.5 macro-F1 points, and widens in geographic holdouts (+3.7 vs +3.0 in-distribution). These results show that ground-view supervision can complement satellite embeddings and improve their robustness without changing inference requirements.

</details>


### [53] [ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566)
*Shuolin Xu,Guanyuan Li,Boyu Li,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Hongchuan Yu,Shuicheng Yan,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架ReferStream，通过Joint Latent与Cache Restoration在流式视频生成中自然注入新主体，并构建ReferStream Bench基准，在文本遵循、主体相似度与计数准确率上优于包括离线方法在内的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成中需要在新视觉参考到达时动态注入新主体，同时保留已有主体与场景。现有方法存在两个问题：独立于当前场景编码参考缺乏整合引导；将参考特征长期驻留内存又会干扰已建立的上下文。

Method: 免训练的ReferStream框架，由两部分组成：(1) Joint Latent——将参考潜变量放置到最新场景潜变量的目标区域，使参考特征在上下文中被提取；(2) Cache Restoration——用这些特征条件化注入chunk，随后恢复被覆盖的全局缓存条目，同时保留新生成的历史。整个流程无需重训生成器或修改已输出帧。另构建ReferStream Bench，含定时到达的参考、演化指令以及InShot/MultiShot生成中的主体出现标注，按事件日程评测。

Result: 在ReferStream Bench上，文本遵循、主体相似度与计数准确率均高于对比基线，包括预先给出全部参考的离线方法。

Conclusion: 结合场景内参考构建与瞬态缓存注入，可在不重训、不修订已生成帧的前提下实现流式视频中的新主体自然注入，新基准为该任务提供了事件级评测协议。

Abstract: Injecting new subjects naturally during streaming video generation requires integrating newly supplied visual references into the existing video while preserving established subjects and the scene. Encoding references independently of the current scene provides little guidance for this integration, while keeping their features in persistent memory can interfere with established context. We propose ReferStream, a framework that requires no additional training and combines reference construction using the current scene with transient cache injection. Joint Latent places reference latents in target regions of the latest scene latent, allowing reference features to be extracted in context. Cache Restoration uses these features to condition the injection chunk, then restores the overwritten global cache entries while retaining the newly generated history. This procedure incorporates new visual conditions without retraining the generator or revising emitted frames. We further introduce ReferStream Bench, a new benchmark with scheduled reference arrivals, evolving instructions, and explicit annotations of subject presence for InShot and MultiShot generation. Evaluation follows the event schedule and measures both the fidelity of new subjects and the preservation of established subjects, alongside video quality, text adherence, and count accuracy. Experiments on ReferStream Bench show higher scores for text adherence, subject similarity, and count accuracy than the compared baselines, including offline methods given all references in advance. Project page: https://xushuolin.github.io/ReferStream/.

</details>


### [54] [ALADDIN: Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37503)
*Ivan Shchekotov,Timofei Gritsaev,Viacheslav Pirogov,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALADDIN 将预训练的多步流匹配模型初始化为生成器，在身份与目标保持约束下进行对抗训练，得到一步人脸交换模型；并指出标准评测对同类身份编码器的偏好偏差，用独立编码器重评基线。它在一步法中真实感与目标保持最优，同身份迁移下匹配最佳多步模型而网络评估少 28 倍、训练对少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多步扩散式人脸交换方法每张图需大量网络评估，速度慢；从零训练的 GAN 式方法虽快，但仅靠对抗信号学习真实感，效果明显偏弱。因此需要兼顾速度与质量的一步式方案。同时，标准评测协议偏向使用与被评方法相同身份编码器训练的方法，导致比较不公。

Method: 提出 ALADDIN（Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping）：以预训练多步流匹配模型初始化生成器，在身份约束与目标属性保持约束下进行对抗训练，实现一步推理；并用三元组伪标签监督训练学生模型，从而在身份迁移与目标属性保持之间提供可控权衡。此外，采用独立身份编码器重评所有基线以消除评测偏差。

Result: 在一步式方法中，ALADDIN 在真实感与目标保持上达到 SOTA，身份迁移相当；与最佳多步模型持平，但网络评估次数少 28 倍，训练数据对少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 将流匹配蒸馏与对抗训练结合可在单步内获得高保真人脸交换，并显著降低推理与训练成本；同时揭示了身份编码器带来的评测偏差，呼吁使用独立编码器进行公平比较。

Abstract: Face swapping transfers a source identity onto a target image while preserving the target's pose, expression, hair, and lighting. The most realistic current methods are diffusion-based, but they need many network evaluations per image. GAN-based methods are fast, yet trained from scratch they must learn realism from the adversarial signal alone and produce visibly weaker results. We present ALADDIN (Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping), a one-step face swapper obtained by initialising the generator from a pre-trained multi-step flow-matching model and training it adversarially under identity and target-preservation constraints. We expose a controllable trade-off between identity transfer and target-attribute preservation by training a student model with triplet pseudo-label supervision. We additionally identify a bias in the standard evaluation protocol, which favours methods trained against the same identity encoder used to score them, and re-evaluate all baselines with an independent one. ALADDIN is state-of-the-art among one-step methods in realism and target preservation at comparable identity transfer, and matches the best multi-step model with 28 times fewer network evaluations and an order of magnitude fewer training pairs.

</details>


### [55] [BoxWorld: Let World Engines Control and Video Generators Render](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36371)
*Zike Wu,Ahmed Hatem,Chintan Trivedi,Wallace Michel Pinto Lira,Xuebin Qin,Lele Wang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoxWorld 将"交互推理"与"生成式渲染"解耦：编码智能体根据用户规则生成 Box World Engine 输出布局框、持久属性与状态变量的轻量状态流，再由 Box-State Generative Renderer 自回归渲染为视频，并配套三阶段训练与 PairedStateBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视频世界模型把交互动力学内嵌于生成器中，难以显式指定对象级规则、难以推演规则后果，也无法对对象运动、状态切换与条件事件做在线精细控制。目标是构建支持用户自定义行为的实时交互视频世界。

Method: 提出 BoxWorld：1) 分离交互推理与渲染，由 coding agent 依用户规则构建 Box World Engine，通过布局框、持久对象属性、动态状态变量的轻量接口暴露结果，外观与物理效果交给生成渲染；2) Box-State Generative Renderer 以自回归方式将状态流渲染为视频；3) 三阶段训练：开放域视频学物体定位（grounding）、自动标注的游戏视频学状态转移、在自生成历史上做 rollout 蒸馏。

Result: 显著优于端到端世界模型，并在多个公开基准上保持竞争力；同时提出用于细粒度交互控制的配对评测基准 PairedStateBench。

Conclusion: 显式状态接口 + 生成式渲染的解耦范式可提升交互可控性与规则可指定性，在实时交互视频世界生成方向优于纯端到端方案。

Abstract: We tackle the challenge of building real-time interactive video worlds with user-defined behaviors. Such worlds must track object states, apply interaction rules, and render the resulting consequences as users act. Yet existing end-to-end video world models typically learn interaction dynamics within the generator, making it difficult to specify object-level rules and reason about their consequences. In this work, we introduce BoxWorld, which separates interaction reasoning from generative rendering. Given user-specified rules, a coding agent creates a Box World Engine that determines interaction outcomes and exposes the resulting object states through a lightweight interface of layout boxes, persistent object attributes, and dynamic status variables, leaving appearance and physical effects to generative rendering. A Box-State Generative Renderer then renders this evolving state stream into video autoregressively, enabling online control over object motion, state switches, and conditional events. Rendering this state stream is non-trivial: the renderer must respond to object-level state updates in real time while remaining coherent with the generated history. To this end, we introduce a three-stage training strategy that learns object grounding from open-domain videos and state transitions from gameplay videos, both annotated automatically, and finally performs rollout distillation on self-generated histories. We further propose PairedStateBench, a paired benchmark for fine-grained interaction control. BoxWorld substantially outperforms end-to-end world models and remains competitive across multiple public benchmarks. Project page: https://boxworld-web.github.io.

</details>


### [56] [Right Click, Wrong Box: Grading GUI Grounding by What a Click Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36831)
*Xiaokang Yuan,Binhui Yao,Luwei Yang,Ruimin Chen,Zeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有GUI grounding基准（如ScreenSpot-v2）用单一标注框判定点击正确，但界面常以多个元素暴露同一动作，导致框评分系统性低估正确点击。作者提出以"执行结果一致"为正确性定义，用无新标注的执行探针生成结果图与执行判官，发现被框评分判错的点击中约1/5至1/3实际达成目标，该偏差与领域报告的新旧模型差距相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI grounder在ScreenSpot-v2上已达93–96%，模型间报告差距仅约1个百分点，此时更有价值的问题是剩余错误究竟是什么。框匹配规则把点击限制在单一标注框内，而真实界面中同一动作可能由重复链接、复选框标签、logo旁标题等多个元素承载——框本身不错，是规则不完备，从而制造了假阴性。

Method: 提出"结果等价"正确性定义：点击后产生与点击标注目标相同的结果即为正确。构建执行探针，每页执行并指纹化约一千次点击，生成结果图（outcome maps）与面向实时Web应用的执行判官，无需新增标注。在代码托管平台与在线商店上执行三个7–8B grounder的预测；并对11个模型（3个7–8B、8个3B变体）在ScreenSpot-v2上做盲审（60单元子集由两名独立评审）；另用3B GRPO研究在奖励中计入等价点击的效果，控制训练种子与测试采样噪声，采用多次运行与配对区间。

Result: 框评分判错的点击中，约1/5与1/3在代码平台与在线商店上实际达成目标；改写为自然任务指令后仍为1/7与1/3。ScreenSpot-v2盲审复现同样效应，且最强grounder的漏判比例最高。基于审计的修正量为0.2–1.8个百分点，与领域报道的模型间差距同量级；框评分的低估不能通过重复运行消除。3B GRPO实验中，奖励计入等价点击相对框奖励在三个种子、四个测试集上无稳定增益。

Conclusion: 框评分低估正确点击，评估环节需要引入结果等价的修正；但该修正作为训练奖励信号时并未带来稳定性能提升。作者开源探针、结果图、审计数据与全部预测。

Abstract: Leading GUI grounders score 93 to 96% on ScreenSpot-v2, and reported gaps between new and prior models are about a point. At this level the useful question is what the remaining errors are. Benchmarks count a click as correct only inside one annotated box, but interfaces often expose one action through several elements: a duplicated link, the label of a checkbox, the title next to a logo. The box is not wrong. The rule is incomplete. We call a click correct when executing it produces the same outcome as clicking the annotated target. A probe that executes and fingerprints about a thousand clicks per page turns this definition into outcome maps and an execution judge for live web applications, with no new annotation. We execute the predictions of three strong 7--8B grounders on a code forge and an online shop. About one in five and one in three of the clicks that box scoring rejects reach the intended outcome, and the shares remain one in seven and one in three when the instructions are rewritten as natural tasks. A blind audit of 11 models on ScreenSpot-v2 (three 7--8B grounders and eight 3B variants) finds the same effect on web screenshots, with two independent reviewers on a 60-unit subset. The share is highest for the strongest grounders. Training seeds and test-set sampling add noise that more runs and paired intervals control. Box scoring instead under-counts correct clicks, which repetition does not remove, and the audit-based correction of 0.2 to 1.8 points on ScreenSpot-v2 is as large as the gaps the field reports. The correction is needed in evaluation. In our 3B GRPO study, crediting equivalent clicks in the reward gives no stable gain over box rewards across three seeds and four test sets. We release the probe, the outcome maps, the audit and every prediction.

</details>


### [57] [Visual Autoregressive Generation with Next Token Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36793)
*Ruihang Jiang,Zongyao Zhao,Zhaolin Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Next Token Imagination (NTI)，放宽视觉自回归生成中"仅监督GT下一token"的限制，让模型在受控准则下想象训练数据之外的合理视觉实现，从而提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容空间巨大且近乎无界，任何训练集只能覆盖极小部分。然而生成模型通常只拟合给定图像：在视觉自回归生成中，每个token由前序GT token预测、且仅由GT下一token监督。这种"局限于观测数据"的学习方式限制了模型对整体视觉分布的刻画能力。

Method: 以视觉自回归生成为概念验证（其固定离散token空间便于引导与约束想象）。提出NTI：允许模型超越GT下一token进行探索，但受一套精心设计的准则约束，使想象结果保持在底层视觉分布之内。

Result: 在ImageNet上的大量实验表明，NTI可显著且一致地提升生成图像保真度，在更小模型、更低训练成本下逼近SOTA扩散模型与AR模型。

Conclusion: 让模型在训练数据之外进行受约束的"想象"，是缓解数据覆盖不足、提升视觉生成质量的有效途径；NTI在视觉AR生成上验证了该假设的可行性。

Abstract: The space of possible visual content is enormous, and arguably unbounded. Any training set, however large, therefore covers only a tiny fraction of it. Yet generative models are typically trained to fit only the images they are given. In visual autoregressive (AR) generation, for example, each token is predicted from the preceding ground-truth (GT) tokens and supervised solely by the GT next token. Confining learning to observed data in this way limits how much of the visual distribution a model can capture. In this work, we hypothesize that letting a model imagine beyond its training data, by exploring plausible visual realizations it has never observed, can ease this limitation. We use visual AR generation as a proof of concept, since its fixed, discrete token space makes imagination easier to steer and constrain. We propose Next Token Imagination (NTI), a method that allows the model to explore beyond the GT next token, subject to a carefully designed criterion that keeps this imagination bounded by the underlying visual distribution. Extensive experiments on ImageNet show that NTI substantially and consistently improves the fidelity of generated images, approaching state-of-the-art diffusion and AR models while using a smaller model and lower training cost.

</details>


### [58] [Multi-task Disentangling Fusion Mamba-CNN for MODIS Spectral-Temporal Unmixing with Endmember Variability Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36750)
*Motasem Alkayid,Yimin Zhu,Naser El-Sheimy,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MDF-Mamba，通过谱-时解耦专家架构、mHCFusion融合与像素级可学习变异性权重，实现MODIS时序的高效谱时解混。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MODIS时序谱时解混在丰度制图与光谱库构建中意义重大，但面临三重挑战：(i) 谱-时信息耦合；(ii) 类内端元光谱变异与类间特征混淆；(iii) 多专家表示朴素融合的不稳定性。

Method: 解耦架构：每个光谱通道由独立卷积专家处理，配合专用双向状态空间（Mamba）时序分支编码跨通道季节轨迹，并以基于squeeze-and-excitation的软重加权构成多分支专家集成；引入mHCFusion，将流形约束超连接框架适配用于稳定、可解释的专家输出融合；在扩展线性混合模型中引入逐像素可学习光谱变异权重建模类内端元变异；设计多任务目标，在同一数据上联合监督丰度估计、自监督光谱重建与端元正则化。

Result: 在覆盖Alberta与Saskatchewan两省的MODIS时序数据上，14项指标中12项最优（Alberta 7/7，Saskatchewan 5/7），其余2项排名第二。

Conclusion: 谱-时解耦的专家集成与流形约束融合可有效缓解信息耦合与融合不稳定性，结合变异性建模与多任务学习，构成高效且性能领先的MODIS时序解混框架。

Abstract: Spectral-temporal unmixing of MODIS time series is essential for applications such as abundance mapping and spectral library construction, yet it remains challenging due to (i) spectral-temporal information coupling in MODIS data, (ii) intra-class endmember spectral variability and inter-class signature ambiguity, and (iii) the instability of naively fusing multiple expert representations. This paper presents the Multi- task Disentangling Fusion Mamba (MDF-Mamba) to address these limitations. To resolve spectral-temporal coupling, we design a disentangling architecture that pairs per-channel spectral experts with a dedicated bidirectional state-space (Mamba) temporal branch. Convolutional modules each process a single spectral channel, while the Mamba branch encodes the cross-channel seasonal trajectory, forming a multi-branch expert ensemble with soft inter-expert reweighting based on squeeze-and-excitation. For the integration of these spectral and temporal expert outputs, we introduce mHCFusion, adapting the manifold-constrained hyper-connection framework to merge the outputs in a stable and interpretable manner. Endmember variability is addressed by extending the linear mixing model with pixel-wise learnable spectral variability weights that capture intra-class endmember variation. Finally, we design a multi-task training objective that combines supervised abundance estimation with self-supervised spectral reconstruction and endmember regularization terms on the same training data, yielding a highly efficient framework for spectral-temporal unmixing. Experiments on MODIS time series covering the Alberta and Saskatchewan provinces show that MDF-Mamba achieves the best performance on 12 of 14 evaluation metrics across both datasets (7 of 7 on Alberta; 5 of 7 on Saskatchewan), ranking second on the remaining two.

</details>


### [59] [CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406)
*Haruki Fukuda,Constantin Lux,Wolf Schweitzer,Tadashi Hosoya,Lars Ebert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的CT体渲染转换方法，将CT体渲染结果直接转换为3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）模型，可在普通计算机上实时交互式查看。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CT体渲染虽能直观呈现解剖结构，但依赖CT数据、专用软件和强大GPU；现有医学3DGS方法需对每个场景训练数分钟至数小时，效率低下。

Method: 在毫米级体素的自适应八叉树上放置splat；在GPU上用渲染器光照计算其颜色与不透明度，将体素不透明度解析地转换为splat不透明度；对低于标准3DGS光栅化器1/255不透明度下限的微弱splat采用随机化保留策略。

Result: 6例公开CT（1.5 mm体素）配三种传输函数转换，200万splat模型PSNR达：骨32.6±0.9 dB、增强血管30.1±1.0 dB、皮肤与骨32.3±1.5 dB；单次转换在笔记本需4–30 s、4GB显卡台式机需9–60 s；笔记本上800×800分辨率渲染达68 fps。

Conclusion: 免训练转换可在普通计算机上约一分钟内生成逼真CT渲染的实时3DGS模型，保真度足以支持交互式查看。

Abstract: Photorealistic volume rendering of computed tomography (CT) may convey anatomy intuitively, but it needs the CT data, dedicated software and a powerful graphics processor. Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS) displays photorealistic scenes in real time on less powerful devices, but existing medical approaches train each scene for minutes to hours. We aimed to convert a CT volume rendering directly into a 3DGS model without training. Splats were placed on an adaptive octree of millimetre-sized cells. Their colour and opacity were computed on the graphics processor with the renderer's lighting, cell opacity was converted analytically into splat opacity, and faint splats were retained stochastically above the 1/255 opacity floor of standard 3DGS rasterisers. Six public CT scans (1.5-mm voxels) were converted with three transfer functions and compared with converged references by peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity and a learned perceptual metric. Two-million-splat models reached a PSNR of 32.6 +/- 0.9 dB for bone, 30.1 +/- 1.0 dB for contrast-enhanced vessels and 32.3 +/- 1.5 dB for skin and bone. A conversion pass (usually two or three per model) took 4-30 s on a current laptop and 9-60 s on a desktop with a 4-GB graphics card. On the laptop, the models rendered at 68 frames per second at 800 x 800 pixels. Training-free conversion produced real-time 3DGS models of photorealistic CT renderings in about a minute or less per conversion pass on ordinary computers, with fidelity that we consider sufficient for interactive viewing.

</details>


### [60] [VGIQA: Fine-Grained Image Quality Assessment with Visual Grounding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37186)
*Weichang Wang,Liyu Chen,Shuyue Guan,Jie Hu,Fei Gao,Wei Li,Nannan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGIQA，一种兼具局部与全局质量评估能力的MLLM-based图像质量评估模型，采用"冷启动+强化学习"两阶段训练，并构建局部质量评估数据集VGSR-2K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态大模型的IQA方法多局限于整体、图像级评价，缺乏细粒度、局部化的评估能力，限制了实际应用。

Method: 构建VGIQA模型，采用两阶段训练范式：冷启动阶段 + 强化学习阶段，并设计局部质量评估奖励函数驱动RL优化；同时提出面向局部质量评价的数据集VGSR-2K。

Result: 在VGSR-2K上展现优异的图像质量理解能力，显著优于Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct、Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct等开源MLLM；且局部质量评估任务可有效提升模型在退化感知任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 局部质量评估能力的引入既弥补了现有MLLM-based IQA的细粒度短板，又能通过任务间的正向迁移增强退化感知等相关任务表现。

Abstract: Image quality assessment (IQA) aims to evaluate the perceptual visual quality of images. Recently, the rapid development of Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has significantly advanced the IQA field. By integrating chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and reinforcement learning (RL), these models can generate more accurate and interpretable results while relying on less training data. However, existing MLLM-based methods are predominantly constrained to holistic, image-level evaluations and lack the capability to produce fine-grained, localized assessments, which substantially limits their practical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose VGIQA, a novel IQA model endowed with both local and global quality assessment capabilities. VGIQA is trained via a two-stage paradigm comprising a cold-start phase followed by reinforcement learning. Driven by carefully designed local quality assessment reward functions, our model exhibits exceptional image quality understanding on VGSR-2K, a new dataset we propose specifically for local quality evaluation, significantly outperforming existing open-source MLLMs such as Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct and Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct. Furthermore, the local quality evaluation task can effectively enhance the model's performance on the degradation perception task.

</details>


### [61] [WithinVAE: Compact Visual Autoencoding with Self-Distilled Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36593)
*Yuzhou Ji,Jiepeng Wang,Tianyu Jin,Lizhuang Ma,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WithinVAE：一种不含预训练识别编码器、不依赖外部语义目标的紧凑变分视觉自编码器，通过自编码器自身的EMA副本做DINO/iBOT式自蒸馏，并用位置相关token masking实现4倍压缩，同时提升重建保真度与语义线性可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义tokenizer通常从预训练识别编码器继承语义，但其不变性会丢弃反演所需的外观细节，导致重建保真度、紧凑性与语义结构三者难以兼顾。作者质疑这种"继承"的必要性，主张语义结构可在自编码器自身的重建表示内部学习得到。

Method: 构建紧凑变分视觉自编码器，采用序列化瓶颈（sequential bottleneck）与位置相关token masking，达到4倍压缩比；引入源自自编码器自身EMA副本的DINO/iBOT式自蒸馏作为语义监督，全程不使用预训练表示编码器或外部语义目标。另设latent-to-latent模型复现有序压缩趋势，并用pixel-to-latent模型将该目标迁移至预训练生成空间。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K 256×256上，WithinVAE在全部四项重建指标上均优于RPiAE，PSNR提升1.36 dB，而latent标量容量仅为其一半。冻结评估下，同一latent的线性探针较RPiAE提升6.5点、较纯重建基线提升5.1点，表明语义增益来自自蒸馏而非架构或外部监督。

Conclusion: 语义监督的来源与其使用方式可以分离：紧凑视觉自编码器无需借用识别表示即可自组织出语义结构，并同时保持重建保真度与紧凑性。

Abstract: Visual autoencoders must balance reconstruction fidelity, compactness, and semantic structure. Existing semantic tokenizers typically inherit semantics from pretrained recognition encoders, whose invariances can discard the appearance detail required for inversion. We show that this inheritance is unnecessary: semantic structure can be learned inside an autoencoder's own reconstructive representation. We introduce WithinVAE, a compact variational visual autoencoder with a sequential bottleneck, DINO/iBOT-style self-distillation from an exponential-moving-average copy of the autoencoder itself, and position-dependent token masking to achieve a 4-fold compression ratio, which is free of pretrained representation encoder or external semantic target. On ImageNet-1K at $256\times256$, WithinVAE improves all four reported reconstruction metrics over RPiAE including PSNR by 1.36 dB, while using half its latent scalar capacity. Under frozen evaluation, the same latents improve linear probing by 6.5 points over RPiAE and 5.1 points over our reconstruction-only baseline, attributing the semantic gains to self-distillation rather than architecture or external supervision. An auxiliary latent-to-latent model reproduces the ordered-compression trend, and a pixel-to-latent model transfers the objective to a pretrained generative space. These results separate the origin of semantic supervision from its use: a compact visual autoencoder can organize semantic structure without borrowing a recognition representation.

</details>


### [62] [Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662)
*Jialv Zou,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Yaxin Luo,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视"量化误差决定离散视觉token语义保真度"的假设，指出去量化误差并非可靠指标，有效比特预算才是下游性能的关键；为此提出因子化向量量化（FVQ），在更大比特预算下缓解码本饱和，使离散表示在多模态理解上追平连续表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散视觉token可作为与语言统一的原生接口，但学界普遍认为量化误差导致不可避免的语义损失，从而把连续视觉表示视作语义保真度的固有上界。作者希望验证这一假设是否成立，并寻找突破离散表示性能瓶颈的途径。

Method: 以多模态理解为语义保真度的代理指标，先实证检验量化误差与下游性能的关系；发现有效比特预算（effective bit budget）才是决定性能的关键，而现有量化器随码本增加会饱和。据此提出因子化向量量化FVQ：对视觉特征的互补、固定维度的多个视图，用对称并行的码本进行量化，从而在更大比特预算下持续获益。

Result: 在工业级多模态数据上训练后，模型在离散表示模型中达到SOTA，在九项指标均值上分别超过UniAR和Emu3-Chat 22.8%和59.2%；并且是首个在细粒度OCR等任务上可与Qwen3-VL等强连续表示模型竞争的离散表示模型。

Conclusion: 量化误差不是语义保真度的可靠度量，有效比特预算与码本的可扩展性更为关键；FVQ通过并行因子化码本缓解饱和、提升比特效率，证明离散视觉表示有潜力支撑大规模原生多模态基础模型，作者并总结了实现该竞争力的关键原则与实践配方。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/FVQ

</details>


### [63] [Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626)
*Yucheng Mao,Chongjian Ge,Zhuowen Tu,Hao Tan,Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"自回归预训练获取语义知识 + 扩散后训练实现高保真渲染"的学习范式，系统性研究知识获取与渲染的目标函数、表征与缩放规律，并据此以600小时级算力训练出3B文生图模型，在GenEval和DPG-Bench上分别达到86和87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成同时需要高层语义知识与细粒度像素细节，单一目标难以兼顾；作者希望明确"知识获取"与"高保真渲染"应分别由何种学习目标、表征与缩放方式承担。

Method: 将自回归预训练用于知识获取、扩散后训练用于渲染；通过受控对比比较不同预训练目标；提出仅用语言监督训练、不含图像解码器与重建损失的纯语义tokenizer；并拟合知识获取与渲染的计算最优缩放律。

Result: 自回归是更优的下游渲染预训练目标，提升学习效率与最终生成质量；AR单独生成质量与最终性能不必然相关，语义质量更重要；自回归预训练相比从零训练的像素扩散模型降低了渐近渲染损失；3B模型仅用6,600 H100 GPU小时即达GenEval 86、DPG-Bench 87。

Conclusion: 验证了语义预训练与扩散渲染解耦的范式在大规模下有效，并提供了知识获取与渲染的计算最优缩放指导。

Abstract: Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. We propose a learning paradigm that assigns these roles to autoregressive pretraining for knowledge acquisition and diffusion post-training for high-fidelity rendering. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

</details>


### [64] [As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579)
*Zhongrui Gui,Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“角色命名”（character naming）任务：在仅依赖影片自身（不使用外部演员表、演员照片或预建角色库）的条件下，判断对话中提到的名字对应画面中的哪个可见角色；构建数据集与标注、设计视频-对话联合模型，并与商用多模态大模型比较，同时提出随影片推进逐步构建角色库的渐进式框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影角色是随剧情逐步引入的，而现有角色识别多依赖外部演员表、剧照或预建角色库，无法模拟人类在线理解过程。作者希望实现从影片本身在线、类人地推断角色身份。

Method: 1) 形式化“角色命名”任务：给定对话中提及的名字及对应视频上下文，预测承载该名字的可见角色；2) 提供用于评估角色命名与首次角色出场的数集与标注；3) 构建视频-对话联合模型，利用正反打镜头、自我介绍、直接称呼、第三方指代、视线、时间连续性等电影语言线索；4) 提出渐进式框架，随影片推进逐步构建角色库；5) 与商用多模态大模型对比。

Result: 通过实验对比所提视频-对话模型与商用多模态大语言模型在角色命名任务上的表现（摘要未给出具体数值），并验证渐进式角色库构建的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为无需外部先验、基于影片自身的在线角色身份推断提供了任务定义、评测基准与方法框架，推动了类人的在线电影理解。

Abstract: Films introduce characters incrementally as they progress. We study the problem of identifying principal characters and assigning their names directly from the movie itself, without using external cast lists, actor photographs, or pre-built character banks. We introduce the character naming task: given a name mentioned in dialogue and the associated video context, the model must determine which visible character bears that name. This task is challenging because name resolution often depends on cinematic cues such as shot-reverse-shot structure, self-introduction, direct address, third-party reference, gaze, and temporal continuity. We provide a dataset and annotations for evaluating character naming and first character appearances, develop a video-and-dialogue model for the task, and compare it with proprietary multimodal large language models. We further introduce a progressive framework for building a character bank as the film unfolds. Together, these contributions support online, human-like movie understanding in which character identities are inferred from the film itself.

</details>


### [65] [The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576)
*Xuran Hu,Yuru Jia,Tengxi Wang,Miloš Daković,Ljubiša Stanković,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 $p$-Bridge，一个面向 Tweedie 噪声的统一"测量指标化"扩散框架，用噪声指数 $p$ 与累积测量 $\nu$ 构成噪声平面，统一 Gaussian/Poisson/Gamma 去噪，并以共享反向均值更新与单一指数条件网络实现跨指数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式去噪普遍假设高斯扰动，但真实传感器噪声（如散粒噪声、乘性噪声）服从信号依赖的统计规律，方差与强度呈幂律关系；针对每种噪声单独建模代价高且缺乏统一理论表述。论文希望建立一个统一框架，把高斯、泊松、Gamma 等噪声纳入同一扩散过程，并赋予去噪以可解释的物理含义。

Method: 1) 以噪声指数 $p$（描述方差—强度关系）与累积测量 $\nu$（控制噪声水平）参数化 Tweedie 噪声，形成涵盖 Gaussian、Poisson、Gamma 的噪声平面；2) 用独立增量的随机桥连接测量分布，将去噪解释为测量累积过程，起始与停止指数均可指定；3) 由测量叠加性导出共享的反向均值更新，并配以基于 deviance 的目标函数学习所需条件均值；4) 构造潜变量复合 Poisson–Gamma 表示以支持中间指数的采样；5) 采用单一指数条件网络适配不同 Tweedie 噪声模型，免除逐指数单独训练。

Result: 实验表明，指数条件化提升了跨指数鲁棒性，优于固定指数模型；在使用估计指数时，共享网络达到与"神谕选取"的五个专家模型集合相当的鲁棒性能。在合成图像、FMD 荧光显微图像与多传感器 SAR 图像上均验证了其对不同信号依赖噪声机制的有效性。

Conclusion: $p$-Bridge 为信号依赖的 Tweedie 噪声提供了统一、可解释的扩散去噪范式：参数化的噪声平面、随机桥的物理诠释与共享反向更新使单一条件网络即可覆盖多种噪声模型，并具备实际传感器数据上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $ν$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

</details>


### [66] [Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619)
*Tudor Braicu,Joseph DelPreto,Jared Boyer,Mónika Farsang,Maximilian Stölzle,Tim Niklas Seyde,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建了同步第一人称与五个第三人称视角的网球数据集，系统评测七类时空预测任务下图像编码器、视频编码器与视觉语言模型的表现，并提出条件扩散模型实时生成全身运动学动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷机器人需具备场景理解、交互推理与未来事件预测的时空智能，但基础视觉模型在移动第一人称视角下支撑这些能力的效果尚不明确。

Method: 采集带标注网球数据集（第一人称视频、五个第三人称视频、全局位置、触球时间、击球类型）；训练多类视觉模型并接联合训练预测头；评测七项任务（2D自我玩家与3D球定位、未来玩家与球轨迹、正反手击球预期、下次触球时间、该时刻3D球位置）；额外分析辅助深度信息与图像历史长度的影响；提出条件扩散模型由视觉编码预测生成全身运动学动作。

Result: 最佳模型当前帧定位误差为自我玩家0.14 m、球0.54 m；1.1 s 内预测误差为玩家0.34 m、球0.91 m；触球时间平均误差0.14 s。辅助深度显著提升3D球定位，更长图像历史未改善球预测。

Conclusion: 度量深度推断仍是难点；表征更多利用场景上下文而非单纯帧间运动；条件扩散模型可在实时速率下随预测更新自适应生成动作，为基于第一人称视频的实时动态机器人提供依据。

Abstract: Agile robots require spatiotemporal intelligence to interpret scenes, reason about interactions, and anticipate future events. Yet it remains unclear how well foundation vision models support these capabilities, particularly from a moving egocentric camera, which is a natural view for robots. To investigate this, we collect an annotated tennis dataset and train a suite of vision models on spatiotemporal tasks. Our dataset contains egocentric video synchronized with five allocentric videos, estimated global positions, labeled contact times, and annotated stroke types. We then evaluate seven prediction tasks from egocentric images: 2D ego player and 3D ball localization, future player and ball trajectories, forehand or backhand stroke anticipation, time to the next ego player ball contact, and 3D ball position at that contact. We compare image encoders, video encoders, and vision-language models with jointly trained prediction heads. The best performing models achieve mean current-frame localization errors of 0.14 m for the ego player and 0.54 m for the ball, future forecasting errors of 0.34 m for the ego player and 0.91 m for the ball over 1.1 s, and a mean contact-time prediction error of 0.14 s. Auxiliary depth information substantially improves 3D ball localization, suggesting that inferring metric depth remains a challenge. Longer image histories do not improve ball forecasting, indicating that representations exploit scene context rather than rely solely on inter-frame motion. Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

</details>


### [67] [RePAIR: Inverting Backdoor Attacks in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37015)
*Arnold Brosch,Kira Maag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePAIR：面向语义分割后门攻击的事后、无标签、攻击无关防御，利用“触发偏移在分类头强于内部表征”的观察，从可能受污染的无标签图像池推断受害者-目标关系并做特征与输出校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型对后门攻击脆弱：存在触发器时受害者类像素被重定向到攻击者指定目标类，清洁预测保持不变。现有防御多来自图像分类，专用分割防御稀缺且需重训练或改模型结构。

Method: 基于“后门偏移在分类头强于内部表征、受害者语义可恢复”的观察；从无标签且可能含污染的图像池推断受害者-目标类关系；构建经安全审核的语义参考；通过比较头部预测与内部语义证据定位可疑像素；对特征空间与输出施加朝推断受害者语义的校正，并以保守定位与输出约束限制对良性预测的改动；将攻击建模为对抗性概率输运、修复为其近似逆，推导定位、残余攻击成功率、清洁侧变化、防御诱发的目标预测及攻击无关配对识别的界与充分条件。

Result: 在多种分割架构与攻击设置下，RePAIR 显著降低攻击成功率，同时大体保持清洁性能。

Conclusion: RePAIR 提供无需重训练、无需标签、无需攻击知识的即插即用分割后门防御，兼具理论保证与实证有效性。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in computer vision, semantic segmentation models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, posing a security risk. These attacks redirect pixels of a chosen victim class to an attacker-specified target class when a trigger is present, while preserving clean predictions. Existing defenses are largely derived from image classification, while dedicated segmentation defenses remain scarce and typically require retraining or model modification. We introduce RePAIR, a post-hoc, label-free, attack-unaware defense based on the observation that backdoor-induced shifts can be stronger in the classifier head than in internal representations, leaving recoverable victim-class semantics. RePAIR infers the victim-target relation from an unlabeled, potentially contaminated image pool, constructs a safety-approved semantic reference, and localizes suspicious pixels by comparing head predictions with internal semantic evidence. It then applies feature-space and output corrections toward the inferred victim semantics, while conservative localization and output constraints limit changes to benign predictions. We model the attack as adversarial probability transport and repair as its approximate reversal, deriving bounds and sufficient conditions for localization, residual attack success, clean-side changes, defense-induced target predictions, and attack-unaware pair identification. Experiments across segmentation architectures and attack settings show that RePAIR substantially reduces attack success while largely preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [68] [ORBITAL: Optimization of Robust Brain--Computer Inference Through Architecture-Aware Low-Precision Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36411)
*Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ORBITAL框架，在BCI Competition IV-2a上以结构化压缩+S25-TRADES实现压缩EEGNet模型中最高的对抗鲁棒性，并在Jetson Orin Nano上完成原生边缘验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量EEG解码器可支持嵌入式BCI，但压缩未必带来成比例的硬件节省，且可能改变对抗鲁棒性， robustness与硬件效率需联合考察。

Method: ORBITAL：硬件可实现的结构化压缩、恢复感知的工作点选择、多攻击评估与原生边缘验证；采用S25-TRADES与匹配对照实验（9名被试、3个随机种子），并进行FP16/INT8 Q/DQ量化对比。

Result: S25-TRADES取得压缩EEGNet中最高的平均true-union鲁棒性23.06%（标准Full为21.27%），参数减少26.27%、MACs减少25.10%；匹配对照显示TRADES使union准确率较干净恢复提升6.74个百分点（p=0.0039）；逐样本分析显示边界重分布而非统一裕度扩张；Jetson Orin Nano上FP16降低能耗至多16.20%、时延8.00%；INT8原生Q/DQ虽预测一致率99.3%，但时延与能耗约为FP16的两倍。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性与硬件效率需联合优化：TRADES是鲁棒性恢复的主要来源，压缩后应做恢复感知的工作点选择与硬件感知量化，INT8 Q/DQ并非天然更优。

Abstract: Compact electroencephalography (EEG) decoders enable embedded brain--computer interfaces (BCIs), but compression can alter adversarial robustness without proportional hardware savings. We present ORBITAL, a robustness-constrained framework combining hardware-realizable structured compression, recovery-aware operating-point selection, multi-attack evaluation, and native edge validation. Across nine subjects and three seeds on BCI Competition IV-2a, S25-TRADES achieves the highest observed mean true-union robustness among compressed EEGNet models (23.06% vs 21.27% for standard Full), with 26.27% fewer parameters and 25.10% fewer MACs. Matched controls identify TRADES as the main source of robustness recovery, improving S25 union accuracy by 6.74 percentage points over clean recovery (p=0.0039), while per-example analysis reveals boundary redistribution rather than uniform margin expansion. On Jetson Orin Nano, FP16 compression reduces board energy by up to 16.20% and latency by 8.00%. Despite 99.3% prediction agreement, native INT8 Q/DQ incurs about twice the FP16 latency and energy, demonstrating the need for joint robustness and hardware-aware optimization.

</details>


### [69] [Physics Guided Diffusion Model Based Exposure Interpolation for High Dynamic Range Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37135)
*Zhengguo Li,Chaobing Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种物理引导的扩散模型曝光插值算法，解决单次拍摄双大幅曝光比SDR图像融合时的亮度顺序反转（BOR）伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧HDR存在鬼影问题，单次拍摄双LER SDR图像可避免鬼影，但直接融合会产生BOR伪影，因此需在两幅LER图像曝光时间之间插值出一幅中间曝光图像。

Method: 算法分两部分：物理驱动组件插值出初始图像，再由新的扩散模型组件生成残余高频细节；该扩散模型基于生成模型的相关性分析，采用新的预测目标以降低噪声数据与目标间皮尔逊相关性的波动，并降低拟合误差的放大因子。

Result: 与现有基于CNN的神经网络增强曝光插值算法相比，所提方法在生成更多高频信息方面具有内在优势；实验结果验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 物理引导的扩散模型曝光插值可有效缓解BOR伪影，为单次拍摄HDR成像提供可行方案。

Abstract: Ghosting artifacts are an open problem for popular multi-shot based high dynamic range (HDR) imaging. An effective solution is to capture two large-exposure-ratio (LER) standard dynamic range (SDR) images simultaneously via a single-shot.
