Brightness order reversal (BOR) artifacts are generated when the two LER images are directly fused together. It is crucial to interpolate an image with an exposure time in between the exposure times of two LER images. In this paper, a novel physics-guided diffusion model based exposure interpolation algorithm is proposed to address the BOR artifacts. An initial image is interpolated by a physics-driven component, and the residual high-frequency details are generated by a new diffusion model based component. The new diffusion model is based on the correlation analysis of generative models and includes a new predicted target to reduce the variation of the Pearson correlation between the noisy data and the target, as well as the amplification factor of the fitting error. Compared with existing neural augmented exposure interpolation algorithms on top of convolution neural networks, the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm has an inherent advantage to generate more high frequency information. Experiment results validate the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm.

</details>


### [70] [Systematic Morphometric Brain Autoencoding for Representation Learning in Neurodegenerative Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37074)
*Nuria Balbás-Esteban,Albert Belenguer-Llorens,Elaheh Moradi,Jussi Tohka,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SYMBA——面向结构性脑MRI的自监督预训练框架，融合解剖约束掩码、多尺度结构相似性重建目标与由局部到全局的渐进课程，在冻结线性探测下优于现有神经影像基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构性T1w MRI的神经退行性疾病早期识别受限于标注稀缺与扫描仪导致的分布偏移，监督模型泛化差。现有自监督方法多沿用自然图像范式（对比学习或体素级掩码重建），难以刻画神经退行性变的分布式形态学模式。

Method: SYMBA将神经解剖结构显式嵌入自监督目标：(1) 解剖约束掩码；(2) 多尺度结构相似性（MS-SSIM）重建损失替代体素级损失；(3) 从局部解剖细节过渡到全局脑形态的渐进式课程。在九个异质队列共6,953例T1w MRI上预训练，并以冻结线性探测在两个独立未见数据集上评估分布外泛化。

Result: 多项临床任务上稳定优于近期神经影像基础模型：AD分类AUROC 0.930，MCI转化预测0.772，淀粉样蛋白状态预测表现良好；低标注场景下样本效率高，仅6名标注受试者即可在AD/CN分类上达到约0.90 AUROC。

Conclusion: 将解剖先验与形态学感知的重建目标引入自监督预训练，可显著提升结构性脑MRI表征的跨队列泛化性与样本效率，为神经退行性疾病早期筛查提供有效的表征学习范式。

Abstract: Early identification of neurodegenerative disorders using structural T1-weighted MRI is essential for timely clinical intervention, yet supervised deep learning models often struggle to generalize due to limited annotations and scanner-induced distribution shift. Self-supervised learning offers a promising alternative; however, existing approaches are largely adapted from natural image paradigms, relying on contrastive objectives or generic masked reconstruction with voxel-wise losses, which may not fully capture the distributed morphometric patterns associated with neurodegeneration.
  We introduce SYMBA (SYstematic Morphometric Brain Autoencoding), a self-supervised pretraining framework specifically designed for structural brain MRI. SYMBA integrates anatomically constrained masking, a multi-scale structural similarity (MS-SSIM) reconstruction objective, and a progressive curriculum that transitions from local anatomical detail to global brain morphology. These components explicitly incorporate neuroanatomical structure into the self-supervised objective rather than relying on generic image priors.
  Pretrained on 6,953 T1-weighted MRI scans from nine heterogeneous cohorts, SYMBA is evaluated using frozen linear probing on two independent unseen datasets, enabling a strict assessment of out-of-distribution generalization. Across multiple clinical tasks, SYMBA consistently outperforms recent neuroimaging foundation models, achieving 0.930 AUROC for Alzheimer's disease classification, 0.772 for mild cognitive impairment conversion prediction, and strong performance in amyloid status prediction. In low-label regimes, SYMBA demonstrates high sample efficiency, reaching approximately 0.90 AUROC for AD/CN classification using only six labeled subjects.

</details>


### [71] [ClusterSplat: Semantic Cluster Selection for 3D Visual Grounding in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36462)
*Jaesung Lee,Hyeontaek Hwang,Gabriel Manalu,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ClusterSplat，将 3DGS 中的 3D 视觉定位从“图元级密集相关性分数+启发式阈值”重构为“场景自适应聚类候选的选择”问题，在 ScanRefer/Ref-LERF/ScanNet v2 上取得最优的渲染 2D 掩码与显式 3D 目标指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 下的指代定位需同时输出渲染 2D 掩码与显式 3D 高斯集合。现有方法对稠密图元级相关性分数施加启发式阈值或 top-ratio 过滤来抽取目标，但这类分数在不同场景与提示间波动大，导致启发式敏感、定位不稳定。作者因此希望摆脱对固定阈值的依赖。

Method: 核心是把 3D 目标选择提升为簇级选择：（1）学习实例感知的高斯特征；（2）基于特征构建种子体素；（3）以“描述长度准则下降”为合并条件融合相邻种子体素，得到场景特定的簇候选集合；（4）给定指代表达，用轻量 MLP 构成的查询条件簇打分器对候选排序；（5）选中簇直接定义显式 3D 目标，其簇分数用于渲染得到 2D 掩码。

Result: ScanRefer 上，在所用 3DGS 基线中取得最佳渲染 2D 分割与显式 3D 目标指标；Ref-LERF 上取得最佳平均分数，说明在细粒度指代描述下趋势一致；ScanNet v2 类名查询上渲染 2D 结果最强、3D 匹配精度最高，同时保持有竞争力的 3D IoU。

Conclusion: 以场景自适应簇候选替代图元级阈值筛选，可有效缓解跨场景/跨提示分数漂移带来的不稳定，并统一支撑 2D 掩码渲染与显式 3D 目标输出；实验表明该范式在多种查询设定下均优于同类的 3DGS 定位基线。

Abstract: We study 3D visual grounding in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), where a referring expression should identify a target object as both a rendered 2D mask and a set of explicit 3D Gaussians. Prior methods typically extract 3D targets by applying heuristic thresholding or top-ratio filtering to dense primitive-level relevance scores. However, these scores often vary substantially across scenes and prompts, making such heuristics sensitive and leading to unstable targeting. ClusterSplat addresses this by reframing 3D target selection as cluster-level selection over scene-adaptive candidates. The method first learns instance-aware Gaussian features, forms feature-aware seed voxels, and merges neighboring seed voxels when a description-length criterion decreases, producing a scene-specific set of cluster candidates. Given a referring expression, a query-conditioned cluster scorer ranks the cluster candidates with a lightweight MLP, and the selected cluster directly defines the explicit 3D target while its cluster score is rendered for the 2D mask. On ScanRefer, ClusterSplat achieves the best rendered 2D segmentation and explicit 3D target metrics among the compared 3DGS baselines. It also obtains the best average Ref-LERF scores, supporting the same trend under fine-grained referring descriptions. On ScanNet v2 class name queries, ClusterSplat achieves the strongest rendered 2D results and the highest 3D matching accuracy, while maintaining competitive 3D IoU. Our project page is available at https://jaesung8.github.io/clustersplat/ .

</details>


### [72] [SplatScout: Query-Driven Part-Level Referring Segmentation in Gaussian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37098)
*Yihan Zang,Da Li,Shinkyu Park,Ivan Viola,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SplatScout，将感知置于VLM控制之下的查询驱动闭环3D指代分割方法，并构建部件级基准PartRef-ScanNet++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯场景指代分割方法主要面向整体对象，依赖分割器预先生成固定候选再由VLM选择；这种预定义候选可能遗漏细粒度部件目标或作为识别线索的部件。

Method: SplatScout采用查询驱动闭环框架：模型主动选择视角、直接引导分割、并检查渲染后的3D结果以迭代修正预测，使感知过程由VLM控制。同时构建PartRef-ScanNet++，含12个ScanNet++场景中261条人工核验查询，真值由网格导出。

Result: 在PartRef-ScanNet++上取得0.538 mIoU，显著优于现有方法；消融验证迭代式修正的收益；跨VLM骨干对比表明有效利用工具反馈的重要性；在四个现有指代基准上亦取得更高分数。

Conclusion: 闭环、主动感知与部件级评估有效提升高斯场景指代分割性能，工具反馈利用能力是性能关键。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation methods on Gaussian scenes primarily target whole objects, relying on segmenter-generated candidates from which a vision-language model (VLM) selects. Such predetermined fixed candidates may omit fine-grained parts needed as targets or cues for identifying objects. We introduce SplatScout, a query-driven, closed-loop approach that places perception under VLM control. The model actively selects views, directs segmentation, and inspects rendered 3D results to refine its predictions. For part-level evaluation, we introduce PartRef-ScanNet++, comprising 261 manually verified queries across 12 ScanNet++ scenes with mesh-derived ground truth. On PartRef-ScanNet++, SplatScout achieves 0.538 mIoU, substantially outperforming existing methods. Ablations demonstrate the benefits of iterative refinement, while comparisons across VLM backbones highlight the importance of effective tool-feedback utilization. SplatScout also achieves higher scores on four existing referring benchmarks. Code and the PartRef-ScanNet++ benchmark are available at https://github.com/kaust-violab/SplatScout.

</details>


### [73] [SAGE: Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts for Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36658)
*Tomoyasu Shimada,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Shoichiro Takeda,Shogo Sato,Riku Inoue,Toshihiko Nishimura,Ryuichi Tanida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE将冻结的预训练点云语义表征引入配准流程，并用语义引导的混合专家架构（SG-MoE）缓解低重叠点云中几何相似但语义不同结构造成的错误对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低重叠点云配准困难，仅依赖几何的方法无法区分几何相似但语义不同的结构（如电视角与桌角、椅腿与桌腿），导致错误对应与几何歧义。

Method: SAGE集成冻结的预训练点云语义表征，无需额外传感器或微调语义编码器；采用语义引导的混合专家架构（SG-MoE），通过语义感知路由使各专家专注不同的语义-几何模式；并结合结构感知估计器完成配准。

Result: 在3DLoMatch上取得最高配准召回率，在3DMatch上保持竞争力；在相同精化迭代次数下，两个基准上均取得最高的内点率与配准召回率。

Conclusion: 冻结的语义表征能为解决低重叠配准中的几何歧义提供有效线索。

Abstract: Point cloud registration is an essential task for scene reconstruction, requiring the alignment of partially overlapping point clouds. Under low-overlap conditions, this task remains challenging because existing geometry-only methods cannot distinguish geometrically similar but semantically distinct structures (e.g., TV corners vs. desk corners and chair legs vs. table legs), resulting in incorrect correspondences. To address this geometric ambiguity, we present Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts (SAGE), which integrates frozen pre-trained point cloud semantic representations into the registration pipeline without requiring additional sensors or fine-tuning the semantic encoder. To effectively leverage these semantic distinctions, SAGE employs a Semantic-Guided Mixture of Experts (SG-MoE) architecture. Through semantic-aware routing, SG-MoE enables each expert to specialize in distinct semantic-geometric patterns, reducing mismatches that confound geometry-only methods. With a structure-aware estimator, SAGE achieves the highest registration recall on 3DLoMatch while remaining competitive on 3DMatch. When evaluated with the same number of refinement iterations, SAGE further achieves the highest inlier ratio and registration recall on both benchmarks. These results indicate that frozen semantic representations provide effective cues for resolving geometric ambiguity in low-overlap registration.

</details>


### [74] [Identity or Evidence? Auditing Evidence Dependence in Agentic Life-Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36510)
*Danai Brilli,Maria Georgila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出，agentic生命科学基准若不具备身份盲化设计，所测分数可能来自对实体名称的识记而非证据推理；作者通过独立操控身份与证据，证明在证据稀薄时名称效应巨大，并呼吁基准报告盲化结果、检索遥测与阳性对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic生命科学基准（如Biomni、LAB-Bench）以命名实体提供证据并对照记录结果评分。这类分数只有在"答案由证据而非名称决定"时才真正检验推理能力。作者希望检验这一前提是否成立，即基准是否可能被名称记忆（身份泄漏）所混淆。

Method: 在一个临床结局基准以及Biomni与LAB-Bench的已发表任务上，独立操控"身份"与"证据"两个因素：对比命名与盲化两种条件下的表现；在96道LAB-Bench LitQA2题目上采用固定的段落遮蔽（redaction）方案，计算每实例斜率并进行统计检验；在临床基准上设计无证据的回忆探针（no-evidence recall probe），检验其能否复现预测准确率并反推数据库特有的程序计数。

Result: 当提示中包含决定性的序列或段落时，盲化身份影响很小；但当提示主要是名称与候选列表时影响极大（命名0.742 vs 盲化0.082，随机水平0.093）。在96道LitQA2题目上，固定遮蔽方案下差距增大（+0.000至+0.135；每实例斜率+0.147，p=0.004）。临床基准上，无证据回忆探针的准确率与预测准确率相当，并能恢复出数据库特有的程序计数。

Conclusion: 旨在衡量证据依赖推理的基准应报告身份盲化结果、检索遥测以及阳性对照，以避免名称识别带来的分数虚高。

Abstract: Agentic life-science benchmarks give an agent evidence about a named entity and score an answer against a recorded outcome. That score tests reasoning over evidence only if the evidence, rather than the name, determines the answer. We examine this distinction by varying identity and evidence independently on a clinical-outcome benchmark and published tasks from Biomni and LAB-Bench. Blinding identity matters little when a prompt contains a decisive sequence or passage. It matters a great deal when the prompt is mostly a name and candidate list (0.742 named versus 0.082 blinded; chance 0.093). Across 96 LAB-Bench LitQA2 questions, the gap increases under one fixed passage-redaction scheme (+0.000 to +0.135; per-instance slope +0.147, p= 0.004). On the clinical benchmark, a no-evidence recall probe matches prediction accuracy and recovers a database-specific programme count. So, we argue that benchmarks intended to measure evidence-dependent reasoning should report identity-blinded results, retrieval telemetry, and positive controls.

</details>


### [75] [GarmentSegAgent: Autonomous Garment Segmentation for Web Fashion Images via Textual Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36506)
*Jia Chen,Chao Huang,Xinrong Hu,Mingdi Huang,Ruyang Zhao,Jun Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GarmentSegAgent自主服装分割框架，核心为CAST（受限归因引导的选择性TextGrad），在推理阶段通过文本自精炼纠正服装特定的分割错误，无需人工空间提示且保持基础模型冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络时尚图像视觉异构性强，服装分割困难；现有可提示分割方法仍依赖人工提供的空间提示，难以自主识别并纠正服装特有的分割错误。

Method: 框架整合语义规划、空间执行与视觉评估，通过迭代文本自精炼实现闭环。核心CAST将视觉错误归因与定向文本优化相连接：识别导致分割错误的指令成分，经文本梯度选择性更新，并用服装特定约束验证新指令。

Result: 在DeepFashion2、ModaNet与Fashionpedia上，区域重叠与边界精度均获一致提升，且精炼可靠性优于常规TextGrad。

Conclusion: 让视觉反馈引导受控的指令更新，为自主服装理解提供可行方向，可应用于可扩展的时尚内容分析、视觉检索及消费者到商家匹配。

Abstract: Web fashion images support applications such as visual product search and consumer-to-shop matching, yet their visual heterogeneity makes reliable garment segmentation challenging. Despite advances in promptable segmentation, autonomously identifying and correcting garment-specific errors without human-provided spatial prompts remains difficult. We propose GarmentSegAgent, an autonomous framework that integrates semantic planning, spatial execution, and visual evaluation through iterative textual self-refinement. At its core, we introduce CAST (Constrained Attribution-guided Selective TextGrad), which connects visual error attribution with targeted textual optimization. CAST identifies instruction components responsible for segmentation errors, selectively updates them through textual gradients, and validates the resulting instructions against garment-specific constraints. This enables image-specific error correction at inference time while keeping the underlying foundation models frozen. Experiments on DeepFashion2, ModaNet, and Fashionpedia demonstrate consistent improvements in region overlap and boundary accuracy, alongside more reliable refinement than conventional TextGrad. By enabling visual feedback to guide controlled instruction updates, GarmentSegAgent offers a promising direction for autonomous garment understanding, with potential applications in scalable fashion content analysis, visual retrieval, and consumer-to-shop matching on the Web.

</details>


### [76] [UT-HMR: Uncertainty-Guided Temporal Modeling for Robust 3D Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36203)
*Zhibo Wang,Sirisha Rambhatla,Yuhao Chen,Maria Koshkina,David Clausi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UT-HMR：面向弱观测场景的单阶段3D人体网格恢复框架，用刻画关节相关的不确定性建模歧义，并用时序早融合注入邻帧证据，在VIP-HockeyPose-v2与WorldPose上以ViT-B骨干取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D人体网格恢复在长相机-人距离、遮挡与运动模糊造成的弱观测下高度病态：不同3D姿态可产生相似2D投影。该歧义对直接从整图恢复所有网格、不使用人体裁剪或外部2D线索的一阶段（one-stage）方法尤为严重。

Method: 提出UT-HMR（Uncertainty-guided Temporal HMR），据称是首个捕获跨关节相关姿态不确定性的单阶段HMR框架。每个person query预测完整SMPL姿态上的高斯分布，协方差采用紧凑的“低秩+对角”参数化；该分布被转换为关节级精度权重，用于异方差监督（heteroscedastic supervision），同时保持推理确定性。此外引入时序早融合，在姿态解码前注入相邻帧证据，为消解不确定性所刻画的歧义提供所需视觉信息。骨干为轻量ViT-B。

Result: 在VIP-HockeyPose-v2（冰球）与WorldPose（足球）上达到SOTA，MPJPE、PA-MPJPE、PVE分别最多降低4.7%、3.8%、5.6%；在3DPW上与更大骨干、更高输入分辨率的方法相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 将跨关节相关的不确定性显式建模并作为监督权重，配合时序早融合，是在弱观测下缓解一阶段HMR歧义的有效途径；且无需重型骨干即可在体育等挑战场景取得领先性能。

Abstract: Monocular 3D human mesh recovery (HMR) remains unreliable under weak observations caused by long camera-to-person distances, occlusion, and motion blur. In challenging scenarios such as sports, these factors make the problem highly ambiguous, as different 3D poses may yield similar 2D projections. This ambiguity is especially severe for one-stage HMR, which recovers all meshes directly from the full image without person crops or external 2D cues. To handle this ambiguity, we present UT-HMR ( Uncertainty-guided Temporal HMR), to our knowledge the first one-stage HMR framework to capture correlated pose uncertainty across joints. Each person query predicts a Gaussian distribution over the full SMPL pose with a compact low-rank-plus-diagonal covariance, which is converted into joint-wise precision weights for heteroscedastic supervision while keeping inference deterministic. Temporal early fusion further injects neighboring-frame evidence before pose decoding, supplying the visual evidence needed to resolve the ambiguity that the uncertainty captures. With a lightweight ViT-B backbone, UT-HMR achieves state-of-the-art results on VIP-HockeyPose-v2 (ice hockey) and WorldPose (soccer), reducing MPJPE, PA-MPJPE, and PVE by up to 4.7%, 3.8%, and 5.6%, and remains competitive on 3DPW against methods with larger backbones and higher input resolutions.

</details>


### [77] [OmniGlimpse: Asymmetric Identity Memory for Online Multi-View Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36785)
*Rangya Zhang,Jiaping Xiao,Yujing Shang,Yuhang Zhang,Mingsheng Li,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniGlimpse——非对称“提议-校验”框架，在无离线场景训练与相机标定下实现在线多视角身份维护（OMIM），显著提升IDF1与CVIDF1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角跟踪可弥补遮挡与出视野的信息缺失，但跨视角观测如何匹配到正确身份仍是难题：离线方法依赖场景特定标定与训练，在线自适应又易放大关联错误，且每次初始化后不再提供身份标签。

Method: 将问题形式化为在线多视角身份维护（OMIM）。表示层面：短期局部与跨视角证据映射到目标特定的自适应空间以“提议”关联；长期身份分布则在冻结编码器定义的规范空间中维护用于“校验”。更新层面：短期证据仅指导当前关联，自举之后仅经校验的观测可更新持久记忆或驱动自适应。

Result: 统一的目标指定评测协议下，M3Track上较最优在线基线各项指标提升9.1 IDF1与22.1 CVIDF1；MvMHAT上分别提升20.3与26.2。无需离线场景特定训练即在IDF1上超越离线训练参照，跨视角关联亦具竞争力。

Conclusion: 表明将自适应关联与稳定身份固化分离，可在适应外观变化的同时把可靠证据凝聚为稳定身份锚点，是可行的在线身份维护路径。

Abstract: Multi-view tracking extends visual coverage beyond a single camera, providing complementary observations when targets are occluded or leave the view. This raises a question: how can observations from different views be matched to the correct identities without offline scene-specific training or camera calibration? Progress toward this goal remains limited because many offline formulations assume such deployment-specific knowledge, while online adaptation can amplify association errors. We study this problem as online multi-view identity maintenance (OMIM): targets are initialized once per view, with no further identity labels provided after each initialization. To this end, we introduce OmniGlimpse, an asymmetric propose-verify framework that separates adaptive association from stable identity consolidation. At the representation level, short-term local and cross-view evidence is mapped into target-specific adaptive spaces to propose associations, whereas long-term identity distributions are maintained for verification in a canonical space defined by a frozen encoder. At the update level, short-term evidence may guide current associations, whereas, after bootstrapping, only verified observations may update persistent memory or drive adaptation. Under a unified target-specified evaluation protocol, OmniGlimpse improves over the best evaluated online baseline for each metric by 9.1 IDF1 and 22.1 CVIDF1 points on M3Track. The corresponding gains on MvMHAT are 20.3 and 26.2 points. Despite using no offline scene-specific training, it outperforms offline-trained references in IDF1 and remains competitive in cross-view association. These results support an approach to online identity maintenance that adapts to changing appearances while consolidating reliable evidence into stable identity anchors.

</details>


### [78] [GeoGSplat: Geometry-Grounded Non-Pixel-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36760)
*Jiale Wang,Chen Zhao,Haoyang Zhang,Yanhao Wu,Fei He,Yanhu Shan,Rui Wu,Wei Ke,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoGSplat提出一种几何约束的非像素对齐前馈高斯泼溅模型，用双分支编码器构建Anchors与Triplane，并用层次化解码器逐步解码稠密3D点与高斯原语，在DTU与NRGBD的新视角合成与重建任务上超越现有方法，且大视角变化下泛化更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈高斯泼溅效率高，但像素对齐预测使原语数量随图像分辨率与视图数增长，带来冗余与计算开销;非像素对齐方法虽以固定数量高斯缓解该问题，却缺乏显式射线与深度约束，导致高斯中心错位，在大视角变化下出现渲染伪影。

Method: 在非像素对齐框架中显式引入几何先验：双分支编码器分别构建Anchors与Triplane，前者捕获粗粒度3D结构、后者提取细粒度场景特征;随后由层次化解码器渐进地解码稠密3D点与高斯原语，统一支持3D重建与新视角合成。

Result: 在DTU与NRGBD数据集上，新视角合成与重建指标均优于SOTA的像素对齐与非像素对齐高斯泼溅方法;随目标视角远离输入相机，性能优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 显式几何约束可有效修正非像素对齐范式中的高斯中心错位问题，几何约束范式对大视角变化具有更强泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but pixel-aligned prediction makes the primitive count grow with image resolution and view count, leading to substantial redundancy and increased computational cost. Non-pixel-aligned methods address this limitation by predicting a fixed number of Gaussians. Without explicit ray and depth constraints, these methods yield misplaced Gaussian centers, causing rendering artifacts under large viewpoint changes. To address this issue, we introduce GeoGSplat, a geometry-grounded feed-forward model that explicitly incorporates geometric priors into the non-pixel-aligned framework. A dual-branch encoder constructs Anchors and Triplane to capture coarse 3D structure and fine-grained scene features, respectively. Subsequently, a hierarchical decoder progressively decodes dense 3D points and Gaussian primitives, enabling 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis. GeoGSplat surpasses both state-of-the-art pixel-aligned and non-pixel-aligned Gaussian Splatting methods on DTU and NRGBD in terms of novel view synthesis and reconstruction. The advantage of our geometry-grounded paradigm further increases as target viewpoints move farther from the input cameras, demonstrating stronger generalization to large viewpoint changes. Project Page: https://comedy0913.github.io/projects/geogsplat

</details>


### [79] [Progressively Refining SAM for Accurate Phytoplankton Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36722)
*Jialin Li,Jinkun Jiang,Yuezun Li,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于SAM的浮游植物分割框架PhySeg，通过轻量级多尺度特征融合细化模块逐步恢复细粒度细节，并构建了带实例级像素掩码的浮游植物分割数据集，实验验证了分割精度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮游植物精确分割对生态监测至关重要，但其视觉特征细微、像素级标注稀缺，导致该任务研究不足。

Method: 在SAM基础上构建PhySeg分割框架，附带轻量级细化模块，通过多尺度特征融合逐步恢复细粒度细节；同时为已有数据集人工标注实例级掩码，构建浮游植物分割数据集。

Result: 实验表明PhySeg提升了分割精度，验证了所提细化模块的有效性；同时提供了可用的数据集与开源代码。

Conclusion: 将通用分割基础模型与轻量级多尺度细化相结合可有效应对浮游植物分割中细节细微与标注稀缺的问题，所构建数据集为后续研究提供资源。

Abstract: Accurate phytoplankton segmentation is essential for ecological monitoring, yet it remains largely underexplored due to the subtle visual characteristics of phytoplankton and the scarcity of pixel-level annotations. To address these challenges, we propose PhySeg, a SAM-based segmentation framework equipped with a lightweight refinement module that progressively restores fine-grained details through multi-scale feature fusion. In addition, we construct a phytoplankton segmentation dataset by manually annotating instance-level masks for an existing dataset, providing a valuable resource for future research. Experimental results demonstrate that PhySeg improves segmentation accuracy, validating the effectiveness of the proposed module. Code is available at https://github.com/lijialin404/PhySeg.

</details>


### [80] [CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341)
*Jiafu Tang,Yifan Xu,Eric Tang,Junjie Zhao,Shipeng Luo,Te Sun,Haoxuan Hu,Yiyan Ji,Xinlong Chen,Zihao Wang,Yang Shi,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"代码作为视频字幕"（Code as Video Caption）范式，用可执行程序（图形基元、布局规则、时间函数）作为视觉内容的互补表示，并构建 CodeCap-Bench 基准评估 MLLM 的代码字幕生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言字幕在精确表达细粒度空间结构与时间动态时往往冗长且存在歧义，难以忠实保留视觉信息，因此需要一种更紧凑、无歧义的表示形式。

Method: 1）以可执行程序作为视频字幕，通过图形基元、布局规则与时间函数编码视觉内容；2）在下游问答与视频重建两类任务上与自然语言字幕对比，验证信息保真度；3）构建 CodeCap-Bench，含 323 个视频、覆盖 5 类结构化 2D 内容，配细粒度标注与多层级评测框架；4）对主流 MLLM（闭源与开源）开展系统性实验。

Result: 代码字幕在推理准确率与重建保真度上均优于自然语言字幕；领先的闭源 MLLM 已展现出可观的代码字幕生成能力，但在忠实捕捉复杂时间动态方面仍有困难，开源模型则明显落后。

Conclusion: 代码字幕是一种有效且具互补性的视频表示，但其潜力能否释放取决于 MLLM 的忠实生成能力；CodeCap-Bench 为系统评估该能力提供了基准，并揭示时间动态建模是当前的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Video captioning aims to preserve visual information through compact descriptions, predominantly using natural language. However, precisely expressing fine-grained spatial structures and temporal dynamics in natural language often leads to lengthy and potentially ambiguous descriptions. In this work, we explore Code as Video Caption, a paradigm where executable programs serve as a complementary representation of visual content through graphical primitives, layout rules, and functions of time. To examine whether code captions effectively preserve video information, we compare them with natural language captions on downstream question answering and video reconstruction tasks. Our results show that code captions achieve superior reasoning accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Despite these advantages, realizing the potential of code captioning depends on whether current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate faithful code captions. To systematically assess this capability, we introduce CodeCap-Bench, which comprises 323 videos spanning five categories of structured 2D content, along with fine-grained annotations and a multi-level evaluation framework. Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

</details>


### [81] [ScIntBench: Physical Interaction Plausibility in 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37326)
*Wendi Ying,Daochang Liu,Leonid Sigal,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScIntBench基准，以"交互就绪度"为核心评估文本驱动3D室内场景生成，通过物理仿真中执行推、开门、表面探测等操作，测量任务成功率、能力与进度、交互后完整性与动态合理性；评测12个SOTA系统覆盖10类房间，发现语义与静态物理表现良好并不能转化为交互可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件3D室内场景生成评估主要关注静态下的语义保真度与物理有效性，对交互过程中的物理可靠性评估不足，缺少面向"交互就绪"的统一评测手段。

Method: 构建ScIntBench统一基准：在物理仿真中执行预设操作动作（推动、开启、表面探测等），度量任务成功率、能力与进度、交互后完整性、动态合理性；并以多视角语义评估与静态物理评估作为补充，用于解释交互结果。系统评测12个最先进场景生成系统、覆盖10类房间。

Result: 语义与静态物理表现强并不必然带来交互就绪：静态合理性近乎完美的系统交互完成率仅11.54%；12个系统中有7个生成的场景无法支持任何交互；最佳系统仅在36.92%的预设任务中成功。

Conclusion: 生成交互就绪场景存在两项根本挑战：一是提供必要的物理交互能力，二是确保主动操作下可靠的物理行为；未来场景生成评测应将交互就绪度纳入核心指标。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation largely emphasize semantic fidelity and physical validity at rest, leaving interaction-time physical reliability insufficiently assessed. To address this gap, we introduce ScIntBench, a unified benchmark centered on interaction readiness: it executes prescribed manipulation actions (e.g., pushing, opening, and surface probing) in physics simulation and measures task success, capability and progress, post-interaction integrity, and dynamic plausibility. Multi-view semantic and static physical evaluations complement these metrics and help explain interaction outcomes. We systematically evaluate 12 state-of-the-art scene generation systems across 10 room types. Our results reveal that strong semantic and static physical performance does not necessarily translate into interaction readiness: a system with near-perfect static plausibility completes only 11.54% of interactions, 7 of the 12 systems produce scenes that cannot support any interaction, and even the best system succeeds in only 36.92% of prescribed tasks. These findings highlight two fundamental challenges in generating interaction-ready scenes: providing the necessary physical interaction capabilities and ensuring reliable physical behavior under active manipulation.

</details>


### [82] [HDVS: semi-supervised semantic segmentation via heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37236)
*Yongqi Shan,Yunzhi Zhuge,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出异构双分支投票监督框架（HDVS），通过投票机制融合异构分支伪标签并引入特征级扰动，提升伪标签可靠性，抑制协同训练中的误差累积，在PASCAL VOC 2012与Cityscapes上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督语义分割方法多假设各分支生成的伪标签足够可靠，可用于监督其余分支；但伪标签误差会在协同训练中累积，导致性能次优。

Method: 在交叉监督框架基础上引入投票机制，将异构分支的伪标签进行关联融合，以产生可靠性更高的伪标签；同时采用特征通信模块在各分支的特征层面引入扰动，在保持收敛的同时最大化双分支间的预测多样性。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC 2012与Cityscapes两个基准数据集上的全面实验表明，HDVS优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: HDVS通过提升伪标签可靠性与增加分支预测多样性，有效缓解伪标签误差累积问题，是一种有效的半监督语义分割框架。

Abstract: Semi-supervised semantic segmentation, which aims to utilize large volumes of unlabeled images to achieve accurate segmentation results with fewer human annotations, has attracted increasing attention. Prior methods were primarily based on the assumption that the pseudo-labels generated by each branch were sufficiently reliable to supervise the remaining branches. However, there is a concern that the errors in pseudo-labels could accumulate during co-training, potentially resulting in suboptimal performance. To this end, we present a novel framework called heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision (HDVS), which is designed to enhance the reliability of pseudo-labels and mitigate the issues arising from pseudo-labeling. Specifically, based on the cross-supervision framework, we introduce a voting mechanism that correlates pseudo-labels from heterogeneous branches to produce pseudo-labels with enhanced reliability. Concurrently, we employ a feature communication module to introduce perturbations at the feature level in each branch to maximize prediction diversity across the dual branches while maintaining convergence. Comprehensive evaluations of the proposed HDVS on two benchmark datasets (PASCAL VOC 2012 and Cityscapes) demonstrate its superiority to the state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [83] [Subspace Clifford Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37226)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Subspace Clifford Networks，用分组子空间交互替代全局循环移位，并配套融合 CUDA kernel；在 CIFAR-100 上显著降低参数量与训练时间，层次化变体还提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原方法需在整个 D 维通道空间中采样稀疏循环对角线，导致中间特征扩张大（约 10D）、计算与内存开销高；同时等向列式结构缺乏多分辨率能力。

Method: 将特征投影到对齐空间 R^D̃ = ⊕V_g，划分为 G 个 k 维（k∈{2,3,4}）低维组；组内计算全部 C(k,2) 个楔积坐标并与 SiLU 激活的逐坐标乘积拼接，组间交互夹在两个可学习 1×1 线性投影之间实现跨组通道混合；中间扩张降至 1.5D̃/2.0D̃/2.5D̃；利用固定小 k 实现融合 CUDA kernel，前向交互与基于重计算的分析式反向，使用线程局部变量、不分配全局内存中间缓冲；并将骨干扩展为多分辨率 Hierarchical Clifford Network，支持逐阶段配置子空间维度。

Result: CIFAR-100 实验显示两类模型（等向与层次化）参数量与训练时间均大幅下降，层次化变体相对所评估的基于移位的层次化基线取得分类精度提升。

Conclusion: 以分组子空间楔积替代全局循环移位可在保持表达力的同时压缩中间特征并利于核融合；配合层次化多分辨率设计可同时获得效率与精度收益。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Subspace Clifford Networks, a reformulation that replaces global cyclic rolling with grouped subspace interactions. Instead of sampling sparse cyclic diagonals across the entire $D$-dimensional channel space, we project the features into an aligned space $\R^{\tilde{D}} = \bigoplus_{g=1}^{G} V_g$ partitioned into $G$ low-dimensional groups $V_g \cong \R^k$ with $k \in \{2, 3, 4\}$. Within each $k$-dimensional group, we compute all $\binom{k}{2}$ wedge coordinates and concatenate them with $\operatorname{SiLU}$-activated coordinate-wise products, while sandwiching the grouped interaction between learnable $1\times 1$ linear projections to enable cross-group channel mixing. This design reduces intermediate feature expansion from $2|\mathcal{S}|D$ (typically $10D$ for 5 shifts) down to $1.5\tilde{D}$ ($k=2$), $2.0\tilde{D}$ ($k=3$), or $2.5\tilde{D}$ ($k=4$). Furthermore, exploiting the small, fixed group size $k \in \{2, 3, 4\}$, we implement a fused CUDA kernel that evaluates the forward interaction and a recomputation-based analytical backward pass using thread-local variables without allocating global-memory buffers for elementwise intermediates. Finally, we extend the backbone from an isotropic columnar layout to a multi-resolution Hierarchical Clifford Network with configurable per-stage subspace dimensions. Experiments on CIFAR-100 show that the proposed models substantially reduce parameter count and training time across both regimes, while the hierarchical variants also improve classification accuracy over the evaluated shift-based hierarchical baselines.

</details>


### [84] [A First-Step Bias Restores Lost Diversity in Diffusion Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37110)
*Wenwu Tang,Dong Wang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReSeed：仅在蒸馏学生模型的第一个去噪步加一个seed-response bias adapter，并与教师首段割线速度对齐，以极轻量参数恢复输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布匹配蒸馏使学生模型能用极少步数生成高质量图像，但不同初始噪声在相同提示下被映射到相似输出，多样性显著丧失；需要定位多样性在哪一步损失并加以恢复。

Method: 沿采样轨迹对比教师与学生模型对不同初始噪声的响应，发现最大响应衰减出现在第一个高噪声步；用教师对应轨迹替换第一步、保留后三步即可恢复大部分多样性。据此设计后蒸馏方法ReSeed：仅对第一步加seed-response bias adapter，使其速度与教师第一区间的割线速度对齐，其余学生权重与后续去噪步全部冻结。

Result: 在SD3-Medium、SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-dev及视频模型Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B上验证。4步FLUX.1-dev上仅需0.00196%额外可训练参数、8000条prompt–noise对，无需目标图像、感知损失或对抗损失，DINO多样性提升48.3%且视觉质量具竞争力。

Conclusion: 多样性损失主要集中于首个高噪声去噪步；仅对该步做轻量、数据高效的调优即可大幅恢复多样性，无需微调整个学生模型。

Abstract: Distribution-matching distillation enables high-quality image generation in only a few denoising steps. However, distilled student models often map different initial noise samples to similar outputs under the same prompt. To locate where diversity is lost, we compare the teacher and student along the sampling trajectory. The largest measured reduction in the response to different initial noises occurs during the first, high-noise step. When we replace the first step with the teacher's corresponding trajectory and leave the final three student steps unchanged, most of the output diversity returns. This suggests that the first step is a key point for restoring diversity. Motivated by this finding, we introduce \emph{ReSeed}, a post-distillation method that adds a seed-response bias adapter only to the first student step and aligns its velocity with the teacher's first-interval secant velocity. All student weights and later denoising steps remain unchanged. We evaluate ReSeed on three text-to-image models (SD3-Medium, SD3.5-Medium, and FLUX.1-dev) and one text-to-video model (Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B). For a distilled 4-step FLUX.1-dev model, \emph{ReSeed} requires only $0.00196\%$ additional trainable parameters relative to the base model and is trained on 8,000 prompt--noise pairs, while keeping the distilled student fully frozen and requiring no target images, perceptual losses, or adversarial losses. It improves DINO diversity by $48.3\%$ while maintaining competitive visual quality. These results show that substantial diversity can be recovered through a lightweight, data-efficient tuning of only the first denoising step, without finetuning the full student model.

</details>


### [85] [FrameForge: Harnessing Priors of Video Foundation Models for Unified Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36545)
*Yijing Lin,Zhendong Mao,Mengqi Huang,Yuxin Wang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameForge 通过输入自适应的"虚拟时间关系"建模统一条件图像生成：Mixture of Temporal Experts 动态选择不同虚拟间隔的时序 RoPE 专家以刻画目标与条件的距离，Chain of Temporal Reasoning 借助 latent 中间状态与推理瓶颈注意力掩码刻画条件到目标的变换路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一条件图像生成需单一模型在遵循文本引导的同时保留输入视觉的不同方面。预训练视频模型能编码跨时间的视觉变换，但现有方法把条件与目标固定在特定时序位置上；而条件-目标关系在不同生成任务间差异显著，视频模型本身也在不同时间尺度上编码变换，固定位置因此不匹配任务需求。

Method: 提出 FrameForge，建模输入自适应的虚拟时间关系，含两个互补组件：(1) Mixture of Temporal Experts (MoTE)：通过动态选择具有不同虚拟间隔的时序 RoPE 专家，建模目标相对条件的"距离"；(2) Chain of Temporal Reasoning (CoTR)：通过 latent 中间状态建模条件到目标的"变换"，并用 reasoning-bottleneck attention mask 约束信息沿该路径传播。

Result: 在 DreamBench、COCO2017 与 GEdit-Bench 上，FrameForge 在主体驱动生成、canny-to-image 生成与图像编辑三类任务中均取得优于对比方法的性能。

Conclusion: 将条件-目标关系表述为输入自适应的虚拟时间关系，并在"距离"（MoTE）与"变换"（CoTR）两个互补维度上建模，可有效释放预训练视频模型在统一条件图像生成中的潜力。

Abstract: Unified conditional image generation requires a single model to preserve different aspects of a visual input while producing a target image that follows textual guidance. Pretrained video models provide a promising foundation by encoding visual transformations across time, yet existing approaches typically assign the condition and target to fixed temporal positions. We observe that condition-target relationships vary substantially across generation tasks, while video models naturally encode visual transformations at different temporal scales. This motivates us to model conditional generation through input-adaptive virtual temporal relations. We propose FrameForge, which captures two complementary aspects of such relations. Mixture of Temporal Experts (MoTE) models how far the target lies from the condition by dynamically selecting temporal RoPE experts with different virtual intervals. Chain of Temporal Reasoning (CoTR) models how the condition is transformed into the target through a latent intermediate state, with a reasoning-bottleneck attention mask encouraging information propagation through this pathway. Experiments on DreamBench, COCO2017, and GEdit-Bench show that FrameForge achieves superior performance in subject-driven generation, canny-to-image generation, and image editing among the compared methods.

</details>


### [86] [Task-Utility IQA: Rethinking Image Quality Assessment in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35905)
*Jirong Zha,Yanwei Liao,Yemin Wang,Xiangmin Yi,Siqi Peng,Yingfeng Chen,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张将图像质量评价（IQA）从静态基准上的任务无关分数回归，重构为具身任务效用驱动的目标条件化判断，并提出基于多模态大模型的具身IQA智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IQA方法在静态基准上学习与通用人类评分对齐的任务无关分数，这与具身、交互场景不匹配——后者的图像充分性取决于任务目标、上下文与动作需求；且感知指标与通用MLLM评分难以作为下游VLM任务成功的代理。

Method: 提出MLLM-based具身IQA智能体框架：推理任务需求、定位质量证据、输出面向决策的评估；并通过一个精简的VisDrone证据研究，对比传统感知指标、通用MLLM评分与任务条件化效用分数对下游VLM任务成功的预测能力。

Result: VisDrone证据研究表明，传统感知指标与通用MLLM IQA分数是下游VLM任务成功的弱代理，而任务条件化效用分数能更好地预测视觉输入是否支撑目标任务。

Conclusion: 支持面向任务效用的IQA重构，服务具身、可解释、决策感知的视觉感知；IQA应从分数回归转向由具身任务效用定义的目标条件化判断。

Abstract: Image quality assessment (IQA) underpins embodied imaging pipelines by judging whether visual quality satisfies downstream tasks, yet most existing methods learn task-agnostic scores aligned with generic human ratings on static benchmarks. This objective mismatches embodied and interactive settings, where image adequacy depends on task goals, context, and action requirements that shape an agent's decisions. We argue that IQA should shift from score regression to goal-conditioned judgment defined by embodied task utility. To this end, we propose an MLLM-based embodied IQA agent framework that reasons about task requirements, grounds quality evidence, and produces decision-facing assessments. A compact VisDrone evidence study shows that conventional perceptual metrics and generic MLLM-based IQA scores are weak proxies for downstream VLM task success, while a task-conditioned utility score better predicts whether visual input supports the target task. These findings support a task-utility-oriented reframing of IQA for embodied, explainable, and decision-aware visual perception.

</details>


### [87] [Full Collapse Is Suboptimal: Controlled Partial Neural Collapse for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36460)
*Hyeontaek Hwang,Cheonghwan Hur,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“受控部分神经坍缩”机制，通过CASA分类器与ETF-AM损失两个协同控制，使头类沿ETF完全坍缩、尾类保留类内多样性，在多个长尾基准上取得最优总体精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长尾识别方法将神经坍缩（NC）与固定ETF对齐，作为所有类的统一目标；但强制尾类完全坍缩会过度收缩其样本分布、削弱泛化能力，在类别不平衡下并非最优。

Method: 提出受控部分神经坍缩，在骨干网络上实现两个协同控制：(1) 类自适应稀疏激活分类器（CASA），通过特征条件门控诱导类相关节点专门化，使尾类占据独立特征方向，从而设定坍缩的维度；(2) ETF约束自适应间隔损失（ETF-AM），沿“间隔（clearance）”轴施加频率自适应间隔，头类小间隔、尾类大间隔。

Result: 在CIFAR100-LT、ImageNet-LT、Places-LT与iNaturalist-2018上，总体精度优于已有方法；实证分析显示类内散布从头类到尾类单调递增，与尾类保留变异性而非完全坍缩的预期一致。

Conclusion: 统一的全坍缩目标对长尾场景次优，按类频率差异化地控制坍缩程度（维度与间隔两个轴）可在保持判别性的同时提升尾类泛化，是一种有效的长尾识别范式。

Abstract: Recent long-tailed recognition (LTR) methods incorporate neural collapse (NC) and align features to a fixed Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) as a uniform target for all classes. However, forcing tail classes to fully collapse over-contracts their samples and weakens generalization. To address this, we propose a controlled partial neural collapse, realized through two coordinated controls on a backbone: the Class-Adaptive Sparse Activation (CASA) classifier and the ETF-constrained adaptive margin (ETF-AM) loss. Rather than forcing a uniform full collapse, this regime allows head classes to collapse along the ETF while tail classes retain within-class diversity. This prevents the tail from being forced into full collapse, which is suboptimal under imbalance. To set the dimensionality of collapse, CASA induces class-related node specialization through feature-conditioned gating so that tail classes occupy their own feature directions. For the clearance axis, ETF-AM applies a frequency-adaptive margin, with smaller margins for head classes and larger for tail classes. On CIFAR100-LT, ImageNet-LT, Places-LT, and iNaturalist-2018, our method achieves the best overall accuracy over prior methods. In the empirical analysis, within-class spread increases monotonically from head to tail classes, consistent with tail classes retaining variability rather than fully collapsing.

</details>


### [88] [MRI-to-CT Translation of Spinal Images via Anatomy-Guided Pseudo-Pairing and Adversarial Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37134)
*Eduardo Nieto,Ibai Genua,Davide Scorza,Karen López-Linares*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“解剖引导伪配对+对抗扩散+3D精修”三阶段框架，从未配对脊柱MRI合成CT样图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱疾病诊断常用MRI，但术前规划需CT以更好显示椎体骨性解剖；由MRI合成CT可避免额外辐射暴露并简化术前评估流程，而临床中配对的MRI-CT数据通常难以获取。

Method: 三阶段框架：①解剖引导的椎体匹配系统，利用脊柱分割识别相似的矢状位MRI与CT切片，经刚性配准对齐，从非配对扫描构造伪配对数据；②用这些伪配对训练对抗扩散模型完成MRI→CT转换；③将合成切片拼装为3D体数据，并由精修网络提升层间连续性与结构一致性。

Result: 训练集为逾10,000张非配对矢状位MRI与CT切片，测试集为83例MRI体数据共1,911张切片，覆盖全脊柱区域；评价结合分布类指标与椎体分割指标。相比单独扩散模型，二值3D椎体Dice由0.666提升至0.739；体数据后处理减少了堆叠切片间可见不连续，并改善轴位与冠状位椎体一致性。

Conclusion: 解剖引导伪配对、对抗扩散与3D精修的组合可提升脊柱MRI→CT合成质量：椎体匹配主要增强解剖对齐与保真度，体数据精修主要改善3D一致性；结果支持进一步发展面向术前规划的MRI衍生CT样可视化，重点是在保持体数据一致性的同时保留细微强度细节。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is routinely used to diagnose spinal disorders, but surgical planning often requires Computed Tomography (CT) for better visualization of vertebral bone anatomy. Synthesizing CT from MRI could avoid additional radiation exposure and streamline preoperative assessment.
  We propose a three-stage framework for spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. First, an anatomy-guided vertebral matching system identifies similar sagittal MRI and CT slices using spine segmentations and aligns them through rigid registration, creating pseudo-paired data from unpaired scans. Second, these pairs train an adversarial diffusion model for MRI-to-CT translation. Third, synthesized slices are assembled into 3D volumes and refined by a network that improves inter-slice continuity and structural coherence.
  The framework was trained on over 10,000 unpaired sagittal MRI and CT slices and evaluated on 1,911 slices from 83 MRI volumes spanning all spinal regions. Assessment combined distribution-based measures with vertebral segmentation metrics. Compared with diffusion alone, the proposed approach increased binary 3D vertebral Dice from 0.666 to 0.739. Volumetric post-processing reduced visible discontinuities between stacked slices and improved vertebral coherence in axial and coronal views.
  Combining anatomy-guided pseudo-pairing, adversarial diffusion and 3D refinement improves spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. Vertebral matching primarily enhanced anatomical alignment and fidelity, while volumetric refinement improved 3D consistency. These findings support further development of MRI-derived CT-like visualization for preoperative planning, particularly to preserve fine intensity details while maintaining volumetric coherence.

</details>


### [89] [Look Only When Needed: Certificate-Guided Visual Routing for Efficient Diffusion VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36671)
*Donghyeon Cho,Joowon Kim,Seungho Shin,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOWN 是一种无需训练的扩散视觉语言模型解码方法，在每个解码步自适应决定是否处理图像以及该步掩码多少 token，在十个基准上以平均 35.3% 的解码 FLOPs 保留 LLaDA-V 99.6% 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVLMs 在每个去噪步都处理完整输入，而视觉 token 占输入的 89-97%，导致大部分解码计算被重复处理同一张图像所消耗。视觉 token 剪枝虽能缓解，但会丢失信息且仍在每步处理剩余图像。作者通过对比 LLaDA-V 在有/无图像条件下的预测发现，移除图像仅在平均 22.8%（低至 7.9%）的解码步上改变将被解掩的 token，说明图像并非每步都必需。

Method: LOWN 逐步决策：(1) 先在不使用图像的情况下预测掩码 token；(2) 仅当该预测与上一次图像条件预测不一致时才处理图像；(3) 一致则认证先前预测可复用，并以被认证 token 的数量决定该步解掩的 token 数。方法无需训练、无需调阈值，并可适配不同基准对图像需求的差异，而非固定调度。

Result: 在十个基准上保留 LLaDA-V 99.6% 的分数，同时平均仅消耗 35.3% 的解码 FLOPs；在基于 LLaDA 的 DVLMs 上具有泛化性，可与视觉 token 剪枝正交组合，并可在 key-value 缓存下运行以进一步提速。

Conclusion: 图像信息在扩散视觉语言模型解码中具有显著的步级冗余性；LOWN 通过"按需处理图像 + 认证式自适应解掩"在不训练的前提下大幅降低解码计算量并保持性能，提供了一种通用、可组合的高效解码范式。

Abstract: Diffusion vision-language models (DVLMs) process their entire input at every denoising step. Since visual tokens make up 89-97% of this input on the benchmarks we study, most of the decoding computation is spent repeatedly processing the same image. Visual token pruning methods partially relieve this burden by removing part of the visual tokens, but they lose the removed information and still process the remaining image at every step. To measure how often the image is actually needed, we compare LLaDA-V's predictions with and without the image at every decoding step and find that removing the image changes a token being unmasked at only 22.8% of steps on average and at as few as 7.9%. We therefore propose LOWN, a decoding method that decides at every step whether to process the image and how many tokens to unmask. LOWN predicts the masked tokens without the image and processes the image only when this prediction disagrees with the last image-conditioned prediction. Agreement certifies the earlier prediction for reuse, and the number of certified tokens sets how many are unmasked in that step. We further observe that the need for the image differs across benchmarks, so LOWN's adaptive decision is needed rather than a fixed schedule. Without training or a tuned threshold, LOWN retains 99.6% of LLaDA-V's score on ten benchmarks at 35.3% of the decoding FLOPs on average. LOWN also generalizes across LLaDA-based DVLMs, composes orthogonally with visual token pruning, and runs under key-value caching for further efficiency gains. Code and project page are available at https://hyeon9698.github.io/LOWN/

</details>


### [90] [Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37668)
*Prakhar Kaushik,Soumava Paul,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hexels 提出平面六边形表面元 + 一阶空间颜色场，以更少的更大基元表示 3D 场景与 2D 图像，在更低训练显存下显著提升重建与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场与 splatting 在新视角合成与三维重建成效显著，但其内存与计算开销限制了在资源受限场景中的应用；现有表面元基元在表示效率（基元数量、边界/面积利用率、外观变化建模）上仍有改进空间。

Method: 设计平面六边形表面元（Hexels），其六边形支撑域在面积与边界效率上更优，可在局部平面区域内用更少、更大的基元覆盖；每个基元附加一阶空间颜色场以建模基元内部平滑变化的外观。方法作为通用基元，可迁移到 2D 图像表示。

Result: 34 个场景上，内存对齐 2DGS 时 PSNR 提升 4.53–7.38 dB 且训练 VRAM 更低；单基元对比中最多提升 4.74 dB，VRAM 更低且 FPS 快 2.31 倍；15 个 DTU 扫描误差降低 28.9%；6 个 Tanks and Temples 场景平均 F1 最高（34.38% vs. 2DGS 31.72%）；2D 图像表示优于 Image-GS。

Conclusion: Hexels 在质量—效率权衡上推进了表面元基元的 Pareto 前沿，兼顾渲染质量、几何重建精度与显存/速度效率，适用于资源受限环境。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction have advanced rapidly with radiance fields and splatting, yet their memory and computational demands limit their use in resource-constrained settings. We introduce Hexels: novel planar hexagonal surface elements with a first-order spatial color field for efficient 3D scene and 2D image representation. Hexels exploit locally planar regions with smoothly varying appearance using fewer, larger primitives. Their hexagonal support provides an area- and boundary-efficient domain, while the local color field captures appearance variation within each primitive. Across 34 scenes, Hexels improve PSNR over memory-matched 2DGS by 4.53-7.38 dB across datasets while requiring lower training VRAM. We further ablate Hexels by showing that per primitive, Hexels outperform 2DGS by upto 4.74 dB while using lower training VRAM and having 2.31 times faster FPS. Even in unconstrained settings, Hexels offers complementary quality-memory trade-offs. For efficient 3D surface reconstruction, Hexels reduce error by 28.9% compared with memory-matched 2DGS across 15 DTU scans. On 6 Tanks and Temples surface scenes, Hexels achieves the highest mean F1 among compared methods (34.38%, versus 31.72% for 2DGS). We further also show that Hexels, as a general primitive, improves 2D image representation over Image-GS. Hexels push the Pareto frontier on the quality-efficiency trade-off of surface-aligned primitives.

</details>


### [91] [TopoField: Technical Report for ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37218)
*Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Mingyu Zhang,Jiale Huang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoField 是一个确定性解码器，将逐点语义概率场转换为置信度排序的实例掩码，在 ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge 中取得第二名（AP 28.286% / AP50 41.383% / AP25 47.558%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能分割任务需在室内点云中定位旋钮、按钮、插座等小型交互部件并输出带置信度排序的实例掩码；但逐点语义预测器仅产生连续概率场，而基准评测的是离散且带分数的实例集合，二者之间存在显著的“场到实例”鸿沟。此外，实验显示候选构造与排序是互补的两个瓶颈（67.2% 实例存在 IoU-0.5 有效候选，但 top-1 覆盖率仅 20.0%），因此需同时关注两方面。

Method: 基于 VOLT 构建确定性解码器，分三阶段：(1) 增强阶段——将 13 次测试时前向传播转为语义共识场，保留每片段的处理后点身份，在公共点数组中累加概率并按覆盖次数归一化后融合缓存；(2) 解码阶段——分离高置信核心区与更广的支撑区，构建对称的半径剪枝点图，采用多源图测地划分拆分含多个种子的支撑区域；(3) 按类别感知方式对候选排序。

Result: 在 30 个验证场景的 561 个真值实例上，67.2% 的实例存在 IoU-0.5 有效候选，top-1 覆盖率为 20.0%；最终取得 AP 28.286%、AP50 41.383%、AP25 47.558%，位列 ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge 第二名。

Conclusion: 通过共识场聚合与图测地实例解码的确定性流程，可有效弥合概率场与离散实例评测之间的鸿沟；候选构造与候选排序仍是后续提升 AP 的关键方向。

Abstract: Functional segmentation requires locating small interactive parts, such as knobs, buttons, and sockets, in indoor point clouds and returning confidence-ranked instance masks. A point-wise semantic predictor produces a continuous probability field, whereas the benchmark evaluates a discrete set of scored instances. This creates a substantial field-to-instance gap. We present TopoField, a deterministic decoder built on top of VOLT. TopoField first converts 13 test-time forward passes into a semantic consensus field, then decodes instance candidates from the consensus geometry, and finally ranks the candidates in a class-aware manner. The augmentation stage retains the processed-point identity of every fragment prediction, accumulates probabilities in a common point array, normalizes by coverage count, and fuses the resulting caches. The decoding stage separates high-confidence cores from broader support regions, constructs a symmetric radius-pruned point graph, and uses a multi-source graph-geodesic partition to split support regions that contain multiple seeds. On 561 ground-truth instances across 30 validation scenes, an IoU-0.5-valid candidate exists for 67.2% of instances, and top-1 coverage is 20.0%. These complementary measurements motivate joint attention to candidate construction and ranking. TopoField achieves 28.286% AP, 41.383% AP50, and 47.558% AP25. TopoField achieved second place on the ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge.

</details>


### [92] [INTR++: INterpretable TRansformer with better Accuracy, Interpretability, and Adaptability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37178)
*Dipanjyoti Paul,Arpita Chowdhury,Maneet Chatterjee,Daichi Kitaguchi,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Tetsuya Sakurai,Wei-Lun Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 INTR++，通过轻量级 Prompt Adapter Module (PAM) 使 Prompt-CAM 的固定提示变为可自适应，在保持端到端训练与少量额外参数的前提下，实现跨域一致的ViT可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释 Transformer（如 INTR、Prompt-CAM）在特定领域表现良好，但跨域泛化差。Prompt-CAM 依赖的可学习提示本质固定，难以适应输入图像特征差异大的领域（医学、显微、遥感等），导致可解释性下降。

Method: 在 Prompt-CAM 基础上引入轻量级 Prompt Adapter Module (PAM)，用领域特定知识丰富并调节提示，使提示具备自适应性；保留可解释 Transformer 的标准架构与端到端学习流程，仅增加少量参数。

Result: 在动物、植物、医学影像、显微影像、遥感影像及以对象为中心的数据上，INTR++ 在各评估维度上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过提示适配即可显著提升可解释 Transformer 的跨域一致性与适用广度，且代价很小，是迈向 ViT 级别通用性的可行路径。

Abstract: Interpretable Transformers often perform well in domain specific settings but lose effectiveness beyond that. Achieving ViT-like broad applicability requires models with consistent performance across domains. Recent works on Interpretable Transformers, INTR and Prompt-CAM, were developed keeping domain adaptability in mind. Prompt-CAM leverages learnable prompts to promote adaptability. However, these prompts are fixed in nature and therefore struggle to adapt to domains with substantial variation in input image characteristics, resulting in reduced interpretability. To address this limitation, we propose INTR++, which extends Prompt-CAM by incorporating a lightweight Prompt Adapter Module (PAM). PAM enables prompts to be adaptive by enriching them with domain-specific knowledge, thereby ensuring consistent interpretability across diverse domains including those with varying image characteristics such as medical imaging, micro-scopic imaging, and remote sensing imagery. INTR++ retains the standard architecture and end-to-end learning of Interpretable Transformer based models, while adding only a small number of additional parameters compared to Prompt-CAM. We demonstrate that INTR++ delivers consistent improvements in all aspects across domains like animals, plants, medical imaging, microscopic imaging, remote sensing imagery and object-centric data.

</details>


### [93] [MixSIS: Mixed-supervised instance segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37138)
*Christian Wilms,Emre Gülsoylu,Sven Schreiber,Simone Frintrop*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合监督实例分割任务MixSIS，允许同一数据集中灵活混合图像级、点、框、掩码标注，并提出AnnsMixer基线从各级别标注生成伪掩码进行训练，性能大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督实例分割（WSIS）方法通常只针对单一标注级别，忽略了不同实例的标注难度差异：简单目标用点标注即可，复杂目标则需要框甚至掩码标注。因此需要一个能在一数据集中灵活混合多种标注级别的方法，以在降低标注成本的同时保证分割质量。

Method: 提出MixSIS任务并设计AnnsMixer基线。AnnsMixer是真正的混合监督系统，可从图像级、点、框、掩码等所有标注级别生成伪掩码标注，且支持单张图像内部标注级别的混合，据此训练实例分割模型。

Result: 在WSIS与MixSIS两项任务上均大幅超越当前最优方法；三项案例研究表明，借助简单标注规则或异构数据，MixSIS与AnnsMixer可将标注时间减少约90%，或将实例分割效果提升约34%。

Conclusion: MixSIS具有高度实用价值，可通过按实例难度分配不同标注级别来优化标注流程；AnnsMixer作为有效基线验证了混合监督实例分割的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Weakly-supervised instance segmentation (WSIS) based on either box-, point-, or image-level annotations has been proposed to overcome the labor-intensive annotation process in fully-supervised instance segmentation. Despite decent results, most WSIS methods only focus on a single annotation level, ignoring that some objects are easy to spot from point annotations, while others require box or even mask annotations. Therefore, we propose the Mixed-Supervised Instance Segmentation (MixSIS) task, which allows a flexible mix of annotation levels (image, point, box, and mask) within a dataset. MixSIS has a high practical relevance, as it allows optimizing the annotation process by annotating challenging objects in more detail, while saving time on simpler instances with less detailed annotations. To address MixSIS, we propose AnnotationsMixer (AnnsMixer) as a baseline. AnnsMixer is a truly mixed-supervised instance segmentation system, which generates pseudo mask annotations from all annotation levels, even mixed within a single image. We evaluate AnnsMixer on both WSIS and MixSIS tasks, largely outperforming state-of-the-art methods. In three case studies, we also demonstrate how the unique features of MixSIS and AnnsMixer can substantially reduce the annotation time (-90%) or improve the results (+34%) in instance segmentation with a simple annotation rule or leveraging heterogeneous data. Our code is available at https://github.com/chwilms/MixSIS

</details>


### [94] [Multibranch Ensemble Head in Vision Foundation Models for Efficient Cucumber Phenological Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37118)
*Kostiantyn Khabarlak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种正则化多分支集成头（低/高容量分支、训练时随机采样单分支、推理时概率空间平均），用于将DINOv2等视觉基础模型适配到小样本域偏移数据集，在黄瓜物候期分类上以12.2 GFLOPs、22.3M参数达到Macro F1 0.978。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督预训练的视觉基础模型（如DINOv2）特征迁移性强，但在小规模、域偏移数据上微调仍具挑战；同时精准农业中黄瓜物候期分类面临果实/花朵被枝叶遮挡、阶段转换主要体现为叶片形态细微变化等困难，且需满足边缘设备部署的算力约束。

Method: 设计正则化多分支集成头：并置低容量与高容量分支，每个训练步随机采样单一分支以引入正则化，推理时在概率空间对两分支输出取平均；该结构仅增加可忽略的参数量，且相对单一重头几乎无额外推理开销。在NALEF食品安全数据集（五个黄瓜生长阶段的真实场景图像）上，对DINOv2、DINOv3的ViT与ConvNeXt变体及强监督基线（ResNet、EfficientNet、ViT）进行广泛对比评估。

Result: DINOv2 ViT-S 配多分支头取得 Macro F1 0.978、Cohen's Kappa 0.971，性能与参数量和GFLOPs均大十余倍的模型相当，而自身仅需12.2 GFLOPs与22.3M参数，可部署于智能手机和边缘设备。

Conclusion: 多分支集成头以极低参数与推理开销改善了数据稀缺条件下VFM适配的精度—效率权衡；轻量DINOv2 ViT-S方案在黄瓜物候期分类这一实用场景中兼顾高精度与边缘可部署性。

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) pretrained with self-supervised learning, such as DINOv2, provide highly transferable features, yet adapting them to small, domain-shifted datasets is still challenging. We propose a regularizing multibranch ensemble head: a training and inference scheme that pairs low- and high-capacity branches, samples a single branch stochastically at each training step, and averages both branches in probability space at inference. The design adds negligible parameters and essentially no inference overhead over a single heavy head, while improving the accuracy-efficiency trade-off under data-scarce conditions. Accurate classification of plant growth stages is critical for precision agriculture, enabling timely decisions on fertilization, irrigation, and harvesting. Therefore, as a challenging practical case we use cucumber phenological stage classification, in which flowers and fruits are frequently occluded by foliage, and stage transitions manifest primarily as subtle changes in leaf morphology. The NALEF Food Safety dataset contains images of five cucumber growth stages captured under diverse real-world conditions. On it we evaluate a broad range of backbones, including DINOv2 and DINOv3 ViT and ConvNeXt variants, against strongly supervised counterparts (ResNet, EfficientNet, ViT). The proposed DINOv2 ViT-S with multibranch head achieves a Macro F1 of 0.978 and Cohen's Kappa of 0.971, matching the performance of models more than ten times larger in parameters and GFlops, while requiring only 12.2 GFlops and 22.3M parameters, making it deployable on smartphones and edge devices for phenological stage classification.

</details>


### [95] [TriDifNet: A Unified Three-Task Diffusion Network for End-to-End RGB-T Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37097)
*Qianchao He,Jing Liu,Yunhao Li,Xiaoqing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向未对齐RGB-T行人感知的三任务扩散网络TriDifNet，联合优化配准、融合与检测，并引入闭环优化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外与可见光图像因平台运动、视角变化及模态相关畸变常存在错位，配准误差会传播至融合与检测阶段，损害下游性能；现有方法难以端到端协同处理这三个耦合任务。

Method: TriDifNet由三个扩散模块构成：配准扩散模块(RegDM)估计稠密变形场，将未对齐红外图像对齐到光参考；融合扩散模块(FusDM)以残差条件扩散生成面向检测的融合表示，保留光学结构线索与红外显著性互补信息；检测扩散模块(DetDM)将行人检测建模为边界框空间的迭代去噪过程。通过闭环优化机制耦合三任务：检测语义引导融合与配准，融合结果反过来正则化几何对齐。训练上采用分阶段渐进策略，并在后期引入轻量级Nash-MTL多任务精调以平衡三项优化。

Result: 在KAIST多光谱行人基准上，未对齐RGB-T设置下取得优于强系统级对比方法的下游检测性能：Recall 65.44%、mAP50 57.55%、mAP50:95 26.83%。

Conclusion: 扩散框架可作为统一建模配准、融合与检测的有效范式，闭环任务耦合与渐进训练策略能显著提升错位条件下的RGB-T感知鲁棒性。

Abstract: Infrared--visible (IR--VIS) multi-modal perception supports robust scene understanding under challenging illumination. Infrared and visible images are often misaligned due to platform motion, viewpoint changes, and modality-dependent distortions, causing registration errors to propagate into subsequent fusion and detection stages. We propose TriDifNet, a unified three-task diffusion network for end-to-end RGB-T perception. To the best of our knowledge, TriDifNet is the first diffusion-based network that jointly optimizes registration, fusion, and detection for misaligned RGB-T pedestrian perception. TriDifNet consists of three diffusion-based modules, i.e., the Registration Diffusion Module (RegDM), the Fusion Diffusion Module (FusDM), and the Detection Diffusion Module (DetDM). RegDM aligns the unaligned infrared image to the optical reference by estimating a dense deformation field. FusDM performs residual conditional diffusion to generate a detection-oriented fused representation with optical structural cues and complementary infrared saliency. DetDM formulates pedestrian detection as an iterative denoising process in bounding-box space. To couple the three tasks, TriDifNet introduces a closed-loop optimization mechanism, where detection semantics guide fusion and registration, and the fusion result further regularizes geometric alignment. A stage-wise progressive training strategy and a lightweight Nash multi-task learning (Nash-MTL)-based late-stage refinement are further adopted to balance registration, fusion, and detection optimization. Extensive experiments on the KAIST multispectral pedestrian benchmark show that TriDifNet achieves superior downstream detection performance under misaligned RGB-T settings. Against strong system-level competitors, TriDifNet achieves 65.44\% Recall, 57.55\% mAP$_{50}$, and 26.83\% mAP$_{50:95}$.

</details>


### [96] [GraftRay: Physically Based Mesh Insertion into Gaussian Splatting Scenes via Path Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37036)
*Jinxin Ai,Matthias Niessner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraftRay 提出一种将网格物体物理一致地插入 2DGS/3DGS 室内场景的统一方法，通过扩散先验恢复材质与法线、显式建模空间扩展光源并闭式求解其辐射，再用混合渲染器联合路径追踪高斯场景与插入网格，实现视角一致的着色、互反射与软阴影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 2DGS/3DGS 重建场景中插入虚拟物体需物体被场景真实照亮并向场景投射物理合理阴影。现有方法要么在图像空间做外观协调而牺牲多视角一致性，要么仅在插入点用环境贴图近似光照，无法表征室内主导的近场面光源（窗户、灯具）。

Method: 利用扩散先验恢复场景材质与表面法线；显式建模空间扩展发光体并以闭式解求其辐射；构建混合渲染器，对场景高斯与插入网格联合路径追踪。

Result: 在 FIPT 数据集上取得新的 SOTA，渲染保真度与阴影精度优于 ComGS、EAG-PT 等基线，同时保持极具竞争力的渲染效率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明显式建模近场面光源并联合路径追踪高斯场景与网格，是实现视角一致、物理一致物体插入的有效途径，且适用于 2DGS 与 3DGS 两类表示。

Abstract: Inserting virtual objects into scenes reconstructed via 2D or 3D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS/3DGS) is a key capability for content creation, AR, and synthetic data generation. However, realistic insertion requires the object to be seamlessly lit by the scene and, conversely, to cast physically plausible shadows onto it. Existing approaches either harmonize appearance in image space, sacrificing multi-view consistency, or approximate scene illumination with an environment map at the insertion point, which cannot represent near-field area lights such as windows and lamps that dominate indoor lighting. To address this, we present GraftRay, a unified method for physically consistent insertion of mesh objects into indoor scenes represented by either 2D or 3D Gaussians. We recover scene materials and surface normals with diffusion-based priors, and explicitly model spatially-extended emitters, solving their radiance in closed form. A hybrid renderer then jointly path-traces the scene Gaussians and inserted meshes, producing view-consistent shading, interreflections, and soft shadows. On FIPT, GraftRay sets a new state of the art, outperforming recent baselines such as ComGS and EAG-PT in rendering fidelity and shadow accuracy, while maintaining highly competitive rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [97] [Explicit Regional Representations For Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36978)
*Fuyi Yang,Lei Yang,Andrew Liu,Chenyu Wei,Xiaoyi Guo,Ming Kai Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出像素级区域表示QRH（质量-相关性热图），以VLM引导融合局部退化证据与感知先验，无需像素级标注；通过两种确定性片段采样策略接入现有VQA模型，分布保持型可无需重训练近似原采样器，质量感知型配合微调在多数基准上提升指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VQA模型的预测缺乏可解释的区域证据，其区域依据基本是隐式的，缺少显式的像素级区域表示作为下游接口。

Method: 构建Quality-Relevance Heatmap：在视觉-语言模型引导下融合局部退化证据与感知先验，无需像素级标注；将其应用于现有VQA模型，采用两种确定性片段采样——分布保持采样（不重训练即可逼近原采样器的QRH分数分布）与质量感知采样（优先高QRH区域并配合微调）。

Result: 以DOVER在多个基准上的实验表明：分布保持采样与随机采样平均后的预测高度接近；质量感知采样在主流VQA基准上提升多数评估指标。

Conclusion: 显式区域表示可作为下游VQA的有效接口，为端到端模型补充可解释、可利用的区域证据。

Abstract: End-to-end video quality assessment (VQA) methods leave the regional evidence underlying their predictions largely implicit. We introduce an explicit pixel-level regional representation for VQA, instantiated as a Quality-Relevance Heatmap (QRH) that combines localized degradation evidence with perceptual priors under vision-language model guidance, without requiring pixel-level annotations. We apply QRH to existing VQA models through two deterministic fragment-sampling strategies: distribution-preserving sampling approximates the original sampler's QRH-score distribution without retraining, while quality-aware sampling prioritizes regions with high QRH scores and is paired with fine-tuning. Experiments with DOVER on multiple benchmarks show that the former closely approximates predictions averaged over random samples, while the latter improves most evaluated metrics across popular VQA benchmarks. These results support explicit regional representations as a useful interface for downstream VQA.

</details>


### [98] [Any3R: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Any Camera Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36150)
*Jingong Chen,Qingwen Zhang,Youming Deng,Chulwoo Pack,Olaf Wysocki,Boris Jutzi,Kwanghee Won,Kyle Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Any3R 将位置编码与训练损失都条件化于稠密光线场，使前馈几何基础模型可处理任意中心相机，无需按镜头类型重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型从图像集前馈恢复相机位姿与稠密几何，但隐含针孔假设；该假设深入骨干网络：位置编码和损失都将图像位置绑定到空间方向，二者均依赖镜头，导致非针孔（鱼眼、全景等）图像失效。

Method: 以稠密光线场（每像素单位方向）为条件描述任意中心相机，不依赖镜头类型、畸变系数或视场限制。角度坐标使旋转编码使用每视角测得的 patch 光线角度；立体角加权按像素在球面覆盖面积缩放点损失，从而编码与监督均与镜头无关。

Result: 在三个数据集、十三种中心相机的基准上，各方法均以自身预测位姿重建，Any3R 一致性精度最高；可稳健处理单场景内混合多种相机类型，且无需按镜头重新训练。

Conclusion: Any3R 是接受任意中心相机的前馈模型，支撑异构众包与档案图像集合的可扩展三维重建。

Abstract: Geometry foundation models recover camera poses and dense geometry from a set of images with a feed-forward network, but they expect pinhole images. That assumption is inside the backbone too: the positional encoding and the training loss both bind an image position to a direction in space, and both depend on the lens. We condition both on the dense ray field, the unit direction through every pixel, which describes any central camera without a lens type, distortion coefficients or a bound on field of view. Angular coordinates give the rotary encoding the measured ray angle of each patch, evaluated per view, and solid-angle weighting scales the point loss by the area each pixel covers on the sphere, so neither the encoding nor the supervision depends on the lens. Any3R is therefore a feed-forward model that takes any central camera, and is robust to mixing many camera types per scene, with no retraining per lens. On a benchmark of thirteen central cameras across three datasets, where every method reconstructs with its own predicted camera poses, it is the most consistently accurate method. Any3R enables scalable 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous crowdsourced and archival imagery collections sourced across diverse camera types. Project page: https://jingong-chen.github.io/Any3R/

</details>


### [99] [Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583)
*Sadia Kamal,Mahmudul Hasan,Tony Braskich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本足球事件检测框架，联合视觉故事板与解说文本，无需任务特定训练即可检测、分类并定位事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球事件检测方法依赖大规模标注数据和足球领域专用训练，泛化性差、成本高；本文旨在无需任务特定参数训练的前提下实现整场比赛事件的检测、分类与时间定位。

Method: 将整场比赛切分为重叠片段，每个片段用紧凑的视觉故事板（storyboard）与对齐的解说转录文本共同表示，交由通用多模态模型联合处理完成零样本检测、分类与定位；并系统分析模型族、输入表示与提示优化的影响；另引入基于证据的兴奋度评分阶段，用自然语言解释对事件排序以支持集锦选取。

Result: 在3场人工标注、共130个事件的比赛上，最佳配置达72.0% F1@5s；跨数据集设定下优于经SoccerNet训练的CALF与NetVLAD++；故事板表示相较逐帧输入将推理成本降低72%。

Conclusion: 零样本多模态框架可在不进行任务特定训练的情况下有效完成足球事件检测与定位，兼顾性能与效率，并通过可解释的兴奋度评分辅助集锦生成。

Abstract: Automatic soccer event spotting supports highlight generation, video indexing, and sports analytics, but existing methods depend on large annotated datasets and soccer-specific training. We present a zero-shot framework that detects, classifies, and temporally localizes events in full-length soccer broadcasts without task-specific parameter training. Each match is segmented into overlapping clips represented by a compact visual storyboard and the aligned commentary transcript, which a general-purpose multimodal model processes jointly. On three manually annotated matches containing 130 events, the best configuration achieves 72.0% F1@5s and outperforms SoccerNet-trained CALF and NetVLAD++ in a cross-dataset setting, while the storyboard representation reduces inference cost by 72% compared with individual frames. We further analyze the effects of model family, input representation, and prompt optimization, and introduce an evidence-grounded excitement-scoring stage that ranks detected events with natural-language explanations for highlight selection.

</details>


### [100] [Segmentation Confidence for Arbitrary CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37431)
*Baris Oguz,Xing Yao,Claudia Tawil,Alison Pouch,Brett Byram,Gabriel Arenas,Nadav Schwartz,Ipek Oguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可即插即用的体素级不确定性估计方法及由不确定性图推导单一置信度分数的新方法，用于预测CNN医学图像分割在未见数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN广泛用于医学图像分割但缺乏内在置信度度量；现有不确定性估计方法需定制网络结构或专门训练流程、须从头训练，且由不确定性图汇总为单一置信度分数并不直观。

Method: 探索可附加于已有分割流程、无需从头训练的体素级不确定性估计方法；并提出从不确定性图估计单一置信度分数的新方法。

Result: 在不同ROI与模态上的评估表明，所提置信度分数能够预测模型在未见数据上的分割性能。

Conclusion: 体素级不确定性估计可低成本集成到现有分割管线中，且由其导出的单一置信度分数可作为分割质量的可靠预测指标。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used for medical image segmentation. However, they lack an inherent measure of confidence. Existing methods for estimating prediction uncertainty often require a custom network architecture and/or dedicated training setups and need to be trained from scratch. In addition, the deduction of a single confidence score per input from the uncertainty maps is not always straightforward. In this study, we explore voxel-based uncertainty estimation methods that can be added to existing segmentation pipelines without training from scratch. We also propose a novel method to estimate a single confidence score from the uncertainty maps. Our evaluations on different ROIs and modalities suggest that our confidence score can predict segmentation performance in unseen data.

</details>


### [101] [Haste Makes Waste? Towards Safe Few-Step Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36665)
*Lior Ziv,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 少步蒸馏文生图模型存在“速度—安全”权衡：步数越少，不安全生成率越高，且现有概念擦除方法效果显著变差。作者提出 DICE，将擦除方法的潜变量构造与生成保持损失同蒸馏方式对齐，在 FLUX Schnell 上将不安全生成率降至 0.046（对比基线≥0.161），同时基本保持良性生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏模型因生成快而被广泛采用，但加速对其不安全生成率的影响尚不清楚。作者揭示：少步模型往往比其教师模型更不安全，减少推理步数会持续抬高不安全生成率；加速还使不安全概念更难被擦除，现有概念擦除方法在少步模型上明显失效。因此需要针对少步模型的专门擦除方案。

Method: 1) 实证刻画速度—安全张力及擦除方法失效现象；2) 给出机理诊断：少步模型生成过程的脆弱性，以及生成引导（guidance）被蒸馏进模型的方式，共同阻碍了既有擦除方法；3) 提出 DICE（Distillation-Integrated Concept Erasure），一种蒸馏后擦除方法，使潜变量构造与生成保持损失均与产生该模型的蒸馏方法对齐，从而让擦除与少步模型保持一致。

Result: 在 FLUX Schnell 上，DICE 将不安全生成率降至 0.046，而所测先前擦除方法至少为 0.161；同时基本保持良性生成质量。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏模型存在速度与安全的内在冲突，通用概念擦除方法难以迁移。与蒸馏机制对齐的擦除设计（DICE）可在几乎不损良性质量的前提下大幅降低不安全生成率，为少步模型的安全部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While few-step distilled text-to-image models have gained traction due to their fast generation times, the speed-up implications on their unsafe generation rates remain unknown. We uncover an inherent tension between speed and safety: few-step models are often more unsafe than their teachers, and reducing the number of inference steps consistently raises unsafe generation rates. We further find that acceleration makes unsafe concepts harder to erase in few-step models, with existing concept-erasure methods markedly less effective than in teachers. We offer a mechanistic diagnosis for why erasure methods fail on few-step models, finding that the fragility of few-step models' generation process and the way generation guidance is usually distilled into those models may hinder established concept-erasure methods. To address the speed-safety trade-off, we propose DICE (Distillation-Integrated Concept Erasure), a post-distillation erasure method tailored for few-step models. DICE aligns both its latent construction and its generation-preservation loss with the distillation method that produced the model, keeping erasure faithful to few-step models. DICE demonstrates strong results in our evaluation. For example, on FLUX Schnell, DICE reduces unsafe-generation rate to 0.046 (vs. at least 0.161 for prior erasure methods we test), while mostly preserving benign generation quality.

</details>


### [102] [PREEN: Exemplar-Based Color Style Transfer with Smartphone Editor Presets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36896)
*Beomjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PREEN将色彩风格迁移建模为回归确定性图像编辑器29个参数的问题，借助教师模型反演伪预设进行监督，在PST50上以紧凑、可解释的预设实现高质量颜色迁移与单一强度调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有色彩风格迁移多输出栅格图像，结果不可解释、不可编辑；而直接预测编辑器预设又缺乏真实参考图对应的预设真值（supervision缺失）。

Method: 将风格迁移表述为对确定性编辑器29维滑杆参数的回归；用风格迁移教师模型的输出反演到编辑器参数空间得到伪预设作为监督；以CLIP为条件预训练回归器，再用LoRA在图像级CIELAB损失下微调；低维参数空间自然提供单一推理期强度旋钮。

Result: 在人工标注的PST50基准上，在像素级与感知保真度指标上领先基线，同时保持结构保真；预设紧凑，设备端亚秒级推理；单次前向即可沿默认到鲜艳的连续轨迹调节，所有中间状态均为有效预设；双轴人类研究表明评分者偏好鲜艳端以匹配参考、偏好默认端作为独立照片。

Conclusion: 以预设回归替代栅格生成是一种可行且实用的色彩风格迁移范式，兼具可解释性、可编辑性与部署效率，并通过低维子空间实现无需重训练的强度控制。

Abstract: We present PREEN (Preset Regression via an Exemplar-based Editor Network), which casts color style transfer as regression onto the 29 parameters of a deterministic image editor. Given a content image and a style reference, PREEN predicts a preset, a vector of editor slider values that is interpretable and editor-consumable, not a raster image. The key obstacle is supervision, since an arbitrary real reference lacks a preset ground truth. We therefore supervise with a style-transfer teacher, whose target looks we invert into the editor's parameter space as pseudo-presets, then pretrain a CLIP-conditioned regressor and refine it with LoRA under an image-level CIELAB loss. On the human-curated PST50 benchmark, PREEN applies the reference's color while preserving structural fidelity, leading the baselines on pixel- and perceptual-fidelity metrics. The preset is compact and predicted at sub-second on-device latency. The low-dimensional subspace also exposes a single inference-time strength knob that traces a continuous default-to-vivid trajectory in one forward pass, with every intermediate state a valid editor preset. A two-axis human study shows that raters prefer the vivid end for matching the reference but the default as a standalone photo, a trade-off the knob spans without retraining.

</details>


### [103] [HoloRecon: Incremental Reconstruction of Long-Range Panoramas with Global Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36876)
*Zhuoxiao Li,Xinyi Liu,Hongye Hou,Jing OU,Kang Du,Shuai Zhang,Dongli Wu,Taoyu Wu,Siyuan Feng,Xiaohui Zhu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出耦合ORB稀疏全景SLAM前端与微调前馈模型的框架，用预测局部几何对远距离回访的特征匹配做3D验证以获得长程约束，并发布PanoRecon基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列全景重建需兼顾细致场景几何与跨远距离回访的全局一致位姿。全景SLAM与增量SfM依赖特征对应，但重复相似结构、部分重叠小导致长程可靠连接难以建立，漂移无法消除；前馈模型局部几何精细但算力受限、推理窗口小，需额外全局约束。

Method: ORB稀疏全景建图前端 + 微调前馈模型；用预测局部几何对跨回访特征匹配进行3D验证，得到长程相机约束；用稀疏路标校准预测深度；以验证连接优化全局位姿图，并用校正位姿更新稠密几何。另构建PanoRecon基准（七场景，含重复建筑、大小回环、参考位姿与LiDAR几何）。

Result: 在所评估方法中取得最低平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）与较高F-1 Score。

Conclusion: 连续特征跟踪与稀疏建图结合前馈模型的快速高质量局部重建，可同时实现准确定位与全局一致稠密建图；预测几何用于匹配验证是建立长程约束的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-sequence panoramic reconstruction requires detailed scene geometry and globally consistent camera poses across distant revisits. Panoramic SLAM and incremental SfM rely on feature correspondences, but repeated, similar structures and small partial overlap make reliable long-range connections difficult to establish, leaving accumulated drift unresolved. Feed-forward models offer detailed local geometry, but their high computational costs limit inference window size, necessitating additional constraints for global consistency. This motivates combining continuous feature-based tracking and sparse mapping with rapid, high-quality local reconstruction from feed-forward models to achieve accurate localization and globally consistent dense mapping over long sequences. We therefore propose a panoramic reconstruction framework that couples an ORB-based sparse panoramic mapping frontend with a fine-tuned feed-forward model. Our key insight is that predicted local geometry enables 3D verification of feature matches across distant revisits, yielding long-range camera constraints even between regions. We calibrate predicted depths with sparse landmarks and use verified connections to optimize the global pose graph, updating dense geometry with the corrected camera poses. We also introduce PanoRecon, a seven-scene benchmark with repetitive architecture, large and small loops, and reference camera poses and LiDAR geometry for evaluating global consistency. Our method achieves the lowest mean absolute trajectory error and high F-1 Score among the evaluated methods. Project Page:https://zhuoxiaoli.github.io/HoloRecon_page/

</details>


### [104] [CRLoMTL: Conflict-Resolution through Low-rank matrices for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36337)
*Ahmed Agiza,Tasneem Shaffee,Rahil Badkul,Sherief Reda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRLoMTL，在低秩参数更新空间（而非梯度空间）解决多任务学习中的负迁移问题，通过SVD提取任务适配器的共享正交子空间并融合为可折叠进骨干的单次更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习共享表示可提升效率，但任务更新相互干扰导致负迁移；现有冲突缓解方法多在梯度空间操作，需要显式的任务梯度操控与额外优化步骤，代价高且不改变推理结构之外的训练流程。

Method: 框架采用任务特定LoRA更新：每个任务在各自前向过程中独立更新自身的低秩矩阵；随后引入基于SVD的共享子空间解析器（SVD resolver），从各任务适配器中提取共同的低维正交基，将任务更新投影到该共享空间后再合并为单一的部署期更新，该更新可折叠进骨干网络，保持原模型推理结构、无额外运行时分支。

Result: 在PASCAL-Context、NYUD-v2、CelebA上以Swin Transformer为骨干进行实验，取得有利的精度-参数权衡：相较单任务基线分别最高提升2.77%、5.03%、3.50%，同时仅训练单任务模型所使用参数的一小部分。

Conclusion: 在低秩参数更新空间进行冲突消解是一种参数高效的多任务学习方案，无需显式梯度操作即可缓解任务干扰，且融合后的更新可无缝折叠进骨干，兼顾性能、参数效率与推理结构一致性。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) improves model efficiency by sharing representations across related vision tasks, but it often suffers from negative transfer when task updates interfere with one another. Existing conflict-mitigation methods typically operate in gradient space, requiring explicit task-gradient manipulation and additional optimization steps. We propose Conflict-Resolution through Low-Rank Matrices for Multi-Task Learning (CRLoMTL), a parameter-efficient framework that mitigates task interference in the low-rank parameter-update space. CRLoMTL learns task-specific LoRA updates through task-specific passes, where each task updates its own low-rank matrices, and integrates these updates through global shared-subspace fusion. Specifically, we introduce a shared-subspace resolver based on singular value decomposition (SVD), referred to as the SVD resolver, which extracts a common low-dimensional orthonormal basis across task adapters and projects task updates into this shared space before merging them into a single deployment-time update. The resulting update can be folded into the backbone, preserving the inference structure of the original model without additional runtime branches. Experiments with Swin Transformer backbones on PASCAL-Context, NYUD-v2, and CelebA show that CRLoMTL achieves a favorable accuracy-parameter trade-off, improving over single-task baselines by up to 2.77% on PASCAL-Context, 5.03% on NYUD-v2, and 3.50% on CelebA, while training only a small fraction of the parameters used by single-task models.

</details>


### [105] [Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Video Large Language Models: Methods, Trade-offs, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36327)
*Yuqi Li,Chuanyue Yan,Neil Rodrigues,Xianglong Wang,Xiaoqin Feng,Junming Liu,Xi Xiao,Zhongwei Cheng,Wei Li,Lin Chen,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一篇关于免训练视频大模型推理加速的综述，按推理流水线各干预阶段对方法进行分类，并分析干预位置与端到端加速之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大模型（VLMs）虽具备强视频理解与推理能力，但处理长视觉序列带来显著的计算、延迟与内存开销；本文聚焦免训练推理加速，目标是在保留任务相关时空证据的前提下降低推理成本。

Method: 以推理流水线中的干预阶段为组织维度，将现有方法划分为输入、视觉编码、投影器（projector）、预填充（prefill）与解码（decoding）五类，并将训练后量化（PTQ）作为跨阶段扩展单独讨论（其校准需求各异）。进一步分析干预位置对证据保留与计算节省的影响，以及局部效率提升为何未必转化为端到端加速。

Result: 结合已有研究的定量证据与作者在统一评测设置下开展的实验，评估了精度—延迟权衡。

Conclusion: 免训练推理加速需在证据保留与开销削减之间权衡；局部效率优化不等价于端到端加速，应从系统整体视角评估干预位置与加速收益。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video understanding and reasoning, but processing long visual sequences incurs substantial computational, latency, and memory overhead.
