This survey examines training-free inference acceleration, focusing on reducing inference costs while preserving task-relevant spatiotemporal evidence. We organize existing methods by their intervention stages in the inference pipeline, covering input, visual encoding, projector, prefill, and decoding. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is discussed separately as a cross-stage extension with varying calibration requirements. We analyze how intervention position affects evidence preservation and computational savings, and why local efficiency improvements may not translate into end-to-end acceleration. We complement this analysis with quantitative evidence from prior studies and our own experiments under a shared evaluation setting to assess accuracy-latency trade-offs.

</details>


### [106] [4D Reconstruction with Events & RGB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36610)
*Dulanga Weerakoon,Darshana Rathnayake,Van Minh Do,Hemanth Sabbella,Kaichen Zhou,Fangneng Zhan,Alok Prakash,Sanjay E. Sarma,Archan Misra,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该初步研究在纯RGB的4D重建模型Monst3R上仅额外增加一个事件相机输入分支，其余结构不变；在3个基准数据集上Abs Rel平均降低3.4%，且增益随相机与物体运动幅度增大而提升，在高物体运动条件下收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目运动相机重建动态3D场景（4D重建）对机器人与AR/VR至关重要，但现有前馈方法仅依赖RGB帧，继承了传统相机的失效模式，如运动模糊与快速/非均匀运动下的时间混叠。事件相机以微秒级异步记录像素亮度变化，对此类伪影基本免疫，但能否在不做整体架构重构的前提下融入现有RGB 4D重建管线仍是开放问题。

Method: 采用最小化扩展策略：以现有纯RGB的4D重建模型Monst3R为基线，新增一个事件相机输入分支，保持其余架构不变，实现轻量、非侵入式的RGB-事件融合。

Result: 在3个基准数据集上，相对RGB-only的Monst3R基线，Abs Rel平均降低3.4%；敏感性分析显示增益随相机与物体运动幅度增大而增大，在高物体运动条件下增益最大，而这正是RGB-only重建退化最严重的场景。

Conclusion: 即使轻量、非侵入式的事件数据融合也能显著提升4D重建的鲁棒性，说明事件模态可与现有RGB管线兼容；作者据此提出未来需开展更系统的架构层面研究。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes over time (4D reconstruction) from a single moving camera is central to robotics and AR/VR applications, yet state-of the-art feed-forward methods rely solely on RGB frames and therefore inherit the failure modes of conventional cameras, including motion blur and temporal aliasing under fast or non-uniform camera and object motion. Event cameras report per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously at microsecond resolution, offering a complementary signal that is largely immune to these artifacts, but whether this modality can be incorporated into existing RGB based 4D reconstruction pipelines without a full architectural redesign remains an open question. In this preliminary study, we investigate a minimal extension: we augment Monst3R, an existing RGB-only 4D reconstruction model, with an additional event-camera input branch, leaving the rest of the architecture unchanged. Across 3 benchmark datasets, this simple RGB-event fusion reduces Abs Rel by 3.4% on average relative to the RGB-only Monst3R baseline, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the improvement grows with the magnitude of camera and object motion, with the largest gains observed under high object motion conditions where RGB-only reconstruction degrades most. These early results suggest that even a lightweight, non-invasive fusion of event data can meaningfully improve 4D reconstruction robustness, motivating a more thorough architectural investigation in future work.

</details>


### [107] [LabWorld: Learning Interactive 4D Worlds for Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36592)
*Suozhi Huang,Kelsey Fu,Binxu Li,Luna Lyu,Kaize Wu,Wenbo Lu,David Smerkous,Jiahan Li,Siqi Zhu,Zhizhou Sha,Jiacheng Guo,Zhixuan Liang,Shilong Liu,Le Cong,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LabWorld，一个由智能体驱动的Real2Sim框架，从单目RGB视频重建可交互的4D实验室世界，恢复可复用3D资产、度量场景、时变物体状态与流程事件，在移液与涡旋任务上优于通用与专用重建基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验室仿真器依赖人工构建资产和预定义流程，缺乏真实场景泛化能力；而实验室视频虽记录真实设备、布局与交互，却将信息隐含在像素中未被显式提取。因此需要一种能自主从真实视频构建可交互仿真世界的方法。

Method: LabWorld从单目RGB视频重建可复用3D资产、度量尺度场景、时变物体状态与流程事件。利用多帧观测提供资产与场景重建的互补证据；运动重建融合视觉与几何观测，并引入编码实验室交互时空要求的事件约束。

Result: 在自采移液与涡旋视频上对比通用智能体与专用重建基线：移液任务平均Mask IoU为0.501，超最强基线0.033；涡旋任务Tube-Length-Normalized PCK-AUC为0.638，超最强基线0.035；五段移液视频中事件约束重建使物理约束满足率从14.3%(5/35)提升至100%(35/35)。

Conclusion: LabWorld生成的世界以显式、可编辑状态暴露几何、运动、关节与事件，支持回放、检查及未来具身科学系统使用，展示了从真实视频构建可交互实验室仿真的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous scientific agents must not only reason about experiments but also interact with the physical environments in which they are executed. Existing laboratory simulators typically rely on manually constructed assets and predefined procedures, while laboratory videos capture real equipment, layouts, and interactions but leave this information implicit in pixels. We introduce LabWorld, an agent-driven Real2Sim framework for reconstructing interactive 4D laboratory worlds from monocular RGB video. LabWorld recovers reusable 3D assets, a metric scene, time-varying object states, and procedure events. Multi-frame observations provide complementary evidence for asset and scene reconstruction, while motion reconstruction combines visual and geometric observations with event constraints encoding the spatial and temporal requirements of laboratory interactions. We evaluate LabWorld on real pipetting and vortexing videos collected in our laboratory against general-purpose agentic and dedicated reconstruction baselines. For pipetting, LabWorld achieves a mean Mask IoU of 0.501, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.033. For vortexing, it achieves a mean Tube-Length-Normalized PCK-AUC of 0.638, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.035. On five pipetting videos, event-conditioned reconstruction increases the fraction of modeled physical constraints satisfied from 14.3% (5/35) to 100% (35/35). The resulting worlds expose geometry, motion, articulation, and events as explicit, editable state for replay, inspection, and future embodied scientific systems.

</details>


### [108] [Spatial-Frequency Learning for Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36487)
*Hesong Li,Ying Fu,Ziqi Wu,Ruiwen Shao,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出空域-频域融合学习框架用于含噪模糊STEM图像增强，并结合STEM噪声标定方法合成更真实的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始STEM图像受扫描噪声、点噪声与背景噪声等多种退化影响而噪声大、模糊；现有深度学习方法在严重退化下性能受限。

Method: 设计空间-频率融合网络：在频域利用原子周期性排列、在空域恢复局部原子细节，两域特征分别增强后融合；同时提出STEM噪声标定方法，通过统计分析与拟合真实图像估计扫描噪声、点噪声和背景噪声参数，构建覆盖更广原子排列、成像模式与退化的数据集，并用随机掩码组合不同原子排列以模拟多材料界面。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实先进材料图像上取得最先进增强性能，并提升原子定位精度。

Conclusion: 空域-频域联合学习配合噪声标定的数据合成，可有效提升严重退化下STEM图像的增强与原子定位精度。

Abstract: Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy (STEM) enables the observation of atomic arrangements at sub-angstrom resolution and plays a key role in the discovery and characterization of advanced materials. Raw STEM images are often noisy and blurred due to multiple degradations. Although recent deep learning methods improve STEM images, their performance remains limited under severe degradations. In this work, we propose a spatial-frequency learning framework for STEM image enhancement. Specifically, we design a spatial-frequency fusion network that exploits periodic atomic arrangements in the frequency domain and restores local atomic details in the spatial domain. Spatial- and frequency-domain features are enhanced separately and then fused. We also present a STEM noise calibration method for synthesizing more realistic STEM images. The parameters of scan noise, pointwise noise, and background noise are estimated through statistical analysis and fitting of real STEM images. Using these calibrated parameters, we construct a diverse dataset containing a wider range of atomic arrangements, imaging modes, and degradations. Random masks combine different atomic arrangements to simulate interfaces between multiple materials. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art enhancement performance on synthetic datasets and real images of advanced materials while improving atomic localization accuracy.

</details>


### [109] [Confident First, Reflective Second: Reinforcement Learning of Confidence-Preserving Reflection for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36467)
*Zihao Lin,Jingyuan Liu,Yucheng Zhang,Yuguang Yao,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现基于强化学习（RL）的视频问答训练会"侵蚀"模型初始答案的可靠性（confidence erosion），提出"先自信、后反思"（confident first, reflective second）原则，并用 CoFire 方法实现，在多个视频基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型通过采样帧作答，容易错过关键瞬间，常用"先作答—再重看—确认或修正"的反思循环，且多以 RL 学习。作者发现 RL 会侵蚀初始答案：由于错误可在后续被修正，正确的初始答案所获奖励反而更低，导致初始答案质量停滞甚至下降，而最终答案的相对增益却在上升。因此需要保证初始答案自身可靠。

Method: 提出"confident first, reflective second"原则：初始答案须独立保持可靠，且不低于由参考策略设定的下限，反思只能在其基础上增益。方法 CoFire 针对三条侵蚀通道——奖励激励、参数漂移、梯度干扰——各设计一个机制来实现该原则。

Result: CoFire 在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 上平均准确率达 67.0%，比最强的对比 7B 方法高 1.7 分；相比结果奖励型 RL，初始答案与最终答案准确率平均分别提升 4.1 和 3.7 分。

Conclusion: 更强的初始答案能带来更强的最终答案；显式保护初始答案的可靠性优于纯结果奖励 RL 的反思训练范式。

Abstract: Video models answer from sampled frames and may miss the decisive moment. A common remedy is to commit to an answer, re-examine the video, and confirm or revise it, a loop now mainly learned with reinforcement learning (RL). We find that RL erodes the initial answer, a phenomenon we term confidence erosion, where confidence means the label-verified reliability of the initial answer instead of self-reported certainty: because errors can be repaired later, a correct initial answer earns less reward, and the initial answer stalls even as the final answer's gain over it rises. We propose the principle confident first, reflective second: the initial answer must stay reliable on its own, above a floor set by a reference policy, and reflection may only add to it. Our method, CoFire, implements this principle with one mechanism for each erosion channel: reward incentives, parameter drift, and gradient interference. CoFire reaches 67.0% mean accuracy on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench, 1.7 points above the strongest compared 7B method, and improves initial-answer and final accuracy over outcome-reward RL by 4.1 and 3.7 points on average: a stronger initial answer carries through to a stronger final one.

</details>


### [110] [Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Uni: Elastic Self-Refining Unified Diffusion LLM with Cross-Paradigm On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36466)
*Shufan Li,Hanrong Ye,Greg Heinrich,Yonggan Fu,Haotian Zhang,Sifei Liu,Aditya Grover,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NLD-Uni 是一个统一的离散扩散大语言模型（DLLM），通过跨范式的 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）框架和渐进式自精炼扩散过程，同时实现视觉理解与生成，并在多类任务上取得统一 DLLM 中的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一扩散模型难以同时获得自回归 VLM 的视觉理解能力与连续流模型的视觉生成能力；此外，常规掩码扩散直接将干净的图像 token 破坏为无信息的 mask token，训练信号过于粗糙，限制了生成质量与推理阶段的精炼空间。

Method: 1）统一的跨范式 on-policy 蒸馏框架，将自回归 VLM 的视觉理解能力和连续流模型的视觉生成能力迁移到单一 DLLM；2）渐进式自精炼扩散过程，训练时在多个步骤中逐步 corrupt 每个 token，而非一步替换为 mask token；推理时支持对 token 进行中间 commit，从而实现渐进式自精炼。

Result: 在视觉问答、多模态推理、目标 grounding、文生图生成与图像编辑等多样任务上，NLD-Uni 在统一 DLLM 中达到 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 跨范式蒸馏与渐进式自精炼扩散的结合，可使单一离散扩散模型同时具备强视觉理解与高质量视觉生成能力，验证了 DLLM 作为统一多模态骨干的可行性。

Abstract: We propose Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Uni (NLD-Uni), a unified discrete diffusion large language model (DLLM) for visual understanding and generation tasks. Compared with prior unified diffusion models, NLD-Uni introduces two major innovations. First, we propose a unified cross-paradigm on-policy distillation (OPD) framework that successfully transfers the visual understanding ability of autoregressive VLMs and visual generation ability of continuous flow models, to a unified DLLM. Second, NLD-Uni incorporates a novel progressive self-refining diffusion process that gradually corrupts each token across multiple steps during training, as opposed to adopting the conventional masked diffusion formulation that directly corrupts a clean image token to an uninformative mask token. At inference, this design allows NLD-Uni to make intermediate commit to a token, enabling progressive self-refinement for better visual quality. Through extensive experiments, we show that NLD-Uni achieves state-of-the-art performance among unified DLLMs on diverse tasks including visual question-answering, multi-modal reasoning, object grounding, text-to-image generation, and image editing.

</details>


### [111] [FORESEE-MS: Toward Prospective Spatial Forecasting of Future Multiple Sclerosis Lesions from Baseline FLAIR MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36428)
*Maryam Akhavan Aghdam,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出FORESEE-MS评估协议，检验基线FLAIR MRI能否在人群先验图谱之外识别未来病灶形成风险区域；在MSSEG-2数据集39例受试者5折交叉验证中，图谱的池化体素级AUROC/AUPRC最高，深度学习模型未可靠超越，但3D U-Net等在同患者内Recall@Top-K%上均值更优（多数配对置信区间含零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MS影像分析多聚焦于回顾性病灶检测与分割；本文探究基线FLAIR是否携带超越人群级空间模式的患者特异预测信息，并建立公平的评估基线。

Method: 引入FORESEE-MS协议：以折特异的人群先验图谱为基线，采用池化体素级判别（AUROC、AUPRC）与患者内病灶定位（Recall@Top-K%），并给出患者级bootstrap置信区间；在MSSEG-2的39例受试者上以5折交叉验证评估五种深度学习模型（3D U-Net、Attn+ResBlock+VAE、ViT及两种SwinUNETR变体）。

Result: 人群先验图谱取得最高的池化体素级AUROC与AUPRC，无任何深度学习模型在两指标上可靠超越；但在所有评估阈值下，3D U-Net与Attn+ResBlock+VAE的患者内Recall@Top-K%均值高于图谱，不过多数配对差异置信区间包含零。

Conclusion: 基线FLAIR可能包含患者特异的预测信息，但在该小样本队列中，其相对人群先验图谱能否提升病灶预测仍不确定；需更大队列验证。

Abstract: Traditional multiple sclerosis (MS) image analysis has primarily focused on retrospectively detecting and segmenting lesions. This study investigates whether baseline FLAIR MRI can identify regions at risk for future lesion formation beyond population-level spatial patterns. We introduce FORESEE-MS, an evaluation protocol that uses a fold-specific population-prior atlas as a baseline and assesses predictions using pooled voxel-level discrimination (AUROC and AUPRC) and within-patient lesion localization (Recall@Top-K%), with patient-level bootstrap confidence intervals. We evaluated five deep learning models (3D U-Net, Attn+ResBlock+VAE, ViT, and two SwinUNETR variants) using 5-fold cross-validation on 39 subjects from the MSSEG-2 dataset. The atlas achieved the highest pooled voxel-level AUROC and AUPRC, with no deep learning model reliably outperforming it on either metric. However, the 3D U-Net and Attn+ResBlock+VAE achieved higher mean voxel-level within-patient Recall@Top-K% than the atlas at all evaluated thresholds, although most paired confidence intervals for the differences included zero. These findings suggest that baseline FLAIR may contain patient-specific predictive information, but in this small cohort, it remains uncertain whether this information improves lesion forecasting beyond a population-prior atlas.

</details>


### [112] [SpeeDiff: Scalable Pixel-Anchored End-to-End Latent Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36419)
*Bingliang Zhang,Wenda Chu,Yizhuo Li,Linjie Yang,Yisong Yue,Katherine L. Bouman,Yang Song,Qiushan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpeeDiff，一种从零开始联合训练VAE与扩散模型的潜空间扩散方法，通过Tweedie像素重建损失缓解潜空间坍塌，实现大幅加速训练并取得优异FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段方法使用预训练且冻结的VAE，扩散损失梯度无法直接指导VAE编码器，难以形成有利于生成的潜空间；而朴素端到端联合训练又会因扩散损失无约束反传导致潜空间坍塌，性能严重退化。

Method: 提出Tweedie Pixel Reconstruction (TPR) 损失：利用Tweedie公式从中间噪声状态解码出预测的干净潜变量，从而提供额外的像素级反馈以缓解坍塌；并支持全Transformer架构的联合缩放，增强端到端框架内的表征对齐。

Result: SpeeDiff-XL相比Vanilla SiT和REPA训练速度分别快140倍以上和61倍；在ImageNet 256x256上无引导生成FID达1.50；使用32倍压缩VAE时，在ImageNet 512x512上无引导生成FID达1.53。

Conclusion: TPR损失使从零联合训练VAE与扩散模型成为可行且高效，兼顾训练加速与生成质量，并支持高效压缩VAE向高分辨率扩展。

Abstract: We present Scalable Pixel-anchored End-to-end Diffusion (SpeeDiff), a latent diffusion method that jointly trains the VAE and the diffusion model from scratch. In principle, joint training allows the diffusion loss gradient to directly guide the VAE encoder, encouraging the formation of a generation-friendly latent space and potentially yielding faster convergence than the conventional two-stage approach with a pretrained frozen VAE. However, a naive end-to-end implementation severely degrades performance, as unrestricted backpropagation of the diffusion loss leads to latent space collapse. Our main technical contribution is a simple yet effective Tweedie Pixel Reconstruction (TPR) loss, which provides additional pixel-level feedback by decoding a predicted clean latent from an intermediate noisy state using Tweedie's formula, thereby alleviating collapse. Furthermore, our method enables jointly scaling a fully transformer-based architecture and enhances representation alignment within the end-to-end framework. Our SpeeDiff-XL model achieves over 140x and 61x faster training compared to Vanilla SiT and REPA, respectively, while attaining an FID of 1.50 without guidance on ImageNet 256x256 generation. With a more efficient 32x compressed VAE, our model further reaches an FID of 1.53 without guidance on ImageNet 512x512 generation.

</details>


### [113] [A note on data-efficient finetuning of vision foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37510)
*Niclas Hergenröther,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对基础模型家族（Attention、Convolution、Mamba）与迁移范式（VP、LP、FFT、LoRA）之间的交互进行了简明的实证评估，并在VP语境下探索正则化技术以提升性能，为资源受限下的高效微调提供实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Visual Prompting（VP）已成为高效知识迁移的有前景技术，且随新基础模型家族（如Mamba）出现、VP流程（如AutoVP）趋于成熟，亟需对现有方法进行系统评估，以理解高效微调领域的进展、挑战与机遇。

Method: 构建基于先前工作的实证研究：扩大被评估模型与技术（VP、Linear Probing、Full Fine-tuning、LoRA）的选择范围并调优超参数；系统比较最新模型与早期架构及替代微调方法；并在VP场景下应用常见正则化技术。

Result: 提供了基础模型家族（Attention、Convolution、Mamba）与迁移范式之间交互的简明实证概览，并给出了正则化技术在VP语境下提升性能的探索结果。

Conclusion: 通过拓宽评估模型与技术范围并调优超参数，本文为资源受限下的高效微调提供了实用指南，揭示了该领域的进展、挑战与机会。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to a new task within or beyond the training domain is the de facto standard for building specialized machine learning models. Recently, Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as a promising technique for efficient knowledge transfer. As new foundation model families~(like Mamba) get introduced and VP pipelines such as AutoVP reach greater maturity, we find a growing need for a systematic evaluation of current approaches. In this work, we assess the performance of the latest models, comparing them to earlier architectures and alternative fine-tuning methods, to better understand the progress, challenges and opportunities in the field of efficient fine-tuning under resource limitations. Towards this goal, this paper provides a concise empirical overview of the interactions among foundation model families (Attention-, Convolution-, and Mamba-based) and transfer paradigms: VP, Linear Probing (LP), Full Fine-tuning (FFT) and Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Our work builds up on previous findings by broadening the selection of evaluated models and techniques while tuning hyperparameters. In the interest of delivering practical guidelines for the user, we also explore application of prevalent regularization techniques to boost performance in the context of VP.

</details>


### [114] [ObjectTwin: Measuring the Asset Correspondence Gap for Capture-Anchored Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35904)
*Zhenghe Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ObjectTwin测量协议：冻结实例、相机、扫描投影支撑域、深度、度量锚点与评价分母，仅替换物体表征，从而把"生成资产与实采观测的对应性"作为可独立测量的接口，并报告了显著的资产对应缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体级重建越来越多地把生成网格作为可编辑代理插入实采场景，但现有孤立资产指标与位姿估计都不衡量"在实采度量状态下代理能否复现同一实例的标定观测"，该需求缺位。

Method: 提出ObjectTwin——基于扫描的测量协议，固定实例、相机、扫描投影支撑域与记录深度、度量锚点、评价分母，只替换物体表示；以相对于扫描导出的可见几何的观测一致性损失定义"资产对应缺口"，不把扫描视为完整物理真值；覆盖两个室内RGB-D采集域与两个生成后端，并做覆盖度、重叠深度分析与保守位姿精化。

Result: 可见且锚定的生成资产在该替换下MaskIoU下降0.302–0.497；所有测试的场景级估计均为正；同输入下生成器偏好随采集域反转；覆盖与重叠深度分析显示分歧同时存在于可见支撑域与表面放置；所评估的保守位姿精化无法系统性恢复。

Conclusion: 捕获对应性应作为生成资产质量与更强物理/任务级评价之间可单独测量的接口。

Abstract: Object-wise reconstruction increasingly inserts generated meshes into captured scenes as editable proxies for observed objects. This use creates a requirement absent from isolated-asset scores and pose estimates: at the captured metric state, the proxy should reproduce calibrated observations of the same instance. We present ObjectTwin, a scan-grounded measurement protocol that freezes the instance, cameras, scan-projected support and recorded depth, metric anchor, and evaluation denominator, then replaces only the object representation. The resulting asset correspondence gap measures lost observation agreement relative to scan-derived visible geometry without treating the scan as complete physical ground truth. Across two indoor RGB-D capture domains and two generation backends, visible, anchored generated assets lose 0.302-0.497 MaskIoU under this replacement. Every tested scene-level estimate is positive, while same-input generator preference reverses across capture domains. Coverage and overlap-depth analyses locate disagreement in both visible support and surface placement; the evaluated conservative pose refinement does not systematically recover it. These results establish capture correspondence as a separately measurable interface between generated-asset quality and stronger physical or task-level evaluation.

</details>


### [115] [LingGS: Decoupling Feed-Forward Geometry Prediction from Camera Tracking for High-Fidelity Gaussian SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36853)
*Da Li,Yihan Zang,Ivan Viola,Zeyu Wang,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在线单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM系统，将冻结的流式3D基础模型作为虚拟RGB-D传感器提供深度，配合显式几何求解器做相机跟踪，在有限优化预算下兼顾重建质量与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM在有限优化预算下难以同时实现高质量重建与响应式相机跟踪，而已有的学习式相机预测在跟踪精度上存在不足。

Method: 以冻结的流式3D基础模型作为虚拟RGB-D传感器，从单目视频估计深度；再用显式几何求解器通过特征重投影估计相机运动；最后由流式高斯映射器将观测融合为稠密场景表示。

Result: 几何跟踪在代表性流式几何骨干上均提升位姿精度，体现方法普适性；室内外数据集上重建质量显著优于现有方法，单张RTX 4090上约7 FPS，吞吐量为对比SLAM系统的2–3倍。

Conclusion: 结合学习几何先验与显式几何相机跟踪，可有效提升单目高斯泼溅SLAM的跟踪精度与重建质量，并保持高实时吞吐。

Abstract: Achieving high-quality reconstruction and responsive camera tracking remains challenging for monocular RGB Gaussian splatting SLAM under a limited optimization budget. We present an online system that addresses this challenge by combining learned geometric priors with explicit geometric camera tracking, which can substantially outperform learned camera prediction in terms of tracking accuracy. A frozen streaming 3D foundation model serves as a virtual RGB-D sensor, providing depth estimation from monocular video. An explicit geometric solver then estimates camera motion through feature reprojection, and a streaming Gaussian mapper integrates the resulting observations into a dense scene representation. We further show that geometric tracking improves camera pose accuracy across representative streaming geometry backbones, demonstrating the broader applicability of our approach. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets show that our method significantly improves reconstruction quality over existing approaches, achieving about 7 FPS on a single RTX 4090 and delivering 2-3 times the throughput of the evaluated prior SLAM systems.

</details>


### [116] [Hierarchical Sparse Graph for Multi-dimensional Assignment in Event-based Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37321)
*Muxi Zha,Yang Shang,Wenlong Zhang,Qifeng Yu,Banglei Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个事件相机多批次多对多关联方法：用模糊逻辑量化轨迹相关性并构建分层稀疏图，将多维指派转化为图划分，无需训练即在eTram和Ev-UAV上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机跟踪采用在线方式，仅关联相邻批次，时间窗口有限，在长期遮挡或漏检等场景易失败；需引入更广时间上下文。同时事件检测器单批次可为同一目标生成多个空间上不同的正确轨迹，违反经典关联的一对一假设，形成欠定多对多匹配问题。

Method: 构建跨多批次的事件轨迹多维指派；用模糊逻辑对成对轨迹相关性建模，融合多个长程运动学度量，无需硬阈值；以轨迹为节点、模糊相关性为边构建分层稀疏图（HSG）；将指派表述为图划分任务，采用基于顶点亲和度自适应划分的启发式求解；全流程免训练。

Result: 在eTram与Ev-UAV两个挑战性事件数据集上全面实验，优于现有事件类及多数图像类关联模型；在多对多关联场景下各指标均达OC-SORT的2倍以上。

Conclusion: 首次形式化并求解事件相机多对多欠定关联这一NP难问题，模糊逻辑+分层稀疏图+图划分的免训练框架有效利用长程时序上下文，实现鲁棒高精度跟踪。

Abstract: Existing event-based tracking methods typically operate in an online manner, associating tracklets across adjacent batches. However, this limited temporal window often leads to failures in challenging scenarios, such as long-term occlusions or missed detections, where broader temporal context should be incorporated for high-precision tracking. To achieve robust and accurate tracking, this paper concentrates on the event-based multi-dimensional assignment that associates tracklets across multiple batches simultaneously. Unlike image-based detectors, event-based detectors can generate multiple correct yet spatially distinct tracklets for a single object per batch. This violates the one-to-one rule in classic data association, making event-based association an underdetermined many-to-many matching problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tackle this NP-hard problem. To address its underdetermined nature, we formulate the assignment problem via fuzzy logic, where pairwise tracklet correlations are quantified through multiple long-range kinematic metrics. This fuzzy design eliminates the need for hard tracking thresholds and enables multiple heterogeneous metrics to be integrated into a unified correlation. By treating tracklets as nodes and their fuzzy correlations as edges, we construct a Hierarchical Sparse Graph (HSG). Building upon the HSG, we pose assignment as a graph partition task, and employ a heuristic solution that measures vertex affinity for partitioning adaptively. The entire pipeline is training-free. Comprehensive experiments on two challenging event datasets, eTram and Ev-UAV, show that our method outperforms existing event-based and most image-based association models. Particularly, it achieves over 2$\times$ the performance of OC-SORT across all metrics in the many-to-many association scenario.

</details>


### [117] [MvT: Event-based Multi-view Projection for Multiple Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37309)
*Muxi Zha,Banglei Guan,Minzu Liang,Zibin Liu,Yang Shang,Qifeng Yu,Laurent Kneip*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视角流水线 MvT，在保持伪帧 2D 格式的同时引入互补时空视角，解决事件相机单视角下的轨迹歧义问题，且无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机用于多目标跟踪时面临异步数据格式问题。主流伪帧范式虽可直接复用图像技术，但当轨迹重叠或空间邻近时会出现跟踪失败（单视角歧义）。

Method: MvT 包含 Multi-view Projection (MvP) 与 Multi-view Fusion (MvF)。MvP 将事件编码为三个互补时空视角并缓解模式离散化；MvF 将多视角结果统一到 3D 坐标系，并通过带特定判据组合的优化模型进行 tracklet 关联。

Result: 在四个数据集（含自建 Small Objects Dataset, SOD）上，MvT 可无缝集成图像方法，在泛化场景中优于现有非学习与学习型跟踪器，并有效消除单视角歧义。

Conclusion: MvT 为 training-free，适用于真值标注不可行的场景，具有实际应用与数据高效潜力。

Abstract: Event cameras are increasingly used for Multiple Object Tracking (MOT), but their asynchronous event output often requires specialized methods. Existing processing methods primarily follow two paradigms, pseudo-frames and event-by-event. The former is the prevailing approach since its data format aligns with images, making image-based techniques applicable. However, it suffers from tracking failures when trajectories overlap or are spatially close on pseudo-frames. Facing this challenge, we propose a multi-view pipeline, Multi-view Tracking (MvT), which preserves the 2D data format to leverage image-based techniques directly while introducing additional spatio-temporal views to resolve tracking ambiguities in a single view. MvT comprises a Multi-view Projection (MvP) module and a Multi-view Fusion (MvF) stage. MvP encodes events into three complementary spatio-temporal views while mitigating the pattern discretization. Within MvF, multi-view results are unified into a 3D coordinate system, and tracklets are associated through an optimization model subject to specific criteria combination. Evaluations on four datasets, including our self-collected Small Objects Dataset (SOD), show that MvT seamlessly integrates image-based methods and outperforms existing non-learning and learning trackers in generalized scenarios, and effectively resolves the single-view tracking ambiguities. Being training-free, MvT is applicable when ground-truth annotation is infeasible, thereby highlighting its practical, data-efficient potential. Code is available at https://github.com/zhazhabiu/MvTracking.

</details>


### [118] [TRUCO: A Scalable Lock-Free Algorithm for Parallel Contour Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37297)
*Rafael Munoz-Salinas,Francisco J. Romero-Ramirez,Manuel J. Marin-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRUCO：一种无锁并行算法，通过起点所有权规则与推测式追踪，高效地将二值掩码转为几何轮廓，速度比工业标准快一个数量级以上且可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轮廓提取算法（如Suzuki-Abe）依赖顺序处理且需构建拓扑层级，存在额外开销；高分辨率成像趋势下，需要一种能利用多核CPU、又无需分块拼接或复杂同步的并行轮廓提取方法。

Method: 提出TRUCO（Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership）无锁并行算法：引入起点所有权（start-point ownership）规则，结合推测式追踪（speculative tracing）模型——线程向下追踪轮廓进入相邻区域，并采用向上终止逻辑，确保每条轮廓仅被处理一次。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据集上，于消费级与服务器级CPU上进行实验，TRUCO显著优于现有基线：相比工业标准实现获得超过一个数量级的加速，并在多种硬件架构的高分辨率场景下保持稳健的可扩展性。

Conclusion: TRUCO以无锁、无分块拼接的设计实现了高分辨率二值掩码的高效并行几何轮廓提取，兼顾高性能与可扩展性，适用于现代高分辨率分割后处理流水线。

Abstract: Transforming binary masks into geometric contours remains a critical step in many computer vision pipelines, particularly for post-processing high-resolution segmentation outputs. Yet, standard algorithms such as the Suzuki-Abe method are limited by their sequential nature and the overhead of building topological hierarchies. As modern applications transition to high-resolution imaging, there is a critical need for contour extraction that leverages multi-core CPU architectures without the complexity of tile stitching or heavy synchronization.
  This paper presents TRUCO (Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership), a lock-free parallel algorithm designed for high-performance geometric boundary extraction. TRUCO introduces a start-point ownership rule, combined with a speculative tracing model, in which threads trace contours downward into adjacent regions and use an upward-termination logic to ensure each contour is processed exactly once.
  Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets, conducted on both consumer and server-grade CPUs, demonstrate that TRUCO significantly outperforms existing baselines. The method achieves speedups of over an order of magnitude compared to the industry standard while maintaining robust scalability on high-resolution scenes across diverse hardware architectures.

</details>


### [119] [Vision-Language-Action or World-Action? Just Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37219)
*Yonghao Yu,Lang Huang,Runyi Li,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 解耦骨干初始化、未来观测目标与推理期机制三项选择，提出统一框架JAM：预训练视觉骨干+动作专家，仅用动作损失与"前瞻损失"训练，无需视觉生成，在LIBERO/LIBERO-Plus/RoboTwin上优于匹配的FastWAM与ImageWAM，并降低73%在线训练FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA与WAM社区争论控制策略应继承视觉语言模型还是视觉生成器的预训练知识，但该争论将三个可分离的选择混为一谈：骨干初始化、施加于未来观测的目标函数、以及未来损失相关机制是否保留到推理阶段。因此VLA与WAM之间的性能差距无法归因于其中任一单项，需要解耦分析。

Method: 提出JAM（Just Action Model）统一框架：预训练视觉骨干搭配动作专家，训练时同时使用动作损失与前瞻损失（foresight loss）。前瞻损失令当前观测特征预测记录未来的冻结编码，预测头在训练后丢弃，因而训练与推理均不涉及视觉生成，骨干可自由选择。实验分两步：先在固定策略架构内比较目标函数，再跨骨干族验证前瞻配方。

Result: JAM在LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus上优于匹配的FastWAM和ImageWAM，在RoboTwin上取得相近或略高的成功率。相比FastWAM，成功率由97.0%/55.3%/91.2%提升至98.2%/64.5%/91.4%。采用缓存前瞻目标时，每样本每更新的估计在线训练FLOPs相较FastWAM降低73%。生成器初始化与视觉语言骨干均可获得较强前瞻性能。

Conclusion: 未来监督可在不进行视觉生成、不增加推理机制的前提下提升控制性能；强前瞻性能并不依赖特定骨干族，VLA与WAM的差异应归因于具体设计选择而非范式本身。

Abstract: The vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action model (WAM) communities debate whether control policies should inherit pretrained knowledge from vision-language models or from visual generators. The debate, however, bundles three separable choices: backbone initialization, the objective applied to future observations, and whether the machinery for that future loss is kept at inference. A gap between a VLA and a WAM therefore cannot simply be attributed to any one of them. We show that decoupling these choices yields a common framework for both families, which we term Just Action Model (JAM): a pretrained visual backbone with an action expert, trained with an action loss and a foresight loss. The foresight loss trains current-observation features to predict frozen encodings of the recorded future through a prediction head discarded after training, so no visual generation is involved in either training or inference, and the backbone can be chosen freely. We first compare objectives within fixed policy architectures, then evaluate the foresight recipe across backbone families. The resulting JAM policies outperform their matched FastWAM and ImageWAM counterparts on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, and achieve similar or slightly higher success rates on RoboTwin. Compared with FastWAM, JAM improves the success rates from 97.0%/55.3%/91.2% to 98.2%/64.5%/91.4%, respectively. With cached foresight targets, JAM reduces estimated online training FLOPs per sample per update by 73% relative to FastWAM. Our results show that future supervision improves control without visual generation or additional inference machinery, and strong foresight performance is attainable with either generator-initialized or vision-language backbones. Project Page: https://y0uroy.github.io/JAM

</details>


### [120] [3D-SceneQ: Empowering 3D LLM With Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning and Multi-Modal Feature Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37202)
*Yongqi Shan,Lu Zhang,Jiazuo Yu,Yunzhi Zhuge,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D-SceneQ，将查询引导的自适应token剪枝与多模态语义增强统一到一个3D LLM中，显著提升3D场景理解、推理与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-LLM存在两大局限：(i) 输入中混入大量任务无关的物体token，引入噪声，降低推理准确率并加剧幻觉；(ii) 仅依赖点云数据，缺乏2D模态中丰富的颜色、材质、纹理及高层上下文语义。

Method: 设计两个模块：Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning (QGAP) 利用可学习隐查询，依据用户指令语义自适应筛选任务相关物体，保留关键信息并抑制无关噪声；Multi-modal Object-level Feature Enhancement (MOFE) 将预训练2D模型的特征嵌入融合进3D表示，增强物体级语义。

Result: 在多个基准上超越现有SOTA，如Scan2Cap上CIDEr +5.7，SQA3D上EM@1 +17.0，在多种3D语言推理任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过自适应token剪枝与多模态语义增强的联合设计，可有效缓解噪声与语义不足问题，验证了其在3D语言推理任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Current 3D scene understanding pipelines typically concatenate vast numbers of 3D object tokens with text tokens and feed the resulting sequence to a Large Language Model (LLM). However, existing 3D-LLMs for scene understanding encounter two major limitations: (i) excessive inclusion of task-irrelevant object data, introducing noise that reduces reasoning accuracy and increases hallucinations; and (ii) reliance solely on point cloud data, which inherently lacks the rich semantic information available in complementary 2D modalities, such as color, material properties, texture, and high-level contextual relationships. To address these challenges, we introduce 3D-SceneQ, a 3D LLM that unifies adaptive token pruning with multimodal semantic enrichment, markedly advancing scene understanding, reasoning, and grounding. Specifically, we propose a Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning (QGAP) module that adaptively selects task-relevant objects based on the intended meaning of user instructions, leveraging a learnable latent query to retain critical information while effectively suppressing irrelevant noise. In addition, we introduce a Multi-modal Object-level Feature Enhancement (MOFE) module, which integrates 2D feature embeddings from pre-trained models into 3D representation to enrich object-level semantic information. Evaluations show that 3D-SceneQ outperforms current state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks (e.g., +5.7 CIDEr on Scan2Cap and +17.0 EM@1 on SQA3D), demonstrating its remarkable capabilities in various 3D language reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [121] [FastBEVFusion: Simple Multi-Modal Fusion for 3D Object Detection at 50 FPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37153)
*Tom Sanitz,Emre Gülsoylu,Simone Frintrop,Christian Wilms*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FastBEVFusion：将 LiDAR 与相机特征投影到半对齐的模态专属 BEV 空间（无需深度估计），并用轻量交叉注意力动态融合，在 nuScenes 上超过现有实时系统、>50 FPS 且对标定误差鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶要求低延迟的可靠实时 3D 检测，但许多 SOTA 系统精度高却无法满足实时约束；同时基于深度估计的融合计算开销大且易引入误差。

Method: 多模态各自提取特征后投影至半对齐的模态专属 BEV 空间，规避计算密集且易出错的深度估计；随后设计基于交叉注意力的轻量、软性、动态融合模块，依据场景上下文自适应地跨整个 BEV 空间进行特征融合。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上优于现有实时系统，不使用部署库即可达到 50+ FPS，并对标定误差表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FastBEVFusion 是一个简单、快速、鲁棒的融合式 3D 检测系统，兼顾实时性与精度，适用于真实场景部署。

Abstract: Reliable real-time 3D object detection based on LiDAR and camera data is critical for safety in autonomous driving, where systems must accurately perceive their surroundings with minimal latency. Many state-of-the-art systems achieve high-quality detection results but fail to meet real-time constraints, limiting their practical use. To address this challenge, we propose FastBEVFusion, a simple, fast, and robust fusion system for 3D object detection. Based on modality-specific features, FastBEVFusion projects these features into semi-aligned modality-specific BEV spaces, without relying on a computationally intensive and potentially error-prone depth estimation. Given these BEV spaces, we propose a light-weight, soft, and dynamic fusion module based on cross-attention, which adapts dynamically to the scene context and enables feature fusion across the entire BEV spaces. Experiments on the complex nuScenes dataset show that FastBEVFusion outperforms state-of-the-art real-time systems, and achieves over 50 FPS without deployment libraries. Additionally, our system shows strong robustness to calibration errors, making FastBEVFusion a powerful 3D object detection system for real-world applications.

</details>


### [122] [GPA-VGGT: Geometry-Preserving Acceleration of VGGT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37000)
*Muyeol Sung,Jangwon Kim,Jongwoo Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GPA-VGGT：一种无需训练的多视角几何模型加速方法，通过帧内注意力+共享跨帧K/V替换早期全局注意力，并在后期仅合并K/V保留全部Query，实现接近原精度的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT等前馈几何模型以全局注意力为核心，其计算量随帧数呈二次增长，在长序列上主导端到端耗时；已有的token合并等免训练加速方法会带来精度下降，难以替代原模型。

Method: 在前若干block中用帧内注意力并辅以共享的跨帧K/V集合替代全局注意力；在其余block中仅合并key和value而保留全部query，恢复每个被合并key所代表token的注意力质量（attention mass），并将匹配度量随机投影到128维以降低匹配开销。

Result: 在多种多视角几何任务上，GPA-VGGT在无需训练的情况下达到与先前方法相当的加速比，同时保留精确几何估计所需信息，在AUC@3指标下保持VGGT超过99%的位姿精度。

Conclusion: 通过针对注意力结构精心设计的替换与部分K/V合并策略，可在免训练条件下显著加速VGGT类前馈几何模型，同时几乎不损失几何估计精度。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometry models such as VGGT recover camera parameters, depth maps, and point maps from one to hundreds of views in a single forward pass, making them powerful backbones for multi-view 3D perception. However, VGGT's global attention is quadratic in the number of frames and dominates end-to-end runtime on long sequences, which has motivated a range of training-free efficiency methods such as token merging. These methods, however, come with a drop in accuracy that makes them hard to use in place of VGGT. We propose GPA-VGGT, a training-free acceleration method that replaces global attention in early blocks with frame attention augmented by a shared cross-frame K/V set. In the remaining blocks, we merge only keys and values while preserving all queries, restore the attention mass of the tokens each merged key represents, and randomly project the matching metric to 128 dimensions to reduce matching cost. Across diverse multi-view geometry tasks, GPA-VGGT provides training-free acceleration at speedups comparable to prior work while preserving the information required for precise geometric estimation, retaining over 99% of VGGT's pose accuracy under AUC@3.

</details>


### [123] [Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning for Dyadic Relationship Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36249)
*Wang Tang,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Linbo Qing,Ce Zhu,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiRe-DRR分层多模态交互推理框架，从特征层级、交互类型和关系层级三个视角组织关系证据，结合MoI-Former（关系感知稀疏路由的交互专家混合Transformer）与分层条件损失HC-Loss，在三个双人关系基准上优于近期图像/视频/多模态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双人关系识别方法将异构线索投影到共享表示并优化扁平分类目标，忽略了不同关系依赖不同交互模式这一事实：远距离关系常由直接行为对应推断，而亲密关系更依赖情感、行为与多模态的协同响应。

Method: 1) 将面部、身体、声学、文本线索分解为低层与高层表示，构建同质与异质的跨参与者交互；2) 提出MoI-Former，将多种交互视为专门专家，用关系感知稀疏路由筛选与关系相关的证据并保留上下文交互信息；3) 提出分层条件损失HC-Loss，利用社会关系的层级结构进行由粗到细的监督。

Result: 在三个双人关系基准上超越近期代表性图像、视频与多模态方法；路由分析显示远距离关系倾向依赖低层同质交互，而较亲密关系聚焦于高层异质交互。

Conclusion: 分层组织交互证据并配合关系感知稀疏路由与分层监督，可有效实现结构化的关系推理；关系距离与交互抽象层级/同异性之间存在可解释的对应规律。代码已开源。

Abstract: Dyadic relationship recognition aims to identify the latent social relationship between two individuals from their multimodal interactions. Existing methods typically project heterogeneous cues into a shared representation and optimise a flat classification objective, overlooking that relationships may rely on different interaction patterns. For instance, distant relationships can often be inferred from direct behavioural correspondence, whereas close relationships are better reflected by coordinated affective, behavioural, and multimodal responses. To address this limitation, we propose HiRe-DRR, a Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning framework that organises relationship evidence from three complementary perspectives: feature level, interaction type, and relationship hierarchy. HiRe-DRR decomposes facial, bodily, acoustic, and textual cues into low- and high-level representations and constructs homogeneous and heterogeneous cross-participant interactions. We further introduce the Mixture-of-Interaction Transformer (MoI-Former), which treats diverse interactions as specialised experts and employs relation-aware sparse routing to identify relationship-relevant evidence while preserving contextual interaction information. The proposed Hierarchical Conditional Loss (HC-Loss) exploits the hierarchical nature of social relationships through coarse-to-fine supervision, enabling structured relationship reasoning. Extensive experiments on three dyadic relationship benchmarks demonstrate that HiRe-DRR outperforms recent representative image-, video-, and multimodal methods. Our routing analysis further reveals that distant relationships tend to rely on low-level homogeneous interactions, whereas closer relationships focus on high-level heterogeneous interactions. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/tw-repository/HiRe-DRR.

</details>


### [124] [VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613)
*Shilong Li,Xingyuan Bu,Hao Shi,Yun Xing,Weixin Feng,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VideoLVR，用潜在视觉证据（局部区域的视觉 token）为 MLLM 的思维链推理提供可回溯的视觉支撑，避免文本坐标或裁剪重插入带来的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言的推理链无法让模型可靠地重访相关视觉证据，导致"视觉遗忘"；现有方案或用文本坐标（仅符号位置、非视觉内容），或重插入裁剪区域/帧（保留细节但显著扩张上下文）。

Method: 从相关视频时刻的局部区域构造潜在视觉证据；引入视觉 token 预测目标，将视觉 token 重建表述为在由输入视频导出的词表上的预测，从而在统一 token 预测框架内融合证据重建与文本生成；并扩展到强化学习，将采样视觉 token 与文本 token 一并视为动作，以最终答案奖励联合优化策略。

Result: 在多个视频基准上有效，尤其在需要细粒度视觉证据的任务上提升明显；推理中可高效重访任务相关视觉信息，且不显著扩张多模态上下文。

Conclusion: VideoLVR 以潜在视觉证据 + 统一 token 预测 + RL 扩展，实现交错文本与潜在视觉状态的推理，兼顾视觉保真与上下文效率。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially advanced video understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, language-based reasoning traces do not provide a reliable mechanism for models to revisit relevant visual evidence, leading to visual forgetting and impairing their ability to reason over video content. Existing approaches maintain visual grounding during reasoning in two main ways: they either refer to evidence using textual coordinates, which specify symbolic locations rather than the visual content itself, or reinsert cropped regions or frames into the multimodal context, preserving visual details at the cost of substantial context expansion. To address these limitations, we introduce VideoLVR, which constructs latent visual evidence from local regions at relevant video moments, thereby providing visual support for intermediate reasoning steps. To supervise latent visual evidence generation, we introduce a visual token prediction objective that formulates visual token reconstruction as prediction over a vocabulary derived from the input video, thereby unifying visual evidence reconstruction and text generation within the same token prediction framework. This shared prediction framework enables MLLMs to reason over interleaved text tokens and latent visual states while integrating evidence from different moments. We further extend VideoLVR to reinforcement learning by treating sampled visual tokens as actions alongside text tokens and jointly optimizing the policy using final-answer rewards. By efficiently revisiting task-relevant visual information during reasoning, VideoLVR improves video understanding without substantially expanding the multimodal context. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness, especially on tasks requiring fine-grained visual evidence.

</details>


### [125] [GroundControl: Satellite-Conditioned Multi-View Diffusion for Reconstruction-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37545)
*Akshay Krishnan,Matias Turkulainen,Jamie Wynn,Zawar Qureshi,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Sara Vicente,James Hays,Michael Firman,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出卫星图像条件化的多视角扩散模型，用于稀疏街景输入下的户外新视角合成与场景外推，抑制空间布局幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角/视频扩散模型在稀疏图像新视角合成中能生成高质量图像，但在输入视野之外（场景外推）时易产生不合理的空间布局幻觉。作者希望借助卫星图像作为额外信息源为户外场景提供全局布局约束。

Method: 构建卫星条件化的多视角扩散模型，在生成新视角时同时以卫星图像与街景输入图像为条件；采用基于交叉注意力的条件注入方式，保留预训练多视角扩散先验，从而在少量源视角下生成无缝、真实的新视角并遵循卫星图像所反映的场景布局。方法不依赖三维场景重建，仅使用带位姿的街景与卫星视图。

Result: 在户外数据集上的实验表明，该模型在新视角合成上优于仅以卫星为条件或仅以街景为条件的基线，在目标视角与源视角重叠极少的困难场景中优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 卫星图像作为附加条件可有效缓解户外场景外推中的空间布局幻觉，且通过交叉注意力条件化可在不重训扩散先验、不做三维重建的前提下实现布局一致的稀疏视角新视角合成。

Abstract: Multi-view or video diffusion models have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for novel-view synthesis from sparse image collections. While they can produce high quality images, they tend to hallucinate unrealistic spatial layouts when synthesizing views outside the input views (scene extrapolation). We propose to address this challenge for outdoor scenes by using satellite images as an additional information source to condition the multi-view generative model. We introduce a new satellite-conditioned multi-view diffusion model that combines conditioning from a satellite image and street-level input images when generating novel-views. Our cross-attention based conditioning allows us to retain pretrained multi-view diffusion priors to generate seamless, realistic, novel views from a handful of source views, while also adhering to the scene layout as seen from satellite images. Our model does not use 3D scene reconstruction, and only relies on posed street and satellite views. Our experiments on outdoor datasets show that our model is better than both satellite-conditioned or street-view conditioned baselines on novel-view synthesis, especially on challenging cases where target views have very little overlap with source views. Project page: https://nianticspatial.github.io/groundcontrol

</details>


### [126] [How Reliable Is Individualized Functional-Network Attribution in Resting-State fMRI Cognitive Prediction in ADNI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37472)
*Sheethal Bhat,Mahfuzur Rahman Chowdhury,Imam Ahmed Shaik,Rose Dawn Bharath,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估静息态fMRI个体化网络归因（网络消融）的重测信度，发现分半信度近天花板但扫描-重扫描信度明显下降，且存在强群体共享成分，建议同时报告重扫描信度与置换零假设对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化归因估计（指出哪些功能网络驱动某受试者的模型预测）常被作为可解释的rs-fMRI生物标志物提出，但其重测信度很少被量化，可能导致个体特异性被高估。

Method: 针对冻结的静息态功能连接预测器（Schaefer-200 FC预测MMSE/ADAS-Cog13），在ADNI N=875受试者上计算逐受试者的网络消融归因；采用分半信度、扫描-重扫描信度，并设置受试者水平的置换零假设（失配受试者对照）。

Result: 分半信度接近天花板（Spearman ρ=0.948/0.950），扫描-重扫描信度降至ρ=0.609/0.616；在失配受试者零假设下相关性仍达ρ≈0.46–0.47，说明存在强群体共享结构，但匹配配对显著超过该零假设（z≈6.6–6.9，p≤10⁻⁴）。

Conclusion: 在ADNI中，个体化网络归因在强群体共享模式之上仅含适度但非零的个体特异成分；应报告扫描-重扫描信度并结合置换零假设对照，而非仅用同会话代理指标，以避免夸大个体特异性。

Abstract: Individualized attribution estimates which functional networks drive a model prediction for a given subject and is often proposed as an interpretable resting-state fMRI (rs-fMRI) biomarker, but its test-retest reliability is rarely quantified. We evaluate per-subject network-ablation attribution for a frozen resting-state functional connectivity (FC) predictor (Schaefer-200 FC predicting MMSE/ADAS-Cog13) trained on N=875 subjects from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), using, and (iii) a subject-level permutation null. Split-half reliability is near-ceiling (Spearman p=0.948/0.950) but drops on scan-rescan (p=0.609/0.616). Under the mismatched-subject null, correlations remain substantial (p~0.46-0.47), indicating strong population-shared structure, yet matched pairs exceed this null (z~6.6-6.9, p<=10-4). Thus, in ADNI, individualized network attribution contains a modest but non-zero person-specific component atop a strong population-shared pattern. We recommend reporting scan-rescan reliability together with a permutation-null control, rather than within-session proxies alone, to avoid overstating individual specificity.

</details>


### [127] [MoI-Bench: A Benchmark for Image Generation and Editing on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37599)
*Kailai Feng,Haoxiang Rao,Yuxiang Wei,Bo Chen,Junsong Li,Yang Pan,Chengkun Cai,Jagmohan Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向移动用户需求的端侧图像编辑基准 MoI-Bench（64 子任务、1150 样本，基于16种操作×8类视觉目标的解耦设计）及评测方法 MoI-Eval，并引入"质量-可部署性"二维能力图谱评估端侧与云端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发端侧图像模型需兼顾真实用户需求、任务类型与设备资源约束，但现有基准难以揭示端侧模型与云端模型之间的能力差距，也缺乏对紧凑模型改进的细粒度指引。

Method: 构建 MoI-Bench：16种操作（如移除、替换）×8类视觉目标（如人像、文字）两正交轴，组合定义具体任务（如去除面部妆容），共64子任务、1150样本；提出 MoI-Eval，含指令遵循、视觉质量、细节保持等任务特定准则，构建样本级评测问题并沿操作、目标、准则三轴聚合为整体质量与能力画像；评测代表性端侧与云端模型并测量移动部署成本。

Result: 解耦的"操作×目标"设计可提供更细粒度改进指引，能定位需优先补齐的具体能力缺口（如文字替换）而非宽泛应用级目标（如改进文字编辑）；二维质量-可部署性图谱显示当前端侧模型已能实现高效生成或编辑，扩展任务覆盖比进一步提升部署效率更值得关注。

Conclusion: 相比单一分数排名，二维能力图谱更适合评估移动实用场景；未来进展需在提升编辑质量（尤其身份保持与文字渲染）的同时扩大任务覆盖范围。

Abstract: Developing on-device image models requires considering practical user needs, task types and device resource constraints. However, existing benchmarks provide limited guidance on identifying capability gaps between on-device models and server-side models. We introduce MoI-Bench, a benchmark grounded in mobile user needs, comprising 64 subtasks and 1150 samples. It defines 16 operations, such as removal and replacement, and eight visual target categories covering content such as portraits and text. These form two orthogonal axes whose applicable combinations define specific tasks, such as removing facial makeup. To better guide model improvement, we further propose MoI-Eval, which introduces task-specific criteria covering instruction following, visual quality and detail preservation. We further construct sample-level evaluation questions and aggregate their scores into overall quality and capability profiles along the operation, target and criteria axes. We evaluate representative on-device and server-side models and measure mobile deployment costs. Results show that this decoupled Operation x Target design provides finer-grained guidance for improving compact models. For example, it highlights the need to prioritize specific capability gaps (e.g., text-replacement) over broad application-level goals (e.g., improving text editing). Unlike conventional benchmarks that rank models using a single score, we introduce a two-dimensional Quality-Deployability capability map to assess models for practical mobile use. The map shows that current on-device models can achieve efficient generation or editing but expanding their task coverage deserves greater attention than further improving deployment efficiency. Further progress requires improving editing quality, particularly identity preservation and text rendering, while broadening task coverage.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Do We Need an LLM to Know Why Sources Conflict? Decision Models for Knowledge-Conflict Typing in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36915)
*Parv Khatri,Harshul Jain,Mehak Kapoor,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估11个商业推理网关上的“类型化决策模型”（单次前向返回固定选项概率而非生成文本）能否在CONFLICTS基准上快速廉价地完成来源冲突类型分类，并与Gemini系列对比，给出成本、延迟、准确率及级联方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 来源冲突类型（多有效答案、争议话题、过时信息、虚假来源）决定正确的回答行为；Cattan等(2025)证明告知生成器冲突类型可提升约24分，但即便是前沿LLM也难以预测该类型。因此需要一种快速、低成本的方式来准确判定冲突类型。

Method: 使用CONFLICTS基准与原文提示词（逐字复用），在同一输入下评测某商业推理网关所提供的全部11个决策模型（来自7家供应商，经类型化决策接口而非生成文本），并与Gemini模型对比；此外做级联实验：将每个决策模型置信度最低的四分之一查询转交Gemini 3.8 Flash处理；并对分类体系做是/否问题分解、跨模型修复迁移、以及预先设定的诊断实验（日期作用、来源顺序敏感性、植入虚假来源检测）。

Result: 直接询问时决策模型准确率50.0–67.0%，11个中10个达到或超过Gemini 2.5 Flash（61.3%），计费成本低4–39倍（2个免费）、延迟低2.6–6.0倍；Gemini 3.8 Flash（70.0%）显著优于其中8个，但置信度级联在11个中有8个与之统计上打平，成本仅为其25–37%；端到端上，11个中10个用其预测类型生成的回答行为达标频率与用LLM类型时相当。用是/否问题分解分类体系会降低所有模型准确率，且在一个模型上拟合的修复不能迁移；诊断显示日期无显著作用，来源顺序敏感性模型间差异很大；决策模型以硬标签检测植入虚假来源不如LLM可靠，但其概率仍能区分植入与干净来源。

Conclusion: 类型化决策模型在冲突类型分类上可低成本、低延迟地接近或达到商用LLM水平，并可通过置信度级联进一步缩小差距，端到端生成行为基本可比；但分类体系分解会损害性能、修复难以跨模型迁移、虚假来源检测仍是短板。作者开源了代码、提示词及全部模型输出。

Abstract: When the sources retrieved for a query disagree, the right response depends on why they disagree: a question with several valid answers, a contested topic, stale information and a false source each call for different behaviour. Cattan et al. (2025) showed that telling a generator the conflict type improves its responses by about 24 points, but that predicting the type is hard even for frontier LLMs. We study whether typed decision models, which return probabilities over fixed options in a single forward pass instead of generating text, can do this classification fast and cheaply. Using the CONFLICTS benchmark and the original paper's prompt verbatim, we evaluate all 11 decision models served by one commercial inference gateway, from 7 providers, against Gemini models on identical inputs. Asked directly, the decision models reach 50.0--67.0% accuracy; 10 of the 11 match or exceed Gemini 2.5 Flash, the paper's model (61.3%), at 4--39x lower billed cost (2 were free) and 2.6--6.0x lower latency. The current Gemini 3.8 Flash (70.0%) is significantly more accurate than 8 of them, but sending each decision model's least confident quarter of queries to it gives a cascade statistically tied with it for 8 of the 11, at 25--37% of its cost. End to end, answers generated with the types of 10 of the 11 follow the expected behaviour as often as with the LLM's types. Decomposing the taxonomy into yes/no questions lowers accuracy for every model, and a repair fitted on one model does not transfer. Pre-specified diagnostics show that dates play no measurable role and that sensitivity to source order varies widely between models. Decision models detect a planted false source less reliably than the LLM as hard labels, but their probabilities still separate planted from clean sources. We release code, prompts and all model outputs at https://github.com/Parv17k/decision-models-conflict-typing.

</details>


### [129] [Personality Latents: Conditioning Frozen Language Models on Individuals from Their Own Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36805)
*Gunhee Cho,Yun-Gyung Cheong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Personality Latents (PL)：不给人格标签、不写人格档案，直接从个体自身文本中学得一个潜在向量，作为软前缀注入冻结的语言模型，实现可迁移的人格表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人格控制依赖书面档案或既定特质类别（如 MBTI），表达范围受限于作者写下的内容或分类体系所命名的维度。

Method: 遵循生成式潜在优化：为每个训练者设一个自由潜在向量，经冻结模型自身反向优化；同时训练编码器预测该潜在，使新个体只需一次前向即可获得潜变量，并可在测试时可选地精化。通过软前缀注入冻结语言模型。

Result: 在留出个体上，让条件化模型回答中性及访谈问题，并用基于真实人类文本训练的打分器读取人格。MBTI 各轴上 PL 达到的 AUC 优于相同设置下的人格嵌入与提示基线，且该排序在三种不同嵌入空间打分下均保持；用 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 学得的潜在仅通过一个新适配器即可将人格迁移至 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct。

Conclusion: 以无标签、无档案的潜在表示替代显式人格描述，可在冻结模型上实现有效且可跨模型迁移的人格控制。

Abstract: Controlling the personality of a language model usually relies on written profiles or named trait categories, so it can only express what an author writes down or what a taxonomy names. We instead propose Personality Latents (PL), which learn a latent for each person from their own writing, with no personality labels and no authored profile, and inject it into a frozen language model as a soft prefix. Following generative latent optimization, each training person receives a free latent that is optimized through the frozen model itself, while an encoder learns to predict these latents so that a new person needs only one forward pass, with optional refinement at test time. We evaluate on held-out people by having the conditioned model answer neutral and interview questions and reading personality from its answers with scorers trained on real human writing. On the MBTI axes PL reaches an AUC of , ahead of persona embedding and prompt-based baselines under identical settings, and the ranking holds when the answers are scored in three different embedding spaces. A latent learned with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct also transfers personality to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct through a new adapter alone.

</details>


### [130] [DocSkill: Skill-Integrated Agentic Reasoning for Multilingual Multi-Hop Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36759)
*Jing Huang,Zhiya Tan,Changtao Miao,Shutao Gong,Yang Wang,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Jianshu Li,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DocSkill：以可学习的技能编排策略驱动证据获取与推理的智能体框架，配合离线有界MCTS生成轨迹用于SFT与DPO训练，并构建多语言多跳文档问答数据集MMHop-DocBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言多跳文档问答需在复杂版式、跨页与多语种条件下定位并整合证据；现有单轮处理与自适应工具调用方法难以学好"证据获取—跨页整合—答案校验"的协同调度。

Method: 1) DocSkill智能体框架：学习式技能编排策略，依据动态证据状态选择、重访或跳过技能；2) 用有界蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）离线探索替代技能轨迹，产出示范用于SFT、偏好对用于DPO；3) HopGen数据流水线：构建层级化页面-元素图，推导可追溯的跨页证据路径，生成带页级与元素级证据标注的问答对；数据集含6万QA对、10种语言、8类文档领域，划分训练/测试集。

Result: DocSkill（Qwen3-VL-8B）在MMHop-DocBench上取得41.0准确率、49.2 F1，较最强基线分别高5.5和6.3个百分点；在MMLongBench-Doc与DocBench上表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: 将技能编排建模为可学习策略并结合MCTS离线监督（SFT+DPO）可有效提升多语言多跳文档问答；配套的高质量带证据标注数据集为该任务提供了可评测基准。

Abstract: Multilingual multi-hop document question answering (MMHop-DocQA) requires locating and integrating evidence across complex layouts, document pages, and diverse languages. Existing approaches include single-pass processing and adaptive tool use, but learning to coordinate evidence acquisition, cross-page integration, and answer verification remains challenging. To address this challenge, we present DocSkill, an agentic framework with a learned skill-orchestration policy for adaptive evidence acquisition and reasoning. The policy selects, revisits, or skips skills according to the evolving evidence state. To construct supervision for skill orchestration, bounded Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) explores alternative skill trajectories offline. These trajectories provide demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and preference pairs for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). We further introduce MMHop-DocBench, a multilingual multi-hop document QA dataset with training and test splits, containing 60K QA pairs across 10 languages and eight document domains. Its HopGen pipeline builds hierarchical page-element graphs to derive traceable cross-page evidence paths and generate grounded QA pairs with page- and element-level evidence annotations. Experiments show that DocSkill with Qwen3-VL-8B achieves 41.0 accuracy and 49.2 F1 on MMHop-DocBench, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 5.5 and 6.3 points, respectively, and achieving competitive performance on MMLongBench-Doc and DocBench.

</details>


### [131] [No Tokenizer, Still a Gap: Cross-Lingual Fairness of Homoglyph and Invisible-Character Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36432)
*Alexander Nemecek,Yuqiao Xu,Joseph Falkenburg,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 同形字与不可见字符水印绕过了分词器，但水印容量主要由文字系统决定（占96%方差），低资源语言仍处于劣势，公平性问题只是从分词器转移到了脚本/字形层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成时水印依赖分词器对文本的切分方式，在低资源语言上效果差，形成语言间的不公平。同形字/不可见字符水印在字符层面工作、不接触分词器，因此作者要检验这类编辑式水印是否能规避这一低资源语言劣势。

Method: 在24种文字系统、40种语言变体上测量三项指标：每个字符可承载的隐藏比特数（容量）、经过常规处理和针对性移除后的存活率、带密钥检测器所需的最少文本量；并分析容量与文字系统、资源水平的相关性（Spearman ρ）。

Result: 容量跨语言相差3.3倍，其中96%的变异由文字系统解释。拉丁字母内部资源水平无显著差异（ρ=+0.095, p=0.78）；跨文字系统时容量随资源水平上升（ρ=+0.54, 95% CI [+0.14,+0.83]），因低资源语言多用同形字少的文字系统。普通复制粘贴会剥离不可见字符，进一步拉大差距：检测英文或波斯文约需2个码位，老挝文需93个。

Conclusion: 去除分词器并未消除语言间的不公平，只是把不公平的来源从分词策略转移到文字系统的字形资源上；编辑式水印的公平性评估必须以文字系统为单位。

Abstract: Homoglyph and invisible-character watermarks are edit-based watermarks. Instead of changing how a model generates text, they hide a signal in the text after it is written. They swap some characters for homoglyphs, which look the same, or very similar, but have different Unicode code points, or they insert characters that do not display, all without access to the model that wrote the text. Generation-time watermarks work less well for lower-resourced languages due to how tokenizers split their text. Because they work on individual characters, these watermarks never touch a tokenizer, so we ask whether they escape this gap. Across 40 language varieties in 24 scripts, we measure capacity (the hidden bits each character can carry), how much of it survives routine handling and targeted removal, and how much text a keyed detector needs. Capacity varies 3.3-fold and script accounts for 96\% of that variation. Within Latin script, resource level makes no detectable difference ($ρ=+0.095$, $p=0.78$), but across scripts capacity rises with it ($ρ=+0.54$, 95\% CI $[+0.14,+0.83]$), because lower-resourced languages tend to use scripts with few homoglyphs. Ordinary copy-paste, which strips invisible characters, widens the gap: a detector then needs about 2 code points of English or Persian but 93 of Lao. Removing the tokenizer does not remove the inequity, but instead only changes its source.

</details>


### [132] [Frankly Speaking: The Company Election Integrity Keeps in Congressional Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36187)
*Michael A. Robinette,Christina Barsky,Amanda D. Clark,Helen Flannery,Juniper Lovato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于2010–2025年10,420封国会电子通讯与历时词嵌入，发现两党无需不同词汇即可讲述不同的选举叙事——仅通过将相同选举术语置于不同语义邻域来实现；这一分化因多数术语的分化程度并不高于同频词、且两党偏移幅度相当而难以察觉，但"certification"等少数术语显著分化，"rigged""stolen"于2020年后进入并极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选举诚信在美国政治中是两党共同承诺，但国会议员对该概念的实际所指日益分歧。既有研究多关注词汇选择或立场差异，本文追问：分歧是否体现在用词本身，还是体现在相同词汇的语境共现结构上。

Method: 以2010–2025年10,420封国会e-newsletter为语料，针对"integrity""security""fraud"等选举术语，采用历时词嵌入（diachronic word embeddings）从上下文用法中追踪语义变化；量化三项指标：两党对每一术语的使用差异度、各党的历时偏移量、以及两党是否共享最近语义邻居。

Result: 在几乎所有术语与年份上，两党都以完全不同的词汇环绕同一套选举诚信语汇。该分化不易被察觉：多数选举词汇的分化程度不超过其他同频词，且两党用法偏移幅度无显著差异。仅部分术语分化加剧，尤以"certification"最为突出；"rigged""stolen"于2020年后进入选举话语并在此极化。至2024年，民主党将"voter""election""integrity"置于"access"与"administration"语汇中，共和党则将同样术语与"citizenship verification""noncitizen voting""border security"并置。

Conclusion: 对一方而言，诚信意味着保障合格选民投票的能力；对另一方而言，则意味着使选举过程免遭非法选票侵蚀。两套定义共存于同一选举体系之中，选举管理官员被迫承受这一张力。研究提示：语义分化经由共现语境而非词汇替换实现，故常规词频或词汇层面的监测容易漏检。

Abstract: Election integrity is a shared commitment in American politics, but Democrats and Republicans in Congress increasingly mean different things by it. Analyzing 10,420 congressional e-newsletters sent from 2010 to 2025, we examine how the two parties invoke election terms such as ``integrity,'' ``security,'' and ``fraud.'' Using diachronic word embeddings, which track semantic change through how words are used in context, we measure how differently each party uses each term, how much each party shifts, and whether they share nearest semantic neighbors. We show that members of Congress do not need different words to tell different stories about elections. They need only place those same words in different company. Across nearly every term and year, the two parties surround election-integrity vocabulary with entirely different words. That divergence is easy to miss, because most election vocabulary is no more divergent than other words used at roughly the same frequency, and neither party's usage shifted more than the other's. Only a subset of terms grew more divergent, most notably ``certification,'' while ``rigged'' and ``stolen'' entered election discourse after 2020 and polarized there. By 2024, Democrats surround ``voter,'' ``election,'' and ``integrity'' with the language of access and administration. Republicans surround the same terms with ``citizenship verification,'' ``noncitizen voting,'' and ``border security.'' For one party, integrity means protecting eligible voters' ability to cast a ballot. For the other, it means protecting the electoral process from illegitimate ballots. Election officials, who must administer a single system under both definitions, inherit this tension.

</details>


### [133] [3, 7, 14, 17, 42: Instruction Tuning Installs and Sharpens Number-Choice Heuristics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36178)
*Samar Ansari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以无需采样的方式精确计算语言模型对1到N每个整数分配的随机数概率，系统评测18个开放权重模型系列、5种范围、29种解码配置，发现指令微调使分布更不均匀，且主要通过"回避偶数/整数"和强化"回避端点"两种启发式实现，部分符合人类数字选择的心理学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究依赖采样估计语言模型输出随机数的概率分布，存在采样噪声，限制了结论精度；同时缺乏对指令微调如何改变数字偏好、以及这些偏好是否与人类数字选择心理学一致的系统性刻画。

Method: 对固定提示，将每个整数所有评分拼写的概率求和，得到精确概率向量而无采样误差；评测18个开放权重模型系列、范围至N=100的五种区间、29种解码配置、两种推理栈，其中15组具有匹配的基础与指令微调检查点；比较端点回避、整数回避、奇数偏好、众数整数及各解码设置（温度1.0、top-p 0.95核截断）对分布的影响。

Result: 15组配对中指令微调全部使分布更不均匀；微调引入"回避整数"并强化"回避端点"（1和N在基础模型中返回率为内部速率的0.30–0.60，微调模型几乎不返回，10的倍数几乎完全被回避）；奇数偏好在N=100的微调模型中一致，基础模型仅弱显示；端点回避与奇数偏好符合Kubovy与Psotka的人类模型，但整数回避不在该模型中，其预测的中点回避基本缺失（仅N=100略有例外）；微调未产生共享的偏好整数，N>10后模态整数最高仅42%条件共享，P(17)在15对中10升5降；温度1.0下top-p 0.95核截断中位增加0.035的总变差距离。

Conclusion: 语言模型随机数分布可被无采样精确测量并进行细粒度行为刻画；指令微调通过引入/强化特定启发式（回避整数、回避端点）改变分布形状，而非注入统一偏好整数，部分行为与人类数字选择理论吻合，为模型行为分析与对齐研究提供了可复现的量化框架。

Abstract: We measure, without sampling error, the probability a language model assigns to each integer from 1 to N when asked for a random number. For a fixed prompt the model defines one probability vector, which we compute by summing over each integer's scored spellings rather than estimate by sampling, removing the noise that limits earlier studies [1, 4]. We evaluate 18 open-weight model lines at five ranges up to N = 100, under 29 decoding configurations, on two inference stacks; 15 of them have matched base and instruction-tuned checkpoints that both give usable answers. Instruction tuning makes the distribution less uniform in all 15 pairs. It installs one heuristic, round-number avoidance, and sharpens another, endpoint avoidance. Base checkpoints do not avoid round numbers once the endpoints are removed from those sets; tuned checkpoints avoid them, multiples of 10 almost completely. Base checkpoints return the endpoints 1 and N at 0.30 to 0.60 of the interior rate; tuned checkpoints almost never return them. A preference for odd numbers is consistent across tuned checkpoints only at N = 100, where base checkpoints also show it weakly. Endpoint avoidance and the odd preference fit the account Kubovy and Psotka [6] proposed for human number choice; round-number avoidance is not part of that account, and the midpoint avoidance it predicts is essentially absent, with one slight exception at N = 100. Tuning does not install a new favourite integer shared across models: above N = 10 no modal integer is shared by more than 42 percent of tuned conditions, and P (17) rises in 10 of 15 pairs and falls in 5. The concentration is present in the model's own distribution: at temperature 1.0, nucleus truncation at top-p 0.95 adds a median of 0.035 to the total variation distance.

</details>


### [134] [Japanese Discourse Structure Analysis Based on Segmented Discourse Representation Theory Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36667)
*Satomu Tomisaki,Yosuke Mikami,Amane Watahiki,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文用LLM将英文SDRT语料翻译构建日文SDRT语料，发现提示与微调模型在英日上表现相当，并通过消融表明模型主要依赖上下文中的篇章关系结构而非连接词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于SDRT的篇章解析对刻画对话结构动态至关重要，但已有研究集中于英语；由于缺乏日文SDRT语料，其日语有效性尚不明确，需要填补这一空白并检验SDRT解析的跨语言适用性。

Method: 借助LLM翻译现有英文SDRT语料以构建日文SDRT语料；对比基于提示（prompt-based）与微调（fine-tuned）的LLM解析器在英日两种语言上的表现；并针对篇章连接词（discourse markers）与上下文输入开展消融分析。

Result: 英日解析器性能相当，表明SDRT篇章解析具有跨语言可迁移性；消融显示所考察的日语连接词大多影响很小，模型预测强烈依赖上下文中的篇章关系而非上下文子句的具体内容。

Conclusion: 对结构性篇章关系的依赖可解释英日两语言尽管存在显著类型学差异却仍取得相当性能的现象，说明SDRT解析的跨语言表现主要由结构信息驱动。

Abstract: Discourse parsing based on Segmented Discourse Representation Theory (SDRT) is crucial for capturing the structural dynamics of dialogues. However, most prior research focuses on English, and its effectiveness for Japanese remains unclear owing to the lack of Japanese SDRT corpora. To address this issue, we construct Japanese SDRT corpora by translating existing English SDRT corpora using an LLM. Through a comparative evaluation of prompt-based and fine-tuned LLMs, we show that the models achieve comparable performance between English and Japanese, suggesting the cross-lingual applicability of SDRT-based discourse parsing. In order to investigate why the English and Japanese parsers achieve similar performance, we conduct ablation analyses on discourse markers and contextual inputs. Our results reveal that most of the Japanese discourse markers we examined have little impact, and the models' predictions depend heavily on contextual discourse relations rather than on the content of the context clauses. This structural reliance might explain the comparable performance despite the substantial typological differences between English and Japanese.

</details>


### [135] [Structured Maps for Landmark-Based Route Instructions with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36582)
*Mifu Matsumoto,Yuki Nakanishi,Tomoya Hashiguchi,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于 SVG 的结构化文本地图表示，用于路径导航中的地标选择任务，效果与或优于图像输入，并可通过任务微调让小模型高效利用地图布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成基于地标的路径指令要求模型同时理解地图几何与人类地标偏好；LLM 语言能力强，但如何为这类模型组织地图数据尚无定论，需要一种轻量且能保留空间信息的输入接口。

Method: 提出以 SVG 格式编码空间特征的结构化文本表示，使其与渲染地图图像保持几何对应，让语言模型直接访问空间与语义数据；基于人类撰写路径描述与 OpenStreetMap 数据构建新数据集，评测两个任务：转向处地标选择与目的地周边地标选择；对比五个 LLM 的结构化文本输入与 PNG 图像输入，并进行任务特定微调。

Result: 结构化文本输入在性能上与图像输入持平或更优；任务特定微调后，较小的本地 LLM 能有效利用地图布局，取得较高选择准确率。

Conclusion: 结构化文本是面向人类导航的空间推理中高效、轻量的数据接口，可作为图像输入的低成本替代方案。

Abstract: To generate landmark-based route instructions, models must understand both map geometry and human landmark preferences. Although large language models (LLMs) show great abilities in language tasks, the best way to structure map data for these models is not yet established. This paper proposes a structured text representation for landmark selection tasks in route navigation, utilizing SVG format to encode spatial features. The structured text maintains a geometric connection with rendered map images, allowing language models to directly access spatial and semantic data. Using a new dataset created from human-written route descriptions and OpenStreetMap data, we evaluate models on two tasks: landmark selection at turns and around destinations. Experiments with five LLMs show that the proposed structured text input performs as well as or better than image-based PNG inputs. In addition, task-specific fine-tuning enables smaller local LLMs to effectively leverage map layouts to achieve high selection accuracy. These results demonstrate that structured text is an efficient and lightweight interface for spatial reasoning in human-oriented navigation.

</details>


### [136] [ETC-Bench: A Multilingual Benchmark for Evaluation-Side Transcript Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36527)
*Zhiheng Qian,Junhao Du,Jing Peng,Hanqi Li,Kunyang Peng,Feng Xue,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出评估端转写规范化（ETC）任务，构建了覆盖12种语言、181类现象的多语言基准ETC-Bench（最小对比对），提出兼顾语义保持的指标SENA与分类学引导的智能体方法TACS，实验表明TACS智能体在共享语言上优于基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一语音的转写文本可能存在并非由语音信号唯一决定差异（如标点、大小写、数字书写等），这类差异会影响评估的公平性与可比性，因此需要在评估端对转写进行规范化，但此前缺乏系统化定义与基准。

Method: 形式化定义ETC任务：将差异非语音决定的多重表层形式映射为单一规范形式。构建ETC-Bench：12种语言、181种现象类型，每实例为共享句境、仅目标片段不同的最小对比对。提出SENA指标：要求同一最小对的两个成员经独立规范化后完全一致，并以对级相对BERTScore约束防止信息丢失。提出TACS：向LLM智能体提供现象描述与显式规范化契约的分类学引导方法。对比5种基线配置与3种TACS智能体。

Result: 3种TACS智能体在共享语言上优于5种基线配置；SENA可同时衡量规范化成功率与语义保持性；ETC-Bench可系统评测多语言规范化能力。

Conclusion: ETC任务与ETC-Bench为跨语言评估端转写规范化提供了系统化测试平台，分类学引导的智能体方法有效，但多语言覆盖与方法的泛化仍有进一步拓展空间。

Abstract: Written transcripts of the same speech can differ in ways that are not uniquely determined by the speech signal. We formulate evaluation-side transcript canonicalization (ETC), which maps multiple surface forms to a single canonical form when their differences are not determined by speech. To our knowledge, ETC-Bench is the first and most comprehensive multilingual benchmark for evaluation-side transcript canonicalization, covering 181 phenomenon types across 12 languages. Each instance is a minimal pair that shares a sentence context and differs only in a target span exhibiting one phenomenon. To evaluate both canonicalization success and semantic preservation, we propose Semantic-preserving Exact-match Normalization Accuracy (SENA), which combines exact agreement between independently canonicalized pair members with a pair-relative BERTScore constraint against information loss. We further introduce the Taxonomy-guided Agent Canonicalization Skill (TACS), which provides LLM agents with phenomenon descriptions and explicit canonicalization contracts. Experiments with five baseline configurations and three TACS-based agents show that the agents outperform the baseline systems on shared languages. ETC-Bench provides a systematic testbed for evaluation-side transcript canonicalization across languages.

</details>


### [137] [Benchmarking Task Takeover Across Agent Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36523)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Dongyuan Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HSC-Bench基准（1,039项任务），通过"证据恢复"和"状态延续"两条轨道评估多智能体交接中接收模型能否承接先前工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长任务协作中责任会在模型间转移，但任务成功率和记忆保持分数无法刻画"交接时保留的信息"与"接收方如何继续工作"之间的联系。

Method: 构建Handoff State Continuity Benchmark，含1,039项任务，从两方面测评交接：恢复相关仓库证据、以及基于既有对话/工具/文件工作状态继续推进；对六个接收模型进行比较，并结合对后续响应与动作的核查。

Result: 结构化交接状态对所有模型的仓库证据恢复均有提升，对多数模型的延续表现也有提升；两条轨道的最强接收模型不同，说明单凭证据恢复排名无法刻画继承状态下的延续能力。

Conclusion: HSC-Bench同时评估对先前证据的获取与对既定承诺、已完成工作的遵循，为选择接收模型和评估交接表示提供了依据。

Abstract: As agents collaborate on longer tasks, responsibility may pass between models before the work is complete. The receiving model must build on prior decisions, observations, and actions while addressing the remaining requirements. Evaluating this transition requires connecting the information preserved at handoff to how the receiver continues the work, a relationship that task-success and memory-retention scores alone do not capture. We introduce the Handoff State Continuity Benchmark (HSC-Bench), comprising 1,039 tasks that evaluate two complementary aspects of task takeover: recovering relevant repository evidence and continuing from established conversational, tool, and file-work state. Comparisons across six receiving models show that structured handoff state improves repository evidence recovery for every model and continuation performance for most models. The strongest receivers differ between the two tracks, showing that evidence-recovery rankings alone do not characterize performance when continuing from inherited state. By combining these comparisons with checks of subsequent responses and actions, HSC-Bench evaluates both access to prior evidence and adherence to established commitments and completed work. This provides a basis for selecting receiving models and assessing handoff representations according to the work that must be carried forward.

</details>


### [138] [Same Verdict, Different Readers: Personalizing Fact-Checking Explanations from Relative Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37380)
*Haokun Wen,Xuemeng Song,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出忠实个性化事实核查解释重写任务，并给出PERPL框架：用跨人格互配对构建模拟偏好监督，再用渐进偏好学习训练轻量重写器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实核查解释需适配不同背景与偏好的读者，但同一解释难以满足所有人；且缺乏读者撰写的参考解释，同时需区分人格特定偏好与通用解释质量、突破预定义风格的限制。

Method: PERPL含两部分：(1) 模拟偏好构建，通过跨人格互配对从LLM模拟读者的相对偏好中提取人格特定监督信号；(2) 渐进偏好学习，先在构建的监督上训练轻量重写器，再以探索引导的偏好优化进行精炼，使其发现预定义风格之外的优选解释。

Result: 大量实验显示，PERPL在个性化偏好对齐上优于规模远大于自身的通用LLM，同时保持高事实忠实性。

Conclusion: 基于模拟读者相对偏好的渐进偏好学习，可在无需人工参考解释的条件下实现忠实且个性化的解释重写，轻量模型亦可超越大型通用模型。

Abstract: Fact-checking explanations help Web users understand why a claim receives its verdict, yet the same explanation may not satisfy readers with different backgrounds and preferences. We formulate Faithful Personalized Fact-Checking Explanation Rewriting, which adapts an existing explanation to individual readers while preserving factual fidelity. Rather than relying on reader-written reference explanations, we learn personalized rewriting from the relative preferences of LLM-simulated readers. However, this presents two challenges: distinguishing persona-specific preferences from general explanation quality and learning beyond a limited set of predefined explanation styles. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized Explanation Rewriting via Preference Learning (PERPL), a framework consisting of two components. Simulated Preference Construction derives persona-specific supervision from simulated reader preferences through cross-persona reciprocal pairing. Progressive Preference Learning first trains a lightweight rewriter on the constructed supervision and then refines it through exploration-guided preference optimization, enabling it to discover preferred explanations beyond the predefined styles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PERPL outperforms much larger general-purpose LLMs in personalized preference alignment while maintaining high factual fidelity.

</details>


### [139] [REGLAT at ArGuard Shared Tasks: An Ensemble-Based Model Integrating NER for Harmful Content Detection in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37265)
*Ahmed M. Fetouh,Mariam Labib,Nsrin Ashraf,Omer Dawood,Hamada Nayel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段方法，将GLiNER抽取的危害相关实体拼接到原始文本后，输入微调后的MARBERT分类器，对阿拉伯语用户提示进行七类有害内容分类。开发集macro-F1为0.886、准确率0.897；子任务1盲测准确率0.793、macro-F1为0.735，子任务2分别为0.770和0.754。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语有害内容检测面临方言变体多样以及不同危害类别间存在语义重叠的困难，细粒度分类尤其具有挑战性。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段用GLiNER从每条提示中抽取危害相关实体；第二阶段将抽取的实体追加到原始文本，再送入微调的MARBERT分类器完成七类别判定，从而将显式实体信息与预训练阿拉伯语模型的上下文理解能力相结合。评估基于ArGuard 2026共享任务开发集及盲测集。

Result: 开发集macro-F1为0.886、准确率0.897；子任务1盲测准确率0.793、macro-F1为0.735；子任务2准确率0.770、macro-F1为0.754。分析表明相邻/相关类别之间的区分仍然困难。

Conclusion: 将NER与阿拉伯语预训练语言模型相结合是检测有害内容的一种有前景的方法；但细粒度相关类别的区分仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Detecting harmful content in Arabic is challenging because of dialectal variations and the semantic overlap between different harm categories. This paper presents a two-stage approach for classifying Arabic user prompts into seven categories of harmful content. First, GLiNER is used to extract harm-related entities from each prompt. The extracted entities are then added to the original text and passed to a fine-tuned MARBERT classifier for final classification. The approach combines explicit entity information with the contextual understanding of a pretrained Arabic language model. The system was evaluated on the development set of the ArGuard 2026 shared task. The results achieved a macro-F1 score of 0.886 and an accuracy of 0.897, demonstrating promising performance in fine-grained harmful content detection. The system reported an accuracy of 0.793 and Macro-F score of 0.735 on a blind test set for subtask 1. For subtask 2, it achieved an accuracy of 0.770 and Macro-F score of 0.754. The analysis also showed that distinguishing between related categories remains challenging. These findings suggest that integrating NER with Arabic language models is a promising approach to harmful content detection.

</details>


### [140] [What Survives Distillation? A Mechanistic Interpretability Study of GPT-2 and DistilGPT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37157)
*Nabeelah Faumi,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以线性CKA、logit lens、注意力模式分解、稀疏自编码器特征统计与激活修补为工具，系统比较GPT-2与其六层学生DistilGPT-2在IOI电路与Greater-Than电路上的机制差异，提出"压缩—崩塌二分法"，并论证电路可压缩性决定蒸馏下的机制存续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏能压缩大模型并保持总体指标，但深度缩减式蒸馏对内部机制（电路）的影响鲜有刻画；聚合指标可能掩盖电路完整性受损，因此需要机制层面的诊断。

Method: 对GPT-2与DistilGPT-2在IOI与GT两个典型电路上进行对照分析，采用线性CKA度量表示相似性、logit lens追踪层间预测演化、注意力模式分解、稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征统计，以及激活修补定位关键计算头。

Result: 呈现明显的压缩—崩塌二分：IOI电路完全机制崩塌（DistilGPT-2准确率35.2% vs 94.8%，name-mover头零保留）；GT电路则以87.7%的任务性能恢复存活，机制上表现为教师第9层比较头被主动重映射到学生第4层（平均Pearson r = 0.945）。

Conclusion: 电路的可压缩性决定其在蒸馏后的机制存续；聚合指标不足以作为电路完整性的诊断依据，需引入机制级评估。
