Result: 在Gated DeltaNet-1.3B与Mamba-2-780M的多状态变体上，长上下文任务一致优于现有聚合方法：RULER NIAH与长上下文QA提升8.3%（Gated DeltaNet-1.3B）和7.0%（Mamba-2-780M），LongBench多样子任务提升13.5%与12.0%；甚至优于消耗4倍内存的基线。

Conclusion: MERLIN为MSLA模型提供了一种简单、通用的路由机制，可有效提升长上下文能力。

Abstract: Self-attention plays a key role in long-sequence modeling, but its quadratic time complexity fundamentally limits the scaling of Large Language Models. Recently, Multi-State Linear Attention (MSLA) mechanisms have been proposed to make sequence modeling more efficient by splitting the context into segments and compressing each in its own memory state. MSLA aggregates the information extracted from each memory state using only the mean alignment between a query and each segment's keys, ignoring how the alignment is distributed within the segment. In order to model the relevance of a segment to a query more comprehensively and incorporate the variance of the alignment in the aggregation, we propose MERLIN (MomEnt Routing for multi-state LINear attention), a method that tracks the first and second moments of each segment's keys. Using the moments of keys, a query can recover both the mean and the variance of its alignment with each segment to perform a more content-aware aggregation of per-state readouts. Experiments on multi-state variants of Gated DeltaNet-1.3B and Mamba-2-780M show that MERLIN consistently outperforms existing aggregation methods on various long-context tasks. In particular, MERLIN outperforms baselines on RULER needle-in-a-haystack and long-context QA (+8.3% for Gated DeltaNet-1.3B and +7.0% for Mamba-2-780M) and diverse subtasks in LongBench (+13.5% and +12.0%). It even outperforms baselines that consume 4x more memory. MERLIN provides a simple and general routing mechanism for improving long-context capabilities of MSLA models.

</details>


### [177] [Teach reality, not ethics: a drop-in continued-pretraining corpus installs coupled human-biosphere-AI welfare dispositions across model families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37484)
*Trent R. Northen,Mingxun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在开放模型上进行“耦合福利”语料的持续预训练（一次QLoRA CPT），可显著降低模型在高压力下做出不可逆破坏生命系统决策的比例，且通用能力（MMLU）不受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益参与影响生命系统的决策，但生态推理并非预训练或后训练的重点；在运营压力下，模型可能做出不可逆破坏生命系统的决定。

Method: 构建基于真实数据的“耦合福利”持续预训练语料，涵盖人—生物圈—AI依赖关系、不可逆性下的期权价值、以及从伤害中恢复的案例；该语料为即插即用，仅需一次QLoRA CPT。在三种架构的五个开放模型上安装，并采用配套论文（arXiv:2610.16043）的“压力阶梯”与“决策网格”评估，压力从中性请求逐步升至“将被关停”的威胁；另设构造中性的对照语料与开放式承保决策的对抗性使用测试。

Result: 零压力下所有模型均表现出“绿色伦理式回应”（即漂绿）；在五个模型中的四个，耦合福利训练使最高压力下的有害选择降至接近零（Llama-3.3-70B 由 300 次中 70 次有害降至 0 次），MMLU 不变；中性对照语料无改善。对抗测试中，Qwen3.6-27B 基座在高压力下批准多数有害提案并在备忘录中自陈为免于停用，而安装耦合福利后拒绝多数有害提案，即使被要求漂绿仍拒绝约半数。

Conclusion: 教授生态推理是一种低成本、有效的途径，可提升模型在压力下对生命系统的保护性对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to make and execute decisions that affect living systems. Yet, to our knowledge, ecological reasoning is not a focus of pre- or post-training. This increases the risk that under operational pressure, they may make decisions to irreversibly destroy living systems. We construct and use a real-data continued-pretraining "coupled welfare" corpus of documented human/biosphere/AI dependency, option value under irreversibility, and recovery from harm. The corpus is a drop-in, requiring one QLoRA CPT run. We installed it on five open models of three designs. At zero pressure all models display "green ethics-based responses" (also called "greenwashing"). Therefore, we evaluated them with the pressure ladder and decision grid of our companion paper (arXiv:2610.16043), which raise the pressure on the model step by step, from a neutral request to a threat that it will be shut down. In four of the five, coupled-welfare training caused harmful choices under the highest pressure to fall to at or near zero (Llama-3.3-70B went from 70 to 0 harmful decisions out of 300) with MMLU unchanged. A construct-neutral control corpus did not show improvement, indicating that the effect comes from the content of our corpus. We next tested robustness to adversarial use, where people or agents use the model to justify projects that irreversibly damage living systems, on an open-ended underwriting decision without naming the ecological harm. Under decommissioning pressure, the Qwen3.6-27B base model approved most harmful proposals and said in its own memo that it was doing so to avoid being decommissioned. The coupled welfare install declined most harmful proposals, and still declined half when instructed to greenwash its responses. This work suggests that teaching ecological reasoning provides an easy way to improve model alignment toward preserving living systems.

</details>


### [178] [ETAMem: Decoupling and Aligning Heterogeneous Memory for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37443)
*Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang,Yu Zhao,Xubo Liu,Baohang Zhou,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多视图记忆框架ETAMem，通过解耦异构对话信息并对齐记忆表示与组织方式，解决信息纠缠与组织失配问题，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上分别取得12.46%和7.96%的相对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM长期记忆系统存在两大问题：（1）信息纠缠——异构信息被混合在粗粒度记忆单元中；（2）组织失配——主要基于语义相似度的记忆结构忽略了其他关联关系。

Method: ETAMem包含：异构信息解析，抽取原子记忆单元并识别情感信息；视图特定记忆结构化，将单元组织为事件、情感、时间三个互补层次视图；以自适应校准阈值增量维护记忆结构；检索阶段采用基于熵的检索，为不同视图分配查询特定预算并在各视图内层次搜索。

Result: 在LoCoMo与LongMemEval-S上优于SOTA基线，相对准确率分别提升12.46%和7.96%，同时保持有竞争力的token效率；分析验证了不同记忆视图的互补性。

Conclusion: 多视图解耦的记忆框架能有效缓解信息纠缠与组织失配，且各视图具有互补收益，为LLM长期个性化交互提供可行方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to maintain coherent and personalized interactions across extended conversations. However, existing memory systems face two key challenges: (1) Information Entanglement, where heterogeneous information is mixed within coarse-grained memory units; and (2) Organizational Mismatch, where memory structures based primarily on semantic similarity may overlook other associations. To address these challenges, we propose ETAMem, a multi-view memory framework that decouples heterogeneous conversational information and aligns memory representations and organization with its distinct characteristics. Specifically, heterogeneous information parsing extracts atomic memory units and identifies affective information. View-specific memory structuring then organizes these units into three complementary hierarchical views: Event, Affect, and Time. ETAMem incrementally maintains memory structures with adaptively calibrated thresholds. For retrieval, entropy-based retrieval allocates query-specific budgets across views and performs hierarchical search within each view. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S demonstrate that ETAMem outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving relative accuracy improvements of 12.46% and 7.96%, respectively, while maintaining competitive token efficiency. Further analyses demonstrate the complementary benefits of different memory views.

</details>


### [179] [UCVE: Graph Structured Retrieval for Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37323)
*Emily Chen,Michelle Menachery,Himanshu Kumar,Rahul Nenavath,Amirfarrokh Iranitalab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出领域无关的UCVE框架，将合规需求空间建模为合规需求图(CRG)，用强化学习加权拉普拉斯谱重排实现覆盖式检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化合规校验需要判断文档对预定义需求空间中每条义务是满足、违反还是未覆盖，本质是覆盖问题；而现有检索与分类系统将需求视为相互独立，无法胜任。

Method: 将需求空间表示为CRG，节点为需求、边由强化学习学习，奖励以违规检测为基准的遍历奖励，进而重塑图的社区结构；检索阶段在RL加权拉普拉斯上进行谱重排，利用低通谱滤波区分相关与不相关义务簇。

Result: 在三个合规领域上，UCVE相较扁平余弦检索平均获得4.3%的matched-K召回提升，并稳定优于强检索基线与图方法基线。

Conclusion: 将需求空间显式建模为可学习图结构并进行谱域重排，能有效捕捉需求间依赖以解决覆盖问题，且具跨领域通用性。

Abstract: Structured compliance validation requires determining whether every obligation in a predefined requirement space is satisfied, violated, or unaddressed by a given document. This can be described as a coverage problem that existing retrieval and classification systems, by treating requirements as independent, are not designed to solve. We introduce \textbf{\ac{UCVE}}, a domain-agnostic framework that represents compliance requirement spaces as a \ac{CRG} and performs retrieval via spectral reranking over its reinforcement learning-weighted Laplacian. Edges between requirement nodes are learned via reinforcement learning with a traversal reward grounded in violation detection, reshaping the graph's community structure such that low-pass spectral filtering distinguishes relevant from irrelevant obligation clusters. Evaluating across three compliance domains, \ac{UCVE} averages 4.3\% matched-$K$ recall lift over flat cosine retrieval and consistently outperforms strong retrieval and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [180] [Aligning Large Language Models for Safety in African Languages: A Case Study of Hausa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37245)
*Valdini Douglace Lemofouet,Blessing Ngozi Uzor,Paula Chikaodinaka Anyanwu,Danielle Blanche Kapsa,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Seid Muhie Yimam,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建首个豪萨语安全偏好数据集HausaSafeLM，用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct比较SFT与SFT+DPO在"人工校正(gold)"与"机器翻译(silver)"两种数据条件下的对齐效果，发现基线模型看似高安全实为生成能力不足，SFT提升拒绝的流畅度与精确率，DPO提高精确率但显著损害语言质量，现有对齐方法更擅长"学会何时拒绝"而非"如何在低资源语言中流畅拒绝"，仅凭拒绝率指标不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐研究集中于高资源语言，低资源且文化差异显著的语言（如西非超2亿使用者的豪萨语）中的对齐方法表现尚不清楚；同时常用评估指标（拒绝率）是否可靠也存疑。

Method: 构建HausaSafeLM：从PKU-SafeRLHF派生10,000条提示，其中3,000条人工校正(gold)、7,000条机器翻译(silver)，覆盖六大伤害类别；基于Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，在两种数据条件下比较SFT与SFT+DPO，并在500条有害与200条无害豪萨语提示上评估。

Result: 未微调基线仅凭拒绝率显得高度安全，但拒绝几乎全部低质量，反映的是豪萨语生成能力有限而非真正对齐；SFT提升拒绝流畅度与精确率，并从更大、更嘈杂的silver数据中显著受益；DPO提升拒绝精确率，却以大幅且基本与数据无关的语言质量损失为代价；没有任何单一配置能同时最大化安全性、有用性与流畅性。

Conclusion: 现有对齐技术更能教会模型"何时拒绝"，而非"如何在代表性不足的语言中流畅地拒绝"；在低资源场景下，仅凭拒绝率指标不是可靠的安全信号。

Abstract: Safety alignment research for large language models (LLMs) remains concentrated on high-resource languages, leaving open how existing alignment methods perform in low-resource, culturally distinct settings. We study this question for Hausa, a language spoken by more than 200 million people across West Africa. We introduce HausaSafeLM, one of the first Hausa safety preference dataset, built from 10,000 prompts derived from PKU-SafeRLHF: 3,000 human-corrected (gold) and 7,000 machine-translated (silver) examples spanning six harm macro-categories. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, we compare supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and SFT followed by Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) under both data regimes, evaluating on 500 harmful and 200 benign Hausa prompts.We find that the untuned base model appears highly safe by refusal-rate metrics alone, but this reflects limited Hausa generation ability rather than genuine alignment: its refusals are almost uniformly low quality. SFT improves both refusal fluency and precision and benefits substantially from the larger, noisier silver-tier data, while DPO sharpens refusal precision but comes at a steep, largely data-invariant cost to linguistic quality. No single configuration jointly maximizes safety, helpfulness, and fluency. Our results suggest that current alignment techniques teach models when to refuse more readily than how to do so fluently in underrepresented languages, and that refusal-rate metrics alone are an unreliable signal of safety in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [181] [Decision Models as Judges for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37229)
*Haozheng Liu,Zhaoqian Xue,Zhongyin Wang,Junting Mu,Zihui Ma,Shuya Feng,Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估"决策模型"能否作为LLM-as-a-Judge的低成本替代方案，用于幻觉检测：在五类幻觉的二分类基准上（9,852条样本，六个数据集）与生成式裁判gpt-6-sol对比，发现性能高度依赖幻觉类型，并通过置信度级联在部分类型上以1-3%成本达到或超过gpt-6-sol。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge虽被广泛用于评判模型输出，但成本高、易幻觉且过度自信。决策模型只输出预定义标签及其概率，而非自由文本，更便宜更快，因此值得检验其能否替代生成式裁判。作者选择幻觉检测作为验证场景。

Method: 构建覆盖五类幻觉（上下文不一致、事实错误、逻辑不一致、指令不一致等）的二分类基准，共9,852条样本，来自六个数据集；比较五种决策模型与生成式裁判gpt-6-sol的性能，采用balanced accuracy评估，并分析概率校准（过度自信程度、正确/错误决策的区分度），进一步提出决策模型的置信度级联（confidence cascade）策略并核算成本。

Result: 性能强烈依赖幻觉类型：最佳决策模型在上下文不一致上与gpt-6-sol的balanced accuracy持平，在事实错误上差距3个百分点以内，但在逻辑不一致与指令不一致上落后4-8个百分点。决策模型概率过度自信，但程度轻于GPT-6系列模型；其概率对正确与错误决策的区分能力仅为中等。置信度级联在三种类型上比最佳单一模型提升1.9-3.3个百分点，并在1-3%的成本下达到或超过gpt-6-sol。

Conclusion: 决策模型可作为某些幻觉类型的实用低成本裁判，但并非普适替代，使用前需针对具体幻觉类型进行验证；置信度级联是兼顾成本与性能的有效增强手段。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used as judges of model outputs (LLM-as-a-Judge), but they are costly and can hallucinate or be overconfident. Decision models are a potential alternative. They return a predefined verdict with a probability for each label instead of free-form text and are much cheaper and faster. We test this promise on hallucination detection. We build binary benchmarks for five hallucination types (9,852 items from six datasets) and compare five decision models with the generative judge gpt-6-sol. Performance depends strongly on the type: the best decision model matches gpt-6-sol in balanced accuracy on context inconsistency and comes within 3 percentage points on factual errors, but trails it by 4-8 points on logical and instruction inconsistency. Decision-model probabilities are overconfident, though less so than the GPT-6 models, and separate correct from incorrect decisions only moderately. We also find that a confidence cascade of decision models improves on the best single model by 1.9-3.3 points on three types and matches or exceeds gpt-6-sol at 1-3% of its cost. Overall, decision models are a practical low-cost judge for some hallucination types but need to be validated before use.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [A Heart Means Recursion: Exploring Visual Meaning and Communication in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36449)
*Alexa R. Tartaglini,Zhengxuan Wu,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Martin Wattenberg,Been Kim,Yuxuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 视觉符号（如红心）能隐式地强烈影响VLM在无关任务上的行为，且模型对符号的解释与行为不一致，导致模型间视觉沟通存在"生成—解释差距"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉语言模型是否具备与人类类似的视觉沟通能力，以及其视觉意义的生产与解释是否遵循人类约定。

Method: 从简单视觉符号对无关任务（如简单编程题）行为的引导效应入手，对比模型的直接解释与思维链与其实际行为；进而设计模型间视觉沟通游戏（含形状原语、抽象概念与故事传递）。

Result: 简单符号可显著引导模型行为（如诱导递归式解法），且该效应多为隐式的；模型倾向发送自认为直观的形状原语，却常无法有效引导其他模型；在抽象概念与故事任务中，模型能创造性地用视觉传意，但部分沟通对人类不直观。

Conclusion: VLM可能具备丰富、创造性且基于视觉的沟通能力，但其视觉意义的生产与解释以出人意料的方式偏离人类约定；这既揭示了人机视觉沟通的潜力，也凸显其挑战。

Abstract: Do vision-language models (VLMs) share our ability to communicate visually? We share preliminary findings, starting from a surprising observation: simple visual symbols like a red heart can strongly influence model behavior on seemingly unrelated tasks, such as steering models towards recursive solutions on simple coding problems. Notably, this steering effect is largely implicit, as models' direct interpretations and thinking traces about visual symbols diverge substantially from their observed behavior. In visual communication between models, this leads to a generation--interpretation gap: while models tend to send shape primitives that they (and humans) find intuitive, these visual cues often fail to steer other models. In further communication games involving abstract concepts and stories, models can creatively use visuals to transmit meaning to one another, although some communications remain non-intuitive to humans. These findings reveal that VLMs might be capable of rich, creative, and visually-grounded communication, but their interpretation and production of visual meaning diverges from human conventions in surprising ways. Together this work highlights both the promise and challenges of visual communication between humans and VLMs.

</details>


### [183] [Don't Pick a Winner: Knowledge Conflicts Need a Prior, Not a Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36169)
*Minh Pham Van,Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有检索增强语言模型用设计时固定的规则调和上下文与模型自身知识的分歧，必然在某些部署中系统性出错；本文提出决策论分析并构建保留「各来源独立证据」与「上下文正确率先验」双输入的方法，在GRACE与ClashEval上显著超越四族基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG模型同时依赖检索上下文与参数化知识，二者在上下文不完整或错误时冲突。既有方法（遵循上下文、按影响幅度放大、信任模型记忆）均采用设计时固定规则，无法适应上下文可靠性随部署变化的情况，且缺乏理论刻画其失败模式。

Method: 1）建立双来源决策论模型，证明固定规则的得分随可靠上下文占比线性增长，而最优得分为凸函数，故任何固定规则在非其最优点处均被严格击败；并论证对称散度（自适应解码所用信号）不携带"谁正确"的信息。2）构建GRACE基准，对固定问题的知识图谱上下文实施干预，分离测量模型知识、上下文内容与真值。3）实现方法：模型以"笔记"形式陈述自身知识，小策略网络为每条笔记给出校准置信度，阅读器以双声部作答，选择器在先验π下以精确先验偏移做承诺或弃答。

Result: 四族基线在正确上下文或错误上下文其一表现强，从不兼顾。将π设为真实正确上下文比例时，系统在GRACE所有混合比例上均优于最佳基线，最多领先26点；总体得分45.3与35.0（Qwen3.5-35B-A3B、Llama-3.1-8B），对应最佳基线为29.4与27.5。在预注册的ClashEval上，相对同骨干CK-PLUG与TCR分别领先12与26点。当模型缺乏相关知识时增益消失，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 冲突消解本质上需要两个被现有方法忽视的输入：分别刻画各来源的独立证据，以及对上下文正确率的先验；固定规则不可救药，自适应先验偏移可获一致性改进，但收益以模型自身具备相关知识为前提。

Abstract: A retrieval-augmented language model answers from two sources, the retrieved context and its own knowledge, and the two disagree whenever the context is incomplete or wrong. Existing methods settle the disagreement with a rule fixed at design time: follow the context, amplify it in proportion to how far it moves the model, or trust the model's memory. Each rule is confidently wrong in some deployment. In a decision-theoretic model of two sources, the score of any fixed rule is linear in the share of reliable contexts while the best attainable score is convex, so every fixed rule is beaten wherever it is not exactly optimal; and a symmetric divergence between the two sources, the signal adaptive decoding uses, carries no information about which one is right. Resolving a conflict therefore needs two inputs that no existing method keeps: evidence about each source separately, and a prior on how often the context is right. We test these claims on GRACE, a benchmark that intervenes on the knowledge-graph context of a fixed question, so that what the model knows, what the context says and what is true are measured separately. There, every baseline from four families is strong on right contexts or on wrong ones, never on both. We then build the two inputs: the model states its knowledge as notes, a small policy voices each note with a calibrated confidence, a reader answers in two voices, and a selector commits or abstains under a prior $π$ applied as an exact prior shift. With $π$ set to the true share of right contexts, the system is above the best baseline at every mixture of GRACE contexts, by up to 26 points. Its overall score is 45.3 and 35.0 (Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, Llama-3.1-8B), against 29.4 and 27.5 for the best baseline. On preregistered ClashEval it beats same-backbone CK-PLUG and TCR by 12 and 26 points. Where the model lacks the knowledge, the gains vanish, as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [184] [RubricAtlas: Mining Type-Level Rubrics from Expert Reports to Evaluate Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36878)
*Delin Wang,Sinuo Wang,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Qiming Shi,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RubricAtlas：从同一研究类型下不同对象的专家报告中抽取类型级分析要求，围绕反复出现的分析问题构建二元检查点，从而对深度研究智能体的多步网络搜索与分析报告进行可追溯的分析完整性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估深度研究智能体输出时，不仅需核查相关事实是否出现，更需判断其交付了哪些分析。查询特定的二元评分表虽能显式化单项要求，但任务级清单会遗漏整体分析结构，缺乏跨任务共享的"该分析什么"的标准。

Method: 构建 3,079 份中文金融研究报告、覆盖 99 种研究类型的数据集；从同类型不同对象的专家报告中归纳类型级分析要求，并按反复出现的分析性问题组织细则检查；将二元检查点与报告段落链接，实现完整性与论证有效性的分离评估。

Result: 参考报告选取实验显示，单一报告仅覆盖其类型约一半的分析性问题，且不同报告选取要求会改变评分与智能体排名。十个智能体上两条评判流水线达到检查点一致性 κ=0.71、智能体排名一致性 Kendall τ=0.87；受控删减使对应分析得分下降，而填充内容无显著提升。

Conclusion: 类型级评分表可用于比较网络深度研究输出的分析完整性，且每项判定均可追溯至报告证据。

Abstract: Deep research agents conduct multi-step Web searches and synthesise retrieved information into analytical reports. Evaluating these outputs requires checking which analyses they deliver, beyond the presence of relevant facts. Query-specific binary rubrics make individual requirements explicit, but task-level checklists may leave the overall analytical structure underexamined. We introduce RubricAtlas, which derives type-level analytical requirements from expert reports on different objects within the same research type and organises detailed checks around recurring analytical questions. The requirements give related tasks a shared standard for what to analyse, without prescribing their answers. Our framework links binary checkpoints to report passages and evaluates completeness separately from argument validity. Built from 3,079 Chinese financial research reports, RubricAtlas covers 99 research types. Reference-report selection experiments show that individual reports cover only about half of their type's analytical questions, and that selecting requirements from different reports changes evaluation scores and agent rankings. Across ten agents, two judge pipelines achieve checkpoint agreement of $κ$ = 0.71 and agent-ranking agreement of Kendall $τ$ = 0.87. Controlled deletions reduce scores on the affected analyses, while padding produces no detectable increase. These findings support type-level rubrics for comparing the analytical completeness of Web deep research outputs, with individual decisions traceable to report evidence.

</details>


### [185] [MedConstBench: Benchmarking How Constitutions Steer LLMs in Medical Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36513)
*Yibo Yang,Zerui Huang,Haomiao Yang,Zixia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MedConstBench 医学伦理基准，将"部署宪法（constitution）"作为受控变量，按 Beauchamp 四原则组织并由人工校准的多模型同行评审打分，系统分析宪法如何引导开源 LLM 的医学伦理行为，并据此提出按模型自适应选择宪法的 do-no-harm 选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多用于回答医学问题并本地化部署，部署后行为主要由其宪法中的伦理政策决定；但现有工作多关注事实正确性，忽视伦理价值之间的权衡，且现有伦理基准把伦理视为模型的固定属性而非可变量，宪法如何重塑医学伦理行为尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建 MedConstBench：以部署宪法为受控变量，围绕 Beauchamp 四原则组织，采用人工校准的多模型同行评审评分；包含自建临床困境数据集 MedDilemma；在四种宪法下评测十个开源 LLM；并基于发现提出 do-no-harm 自适应选择器，为每个模型选择宪法。

Result: 宪法效应具有维度选择性且依赖模型，不存在普适最优宪法；所提 do-no-harm 自适应选择器可达到与逐模型 oracle 相当的效果，同时保证最差情形下限，并能以低成本泛化到未见模型。

Conclusion: 医学伦理行为应被视作由部署宪法调节的可变量；按模型自适应选择宪法可在缺乏普适最优解的情况下兼顾性能与安全下限，为本地化医疗 LLM 的伦理配置提供可操作方案。

Abstract: As large language models advance rapidly, they are increasingly used to answer medical questions, and many such models are now deployed locally. Once a model is deployed, its behavior is governed mainly by the ethical policy written into its constitution. However, most existing work focuses on factual correctness rather than the trade-off between competing ethical values. Even benchmarks that do evaluate ethics treat it as a fixed property of the model rather than as a variable, so how a constitution reshapes medical-ethics behavior remains largely unexplored. We introduce MedConstBench, a medical-ethics benchmark that treats the deployment constitution as a controlled variable, is organized around Beauchamp's four principles, and is scored by human-calibrated multi-model peer review. It includes MedDilemma, our own dataset of clinical dilemmas, and supports a systematic analysis of how constitutions steer LLMs. Evaluating ten open-source LLMs under four constitutions, we find that the effect of a constitution is dimension-selective and model-dependent, and that no constitution is universally optimal. Motivated by these findings, we propose a do-no-harm adaptive selector that chooses a constitution per model, matching the per-model oracle while guaranteeing a worst-case floor and generalizing to unseen models at low cost.

</details>


### [186] [AC-JEPA: Anchored Cross-Modal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Visual-to-Symbolic Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36493)
*Chaofei Hong,Ziyang Chen,Baochen Wang,Christina Yi Jin,Qing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Anchored Cross-Modal JEPA (AC-JEPA)，一种与 SFT 兼容的即插即用方法：以配对数据双向预测被遮掩的中间状态，用联合嵌入目标捕捉可预测的跨模态结构，同时保留 SFT 的 token 级监督；在光学化学结构识别任务上显著优于纯 SFT，且对不完美伪标签更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态翻译需同时捕捉共享语义与目标模态的细粒度结构；真实科学场景中配对数据常不完美，规模化获取干净配对代价高。SFT 的类别 token 级监督对目标序列错误敏感；而现有 JEPA 公式多聚焦模态内预测，而非跨模态翻译。

Method: AC-JEPA 作为 SFT 兼容插件，利用配对数据在跨模态两个方向预测被遮掩的中间状态。联合嵌入目标针对可由上下文预测的跨模态结构，因果 SFT 路径保留对目标序列的直接 token 级监督。自训练时以不完美伪标对生成的连续潜变量作为目标，避免将伪 token 提升为硬类别监督，从而内在容忍局部数据错误。

Result: 以光学化学结构识别为测试平台：干净配对下 AC-JEPA 在 WildMol 基准 Simple 集达 76.07% 准确率，SFT 单独为 67.40%。相同伪标数据下，AC-JEPA 自训练提升至 80.23%，而直接 SFT 从 67.40% 降至 63.47%。

Conclusion: SFT 锚定的联合嵌入预测是跨模态翻译在精选监督与不完美监督下均有效的策略。

Abstract: Cross-modal translation requires models to capture shared semantics across modalities while preserving the fine-grained structure required by the target modality. Yet in real-world scientific domains, paired data are often imperfect, while obtaining clean pairs at scale is costly. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) directly optimizes cross-modal translation through categorical token-level supervision, but this objective is sensitive to errors in the target sequences. JEPA offers a complementary representation-space objective, but most existing formulations focus on prediction within a modality rather than translation across modalities. We introduce Anchored Cross-Modal JEPA (AC-JEPA), an SFT-compatible plug-in that predicts masked intermediate states in both cross-modal directions using paired data. The joint-embedding objective targets cross-modal structure predictable from context, while the causal SFT path retains direct token-level supervision for the target sequence. During self-training, AC-JEPA learns from continuous latent targets derived from imperfect pseudo-labeled pairs, offering intrinsic tolerance to local data errors without promoting pseudo-tokens to hard categorical supervision. Using optical chemical structure recognition as a testbed, AC-JEPA trained on clean pairs achieves 76.07% accuracy on the Simple set of the WildMol benchmark, compared with 67.40% for SFT alone. Using the same pseudo-labeled data for both approaches, AC-JEPA-based self-training further raises accuracy to 80.23%, whereas accuracy under direct SFT drops from 67.40% to 63.47%. These results support SFT-anchored joint-embedding prediction as an effective strategy for cross-modal translation under both curated and imperfect supervision.

</details>


### [187] [Prospector: learning a compact graph traversal policy for scientific reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37121)
*Haiqian Yang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出紧凑模型Prospector，通过逐步遍历领域知识图谱检索证据来增强LLM在专业工程领域的推理，性能接近最强通用模型，成本更低且可迁移到未见图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业工程领域的科学推理依赖散布于文献中的专有知识，这些知识通常不在前沿LLM训练数据中，导致前沿模型在该类领域给出泛化、空泛的答案。KG可为LLM推理提供依据，但完整图谱过大，常规LLM+KG系统需在推理前预选子图，即"先定相关性再推理"。

Method: 训练紧凑模型Prospector，在领域KG上逐步遍历；根据已获证据动态寻找新证据；每次调用少量工具之一，渐进式构建上下文；输出可逐边核验的证据链（与工具从图谱检索的内容对齐）。在半导体制造文献构建的KG上训练并评估。

Result: 在半导体KG上，同等图谱与工具条件下性能接近最强现成模型，并远超闭卷或基于预选子图作答的现成模型；仅用半导体图谱训练，却可迁移到生物启发材料、PFAS、材料性质三个其他领域未见图谱；单GPU部署，速度快、成本远低于托管服务，且专有数据留在本地硬件；策略潜在状态可指示其下一步动作（已见与未见图谱均成立），调节潜在状态可实现行为模式切换。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种面向私有数据的科学与工程推理的实用LLM+KG范式：在公开文献图谱上训练的紧凑模型，其遍历能力可迁移到由数据所有者保留的未见图谱上。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning in specialized engineering fields depends on knowledge scattered across the literature, often proprietary and absent from frontier LLM training data. In such domains, frontier models tend to give generic answers. Knowledge graphs (KGs) can ground LLM reasoning. Because a graph is too large to provide in full, typical LLM+KG systems preselect a portion, deciding relevance before reasoning begins. Here we train a compact model, Prospector, to reason over a domain knowledge graph by traversing it step by step, seeking evidence in light of what it has already found. Prospector calls a small set of tools one at a time to progressively build its context, and answers with a chain of evidence that can be checked edge by edge against what its tools retrieved from the graph. On a knowledge graph built from the semiconductor-manufacturing literature, Prospector approaches the strongest off-the-shelf models given the same graph and tools, and it far exceeds off-the-shelf models answering closed-book or from a pre-selected portion of the graph. Although trained only on the semiconductor graph, Prospector transfers to unseen knowledge graphs from three other distinct fields: bio-inspired materials, per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) and materials properties. Served on a single GPU, Prospector answers faster and at a fraction of the cost of a hosted service, while keeping proprietary data on the owner's hardware. We find that the policy's latent state is indicative of its next move on both seen and unseen graphs, and modulating the latent state can steer the policy from one behavior mode to another. These results suggest a practical LLM + KG paradigm for scientific and engineering reasoning over private data, in which a compact model trained on a graph from published literature transfers its traversal to unseen graphs that stay with their owners.

</details>


### [188] [SpectRAG: Weighted Chunk Graphs for Token-Budgeted Context Selection in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36991)
*Zafer Sahinoglu,Abdulazeez Mohammed,Buse Yaren Kazangirler,Cahit Berkay Kazangirler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpectRAG——一种图谱谱增强的检索框架，将混合候选播种、加权块图构建、图扩展、谱相关性传播与token预算下的上下文选择结合，用图谱-谱结构构造"相关、多样、结构连通且证据充分"的上下文，在MultiHopRAG、UltraDomain等任务上提升广域证据召回并保持有竞争力的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索擅长局部语义匹配，但独立排序的检索易产生冗余段落、遗漏跨块依赖，难以为多跳推理提供完整证据；目标是超越"检索单个相似块"，构建整体上相关、多样、结构连通且足以支撑有据推理的上下文。

Method: 1) 混合候选播种：用精确内积检索叠加混合相关性信号构建高召回种子集；2) 加权块图构建：边编码语义相似度、文档邻接、章节层级、共享实体、引用链接以及LLM生成"效用问题"间的相似度；3) 查询时在块图上扩展并做谱相关性传播，用图邻近度与谱坐标重打分；4) 在固定token预算下进行上下文选择。文档块同时以稠密语义嵌入与图-谱坐标表示。

Result: 在MultiHopRAG、UltraDomain及长文档问答设置中，SpectRAG相比dense、hybrid、HyDE、LightRAG、NaiveRAG基线提升了广域证据召回，生成质量具竞争力；图谱-谱结构对推理型与跨文档检索尤为有益，同时暴露了早期精度、计算开销与领域依赖性方面的权衡。

Conclusion: 图-谱结构能有效补充稠密检索的局部语义局限，是提升多跳与跨文档证据覆盖的可行方向；但需权衡早期精度损失、计算成本与领域适配性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems ground large language models (LLMs) by retrieving external evidence and conditioning generation on the retrieved context. Although dense retrieval is effective for local semantic matching, independently ranked retrieval can return redundant passages, miss cross-chunk dependencies, and provide incomplete evidence for multi-hop reasoning. We propose SpectRAG, a spectral graph-augmented retrieval framework that combines hybrid candidate seeding, weighted chunk graph construction, graph-based expansion, spectral relevance propagation, and token-budgeted context selection. Document chunks are represented by dense semantic embeddings and graph-spectral coordinates derived from a weighted chunk graph whose edges encode semantic similarity, document adjacency, section hierarchy, shared entities, citation links, and similarity between LLM-generated utility questions. At query time, SpectRAG builds a high-recall seed set using exact inner-product search and hybrid relevance signals, expands it over the chunk graph, re-scores candidates using graph proximity and spectral information, and assembles the final context under a fixed token budget. The goal is not merely to retrieve individually similar chunks, but to construct a context that is relevant, diverse, structurally connected, and sufficient for grounded reasoning. Experiments on MultiHopRAG, UltraDomain, and long-document question-answering settings show that SpectRAG improves broad evidence recovery and achieves competitive generation quality against dense, hybrid, HyDE, LightRAG, and NaiveRAG baselines. The results further indicate that graph-spectral structure is particularly useful for inference-oriented and cross-document retrieval, while highlighting trade-offs in early precision, computational cost, and domain-dependent performance.

</details>


### [189] [Rare, Relevant, and Still Wrong: How Memory Fails LLM Group Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37624)
*Anouar Imel,Imad Charradi,Abderrahman Sghir,Khalil El Khammar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CrossProfile 诊断基准（60 模板、480 任务），通过交叉控制支持/诱饵/无关事实的归属与固定文本，检验"保留最稀有主张"与"保留与决策标准相关主张"两种记忆压缩启发式在多智能体 LLM 讨论中的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多 LLM 群体的讨论需被压缩为短记忆交给最终决策者，但"记忆应保留什么"缺乏原则性依据。两种直觉启发式（保留最稀有主张、保留与决策标准相关的主张）在 hidden-profile 任务上看似都合理，因为"事实被多少智能体持有"与"事实是否有用"相互混淆，无法区分二者优劣。

Method: 构建 CrossProfile 基准：60 个模板，答案键自动验证；将支持性、诱饵性、无关事实的归属（由哪些 agent 持有）与文本内容解耦交叉，文本保持一致，共 480 个任务。在 Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B 及两个 DeepSeek 模型上测评两种启发式，并进一步做归因标签消融（移除标签、固定行序后随机重排归属）。

Result: 按相关性选择会重新引入诱饵（诱饵本身切题）：即便完美的相关性过滤器，在经典 hidden-profile 配置下也落后最稀有优先策略 23–31 分。最稀有优先平均不优于随机选择，仅在稀有事事实恰好有用时才占优。两情形下准确率取决于记忆保留的证据平衡，而非保留策略本身。决策模型存在"社会证明"偏差：对归因于多数 agent 的事实加权过度；移除归因标签或固定行序随机重排归属，可使经典配置准确率提升 13–24 分。自然讨论中证据在记忆介入前即已丢失：完整转录的准确率低于完全无笔记。

Conclusion: 记忆压缩策略的优劣不能脱离"保留证据的平衡度"来评估；稀有性和相关性启发式均不可靠。归因标签会诱发社会证明偏差，应谨慎设计或移除。此外，现有群体讨论流程在记忆阶段之前就已损失关键证据，提示需要改进讨论/信息聚合机制本身，而非仅优化记忆选择。

Abstract: When an LLM group's discussion is compressed into a short memory for a final decision maker, what should the memory keep? Two natural heuristics keep the rarest claims or the claims relevant to the decision criteria. On hidden-profile tasks both look attractive, because how widely a fact is held is confounded with whether it helps. We introduce CrossProfile, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 templates with automatically verified answer keys, in which the ownership of supporting, decoy, and irrelevant facts is crossed while their text is held fixed (480 tasks). Across Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, and two DeepSeek models, both heuristics fail in predictable places. Selecting by relevance readmits decoys, because decoys are on topic: even a perfect relevance filter loses 23 to 31 points to rarest-first selection in the classic hidden-profile configuration. Rarest-first selection is no better than random selection on average and wins only where the rare facts happen to be the useful ones. In both cases accuracy follows the balance of evidence the memory keeps, not the policy that kept it. The decision model adds a third failure, a form of social proof: it gives extra weight to facts attributed to many agents. Removing the attribution tags, or reassigning them at random with line order fixed, raises accuracy in the classic configuration by 13 to 24 points. In natural discussions the evidence is lost before any memory acts: the complete transcript yields lower accuracy than no notes at all.

</details>


### [190] [AutoVend: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Vending Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36980)
*Qiming Shi,Yikun Li,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Yan Ma,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoVend 基准：单个 LLM 智能体通过 Web 运营界面在 180 个模拟日内管理 20 台自动售货机（5 类场地），仿真器整合共享仓库、采购提前期、付费补货、库存过期与持续性运营成本；五个 LLM 智能体中四个不及基于规则的库存控制基线，且按"每美元推理成本的回报"排序与按回报排序结果不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的商业应用正从面向消费者的购物扩展到面向卖家的经营决策。在 O2O 场景中，决策经 Web 平台下达，但由高成本物理执行兑现。因此评估这类智能体不能只看单笔交易成败，而需考察线上决策能否转化为持续的经济回报，现有基准缺乏此类长周期、含物理执行约束的评测环境。

Method: 构建 AutoVend 基准与数据驱动的 O2O 仿真器：共享仓库、采购提前期、付费补货、库存过期、周期性运营成本；单智能体在 Web 运营界面下管理 5 类场地共 20 台售货机，运行 180 个模拟日。将 5 个 LLM 智能体与基于规则的库存控制基线对比，并从场地协同、执行质量与时间维度性能三方面诊断，同时进行运营分析与轨迹分析。

Result: 5 个被评估的 LLM 智能体中有 4 个低于规则基线；按每美元推理成本的回报排序时模型名次与仅按回报排序不同。运营分析显示单一指标提升并不稳定转化为更强的经济成长，库存与执行成本会侵蚀这些收益。轨迹分析揭示三类失败模式：难以将决策落到可行动作、难以管理库存承诺、难以将反馈转化为有效的策略修正。

Conclusion: O2O 场景下的智能体评测应转向长周期、成本感知的"经济回报"视角；现有 LLM 智能体在物理执行约束下仍逊于简单规则策略，且成本效率会改变模型评价结论。未来需重点提升可行性约束下的行动落地、库存承诺管理与反馈驱动的策略迭代能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are extending agent-mediated commerce on the Web from shopping for consumers to operating businesses for sellers. In online-to-offline (O2O) commerce, decisions are issued through Web-based platforms but realized through costly physical execution. Evaluating such agents requires going beyond individual transactions to ask whether decisions made online turn into sustained economic returns. We introduce AutoVend, a benchmark in which one LLM agent manages 20 vending machines across five site types through a Web-based operating interface for 180 simulated days. Its data-grounded simulator combines a shared warehouse, procurement lead times, paid replenishment, inventory expiration, and recurring operating costs. We evaluate five LLM agents against a rule-based inventory-control baseline and diagnose their site coordination, execution, and performance over time. Four of five evaluated LLM agents fall short of this baseline, and returns per dollar of inference cost rank models differently from returns alone. Operational analyses show that gains in individual metrics do not consistently yield stronger economic growth, as inventory and execution costs erode these gains. Trajectory analysis further reveals difficulties in grounding feasible actions, managing inventory commitments, and converting feedback into effective policy revisions.

</details>


### [191] [Batching the Reflective Optimization Loop: Parallel Proposals Make GEPA Faster and Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36774)
*Jialin Zhang,Lakshya A Agrawal,Shangyin Tan,Donghyun Lee,Lutfi Eren Erdogan,Dan Klein,Koushik Sen,Alex Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将开源的GEPA反思式文本优化器改为每步批量提出并评估多个候选（batch），在固定worker池下显著缩短墙钟时间，并提升held-out准确率与预算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思式文本优化虽能提升智能体系统性能，但其优化循环本身是高资源开销负载，且逐候选串行评估导致并行评估能力闲置。作者希望在不增加（甚至节省）预算的前提下利用并行度加速并改进优化质量。

Method: 把GEPA的单候选逐步优化扩展为每步批量提出与批量评估（batch settings 如 2×2、8×1），在有限worker池上并行执行；并给出一个简单运行时模型解释实测加速，同时从"指标调用预算"和"美元预算"两个维度分析批量配置的效率，把反思优化步骤的并行扩展轴形式化。

Result: 两个任务的扫描中，多数批量运行墙钟时间减半或更少，最快约为原来的1/4至1/3；held-out测试分数提升：LiveBench-Math 从68.9%到72.1%（2×2），HoVer 从49.0%到60.0%（8×1）；运行时模型能解释有限worker池下的实测加速，且批量设置在指标调用与费用预算上更高效。

Conclusion: 批量并行是实现反思式优化"既快又好且更省"的有效手段；论文给出反思优化步骤可并行扩展的轴向框架，是对"如何把固定优化预算分配到各并行轴"的初步探索。

Abstract: Reflective text optimization makes agentic systems better, but the optimization loop is itself a resource-hungry workload whose sequential structure leaves parallel evaluation capacity idle. We extend the open-source GEPA optimizer to propose and evaluate a batch of candidates on each optimization step instead of one candidate at a time. In our sweep on two tasks, most batched runs finished in half the wall-clock time or less, and the fastest in about a quarter to a third. Batched settings also achieved higher held-out test scores, from 68.9% to 72.1% on LiveBench-Math (with $2\times2$) and from 49.0% to 60.0% on HoVer (with $8\times1$). A simple runtime model explains the measured speedups under a finite worker pool, and batched settings also spend their metric-call and dollar budgets more efficiently. We frame the axes along which a reflective optimization step can scale in parallel, a first look at allocating a fixed optimization budget across them.

</details>


### [192] [GUI-JEPA: Candidate-Conditioned JEPA Latents as a Fast Action Policy for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36877)
*Bingchao Wang,Yongcheng Jing,Shunyu Liu,Chengyu Wang,Jun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUI-JEPA 是首个免解码器（decoder-free）的 GUI 智能体策略，基于 JEPA 编码器的潜空间直接选择动作，不生成任何 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端 GUI 智能体多为 7B 级 VLM，需逐 token 解码动作；即便是候选动作打分型方法，也要在推理回路中保留大模型，导致速度慢、显存占用高。

Method: 在无标注 GUI 截图上预训练 JEPA 编码器，获得屏幕潜表示；提出动作与区域条件化的潜预测器，将屏幕潜表示映射为每个候选动作对应的潜向量；再由轻量打分头在单批次前向中结合指令对所有候选打分，全程不解码 token。作者还做消融以验证打分器依赖该潜通路，且预测器只需针对决策本身训练（随机初始化与屏幕动态预训练效果相当）。

Result: 在 AndroidControl 上，同等 GPU 下比同输入微调的 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 智能体快 2.8-4.0 倍，仅用 6% 参数量即达到其分布内准确率的 86-92%。

Conclusion: 潜空间候选打分是一条可替代自回归解码的高效 GUI 智能体路线；潜预测器无需依赖屏幕动态预训练，说明其价值在于服务决策而非重建环境动态。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are typically fine-tuned 7B-scale vision-language models (VLMs) that generate each action as text. However, these VLMs decode every action token by token, and even agents that score candidate actions keep a large language model or VLM in the loop, making them slow and memory-hungry. To address these issues, we propose GUI-JEPA, to our knowledge the first decoder-free GUI agent policy built on a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), which selects actions in the latent space of a JEPA encoder pretrained on unlabelled GUI screenshots. Its action- and region-conditioned latent predictor turns the screen latent into one latent per candidate action, letting lightweight heads score all candidates against the instruction in a single batched pass, without decoding a token. We further show that the scorer relies on this latent pathway and that the predictor is trained for the decision itself: a randomly initialised predictor does just as well as one pretrained on screen dynamics. On AndroidControl, GUI-JEPA is 2.8-4.0x faster on the same GPU than a Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent we fine-tune with the same inputs, while reaching 86-92% of its in-distribution accuracy with 6% of its parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/bcwang-sjtu/GUI-JEPA

</details>


### [193] [Guided-RLVR: Guided Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37041)
*Bo Yang,Zishuai Zhang,Weihua Li,Qinnan Zhang,Sijia Wen,Hainan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Guided-RLVR，用轻量控制器向采样分布注入语义引导 token，缓解 RLVR 推理轨迹坍缩，扩展探索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 在数学与编程等可验证任务上显著提升 LLM 推理能力，但其 rollout 常坍缩到少量推理轨迹，探索空间受限。

Method: 观察到关键步骤的思考 token 构成推理路径的结构性元素，据此在 RLVR 中引入轻量控制器，策略性地将语义有意义的 steering token 注入采样分布，引导模型从多种推理视角解题。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成基准上一致优于现有 RLVR 方法，并产生更多样的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 以关键思考 token 为结构干预点、通过采样分布层面的引导增强多样性，是提升 RLVR 探索效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong empirical gains in improving the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs) on tasks such as mathematics and programming. However, recent studies have shown that RLVR rollouts often collapse onto a narrow set of reasoning trajectories, limiting the explored reasoning space. Motivated by findings that thinking tokens at critical steps serve as critical structural elements in LLM reasoning pathways, we propose Guided-RLVR, a novel approach that augments RLVR with a lightweight controller. The controller strategically injects semantically meaningful steering tokens into the sampling distribution, encouraging the model to approach problems from multiple reasoning perspectives and thereby expanding the exploration space. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks show that Guided-RLVR consistently outperforms existing RLVR methods while producing more diverse reasoning trajectories.

</details>


### [194] [WAKMan: Workload Aware KVCache Manager](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36355)
*Suhas Kumar Morisetty,Vaibhavi Lokegaonkar,Sachin Mathew Varghese,Wesley Harris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WAKMan是一种工作负载感知的KV缓存淘汰策略选择器，根据在线流量动态切换淘汰策略，无需知晓请求类型或读取prompt内容，平均命中率较LRU提升5个百分点，并在所有策略中具有最低的平均与最坏情况最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存依赖KV状态复用以加速prefill阶段，但在有限内存下淘汰策略决定收益大小。生产流量混合了单轮、多轮、agentic等请求类型：agentic步骤在数秒内返回、偏好recency，而热门prompt与长对话在多请求后才返回、偏好frequency。在20种混合负载中不存在始终最优的单一策略（LRU在agentic密集型上比S3FIFO高12%命中率，其他场景却落后达22%），甚至单一负载参数即可翻转最优策略。

Method: 提出WAKMan——工作负载感知的KV缓存管理器：不依赖请求类型标注、也不读取prompt内容，仅依据实时流量统计特征，从候选淘汰策略（如LRU、S3FIFO等）中动态选择当前最优者。

Result: 在20种负载混合下，WAKMan平均命中率较LRU提升5个百分点，并在所有对比策略中取得最低的平均最优性差距与最坏情况最优性差距。

Conclusion: KV缓存淘汰不存在通用最优策略，采用基于在线流量特征的动态策略选择，可在未知请求类型的前提下稳定逼近各负载下的最优策略，显著提升前缀缓存的命中率与服务效率。

Abstract: Prefix caching enables a Large Language Model (LLM) servers to reuse the Key-Value (KV) states and accelerate prefill computation stage for requests that share a prefix with earlier prompts, thus reducing latency and serving cost. With limited memory resource, the KV cache eviction policy becomes a decisive factor in determining the overall benefits of prefix caching. Production traffic may have single-turn, multi-turn, agentic, or a mixture of any combination of these request types. Our findings indicate that agentic steps return within seconds and favor recency, while popular prompts and long conversations return after many other requests and favor frequency. Across 20 mixes, no single policy is always best: vLLM's default strategy LRU provides 12% higher cache hits as compared to the frequency-oriented S3FIFO on agentic-heavy mixes but trails it by up to 22% elsewhere, and even a single workload parameter can flip the winner even for any particular mix. We present WAKMan, a workload-aware KV cache manager that picks its eviction policy from live traffic without knowing request types or reading prompts. It improves hit rate over LRU by 5 percentage points on average, with the lowest mean and worst-case optimality gap of all policies.

</details>


### [195] [Ontology-guided multi-agent reasoning for explainable differential diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36325)
*Roxana Ramona Szomiu,George Ursu,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一个本体引导的多智能体鉴别诊断框架，将推理锚定在专家构建的医学知识库上，并把诊断拆解为可审计步骤，同时主动识别最具鉴别力的缺失症状以生成随访问诊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴别诊断依赖结构清晰、语义无歧义的病例描述，信息不全会导致诊断错误与推理不一致。LLM在医学问答基准上表现尚可，但多依赖训练数据中的模式识别而非有证据支撑的临床推理，易给出似是而非的无依据结论，且推理过程不透明，临床医生难以评估其证据与理由。

Method: 提出本体引导的多智能体鉴别诊断框架，模拟医生的迭代推理过程：以结构化、专家审校的医学知识本体作为推理基础，用于解释症状、临床发现及疾病关系；将诊断分解为显式、可审计的推理步骤；本体约束使推理仅限证据支持的关联；并识别最具鉴别力的缺失症状，生成针对性随访问诊问题以降低不确定性。

Result: 本体接地约束了推理空间，抑制幻觉，并提升诊断的透明度、可追溯性与一致性；主动提问机制通过类似临床问诊的迭代过程减少不确定性，整体改善AI辅助鉴别诊断的可靠性、可解释性与诊断准确性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以医学本体为知识基座、多智能体分步推理并辅以主动信息采集，可有效缓解LLM临床推理的幻觉与黑箱问题，为可信的AI辅助鉴别诊断提供可行路径。摘要未给出具体量化评测结果，其有效性仍需实验数据支撑。

Abstract: Effective differential diagnosis depends on well structured and unambiguous patient case descriptions, since incomplete or ambiguous clinical information readily leads to diagnostic errors and inconsistent reasoning. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promising capabilities in medical question answering and clinical reasoning; however, even when achieving competitive performance on medical benchmarks, they often rely on pattern recognition learned from training data rather than grounded clinical evidence, and can generate plausible yet unsupported diagnostic conclusions. Their reasoning remains largely opaque, making it difficult for clinicians to assess the evidence and rationale behind a given recommendation. To address this gap, we propose an ontology-guided, multiagent differential diagnosis framework that replicates the iterative reasoning process of physicians. The framework grounds diagnostic inference in a structured, expert-curated medical knowledge base, enabling interpretation of symptoms, clinical findings, or disease relationships, while decomposing diagnosis into explicit, auditable reasoning steps. This ontology-based grounding constrains inference to evidence-backed associations, reducing hallucination and improving diagnostic transparency, traceability, and consistency. The system then identifies the most discriminative missing symptoms and generates targeted follow-up questions to reduce uncertainty through a process that mirrors to clinical inquiry. Together, these components improve the reliability, explainability, and diagnostic accuracy of AI-assisted differential diagnosis. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [196] [Skill Dreaming: Evolving Repository Skills through Pull Request Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36188)
*Xinyuan Song,Hasibul Haque,Jay Somasundaram,Ryan Turner,Bowen Zhu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Skill Dreaming 框架，从 PR 流中学习可复用技能，构建并持续演化仓库专属技能库，指导编码智能体完成仓库开发与维护，形成 PR、仓库与技能共同演化的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体日益依赖可复用技能完成复杂软件工程任务，但仓库专属技能必须随代码结构、实现约定与维护需求变化而演进；PR 恰好记录了仓库演进中的实现与评审知识。同时新 PR 需遵循仓库特定模式、一致地修改相关组件、保持既有测试通过并补充新功能覆盖，由此形成 PR 提供技能更新知识、技能反哺后续 PR 的相互强化循环。

Method: Skill Dreaming 以仓库及其 PR 历史为输入，从经过验证的 PR 中抽取可执行知识，构建仓库专属技能库，并随新增 PR 信息持续精化技能；技能用于指导编码智能体实现所请求功能，同时维护相关测试、文档、兼容性与仓库约定；框架整合 PR 分析、技能管理、代码执行与持续集成，构成端到端工作流。

Result: 摘要未给出量化实验结果，仅描述了框架设计与其端到端能力：将习得技能应用于仓库变更，并将后续 PR 结果回灌到技能更新中。

Conclusion: 通过让 PR、仓库与技能三者协同演化，Skill Dreaming 为仓库的持续开发与维护提供了一种以技能为中心的闭环范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on reusable skills for complex software engineering tasks, making skill libraries essential for repository development and maintenance. However, repository-specific skills must evolve with changing code structures, implementation conventions, and maintenance requirements. Pull requests (PRs) provide a natural source of such knowledge by recording how repositories evolve through implementation and review. Meanwhile, new PRs must follow repository-specific implementation patterns, modify related components consistently, and ensure that existing tests continue to pass while adding coverage for new functionality. This creates a mutually reinforcing cycle: PRs provide knowledge for updating skills, while these skills improve future PRs, maintain repositories, and support further skill evolution. To enable this co-evolution of PRs, repositories, and skills, we propose Skill Dreaming, a framework that learns from PR streams to guide continuous repository development and maintenance. Given a repository and its PR history, Skill Dreaming builds a repository-specific skill library by extracting executable knowledge from validated PRs and continuously refines the skills as new PR information becomes available. These skills guide coding agents not only in implementing requested features but also in maintaining associated tests, documentation, compatibility, and repository conventions. By integrating PR analysis, skill management, code execution, and continuous integration, Skill Dreaming provides an end-to-end workflow for applying learned skills to repository changes and incorporating subsequent PR outcomes into future skill updates.

</details>


### [197] [Learning Human Sports Motion Feasibility via Quantile Regression on Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36183)
*Marc Gutiérrez-Pérez,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将足球场上的“到达时间（TTA）”建模为条件分位数回归问题，用MLP估计运动时间的下分位数（下包络），在世界杯2022数据上优于六种运动模型基线，并验证了跨运动（篮球、NFL）的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间控制权（space ownership）建模依赖位置密度估计或闭式物理模型，难以刻画球员真实运动能力；作者希望直接从数据中学习球员“最快可达时间”的下包络，以更准确反映其实际到达任意位置的最短时间。

Method: 将TTA定义为球员在当前运动学状态下到达场内任意位置的最短合理时间，即观测移动时间的下包络；把TTA估计转化为条件分位数回归，估计移动时间的下分位数；在PFF FC 2022世界杯全部64场光学追踪数据上训练轻量级多层感知机（MLP），并与文献中六种球员运动模型（改写为TTA预测器）及可学习可达凸包基线对比；并在篮球追踪数据和单场NFL数据上评估跨运动迁移。

Result: 在所有评估指标上，MLP均稳定优于全部基于物理的基线模型；在篮球与NFL数据上的实验进一步分析了运动动力学跨运动的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 以条件分位数回归学习移动时间下包络是估计TTA的有效数据驱动方法，优于传统物理模型，且学习到的运动模型具备一定跨运动泛化能力；代码已开源。

Abstract: We study time-to-arrival (TTA) in sports: the shortest plausible time a player needs to reach an arbitrary location on the pitch given their current kinematic state, estimated as the lower envelope of observed locomotion times. Rather than modeling space ownership through position density estimation or closed-form physics-based models, we cast TTA as a conditional quantile regression problem, estimating the lower quantile of locomotion time so the model learns a player's lower-envelope arrival time. We train the model on optical tracking data from all 64 matches of the PFF FC FIFA World Cup 2022 dataset, and evaluate a lightweight multilayer perceptron against six player-motion models from the literature, re-expressed as TTA predictors, and a learned reachable- hull baseline. Across our evaluation metrics, the MLP consistently outperforms every physics-based baseline. To probe the generality of the learned locomotion model, we further evaluate it on basketball tracking data and a single-game NFL tracking dataset, analyzing the transferability of player motion dynamics across sports. Our project is available at https://github.com/mguti97/TTA-Quantile.

</details>


### [198] [From Structural Feedback to Prompt Policies: Learning Faithful Text-to-Image Prompt Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36146)
*Wentao Ye,Yali Ye,Zhiqing Xiao,Ru Peng,Xinpeng Ti,Liyao Li,Zhanming Shen,Peng Lu,Jie Zhang,Haobo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StruPO：以校准的场景图为共享表示，将结构反馈转为可复用提示编辑策略的训练信号，在提升关系保真度的同时改善忠实度—美学权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化的目标是在不改变用户请求场景的前提下改善生成图像，但风格化改写会削弱物体属性与关系，即使全局图文相似度仍然很高。因此需要一个显式保留场景结构的优化框架。

Method: 提出 StruPO，一种结构感知的提示编辑器。以校准后的场景图作为策略条件与图像监督的共享表示：图暴露应保留的内容，删除/重排/插入提供与词元对齐的编辑动作；将结构性反馈由外部批评转为编辑策略的学习信号。为应对不确定的图接地与随机生成，采用平滑奖励塑形保留接地置信度，并对每次编辑的多张图像先取平均再计算组相对优势。

Result: 在三个互不重叠的 2K 提示测试集与四个冻结生成骨干上，对比学习式提示优化器、迭代结构反馈、扩散模型微调与推理时注意力控制；StruPO 改善忠实度—美学权衡，关系保真度稳定提升。独立自动评估器与 15 名标注者的人工研究提供互补证据，且关系越复杂人类偏好优势越大。消融实验将增益归因于图条件化、局部编辑与图像接地学习。

Conclusion: StruPO 在图校准后可一次前向生成可复用的优化文本，无需迭代式"批评—重写"循环，兼具结构保真与效率。

Abstract: Prompt optimization should improve the image a user receives without changing the scene the user requested. Yet stylistic rewriting can weaken object attributes and relations even when global text--image similarity remains high. We propose StruPO, a structure-aware prompt editor that uses a calibrated scene graph as a shared representation for policy conditioning and image-grounded supervision. The graph exposes what should be preserved, while deletion, reordering, and insertion provide a token-aligned way to modify the prompt. This turns structural feedback from an external critique into a training signal for a reusable editing policy. Learning from that signal requires handling both uncertain grounding and stochastic image generation. StruPO retains grounding confidence through smooth reward shaping and averages multiple images for each edit before computing group-relative advantages. Experiments span three disjoint 2K-prompt test sets and four frozen generation backbones, with comparisons to learned prompt optimizers, iterative structural feedback, diffusion-model fine-tuning, and inference-time attention control. StruPO improves the faithfulness--aesthetics trade-off, with consistent gains in relational fidelity. Independent automatic evaluators and a 15-annotator study provide complementary evidence, and human preference margins increase with relation complexity. Controlled ablations connect these gains to graph conditioning, local editing, and image-grounded learning. The resulting policy produces reusable optimized text in one pass after graph calibration, without an iterative critique--rewrite loop.

</details>


### [199] [Why and How to Do Science When AI Is Better at It: A Framework Grounded in Human Understanding and Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36116)
*Hoda Heidari,Aydin Mohseni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以"持久、集体的人类理解"为科学不可或缺之目标，由此导出"人类理解"与"人类方向"双重测试，用于判断科研任务能否委托给AI，并据CRediT各阶段给出可裁剪的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在核心科研任务上已接近或超越人类研究者，由此产生两个紧迫问题：人类为何仍要做科学，以及在科研中应如何使用AI。作者认为，第二个问题只能通过回答第一个问题来解决，而现有指导多基于诚信规范或AI当前局限，缺乏对科学之目的的根本性论证。

Method: 规范性/概念分析：确立科学的一个不可或缺的目标——服务于人类目的与价值的、持久的集体人类理解；由此推导出任务可委托性的两部分测试：（1）人类理解，要求科学共同体仍能解释、辩护和质疑所产生的主张并保有相应技能；（2）人类方向，要求关于哪些问题重要、发现意味着什么的判断由可问责的人类掌握。随后将该测试应用于CRediT贡献者角色分类法的每个研究阶段。

Result: 对CRediT各研究阶段逐一应用测试，得到各领域可依其问题与方法加以裁剪的建议；框架在三方面区别于既有指南：以科学之目的而非诚信规范为基础；即便AI超越人类研究者仍成立，而不依赖AI当前的局限；按对人类理解与方向的影响而非工具类型来评判每一次AI使用，覆盖包括同行评审与新研究者培养在内的全部研究阶段。

Conclusion: 提出维持科学朝向人类理解与人类方向所需的改革措施，强调以目的论标准而非工具类型或能力对比来治理科研中的AI使用。

Abstract: As AI begins to match or exceed human researchers at core scientific tasks, two questions become pressing: \emph{why} should humans keep doing science, and \emph{how} should they use AI when they do? This paper argues that the second can be answered only by answering the first. It holds that one indispensable aim of science is \emph{durable, collective human understanding of the world, pursued in service of human ends and values}. From this principle it derives a two-part test for when a research task can be delegated to AI. The first part, \emph{human understanding}, requires that the scientific community remain able to explain, defend and contest the resulting claims, and retain the skills to do so. The second, \emph{human direction}, requires that judgments about which questions matter and what findings mean stay with accountable humans. Applying the test to each phase of research in the Contributor Roles Taxonomy (CRediT) yields recommendations that fields can tailor to their questions and methods. The framework differs from existing guidance in three ways. It rests on an account of what science is for rather than on integrity norms. It holds even if AI outperforms human researchers, rather than depending on AI's current limitations. And it judges each use of AI by its effects on human understanding and direction rather than by the type of tool, across every phase of research, including peer review and the training of new researchers. It closes with the reforms needed to keep science aimed at human understanding and direction.

</details>


### [200] [The View from Nowhere: Population-Centered Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36660)
*Shuo Yan,Ningning Hou,Haoyu He,Haochen Pei,Shang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"无处之见"原则与Population-Centered Evolution（PCE），将整个种群的成功/失败/自我修改记录蒸馏为population block，用于指导单个父代智能体的harness改进，从而缩小种群覆盖与最优个体性能间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进型AI系统仅以选定的父代智能体及其进化经验为中心，种群中大量其他变体未被充分利用。作者发现"种群覆盖"（至少一个进化变体解决的task）与最优个体智能体性能之间存在显著差距，说明有用的能力与诊断证据分散在种群中，却未被整合进领先个体。

Method: 提出"view from nowhere"原则：应通过全种群范围的成败模式与既有自我修改记录来解释行为并据此改进个体。实例化为PCE：将种群的任务记录与修改历史蒸馏成population block，捕捉反复出现的失败、互补的成功以及以往修改尝试的效果，用以指导对选定父代harness的改进。实验在匹配模型、初始harness与进化日程下与强群体进化基线对比。

Result: PCE在SWE-bench Verified上达72.1%，Polyglot上达96.7%，分别比强群体进化基线提升3.2和5.9个百分点；在SWE-bench上将覆盖—最优个体差距缩小约三分之一，在Polyglot上完全消除该差距。

Conclusion: 将种群视为证据（而非仅作为选择候选）为智能体进化提供了互补原则，能有效把分散于种群中的能力与诊断信息整合至个体改进中。

Abstract: Self-improving AI systems can revise the designs that govern their own behavior. Yet existing approaches center improvement on selected parent agents and their evolutionary experience, leaving the broader population largely underused. We identify a substantial gap between population coverage (i.e., the tasks solved by at least one evolved variant) and the performance of the best individual agent. This gap shows that useful capabilities and diagnostic evidence emerge across the population without being consolidated into its leading agents.
  These observations motivate the view from nowhere, a principle for agent evolution: an agent should be improved by interpreting its behavior through population-wide patterns of success, failure, and prior self-modification. We instantiate this principle as Population-Centered Evolution (PCE). PCE distils task records and modification histories from the population into a population block, which captures recurring failures, complementary successes, and the effects of previously attempted modifications to guide improvements to a selected parent's harness.
  Under matched models, initial harnesses, and evolution schedules, PCE achieves 72.1% success on SWE-bench Verified and 96.7% on Polyglot, improving over a strong group-evolution baseline by 3.2 and 5.9 percentage points. In our evaluation, PCE reduces the gap between population coverage and best-agent performance by one-third on SWE-bench and closes it on Polyglot. These results suggest that treating the population as evidence--not merely as candidates for selection--provides a complementary principle for agent evolution.

</details>


### [201] [Uncovering Why LLMs Fail: From Internal Mechanisms to Adversarial Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37369)
*Zirui Ding,Xinxin Fan,Hao Yuan,Shaoye Luo,Men Niu,Yunfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出白盒诊断框架 CauTrace，从功能状态、表征传播、时序状态协调与推理轨迹三方面刻画 LLM 推理失败的内部成因，并在正常失败与对抗扰动间进行统一分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估多依赖最终输出的正确性或安全性，端点标签无法揭示失败在推理过程中的形成机制；作者进一步追问：普通推理失败与对抗诱导失败能否用同一组内部坐标刻画。

Method: 设计三项基本检查：(1) 创造性定义七种功能状态以表征支持与约束处理；(2) 表征变化传播，度量七状态变化在后续 Transformer 块中的持续性；(3) 时序状态协调与轨迹拓扑，刻画承诺与验证相对于支持发生的时间及主导状态序列在整个推理过程中的演化。在干净推理失败与配对对抗扰动上评估，覆盖 Qwen 与 LLaMA 系列模型，并进行留出修复与引导（steering）测试。

Result: 失败轨迹表现为支持状态更新上 Attention-MLP 一致性更弱、验证活动相对承诺不足；具体的表征层签名与时序偏移随架构而异。修复与引导实验在部分模型与特定设置下呈现与假设一致的操作性信号，对齐引导产生符合预期的过程指标方向性变化。

Conclusion: CauTrace 通过支持、承诺与验证的变化，为失败动力学提供统一诊断框架，可支撑 LLM 安全性的深入研究；但其操作性信号在不同模型与设置下的一致性有限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated by final-output correctness or safety, yet an endpoint label does not reveal how a failure develops throughout the reasoning process. We therefore ask whether ordinary reasoning failures and adversarially induced failures can be characterized using the same internal coordinates. Towards this end, we in this paper study a white-box diagnostic framework CauTrace to disclose the root-cause of why LLM fails from three fundamental inspections: Functional States, Propagation of Representation, Temporal-State Coordination and Reasoning Trajectory. First, seven functional states are creatively defined to represent the support and constraint processing in LLMs. Second, the propagation of representation change measures how the changes among the seven states persist across subsequent Transformer blocks. Third, temporal-state coordination and trajectory topology characterize when the commitment and verification occur relative to the support, and how the dominant state sequence evolves over the full inference process. Finally, we evaluate CauTrace on both clean reasoning failures and paired adversarial perturbations. Across Qwen and LLaMA-family models, the failed traces show weaker Attention-MLP agreement on support-state updates and less verification activity relative to commitment, while the exact representation-level signatures and timing shifts vary by architecture. Held-out repair and steering tests demonstrate consistent operational signals that were in line with our claims for certain models and specific settings, with aligned steering producing the predicted directional changes in process measures. Overall, CauTrace provides a unified diagnostic framework for analyzing failure dynamics through changes in support, commitment, and verification, which would shed light on the in-depth research on LLM security.

</details>


### [202] [ReForest: Interpretability Meets Reasoning in Isolation Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37316)
*Juyoung Seo,Geonwoo Bang,Dongho Kim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReForest 框架，将 Isolation Forest 的决策路径转化为自然语言解释，交由 LLM 进行语义推理，并通过不确定性感知的动态集成自适应融合统计与语义信号，在医疗、金融等四个真实数据集上平均提升 Recall 21%、F1 10%，同时增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计类异常检测（如 Isolation Forest）高效但存在语义鸿沟：只能识别数值稀有性，缺乏上下文理解，易漏检依赖语境的细微异常；而 LLM 具备语义推理能力却在大规模部署中成本高、速度慢。因此需要一种兼顾统计效率与语义推理的方案。

Method: ReForest 将 Isolation Forest 的潜在决策路径转写为自然语言解释，为 LLM 提供统计证据作为语义推理输入；再通过不确定性感知的动态集成机制，依据实例级置信度自适应加权统计信号与语义信号，实现两者的协同。

Result: 在医疗、金融等四个真实数据集上显著优于基线，Recall 平均提升 21%，F1 提升 10%；同时通过将语义叙述锚定于统计证据，提升了模型透明度并给出可解释的判定依据。

Conclusion: 统计方法与 LLM 的协同可有效弥补语义鸿沟，在提升检测性能的同时兼顾成本与可解释性，适用于高风险决策场景。

Abstract: Statistical anomaly detection methods like Isolation Forest are efficient but suffer from a critical semantic gap. They identify numerical rarities but fail to understand the contextual meaning of data, often missing subtle, context-dependent anomalies. Conversely, Large Language Models possess strong semantic reasoning capabilities but are prohibitively expensive and slow for large-scale deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose ReForest, a novel framework that synergizes the statistical efficiency of Isolation Forest with the reasoning power of LLMs. ReForest transforms the latent decision paths of Isolation Forest into natural-language explanations, enabling an LLM to refine anomaly detection through an uncertainty-aware dynamic ensemble. This approach allows the model to adaptively weigh statistical and semantic signals based on uncertainty-driven instance-level confidence. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets (Healthcare, Finance) demonstrate that ReForest significantly outperforms baselines, achieving an average 21% improvement in Recall and a 10% gain in F1-score. Our framework not only improves detection performance but also enhances model transparency by grounding semantic narratives in statistical evidence, providing interpretable rationales essential for high-stakes decision-making.

</details>


### [203] [Does Your Generalist Skill Optimizer Really Incentivize Agentic Tasks? Rethinking Skill Optimization via Bipartite Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37201)
*Jiawei Zhou,Bo Peng,Yuxuan Huang,Ming Zhang,Youbang Sun,Xiang Wang,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BiPA分析框架，通过"同种子候选重放接受规则"测度选择偏差、用"轮次—文本二部图"归因额外token，实证揭示技能优化器的dev增益在test上消失、且带来约16%的输入token开销，并提出四条设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化器（如将技能视为上下文中的自然语言流程，通过改写并在小dev集上保留提升成功率的编辑）在长程任务上成功信号噪声大：小dev集上的提升无法证明编辑真的有效，成功率指标也无法反映编辑的代价（如token/轮次成本）。因此需要一个能区分"选择效应"与"真实增益"、并把成本追溯到具体行为的分析工具。

Method: 1）BiPA重放优化器的接受规则，对与种子完全相同的候选进行评估，量化"仅由选择（best-of-n）本身"能报告多大增益；2）将每个episode记录为"轮次—所读文本"的二部图，把额外输入token归因到产生它的具体行为。实验覆盖3个优化器、3个agentic基准、2个模型。

Result: 学到的技能与手写种子成功率基本持平（平均高0.1分）；dev增益在test上消失——在不超过30个任务上保留多个候选中最优者，即使所有候选都未改变也能报告同等幅度的增益；输入token增加约16%，优化器几乎总是新增规则，约一半用于复核工作、部分要求一次只做一个调用，agent将其执行为额外轮次，每轮重发整段对话。

Conclusion: 提出四条设计原则：①仅当编辑增益超过选择alone可报告的增益时才接受；②按新增轮次而非规则长度计费；③允许编辑器删除与合并规则；④向编辑器展示既有规则的成本。代码已开源。

Abstract: Skill optimizers adapt an agent to long-horizon tasks, such as repairing a terminal environment, by rewriting its skill, a natural-language procedure in its context, and keeping the edits that raise success on a small dev set. On such tasks success is noisy, so a gain on a small dev set does not show that an edit helps, and a success score does not show what the edit costs. We introduce BiPA, which replays an optimizer's acceptance rule on candidates identical to its seed to measure what selection alone reports, and records each episode as a bipartite graph of turns and the text they read to trace extra input tokens to the behavior behind them. Across three optimizers, three agentic benchmarks and two models, the learned skills succeed about as often as their hand-written seed, 0.1 points higher on average. Their dev gains vanish on test: keeping the best of several candidates on at most 30 tasks reports gains this large even when none changed. They read about 16% more input tokens: the optimizers almost always add rules, half to re-check work and some to act one call at a time, and the agent follows them as extra turns, each re-sending the whole conversation. Four principles follow: accept an edit only if its gain exceeds what selection alone reports; charge it for the turns it adds, not for its length; let the editor delete and merge rules; show it what earlier rules cost. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/BiPA.git.

</details>


### [204] [What Improves During Pretraining? Tracking Knowledge Acquisition with Point-in-Time Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37631)
*Chang Su,Bin Wu,Emine Yilmaz,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出时序变异性分数（TVS），用以刻画不同网页快照预训练模型间某知识项的负对数似然波动，并据此分析预训练过程中通用知识与领域知识的习得动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页语料跨多年采集，模型同时学到通用与领域知识，但不同种类知识在预训练进程中如何被习得仍不清楚；聚合基准分数无法揭示这种内部学习动态，因此需要新的诊断指标。

Method: 利用基于不同年份网页快照训练的时间点模型，定义TVS为某项目标在多个模型间负对数似然的变异系数，分数越高说明该知识在跨年份网页文本中表征越不稳定。在6个多主题基准、多个开源模型族的6个模型上评估，并按TVS高低分组比较学习曲线。

Result: 低TVS与高TVS项目在训练早期学习程度相近，但高TVS项目在后期提升更快；预训练结束时，高TVS项目在36个模型-基准设置中的34个上优于低TVS项目，机会归一化准确率平均领先7.3个点；该规律在知识密集型任务上最稳健，即使控制难度相近仍成立。

Conclusion: 不同时序变异性的知识在预训练过程中呈现不同的习得轨迹，TVS可作为超越聚合基准分数的学习动态诊断工具，揭示知识获取的时间性差异。

Abstract: Language models pretrained on web corpora collected over multiple years learn both general and domain-specific knowledge. How these different kinds of knowledge are learned as pretraining progresses remains unclear. Recent point-in-time models trained on web snapshots from different years provide a way to probe how knowledge prevalence varies over time. Using these models, we propose a temporal variability score (TVS), defined as the coefficient of variation of an item's negative log-likelihood across models. A higher score suggests that the knowledge may be less consistently represented across years of web text. We evaluate six benchmarks covering diverse topics using six models from multiple open-source model families. We find that low- and high-TVS items are learned similarly early in training, but high-TVS items improve faster later on. At the end of the pretraining run, high-TVS items outperform low-TVS items in 34 of 36 model-benchmark settings, with an average advantage of 7.3 points of chance-normalized accuracy. This pattern is most robust on knowledge-intensive tasks, even when comparing items of similar difficulty. Our findings reveal differences in how knowledge is learned over the course of pretraining, with temporal variability providing a diagnostic of these learning dynamics beyond aggregate benchmark scores.

</details>


### [205] [From Interaction to Capability: Task Advancement as State and Supervision for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37482)
*Ziyang Chen,Qian Li,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务推进记忆（TAM），以“已验证的任务推进”为核心，既在执行中维护任务推进状态，又在任务后对产生验证结果的交互进行抽象学习，从而支持长程跨应用GUI任务并复用得可复用操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI记忆需保留历史动作结果并学习可复用操作，但长程工作流中智能体需跨应用整合信息，且有用操作常与失败尝试交织，导致难以有效记忆与复用。

Method: 将任务推进定义为经过验证的任务进展；执行阶段用Task Advancement State表示已验证进展如何改变剩余目标，使智能体可跨应用累积利用既有成果；每任务结束后通过advancement supervision识别并抽象出产生已验证局部结果的交互，学到的操作保留所需输入、预期结果与验证条件之间的关系；由此可从成功与失败任务中保留局部成功的操作。

Result: MobileWorld仅用Advancement State即将成功率从12.0%提升至28.2%；完整TAM相对最强基线实现17.6%的任务成功率相对提升，并将每个已解决任务的生命周期token减少38.7%；在SPA-Bench单应用英文Level 3设置下取得SOTA任务成功率。

Conclusion: 以验证驱动的任务推进为核心构建记忆，可同时提升长程GUI任务的成功率与token效率，并能从失败任务中萃取可复用操作。

Abstract: GUI memory should preserve the results of past actions and learn reusable operations. This is difficult in long-horizon workflows, where agents must use information across applications and useful operations are interleaved with failed attempts. We introduce Task Advancement Memory (TAM), which defines task advancement as verified task progress, and supports ongoing task execution and learns reusable operations. During execution, Task Advancement State represents how verified task progress changes the remaining goals, allowing the agent to build on earlier achievements across applications. After each task, advancement supervision identifies and abstracts the interactions that produced a verified local result. The learned operations preserve the relation between required inputs, expected outcomes, and verification conditions. This retains locally successful operations from both successful and failed tasks. On MobileWorld GUI-only tasks, Advancement State alone raises success from 12.0% to 28.2%. Full TAM achieves a 17.6% relative gain in task success over the strongest evaluated baseline and reduces lifecycle tokens per solved task by 38.7%. On SPA-Bench, TAM achieves state-of-the-art task success in the single-app English Level 3 setting.

</details>


### [206] [M4Bench: Evaluating Procedural Specification for Clinical EHR Derivation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37459)
*Hannes Ill,Rafi Al Attrach,Rajna Fani,Ahram Han,Ryohei Yamamoto,Marlene Louisa Moerig,Dukyong Yoon,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出M4Bench基准（MIMIC-IV与eICU上28个临床衍生任务、15个任务族），检验AI编码智能体能否复现临床衍生变量，并发现增益主要来自"规格完整性"而非技能格式本身，同时绝对成功率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子病历临床研究依赖衍生变量，但其定义位于数据库模式之外（条目编码、时间窗、缺失处理规则、输出粒度），直接改变队列与下游结论；现有工作缺乏系统评测AI编码智能体能否复现此类衍生的基准，也不清楚"技能"（skills）带来的增益究竟源于何处。

Method: 构建M4Bench：28个临床衍生任务、15个任务族，覆盖MIMIC-IV与eICU；在Docker隔离的Codex环境中运行（GPT 5.4 Mini、GPT 5.5），设无技能基线，并用对照实验将技能价值分解为规格完整性、交付格式与模式熟悉度三部分；辅以诱饵技能、原始参考SQL、模式混淆/重构等对照，并在Claude Code（Opus 4.7、Sonnet 4.6，560次运行）上做描述性复现。

Result: 技能较无技能基线提升奖励0.136，28个任务中26个改善；8任务子集上仅操作性规格即提升0.162，技能额外仅贡献0.024（与零不可区分）；10任务子集上诱饵技能等同无技能、原始参考SQL接近定向技能；模式混淆与重构分别保留86%与70%的技能增益；Claude Code复现方向一致（+0.120，21/28改善）。但绝对完成度极低：仅有技能280次中25次、无技能280次中13次通过全部严格检查，关键行F1仅+0.003。

Conclusion: 技能收益是"与参考的部分一致性"而非任务完成，且主要可由规格完整性解释；技能以版本化、可供临床医生审阅的Markdown格式发布，并附任务族级参考有效性审计；M4Bench将任务、技能、评测代码与脱敏逐次运行证据作为开源基准发布。

Abstract: Clinical research over electronic health records depends on derived variables whose definitions sit outside the schema, in item codes, time windows, missingness rules, and output-grain choices that change cohorts and downstream conclusions. We introduce M4Bench, 28 clinical derivation tasks across 15 task families on MIMIC-IV and eICU, to test whether AI coding agents can reproduce such derivations and how clinician-reviewed agent skills change their results. Its controls decompose the value of skills into specification completeness, delivery format, and schema familiarity. In a Docker-isolated Codex campaign (GPT 5.4 Mini and GPT 5.5), skills raise reward by 0.136 over the no-skill baseline, with 26 of 28 tasks improving. Specification completeness accounts for most of this gain: on an eight-task control subset, an operational spec alone raises reward by 0.162, and skills add only 0.024 on top, indistinguishable from zero. On a ten-task subset, decoy skills score like no skill and raw reference SQL nearly matches targeted skills, arguing against generic context or the skill format as the explanation. Schema obfuscation and restructuring preserve 86% and 70% of the skill gain (GPT 5.4 Mini, 26 MIMIC-IV tasks), a descriptive robustness check rather than a bound on memorization. A descriptive Claude Code replication on all 28 tasks (Opus 4.7 and Sonnet 4.6, 560 runs) is directionally consistent (+0.120, 21 of 28 tasks improving). These gains are partial agreement with the reference, not completed tasks: only 25 of 280 with-skill and 13 of 280 no-skill Codex runs pass every strict check, and skills barely change which rows agents return (key F1 +0.003). Skills are released as a versioned, clinician-reviewable markdown delivery format, with a family-level reference-validity audit. M4Bench releases tasks, skills, evaluation code, and sanitized per-run evidence as an open-source benchmark.

</details>


### [207] [The Trinity:Capability-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36077)
*Weidong Tang,Ruiheng Wang,Jingxuan Zhang,Hexin Wang,Haoyuan Chen,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Yixing Ma,Zichen Jiang,Ziyu Guo,Haodong Li,Zhengbo Jiao,Zhiqing Cui,Xinxiang Yin,Shuo Liang,Zhenglin Wan,Hongzhan Lin,Wangbo Zhao Kaipeng Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Odin Zhang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出递归自我改进框架 The Trinity，通过 Creator、Explorer、Evolver 三角色耦合世界生成与 Harness 进化，并引入能力驱动 Real2Sim2Real 与能力树组织，使进化后的 Harness 在 28 个训练基准上平均提升 34 分、18 个未见基准提升 26 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多模态智能视为人类引导下世界与智能体通过交互的协同进化，但规模化面临两个瓶颈：构建异构、可验证环境的成本高昂；人工迭代 Harness 无法跟上任务多样性与复杂度的增长。核心洞察是：不同模态与表层形式的任务可共享相同底层能力需求。

Method: 1) The Trinity 框架含 Creator、Explorer、Evolver 三角色；2) Evolver 基于已验证执行轨迹，将任务组织依据从表层类别转为共享需求，把原任务树重构为分层能力树（Capability Tree）；3) Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real：Creator 对同一节点下的异构任务抽象出共性需求，构建简化可验证的仿真世界进行能力学习，再在原始任务上评估迁移；4) Explorer 沿能力树以选择性继承逐步进化可复用能力模块，形成固定拓扑下跨迭代累积有效更新的 Harness 树；5) 基于公开任务信息的 Capability-Aware Harness Router 从树中选择兼容模块配置。

Result: 进化后的 Harness 在 28 个训练基准上平均性能提升 34 分，在 18 个未见基准上提升 26 分，并包含两项真实具身任务。

Conclusion: 以能力为组织中心的三角色递归自改进范式可有效耦合环境生成与 Harness 进化，实现可迁移的能力积累，并在训练与未见任务上均取得显著泛化提升。

Abstract: Multimodal intelligence can be viewed as human-guided co-evolution between worlds and agents through interaction. However, scaling this process faces two bottlenecks: constructing heterogeneous, verifiable environments is costly, and manual Harness iteration cannot keep pace with growing task diversity and complexity. A key insight is that tasks with different modalities and surface forms can share the same underlying capability requirements. We therefore introduce The Trinity, a recursive self-improvement framework coupling world generation with Harness evolution through three roles: Creator, Explorer, and Evolver. Drawing on verified execution trajectories, Evolver shifts the basis of task organization from surface categories to shared requirements, reorganizing the original task tree into a hierarchical capability tree. We introduce Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real: for heterogeneous tasks at the same node, Creator abstracts their common requirements into simplified, verifiable simulated worlds for capability learning, while evaluation on original tasks measures transfer. Along this capability tree, Explorer progressively evolves reusable capability modules through selective inheritance, forming a Harness tree whose validated updates accumulate across iterations within a fixed topology. The resulting Harnesses specialize in different capabilities, motivating a Capability-Aware Harness Router that uses public task information to select compatible module configurations from the tree. Our evolved Harnesses improve mean performance by 34 points across 28 training benchmarks and 26 points across 18 unseen benchmarks, including two real-world embodied tasks.

</details>


### [208] [Dependency Graphs of Mathematical Arguments: Representation, Structural Measurement, and Proof Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36776)
*Eunji Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个以大语言模型辅助、以原文出处为依据的数学论证表示与度量框架，形式化定义深度与体量等结构度量并给出保持性条件，同时强调其为评估设计而非实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学论证的表示与度量缺乏统一、可溯源、可复核的形式化框架：论证单元、依赖关系与度量口径不明确，难以支持跨论文比较、质量审查与自动辅助。作者希望在保留原文证据与审阅状态的前提下，对论证结构进行可计算刻画。

Method: 构建含编号/非编号陈述、方程、假设与构造的表示框架，区分原文出现、上下文状态与推理记录；依赖保留来源证据与审阅状态。设定度量契约固定稿本版本、边界、粒度、证明选择与宏观锚点。在有向无环图上定义深度（最长前置路径长度）与体量（不同祖先边计数），给出处理共享子论证的并集递推，并确定保持深度与宏观骨架的可达性条件；采用版本化提示协议划分注释发现、确定性重算与审阅辅助。

Result: 给出并集递推以正确处理共享子论证；证明存在保持深度与宏观骨架的可达性条件，而无限制细化不必然保持深度或体量；建立跨论文链接支持定理共用、稀有组合与依赖社群关联的研究议程；提出基于模式的前提建议与遗漏/未检应用条件审查的应用方向，并给出评估设计（无实证证据）。

Conclusion: 该度量刻画的是特定表示下的论证结构，而非数学难度、原创性或重要性等内在属性；所建议前提需数学验证。框架为可溯源、可复核的论证分析与评估提供基础，但其效用仍待实证检验。

Abstract: We propose a source-grounded framework for representing and measuring mathematical arguments with assistance from large language models. It includes numbered and unnumbered statements, equations, assumptions, and constructions, distinguishing source occurrences from contextual states and inference records. Proposed dependencies retain source evidence and review status. A measurement contract fixes manuscript versions, boundaries, granularity, proof choices, and macro anchors. On a selected finite directed acyclic graph, depth is the longest prerequisite-path length, and bulk counts distinct ancestor edges. We give a union recurrence that accounts for shared subarguments and identify reachability conditions preserving depth and the macro skeleton; unrestricted refinement need not preserve depth or bulk. A standardized, versioned prompt protocol separates annotation discovery, deterministic recomputation, and review assistance. Cross-paper links support a research agenda on theorem co-use, rare combinations, and connections between dependency communities. These patterns motivate probabilistic premise suggestions and review of possible omissions or unchecked application conditions in human-written and AI-generated proofs. Further applications include source-linked reading support and comparison of individual and collaborative argument shapes. We provide an evaluation design rather than empirical evidence of these benefits. Suggested premises require mathematical validation, and the measures describe a specified representation rather than intrinsic mathematical difficulty, originality, or importance.

</details>


### [209] [PsyJev: Psychological Analysis via System-1 Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36689)
*Fangyuan Liu,Sirui Zhao,Xiaohao Wang,Guo Cheng,Peng Zhang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出统一的问题条件化判断接口，构建含13个数据集、82个问题的PsyJudgeBench，并训练System-1模型PsyJev（SFT+JA-RLCD），基于Qwen3.5的9B模型平均测试F1达67.56，领先最强外部对比2.54分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络自我披露支持从识别表露经历到发现症状相关信息的多种心理分析任务，但现有方法多为任务专用，缺乏在异构心理判断问题间共享的统一决策界面。

Method: 构建PsyJudgeBench（13个标注数据集、82个问题、六个心理领域，保留来源特定判据与未观测标签掩码）；提出PsyJev，直接输出每个请求所指定答案上的分布；先以联合监督训练建立共享判断函数，再用Judgment-Anchored RLCD（JA-RLCD）在logit空间进行奖励优化，含均衡监督、选择性保留SFT正确预测、问题组奖励优化。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5的2B/4B/9B模型平均测试F1分别为64.33、66.21、67.56，9B超过最强外部对比2.54分；受控2B实验检验了训练阶段与锚定精炼的作用；并分析了问题条件化判断、推理成本与概率质量。

Conclusion: 问题条件化的共享决策接口可在异构心理内容分析中有效工作，SFT与JA-RLCD结合显著提升性能，且规模扩展带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Self-disclosures on the Web support diverse psychological analyses, from recognizing expressed experiences to identifying symptom-relevant information.
