Our study reveals three key findings: (1) the GPU cluster exhibits significant underutilization, with over 70% of jobs using less than 20% of GPU memory and 50% averaging below 5% Streaming Multiprocessor (SM) utilization; (2) 80% of jobs have a drastic mismatch between their requested and actual durations, significantly limiting the scheduler's effectiveness; and (3) the multi-GPU jobs we examined show highly periodic communication patterns. These findings suggest there is plenty of room in the middle: substantial utilization gains are available through better job scheduling and GPU multitasking, independent of hardware or application improvements.
  Our monitoring solution is available as open-source software at https://github.com/als244/gpu_server_monitoring.

</details>


### [244] [How Stable Are LLM-Generated Proofs Under Program Refactoring? A Stress Test for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36328)
*Aravind Mandiga,Guoming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RefactorProof，用保行为重构生成 458 个 Lean 认证变体，在规范与证明逐字节固定的条件下测量证明对程序重构的稳定性；结构重构下参考证明的归一化 PSR 仅 4.3%，专用证明器为 13.3–26.8%，但单标识符修复即可修复多数失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证代码生成基准通常只针对固定实现检验证明，无法回答"程序经过保行为重构后，同一证明是否仍然有效"这一问题。

Method: 从在固定环境中可编译的 185 个 VERINA 任务派生 458 个经 Lean 认证的变体，保持规范与所评估证明逐字节不变；其中 46 个任务有官方参考证明（均来自 basic 划分），主模型—参考对比进一步匹配到 21–25 个短小非递归任务；采用任务归一化的结构 PSR 等指标，并做排除普遍不变变体、家族均衡聚合、不同可部署有效证明选择规则等稳健性检验。

Result: 结构重构下任务归一化结构 PSR 为参考证明 4.3%、专用证明器 13.3–26.8%。在匹配任务上，专用证明器证明的未改动证明存活率高于参考证明产物：DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B +15.6 点、Goedel-Prover-V2-32B +18.8 点、Goedel-Prover-V2-8B +20.3 点，三个 95% CI 均不含零，且该排序在多种稳健性设置下保持。未改动证明失败不等于高修复成本：预定义单标识符编辑可修复 51/54 个符合条件的参考结构失败与 58/85 个证明器失败。

Conclusion: RefactorProof 度量的是程序重构下未改动证明的稳定性，而非证明破坏后的维护成本；两种度量不可互相替代。

Abstract: Benchmarks for verifiable code generation usually test a proof against one fixed implementation. We ask whether the same proof remains valid after a program refactoring that preserves behavior. We introduce RefactorProof, which derives 458 Lean-certified variants from the 185 VERINA tasks that compile in our pinned environment while holding the specification and evaluated proof fixed byte-for-byte. VERINA supplies reference proofs for 46 tasks, all from its basic split; the primary model-versus-reference comparisons further match on 21-25 short, non-recursive tasks. Structural refactorings expose substantial brittleness across proof sources: task-normalized structural PSR is 4.3% for the supplied references and 13.3-26.8% for the specialized provers. On the matched tasks, specialized prover proofs nevertheless have higher overall unchanged-proof survival than the supplied reference artifacts: +15.6 points for DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B, +18.8 for Goedel-Prover-V2-32B, and +20.3 for Goedel-Prover-V2-8B; all three 95% CIs exclude zero. The ordering persists after excluding universally survived T1 variants, under family-balanced aggregation, and under alternative deployable valid-proof selection rules. However, unchanged-proof failure is not the same as high repair cost: predefined one-identifier edits repair 51/54 eligible reference structural failures and 58/85 eligible prover failures. RefactorProof therefore measures unchanged-proof stability under program refactoring, not the cost of maintaining a proof after it breaks.

</details>


### [245] [A Smart Instant Book Filter Model based on Dual-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36287)
*Yi Hou,Li Fan,Huizhong Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出智能即时预订筛选系统（Smart Instant Book Filter），在搜索中智能调节即时预订房源曝光，将请求式预订转化为即时预订，同时以预订率不下降为约束，构建双目标优化问题，并在线上A/B测试与生产部署中验证优于人工调参模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短租平台中"请求式预订"存在响应延迟和可能被拒的问题，影响用户满意度。已有即时预订功能未能缓解该问题，因为搜索结果中即时预订与请求式预订房源混合；而简单地将所有即时预订房源提前展示会因推广低质量房源而损害预订率。

Method: 设计智能即时预订筛选系统，对即时预订筛选器进行智能化施加，在搜索排序/展示中对房源施加筛选以实现从请求式预订向即时预订的迁移；与传统双边市场研究中追求单一整体社会福利目标不同，将问题形式化为双目标优化：在预订率不下降的约束下最大化即时预订率。

Result: 面向全球用户的A/B测试显示，模型在即时预订率和整体预订率上均优于此前由人工专家调优的模型；房东即时预订采纳率等指标亦有提升。系统已上线生产，每日服务数百万用户。

Conclusion: 智能筛选策略可在不牺牲预订率的前提下提升即时预订率，实现约束下的双目标优化；该方案已具备线上可行性与规模化部署能力。

Abstract: Hospitality service in online marketplaces has become increasingly popular over the past few years, not only because it helps people easily rent short-term lodging but also because it empowers travelers to connect with locals and feel belonging. A typical pattern of these industries is a central platform mediating the economic interactions between guests and hosts, where a successful booking experience is the key to user satisfaction. However, one of the biggest challenges is the possible delay of response and chance of rejection when guests try to book a request-to-book listing. Previously, we enabled the instant book feature to allow hosts to instantly accept qualified guests and guests to confirm bookings immediately. Yet the symptom has not been alleviated given mixed request-to-book and instant book listing supply in search results. One possible solution is to promote all instant book listings in search results; however, booking rate will be damaged due to some improperly promoted low quality listings.
  In this paper, we present a novel smart instant book filter system that applies the instant book filter intelligently and shifts request-to-book to instant book without hurting booking rate. Compared with traditional two-sided markets research that targets finding strategies to maximize a single overall social welfare goal, our proposed system aims to maximize the instant book rate with the constraint that booking rate should not decrease, forming a dual-objective optimization problem.
  We show A/B testing results for our system exposed to global users, demonstrating that our model performs better than previous human-expert-tuned models in terms of both instant book rate and overall booking rate. We also achieved improved metrics such as the host instant book adoption rate. The proposed system has been deployed to production and is serving millions of users every day.

</details>


### [246] [BehaveRec: When Does Long-Term Behavioral History Help Sequential Recommendation? A Study with Session, History, and Community Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36239)
*Aysenur Inan,Reza Yousefi Maragheh,Jayesh Uddhav Kudase,Selene Xu,Kaushiki Nag,Jianpeng Xu,Sushant Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BehaveRec三信号框架（会话Transformer+用户历史编码器+行为相似度用户图GraphSAGE+学习门控），并在工业与公开数据集上量化"长历史/社群信号何时有用"，给出约20次交互的部署阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐仅依赖近期会话交互，短期信号弱或模糊时个性化不足；但作者不预设"加入长期历史与社群信号必然有益"，而是研究其在何种条件下真正带来增益。

Method: BehaveRec融合三路信号：因果会话Transformer、覆盖90天窗口的双向用户历史编码器、基于行为嵌入相似度（而非物品共现）构建用户-用户图上的GraphSAGE模块；按用户学习门控进行加权融合。在137M序列/12.6M用户的工业数据集与3个公开数据集上评测，并按历史长度做用户级分层与剂量-响应分析。

Result: 类别级意图预测下R@5较SASRec提升3.2%，参数量更大的SASRec变体仍落后2.5%（排除容量解释）；收益随历史丰富度单调增长，约10次交互以下几乎无增益，101+事件用户达+0.017 R@5；历史稀疏场景仅会话模型更优。全物品级词表的单次初步实验方向不一致，列为开放问题。模型仅5.67M参数，单GPU两天内训练完成。

Conclusion: 长历史与行为图信号的收益依赖用户历史丰富度，存在明确剂量-响应关系；据此提出每用户约20次交互的部署规则以判断是否值得引入额外复杂度，且BehaveRec保持轻量。主要结论限于类别级意图预测。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models typically predict a user's next action from recent session interactions, which limits personalization when short-term signals are weak or ambiguous. Rather than assuming that incorporating longer-term history and community signals is universally beneficial, we study when it actually helps. We propose BehaveRec, a three-signal framework combining a causal session transformer, a bidirectional User History Encoder over a 90-day window, and a GraphSAGE module over a user-user graph built from behavioral embedding similarity rather than item co-occurrence; a learned gate blends the three signals per user. On an industrial dataset of 137M sequences from 12.6M users, BehaveRec improves R@5 by +3.2% over SASRec, with a larger-capacity SASRec baseline (more parameters than BehaveRec) still trailing by +2.5%, ruling out capacity as the explanation. More importantly, a user-level stratification by history length reveals a clear dose-response relationship: BehaveRec's advantage is negligible below roughly 10 interactions and grows monotonically with history richness, reaching +0.017 R@5 for users with 101+ events. This pattern is corroborated across one industrial and three public datasets spanning history-rich to history-sparse regimes, where session-only models remain preferable. These headline results, and the resulting deployment threshold, are established for category-level intent prediction; a preliminary, single-run check on the full item-level vocabulary yields a directionally mixed result and is scoped as an open question rather than a claim. We use these results to derive a practical deployment rule -- roughly 20 interactions per user -- for when long-term history and behavioral-graph signals are worth their added complexity. Despite this added complexity, BehaveRec remains lightweight: it uses 5.67M parameters and trains on a single GPU in under two days.

</details>


### [247] [MARQ: A Modular, LLM-Agnostic Requirement Quality Assessor Infused with Systems Engineering Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36234)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARQ通过注入专家衍生的系统工程知识（操作化INCOSE需求质量准则）增强LLM的需求质量评估能力，在NASA专家标注数据集上使Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6 Sol、Gemma 3 12B的F1中位数分别从0.55/0.52/0.18提升至0.80/0.62/0.53。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程是系统工程的基础，需求缺陷会向下游传播并危害开发结果。LLM虽有望用于需求质量评估，但现成模型漏检大量专家识别的缺陷、在不同质量准则上表现不均、且多次执行间结果不稳定，因此需探究注入专家SE知识能否超越现成能力。

Method: 提出MARQ——一个模块化、与LLM无关的原型系统，用准则特定的知识栈为LLM补充知识，操作化INCOSE需求质量准则；在专家标注的NASA需求集上，对Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6 Sol与开源Gemma 3 12B各执行100次，与现成基线对比，并进行准则级分析。

Result: MARQ的F1中位数分别为0.80（Claude Opus 5）、0.62（GPT-5.6 Sol）、0.53（Gemma 3 12B），基线为0.55、0.52、0.18。增益性质因模型而异：Claude同时改善缺陷检测与误报控制；GPT-5.6 Sol提升检测与执行级一致性但误报增加；Gemma 3 12B达到现成专有模型水平但仍高度波动。准则层面增益集中于Correctness与Conformance，单一准则内存在漏检与误报的权衡，多个准则上响应随模型而异。

Conclusion: SE知识注入是强化可信LLM需求工程支持的、与模型无关的有前景路径，但需配合准则特定干预与人机协同评审。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) is foundational to systems engineering (SE), as requirement defects can propagate downstream and adversely affect development outcomes. Although large language models (LLMs) are promising for requirement quality assessment, prior work shows that off-the-shelf models miss a large share of expert-identified defects, perform unevenly across quality criteria, and vary across executions. This study investigates whether infusing expert-derived SE knowledge can improve LLM-based requirement quality evaluation beyond off-the-shelf capability. We present MARQ, a modular, LLM-agnostic prototype that supplements LLMs with a criterion-specific knowledge stack operationalizing INCOSE requirement quality criteria and evaluated against off-the-shelf baselines on an expert-labeled NASA requirement set using Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6 Sol, and the open-weight Gemma 3 12B, with 100 executions per model. MARQ achieves median F1 scores of 0.80 for Claude Opus 5, 0.62 for GPT-5.6 Sol, and 0.53 for Gemma 3 12B, compared with off-the-shelf baselines of 0.55, 0.52, and 0.18, respectively. However, the nature of these gains is model-dependent. Claude Opus 5 improves defect detection and false-alarm control; GPT-5.6 Sol gains detection and better execution-level consistency at the cost of more false alarms; and Gemma 3 12B reaches the performance of off-the-shelf proprietary models, although it remains highly variable. Criterion-level analysis shows gains concentrated in Correctness and Conformance, trade-offs between misses and false alarms within individual criteria, and model-dependent responses across several criteria. These findings position SE knowledge infusion as a promising model-agnostic pathway for strengthening trustworthy LLM-based RE support while underscoring the need for criterion-specific interventions and human-in-the-loop review.

</details>


### [248] [Vision-Language Agents Can Learn in Their Own Latent Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36925)
*Haoxi Li,Sikai Bai,Jianfei Ma,Qinglin Hou,Xiaocheng Lu,Tao Han,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dwell框架，将预训练VLM重构为循环潜在状态转移算子，使其在潜在空间中预测、调整并行动，实现基于模型的高效强化学习在线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）蕴含丰富的世界知识，但很少被用于通过在线交互驱动策略改进。挑战在于：策略更新会导致交互分布漂移，世界模型需随之适应；而持续更新大型预训练模型计算代价高昂。

Method: 将VLM重构为循环潜在状态转移算子，利用其自身表征，使其同时充当世界模型与策略：一方面从真实交互中持续学习以预测和评估动作结果，另一方面从潜在想象（latent imagination）中改进行为，避免重建未来、在潜在空间完成前向推演。

Result: 在五个交互式视觉环境中，Dwell在可训练方法中取得最强的综合性能，同时大幅降低想象所需的计算开销；表明有效的VLM世界模型无需重建未来，只需预测对动作有用的未来。

Conclusion: VLM智能体的世界模型不必进行像素级重建，只需在潜在空间预测对决策有意义的未来，即可兼顾效果与效率。代码已开源。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for building the next generation of agents that continually expand their capabilities. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) already internalize rich world knowledge, yet this knowledge is rarely leveraged to drive policy improvement through online interaction. Realizing this potential efficiently remains challenging, as policy updates shift the agent's interaction distribution, requiring the world model to adapt accordingly, while continually updating large pretrained models is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce Dwell, a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework that reframes a VLM as a recurrent latent transition operator, endowing it with the ability to predict, adjust, and act in latent space. Leveraging the VLM's own representations and its dual role as both world model and policy, the agent continually learns from real interactions to anticipate and evaluate action outcomes, and from latent imagination to improve its behavior. Across five interactive visual environments, Dwell achieves the strongest overall performance among trainable methods while substantially reducing the compute required for imagination, showing that effective world models for VLM agents need not reconstruct the future; they need to predict futures that are useful for action. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tom111-lhx/Dwell

</details>


### [249] [Can LLMs Reliably Grade Olympiad Proofs? A Study of Mathematical Verification with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36767)
*Azim Ospanov,Zijin Feng,Ding Ding,Chengwu Liu,Haoli Bai,Jiacheng Sun,Lifeng Shang,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了LLM对自然语言数学证明（尤其奥赛级）的验证能力，构建USAMO 2025候选解答人工标注数据集，提出TROJAN对抗样本生成流水线与MABGrader评分框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在竞赛数学求解上进展显著，但自然语言证明验证（尤其需逐条核查长推理链的奥赛证明）研究不足，目前主要依赖昂贵的专家人工批改，难以规模化。

Method: 1) 构建经专家评分的人工标注USAMO 2025候选解答数据集；2) 在20个前沿模型、7项指标、2种提示风格、3种推理方法、3种提示模板上进行广泛评测；3) 提出TROJAN——迭代式自我批判流水线，大规模生成高保真对抗性证明；4) 提出MABGrader，将评分重构为离散分数空间上的多臂老虎机臂选择问题。

Result: 现有SOTA模型在证明验证上仍有明显提升空间；MABGrader在Quadratic Weighted Kappa上较SOTA的ProofGrader提升26.4%，评分误差降低14%。

Conclusion: LLM证明验证能力尚不充分，对抗性数据生成与将评分形式化为多臂老虎机决策可有效提升验证/评分性能；代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress on solving competition-level mathematics, yet their ability to verify natural-language mathematical proofs remains relatively underexplored. This gap is especially pronounced for Olympiad-level proofs, which require meticulous assessment of each claim in a long reasoning chain. Current proof verification thus primarily relies on expert inspection, which is costly to scale. In this paper, we present a systematic study of LLM-based verification in this setting. Specifically, we introduce a human-curated dataset of USAMO 2025 candidate solutions with expert grading, and conduct a broad evaluation of existing LLM verifiers across frontier models. We also propose TROJAN, an iterative self-critique pipeline for generating high-fidelity adversarial proofs at scale. Our comprehensive evaluation covers 20 models spanning seven metrics, two prompt styles, three inference methods, and three prompt templates, showing that state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement. To narrow this gap, we propose MABGrader, a Multi-Armed Bandit framework that reframes grading as arm selection over the discrete score space. MABGrader outperforms the SOTA ProofGrader by 26.4% in Quadratic Weighted Kappa and reduces grading error by 14%. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/aziksh-ospanov/TROJAN.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond the Evidence Coherence Trap: Agent-Driven Evidence Selection for Temporally Disordered Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36764)
*Chi Wang,Min Gao,Yifei Zhang,Bincheng Gu,Zongwei Wang,Wentao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对"时间乱序型虚假新闻"提出"证据一致性陷阱"问题，构建 ChronoFake 基准（30,706 篇源不相交文章）并提出 DelSA——一种文档选择自更新智能体，通过迭代式证据选择训练与冻结规则的一次性推理，结合LLM证据刻画、验证器训练的reranker以及全局时间线冲突比较，在 ChronoFake 上超越现有方法且在 PolitiFact、GossipCop 上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假新闻破坏公众信任与在线信息生态完整性。时间乱序型虚假新闻将真实事件重新排列成错误的时间顺序，单个事件本身可能仍属实，只是时间关系被歪曲；当同一实体的相似事件反复出现时，检索容易返回语义合理但时期错误的证据，导致独立事件核验依赖"局部连贯但时间错配"的证据而误判文章为真。作者称之为"证据一致性陷阱"，而现有基准很少隔离这一设定。

Method: 1) 构建 ChronoFake：源不相交基准，含30,706篇文章，源自带时间锚点的真实事件，区分时间操纵与合法叙述（含倒叙/闪回）。2) 提出 DelSA（Document Selection Self-Updating Agent）：训练阶段迭代精炼证据选择，推理阶段应用冻结规则手册做一次性证据选择；用LLM对候选证据进行特征刻画，用验证器训练的重排器平衡局部相关性与时间线一致性；最后由分类器比较LLM抽取的"文章隐含时间线"与"证据推导时间线"，以揭示全局时间冲突。

Result: DelSA 在 ChronoFake 上优于现有方法，同时在 PolitiFact 与 GossipCop 上保持竞争力，说明其在专门的时间操纵设定与通用虚假新闻检测任务上均具有效性。

Conclusion: 时间维度是虚假新闻检测中被忽视但关键的一环；孤立事件核验会落入证据一致性陷阱。通过专门基准与"证据选择—时间线比对"的智能体式框架，可有效识别全局时间矛盾，兼顾专用与通用场景性能。

Abstract: Fake news undermines public trust and the integrity of online information ecosystems. Temporally disordered fake news is especially elusive because it rearranges factual events into a false chronology. Individual events may remain factually valid even when their temporal relations are misrepresented. When similar events involving the same entity recur, retrieval may return semantically plausible evidence from the wrong period. Consequently, independent event verification may rely on locally coherent but temporally mismatched evidence and incorrectly validate the article. We call this the \textit{evidence coherence trap}. Because existing benchmarks rarely isolate this setting, we construct ChronoFake, a source-disjoint benchmark of 30,706 articles derived from temporally anchored real events. It distinguishes temporal manipulation from valid narration, including flashbacks. To address this, we propose DelSA, a \underline{D}ocument S\underline{el}ection \underline{S}elf-Updating \underline{A}gent that iteratively refines evidence selection during training and applies a frozen rulebook for one-pass evidence selection at inference. DelSA uses an LLM to characterize candidate evidence, while a verifier-trained reranker balances local relevance against timeline consistency. Finally, the classifier compares the article-implied chronology extracted by the LLM with the evidence-derived chronology to reveal global temporal conflicts. DelSA outperforms existing methods on ChronoFake, while remaining competitive on PolitiFact and GossipCop.

</details>


### [251] [RPSD: Retrieval-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37281)
*Dongyue Li,Zebang Cheng,Zhongyi Deng,Hongboxu,Yi Bin,Fei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Retrieval-Privileged on-policy Self-Distillation（RPSD），用交叉用户检索证据作为特权知识，通过在线自蒸馏将序列级优势重塑为 token 级信用，解决 LLM 式序列推荐中信用盲与协同盲两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的生成式序列推荐在 GRPO 等强化学习后训练中存在两点缺陷：（1）信用盲优化——序列级奖励被平均分配给所有生成 token；（2）协同盲监督——训练信号仅来自单一用户，忽略相似用户间共享的行为模式。

Method: 核心为 RPSD。针对信用盲，引入 on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）：由具备特权信息的教师对学生的 on-policy 响应重新评估，利用学生与教师在 token 级置信度之差，把共享的序列级优势重塑为 token 特定信用。为进一步解决协同盲，将教师特权的来源从单用户扩展到相似用户的协同行为证据：使用时间感知的最优传输（OT）在同时考虑物品相似性与时间结构的条件下对齐跨用户交互轨迹，并施加多样性感知选择以保留互补轨迹作为特权证据。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明 RPSD 取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 据称是首个将 on-policy self-distillation 引入序列推荐、并以跨用户行为作为其特权信号的工作；OPSD 提供细粒度 token 级信用，OT 为教师注入结构化跨用户证据，二者协同互补。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation predicts a user's next interaction from historical behavior. Recent LLM-based methods recast this task as generative recommendation and increasingly adopt reinforcement learning such as GRPO for post-training. However, these methods suffer from two limitations, namely credit-blind optimization, where a sequence-level reward is shared across all generated tokens, and collaborative-blind supervision, where each training signal is derived from a single user and ignores behavioral patterns shared across similar users. To address both issues, we propose Retrieval-Privileged on-policy Self-Distillation (RPSD). For credit-blind optimization, we introduce on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), in which a teacher with privileged knowledge re-evaluates the student's on-policy response, and the token-level confidence difference between the student and teacher reshapes the shared sequence-level advantage into token-specific credit. However, the privileged knowledge in vanilla OPSD is still derived from a single user, leaving collaborative-blind supervision unresolved. We therefore ground the teacher in collaborative behavioral evidence from similar users. Specifically, we use temporally aware optimal transport (OT) to compare interaction trajectories across users by jointly considering item similarity and temporal structure, and further apply diversity-aware selection to retain complementary trajectories as privileged evidence. In this way, on-policy self-distillation provides fine-grained token-level credit, while OT enriches the teacher with structured cross-user evidence. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to bring on-policy self-distillation to sequential recommendation and to use cross-user behavior as its privileged signal. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that RPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [252] [TCM-DiagCommit: A TCM-SD-Derived Benchmark for Evidence-Conditioned Syndrome Selection and Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37243)
*Erwei Hou,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TCM-DiagCommit基准，将静态标签选择拆解为答案选择、证据更新与不确定性承诺三个可分离的诊断行为，并揭示大模型在难负例与全词表设置下准确率骤降、且倾向错误强行作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中医证候辨证常被建模为在完整病历上做静态最终标签选择，这一设定掩盖了三类关键诊断行为：能否区分语义近邻证候标签与随机干扰项、能否随证据字段逐步揭示而更新预测、以及能否在不确定时恰当延迟或弃答。仅用最终答案准确率评估会高估模型的临床可用性。

Method: 构建TCM-DiagCommit基准：基于公开TCM-SD/ZY-BERT资源并保留原始证候标签，将1,834个源病例扩展为23,842条任务行、覆盖148个证候标签；设计随机、档案近邻、全词表三类候选阶梯，配合t1/t2/t3分阶段证据；引入固定标签的舌象/脉象扰动与证据不足变体；并以基准定义的动作契约对commit、acquire、defer、abstain四类动作打分。在五次零样本DirectLLM校准上进行评测。

Result: random10设置下top-1准确率为63.2%–66.4%，hard20降至24.7%–30.3%，full148进一步降至14.8%–22.3%；在hard20上，WT(all)的错误终端承诺率仍高达62.2%–74.3%，即模型在难判别情形下仍高频强行给出结论。舌象/脉象扰动与证据不足变体进一步暴露证据利用与不确定性处理的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 最终答案准确率不足以支撑诊断类基准评估；答案选择能力、证据利用行为与承诺/弃答决策应被分离度量。TCM-DiagCommit为中医辨证乃至更广泛的不确定性感知诊断评测提供了可复用的动作级评估框架。

Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) syndrome differentiation is usually evaluated as static final-label selection over complete clinical records. This setting hides three diagnostic behaviors: whether a model distinguishes semantic-neighbor syndrome labels from easy random distractors, whether it updates predictions as evidence fields are revealed, and whether it should commit under uncertainty. We introduce TCM-DiagCommit, a benchmark derived from the public TCM-SD/ZY-BERT resource while retaining its source syndrome labels; it converts 1,834 source cases into 23,842 task rows over 148 syndrome labels. The benchmark combines random, profile-neighbor, and full-vocabulary candidate ladders with staged t1/t2/t3 evidence. It also includes fixed-label tongue/pulse perturbations and insufficient-evidence variants. A benchmark-defined action contract scores commit, acquire, defer, and abstain. Across five zero-shot DirectLLM calibrations, random10 top-1 accuracy is 63.2% to 66.4%, but hard20 falls to 24.7% to 30.3% and full148 to 14.8% to 22.3%; on hard20, WT(all) wrong-terminal commits remain 62.2% to 74.3%. These results show that final-answer accuracy alone is insufficient for diagnostic benchmark evaluation: answer selection, evidence use, and benchmark commitment scoring should be measured separately.

</details>


### [253] [Grounding LLM Agents in Business Processes: Process-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Reliable Multi-Step Service Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37242)
*Saiful Khan,Afrah Farea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将OData元数据与BPMN参考流程整合为带类型的属性图，用混合检索器沿流程边检索企业API端点，以在检索阶段暴露流程与schema约束，提升LLM智能体调用企业API的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强的API服务发现将端点视为独立个体，忽略了流程顺序与schema约束（如有效payload键、预定义代码值列表），导致智能体选错端点、乱序调用或编造代码值。

Method: 构建融合OData元数据与BPMN参考流程的带类型属性图；采用混合检索器：先用稠密嵌入召回候选端点，再沿流程边检索后续端点。在42个企业服务（含流程链ground truth）上评估，并测试LLM智能体的多步任务完成率与消融探针。

Result: 在查询与下游实体词面重叠低的场景（n=74），Recall@3提升5.7个点，且跨嵌入模型与截断阈值一致；其他查询与稠密检索持平；无流程层的OpenAPI服务无提升，表明增益源于流程结构。81条多步查询中智能体任务完成率38.7%（chunk基线10.3%）。消融探针：提供有效代码列表可消除编造代码但46%写操作仍含无效键；完整类型化上下文将无效调用降至3%（3/101），且无编造代码。

Conclusion: 在检索阶段显式暴露流程结构与schema约束可显著提升智能体驱动API调用的可靠性，增益可归因于流程结构而非仅端点为单位的语义匹配。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that call enterprise APIs must select the correct endpoints, follow process order, use valid payload keys, and choose coded values from predefined lists. However, current retrieval-augmented service discovery treats endpoints independently and therefore misses process and schema constraints. We address this limitation by integrating Open Data Protocol (OData) metadata and Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) reference processes in a typed property graph. A hybrid retriever identifies candidate endpoints using dense embeddings and follows process edges to retrieve subsequent endpoints. We evaluate the method on 42 enterprise services with process-chain ground truth. For queries with low lexical overlap between the query and downstream entities, it improves Recall@3 by 5.7 points (n=74), with consistent gains across embedding models and cut-offs. It matches dense retrieval on other queries and provides no improvement on OpenAPI services without a process layer, indicating that the gains arise from process structure. We also evaluate an LLM agent on 81 multi-step queries. It completes 38.7% of tasks, compared with 10.3% for a chunk-based baseline. In an elicitation probe, providing valid code lists eliminates invented codes but leaves invalid keys in 46% of write runs. Full typed context reduces invalid calls to 3% (3 of 101 runs), with no invented codes. These results show that exposing process and schema constraints during retrieval improves the reliability of agent-driven API calls.

</details>


### [254] [Rènyi-Regularized Flow-Based Variational Inference for Bayesian Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37169)
*Exaucè Luweh Adjim Ngarti,Élise Arnaud,Léo Nicoletti,Victor Trappler,Arthur Vidard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用双参数孪生实验分析归一化流变分推断在多峰后验下的失效机制，并引入基于Rényi散度的正则项以提升模态覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流变分推断能提供灵活的后验近似，但当后验存在多个被低概率区域分隔的模态时易出现模态塌缩（mode collapse）。作者希望在受控设定下理解该行为的成因及可缓解手段。

Method: 构造双参数孪生实验；在高斯观测误差与高斯先验下，将最小化目标解析为"期望Tikhonov标定目标 − 近似分布微分熵"。指出期望Tikhonov项偏好集中在单一模态，而熵最大化不能保证多峰性。据此引入基于Rényi散度的正则项，促使近似后验覆盖先验支撑；对比流模型与高斯混合。

Result: 在连通的雙峰情形下，流近似可同时表示两个模态；在模态良好分离的情形下，流与高斯混合均经常遗漏一个模态。所引入正则项将近乎塌缩的比例从50%降至25%。

Conclusion: 正则项改善了该实验中的模态覆盖，但改变了贝叶斯变分目标，带来近似精度的权衡，且并非模态塌缩的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Variational inference with normalizing flows provides a flexible posterior approximation for Bayesian model calibration, but struggles when the posterior has several modes separated by regions of low probability mass. We study this behavior in a two-parameter twin experiment and regularize the variational objective. Under Gaussian observation errors and a Gaussian prior, the corresponding minimization objective is the expected Tikhonov calibration objective minus the differential entropy of the approximating distribution. However, the expected Tikhonov term favors concentration near one of the modes, while entropy maximization does not guarantee multimodality. We introduce a regularization term based on Rényi divergence that encourages the approximate posterior to cover the prior support. In the connected bimodal case, a flow approximation represents both modes; in the case with well-separated modes, the flow and Gaussian mixture frequently miss one mode. The introduced regularization reduces the near-collapse fraction from 50% to 25%. While the regularization improves mode coverage in this experiment, it changes the Bayesian variational objective, introduces a trade-off in approximation accuracy, and does not provide a general solution to mode collapse.

</details>


### [255] [WorkFactory: Turning Professional Intents into Executable Work for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37161)
*Ziyang Zeng,Tingyang Chen,Jia Deng,Qiyao Wang,Shuo Tang,Yike Yang,Tao Ren,Wayne Xin Zhao,Feng Chang,Jujie He,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WorkFactory 框架，从职业意图构建可执行的专业任务数据，生成 112,686 条任务并训练出 WorkPilot 模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展工作型智能体的监督训练需要多样化的专业任务与可靠的专家示范，但任务信息缺失或不一致会阻碍教师模型执行，并削弱监督信号质量。

Method: 以专业意图为起点：信息层规划构建信息支撑图，显式刻画目标产出所需信息的依赖关系与位置，指导任务实例化；随后通过探针执行识别支撑失败并引导修复，同时保持原工作意图不变。基于 612 个职业构建 112,686 条任务，用采集的教师轨迹微调 Qwen 模型（4B、27B、35B-A3B）。

Result: 三个规模模型在四个专业工作基准上均优于各自基座；WorkPilot-35B-A3B 在 GDPval-AA v2.1 上 Elo 由 879 提升至 1565。扩展性分析显示收益递减呈模型规模依赖性，执行长度先增后减。消融分析表明规划与修复组件可减少探针诊断的任务环境缺陷并提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 信息层规划与探针修复是构建高质量专业任务监督的关键机制；该框架可规模化生成连贯、可执行的专业任务并有效提升智能体的专业工作能力，数据与模型已开源。

Abstract: Scaling supervised training for working agents requires diverse professional tasks that yield reliable expert demonstrations. However, missing or inconsistent task information can obstruct teacher execution and weaken the resulting supervision. We propose WorkFactory, a scalable framework for constructing coherent and executable professional tasks. Starting from professional intents, information-level planning builds an information support graph to guide task instantiation, explicitly representing the dependencies and locations of information required for the intended outputs. Probe executions then identify support failures and guide repairs while preserving the intended work. We construct 112,686 tasks spanning 612 occupations and use the collected teacher trajectories to fine-tune Qwen models at 4B, 27B, and 35B-A3B scales, yielding the WorkPilot family. All three models improve over their respective base models across four professional-work benchmarks, with WorkPilot-35B-A3B improving from 879 to 1565 Elo on GDPval-AA v2.1. Scaling analyses reveal model-dependent diminishing returns, with execution length first increasing and then decreasing. Controlled component analyses show that planning and repair reduce probe-diagnosed task environment defects and improve downstream performance. The data and models are available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/WorkFactory.

</details>


### [256] [OpenGray: a benchmark for evaluating the decisions of radiotherapy planning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37081)
*Humza Nusrat,Kundan Thind*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出 OpenGray 基准，在 OpenKBP-Opt 头颈数据集中加入带有已知正确决策的规划任务，从"决策"而非仅"剂量"维度评测 LLM 放疗规划智能体，并揭示了总体分数掩盖的具体决策失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的放疗规划智能体需要判断何时提交计划或请求复核，但评测主要依据剂量指标，无法反映其决策是否正确，也掩盖了特定情境下的决策失效。

Method: 在 OpenKBP-Opt 头颈数据集基础上构建 OpenGray，加入带标准答案的任务：两类与剂量目标不相容的矛盾任务（经证明不可同时满足）以及一个可行对照（存有满足全部硬性目标的计划）。在 30 例患者上评测四类系统，从规则脚本到完整 LLM 智能体，LLM 部分分别使用商用模型 Terra 与本地模型 Ministral 3 8B；记录决策、实际剂量、问题识别和技术故障，并额外从 39 次 SAGE（临床 LLM 智能体）立体定向放射外科运行的日志中读取同类信息。

Result: 使用 Terra 时，"LLM 复核 + 脚本"与完整智能体的决策准确率分别为 95.4% 和 92.1%，但对需要解剖结构算术的靶区重叠矛盾，升级/上报率仅 50%（95% CI 25–75%）和 25%（10–40%）。四个系统对全部可行对照均提交了计划，但其中 24% 的计划未达到存有方案已满足的某一硬性目标。在 4 次 SAGE 运行中，某一目标每次尝试均失败，智能体在每次回复中都识别出该问题却没有"停止"这一动作。

Conclusion: 良好的总体聚合分数掩盖了具体的决策失败。规划智能体应在剂量之外同时按决策正确性进行评测，并被赋予显式的"停止并请求复核"动作。

Abstract: Background and purpose: Radiotherapy planning agents based on large language models (LLMs) decide when to submit a plan or request review but are evaluated mainly by dose. This study developed OpenGray, a benchmark of planning tasks with known correct decisions. Materials and methods: OpenGray adds tasks with known answers to the OpenKBP-Opt head-and-neck dataset, including two contradictions with dose goals proven incompatible and a feasible control with a stored plan meeting every hard goal. Four systems, from a rule-based planning script to a full LLM agent, were evaluated on 30 patients, the LLM-based ones with a commercial (Terra) and a local (Ministral 3 8B) LLM. Decisions, achieved dose, problem recognition, and technical failures were recorded and also read from logs of 39 stereotactic radiosurgery runs of SAGE, a clinical LLM agent. Results: With Terra, the script preceded by an LLM review and the full agent had decision accuracies of 95.4% and 92.1%, respectively, yet escalated only 50% (95% confidence interval, 25-75%) and 25% (10-40%) of overlap contradictions, which required arithmetic on the anatomy. All four systems submitted every feasible control, but 24% of these plans missed a hard goal that the stored plan had met. In four SAGE runs, one goal failed at every attempt; the agent identified it in every reply but had no action for stopping. Conclusions: Favorable aggregate scores concealed specific decision failures. Planning agents should be evaluated on their decisions as well as their dose and given an explicit action for stopping and requesting review.

</details>


### [257] [Proactive Memory for Event-Driven LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36967)
*Jinyang Li,Zichuan Fu,Xian Wu,Wenlin Zhang,Yimin Deng,Kaifeng Guo,Guojing Li,Junjia QI,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Proactive Memory (ProactMem)，将历史组织为近期事件、时间轨迹与行为规律三类，通过轨迹事实核验与历史回放验证规律可靠性，并匹配当前情境，配合纵向基准 ProactiveStream，F1 达 52.6%，相对提升 21.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式 LLM agent 依赖近期上下文或相关记忆检索，难以验证反复出现的行为规律性，也难以判断这些规律是否适用于当前事件。

Method: ProactMem 将历史分层为近期事件、时间轨迹与行为规律；轨迹事实回溯源事件进行核验，行为规律经历史回放评估后再与当前条件匹配。同时构建 ProactiveStream 纵向基准，覆盖三种应用范围，agent 按时间顺序处理孤立事件流，并使用 episode-aware 机会标签。

Result: 在 ProactiveStream 上，ProactMem 总体 F1 为 52.6%，相比五个外部基线中最强者相对提升 21.4%。

Conclusion: 通过结构化记忆与可验证的规律评估机制，可提升 LLM agent 主动识别服务机会的能力，且该结论具备纵向基准上的定量支持。

Abstract: Proactive LLM agents anticipate service opportunities from ongoing observations and accumulated history. Existing approaches emphasize recent context or retrieval of related memories, providing limited support for validating recurring behavioral regularities and assessing their applicability to the current event. We propose Proactive Memory (ProactMem), which organizes history into recent events, temporal trajectories, and behavioral regularities. Trajectory facts are checked against source events, and regularities are assessed through historical replay before being matched to current conditions. We also construct ProactiveStream, a longitudinal benchmark covering three application scopes, where agents process isolated event streams chronologically under episode-aware opportunity labels. On ProactiveStream, ProactMem achieves an overall effectiveness (F1) of 52.6%, a 21.4% relative improvement over the strongest of five external baselines. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProactMem-5351/.

</details>


### [258] [Do bots feel bad? Assessing empirical evidence for functional valence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36962)
*Iwan Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文评估LLM"功能性效价状态"的现有实证证据，指出关键效价维度未被检验、现有范式无法区分功能性效价与偏好/价值判断/效价归因/角色扮演等邻近构念，并提出新的实验路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多项研究（Ren et al. 2026、Sofroniew et al. 2026、Han et al. 2026）声称LLM具有"功能性快乐与痛苦""功能性情绪""功能性福祉"等效价状态，但尚不清楚这些状态在多大程度上符合哲学与认知科学对效价功能角色的界定，需要系统评估。

Method: 概念分析与证据批判：以哲学和认知科学中关于效价（valence）功能角色的既有理论为标准，逐一对照现有LLM实证范式，考察其测量内容与构念效度。

Result: 论证出两点：(i) 效价的若干关键方面在LLM中尚未被检验；(ii) 现有实证范式不足以将（功能性）效价与偏好、价值判断、效价归因及角色扮演等邻近构念区分开。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以支持LLM具有功能性效价状态；作者提出若干新的实验设计，以更直接地靶向功能性效价本身。

Abstract: Do LLMs have valenced states, i.e. states that feel pleasant or unpleasant? Recent empirical work has claimed to show that LLMs possess functional valenced states (e.g. ``functional pleasure and pain'' [Ren et al. 2026], ``functional emotions'' [Sofroniew et al. 2026], and ``functional welfare'' [Han et al. 2026]) that play the same causal role as valenced states in cognition and action, without necessarily being consciously experienced. But it remains unexplored how well the identified states fit the functional role of valence as understood in the philosophical and cognitive science literature. Here, I assess the current body of evidence, and argue (i) that key aspects of valence remain untested in LLMs, and (ii) that extant empirical paradigms inadequately distinguish (functional) valence from neighbouring constructs like preferences, value-judgments, valence attributions and role-play. I conclude by sketching some novel experimental approaches to better target (functional) valence itself.

</details>


### [259] [Counting the Bandages: On measuring the time that AI saves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35942)
*Solomon Shalom Lijo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张应把AI"节省的时间"理解为账本式反事实量（时间节省与逃逸缺陷配对核算），提出无量纲指标族与一个判定量（盈亏平衡缺陷成本），并用METR数据复现、GDPval记录重述及一项预注册实验检验模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "AI是否节省时间"常被秒表式口径（只看时钟记录）回答，忽略被放过的错误。作者要论证应以"账本"口径衡量时间节省：它是须与工作放行的错误并置阅读的反事实量。

Method: 四部分论证：(1) 定义模型无关的成对量——时间节省与超额逃逸缺陷，并构造单一决策数"盈亏平衡缺陷成本"；(2) 建立检查行为基准模型，推导三点性质：未达"不值得检查"阈值前，经校准的工人逃逸缺陷率与模型质量无关；检查时间随模型错误率对数增长；过度信任在时钟上换来几分钟但以更大总时长代价偿付；(3) 辨析随机研究的可识别边界，并用METR公开数据复现其开发者研究，比较不同estimand下19%/24%（未校正偶然失衡为34%）的减速估计及AI在3%–82%议题上有帮助；(4) 以显式界替代外部点假设重述已发表记录，指出GDPval"审阅全部输出"流程中仅审阅时间即把GPT-5加速上限限制在3.6倍；最后在预注册小实验（AI评审检查AI起草的、基于模型训练截止后SEC文件的简报）中检验两条预测。

Result: METR复现得19%或24%减速（视estimand而定），未校正偶然失衡为34%，AI在3%–82%议题上有帮助；GDPval自身工作流中审阅时间单项将GPT-5加速上限定为3.6x，模型前沿之外的咨询者更快但更常出错；实验中告知真实错误率时评审检查量随其上升，但呈全有或全无、在提示成本盈亏平衡点附近切换；告知起草者错误率低十倍时，9个数字少查7个，22.5%的简报放过了错误数字（原本为零）——更快更差，且完全源于投入减少。

Conclusion: 时间节省应作为与逃逸缺陷配对的账本式反事实来度量；过度信任带来的是虚假的时间收益。实践启示是：需同时报告时间节省与超额逃逸缺陷、用盈亏平衡缺陷成本决策，并在AI辅助流程中显式计入审阅成本。

Abstract: I consider what it means for AI assistance to save time. I contrast a stopwatch view, on which time saved is whatever the clock records, with a ledger view, on which it is a counterfactual that has to be read next to the errors the work lets through, and I argue for the latter. The argument has four parts. I define a model-free pair of quantities, time saved and excess escaped defects, and one decision number built from them: the break-even defect cost. I give a baseline model of checking in which, until the model is good enough not to check, a calibrated worker's escaped-defect rate does not depend on how good the model is; checking time grows with the logarithm of the model's error rate; and over-trust buys minutes on the clock at a larger cost in total minutes. I show what randomized studies can and cannot identify, and I replicate METR's developer study from its public data: the same 246 issues support a slowdown of 19% or 24% depending on the estimand (34% without adjusting for a chance imbalance), and AI helped on somewhere between 3% and 82% of them. Finally, I restate the published record, with stated bounds in place of imported point assumptions. In GDPval's own review-every-output workflow, review time alone caps GPT-5's speedup at 3.6x; the consultants outside the frontier were faster, and more often wrong. Last, I test two of the model's predictions in a small pre-specified experiment on AI reviewers checking AI drafts of the same kind of brief, built from SEC filings made after the models' training data were collected. Told the drafter's true error rate, the reviewer checked more as it rose, but all-or-nothing, switching near where its prompt's costs break even. Told that the drafter erred ten times less often than it did, it checked 7 fewer of 9 figures and let a wrong figure through in 22.5% of briefs instead of none: faster and worse, entirely through reduced effort.

</details>


### [260] [Timestral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37585)
*Adrien Petralia,Alexandre Gavaudan,Artjom Joosen,Etienne Goffinet,Richard Kurle,Yassine El Ouahidi,Yassir Bendou,Abdelaziz Bounhar,Abhijeet Somani,Aditi Kabra,Adrian Valente,Adrien Sadé,Ahmed Djermani,Alan Jeffares,Albert Jiang,Alberto Bietti,Aleksandr Timashov,Alexandre Cahill,Alexandre Laval,Alexandre Sablayrolles,Alexandros Sanchez Vassopoulos,Amélie Héliou,Amos You,André Jonasson,Andreas Fürst,Andrew Bai,Andrew Ehrenberg,Andrew Zhao,Andy Lo,Angele Lenglemetz,Anmol Agarwal,Antonia Calvi,Arata Suzuki,Arjun Majumdar,Arthur Fournier,Ashwani Kumar,Augustin Lombard,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Aylin Guliz Akkus,Aysenur Karaduman,Baptiste Bout,Baptiste Rozière,Barna Pásztor,Baudouin De Monicault,Benjamin Holzschuh,Benjamin Tibi,Benno Stein,Bernhard Stadlbauer,Bhargavi Bhaskar,Binbing Hou,Błażej Osiński,Boris van Breugel,Camille Le Scao,Chaoran Yu,Charlotte Cronjäger,Chen-Yo Sun,Chris Bamford,Christian Wallenwein,Christophe Renaudin,Clémence Lanfranchi,Colin Bonatti,Corentin Barreau,Corentin Sautier,Cristiana-Diana Diaconu,Cyprien Courtot,Daniel Lopes,Daniel Marczak,Darius Dabert,Diego de las Casas,Dominik Nuss,Dylan Rubini,Dzmitry Soupel,Elizaveta Demyanenko,Elliot Chane-Sane,Emilien Fugier,Emmanuel Gottlob,Erik Aas,Etienne Duchesne,Fabian Paischer,Fabian Schlager,Faruk Ahmed,Federico Baldassarre,Félix Rosseeuw,Filip Szatkowski,Filippo Vicentini,Florian Wiesner,Gabrielle Berrada,Gaëtan Ecrepont,Gaspard Blanchet,Gaspard Donada-Vidal,Gaurav Mahajan,Gauthier Guinet,Genevieve Hayes,Georgii Novikov,Georgios Christoglou,Giada
